### [1] [Fysiverse-3D-SimReady Technical Report: Agentic Physical Simulation for Pragmatic 3D World Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31715)
*Lintao Wang,Mingyang Sun,Yang Liu,Dingkang Yang,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Fysiverse-3D-SimReady，一个从单张 RGB 图像重建"仿真就绪"多物体场景的 grounded refinement 框架，通过重力对齐、可微渲染位姿精修与支撑/接触校正，生成稳定可执行的物理场景状态，支持目标驱动的物理交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic recognition 要求视觉感知超越静态场景理解，输出支撑"感知—推理—行动"闭环的结构化场景表示。然而现有单图 3D 生成方法大多只产出视觉上合理的物体资产，而非可直接用于仿真的场景状态：将独立生成的网格拼到同一空间时，会出现与输入相机不对齐、违反重力、与邻近物体穿模、在物理仿真下不稳定等问题。

Method: Fysiverse-3D-SimReady 框架：在实例与地面提示下，将生成的目标网格放入统一的重力对齐场景；通过可微渲染精修其相机空间位姿；再在场景层面校正支撑关系与接触，以保证物理执行的稳定性。随后由 agentic 仿真工作流将场景特定的任务目标转化为可执行的物理 rollout，并从原始相机视角渲染。

Result: 实验表明，相较于现有单图重建与场景生成基线，该方法在输入视角对齐、接触合理性、物理稳定性上均有提升，并能够仅凭单张图像实现目标条件化的物理交互。

Conclusion: 该工作将单图 3D 生成从"视觉合理的资产"推进到"仿真就绪的场景状态"，为基于单图的 agentic 物理交互提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Agentic recognition requires visual perception to move beyond static scene understanding and produce structured scene representations that support the perception--reasoning--action loop. Existing single-image 3D generation methods, however, mainly produce visually plausible object assets rather than simulation-ready scene states. When independently generated meshes are composed in a shared space, they may fail to align with the input camera, violate gravity, interpenetrate nearby objects, or become unstable under physics simulation. We present Fysiverse-3D-SimReady, a grounded refinement framework for reconstructing simulation-ready multi-object scenes from a single RGB image with instance and ground prompts. The method places generated object meshes into a shared gravity-aligned scene, refines their camera-space poses through differentiable rendering, and corrects scene-level supports and contacts for stable physical execution. The scene is then used by an agentic simulation workflow, which converts a scene-specific task goal into an executable physics rollout rendered from the original camera view. Experiments show that Fysiverse-3D-SimReady improves input-view alignment, contact plausibility, and physical stability over existing single-image reconstruction and scene generation baselines, while enabling goal-conditioned physical interactions from a single image.

</details>


### [2] [PanoFuse: Panorama-Enhanced Vision-Language-Action Learning with Decoupled Semantic-Geometric Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31717)
*Peng Xu,Haoran Lin,Wanjun Jia,Kai Luo,Wenrui Chen,Zhiyong Li,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoFuse 通过引入全景分支与解耦语义-几何路由（DSGR），为 VLA 策略补充全局全景感知，在多个真实操作评测中取得 52.9% 的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略多依赖视野有限的透视相机，缺乏全局场景上下文，在视觉遮挡、干扰物和未见环境下操作不可靠。

Method: 提出 PanoFuse 全景增强 VLA 框架：使用预训练全景基础模型构建全景分支，从全向观测中提取互补的语义与几何表示；设计 DSGR，将语义和几何作为独立上下文流，通过结构化块级注意力选择性路由到下游状态与动作表示；并构建同步数据采集管线，采集包含全景 RGB、腕部视图、语言指令、机器人状态与动作的真实操作数据集。

Result: 在七个评测设置中平均成功率为 52.9%，优于所评估基线，并在新物体、未见背景和干扰物丰富场景下取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 全局全景感知与解耦语义-几何路由能有效补充局部操作观测，提升 VLA 策略在遮挡、干扰和未见环境中的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have shown promising performance in language-conditioned robotic manipulation. However, most existing VLA systems rely on conventional perspective cameras with limited fields of view, often missing global scene context and leading to unreliable manipulation under visual occlusions, distractors, and unseen environments. In this work, we propose PanoFuse, a panorama-enhanced VLA framework that complements local manipulation observations with global panoramic perception. PanoFuse introduces a dedicated panoramic branch that leverages a pretrained panoramic foundation model to extract complementary semantic and geometric representations from omnidirectional observations. Rather than directly mixing these heterogeneous features, we introduce Decoupled Semantic-Geometric Routing (DSGR), which maintains semantic and geometric representations as separate context streams and selectively routes both to downstream state and action representations through structured block-wise attention. This design provides the action expert with global spatial context while preserving task-relevant semantic information from the pretrained VLA backbone. We further develop a synchronized data collection pipeline and construct a new real-world manipulation dataset containing panoramic RGB observations, wrist-view images, language instructions, robot states, and actions. Across seven evaluation settings, PanoFuse achieves an average success rate of 52.9%, outperforming the evaluated baselines and achieving consistent gains under novel-object, unseen-background, and distractor-rich settings. Code and data will be released publicly at this https URL .

</details>


### [3] [TriO: Tri-Modal Unsupervised Occupancy World Model for Anything Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32013)
*Quinlan Sykora,Sourav Biswas,Christopher Diehl,Andrew Cunningham,Thomas Gilles,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriO 是一个多模态无监督世界模型，以相机、LiDAR 和 RADAR 三种传感器同时作为输入与自监督信号，预测 4D 占用、障碍分割、流和 LiDAR，无需额外人工标注，并在多项 3D/4D 任务与零样本道路障碍分割上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖人工标注，且开放集方法在长尾物体分割上存在局限；因此需要一种无需人工标注、能利用多模态自监督提升泛化能力的世界模型。

Method: 提出 TriO：一种多模态无监督世界模型。它将相机、LiDAR、RADAR 三种传感器模态同时作为输入和自监督来源，无需额外人工标注；通过新颖的监督方式，实现从可行驶表面分割任意占用，从而克服现有开放集方法对长尾物体的处理限制。

Result: 在多个 3D 与 4D 任务上取得 SOTA，包括占用预测、流预测和 LiDAR 预测，以及零样本道路障碍分割；在 Argoverse 2、Spotting the Unexpected 等多个数据集上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 多模态自监督可有效替代人工标注，并显著提升长尾开放集分割与 4D 世界建模性能，为无监督多模态感知与预测提供了新方向。

Abstract: We present TriO, a multi-modal unsupervised world model that predicts 4D occupancy, obstacle segmentation, flow and LiDAR. In contrast to prior work, TriO utilizes three distinct sensor modalities (camera, LiDAR, and RADAR) as both inputs and sources of self-supervision, eliminating the need for additional human annotations. Thanks to its novel supervision, the model is able to segment any occupancy from the drivable surface, overcoming the limitations of existing open-set methods in handling long-tail objects. TriO achieves state-of-the-art results in multiple 3D and 4D tasks, including occupancy, flow, and LiDAR prediction, as well as zero-shot road obstacle segmentation across multiple datasets such as Argoverse 2, and Spotting the Unexpected.

</details>


### [4] [CausalDriveBench: Evaluating Causal Reasoning in Vision-Language-Action Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32157)
*Narendiran Chembu,Navvrat Rao,Shreedhar Shreeshail Kodate,Gayatri Srujana Banda,Arko Sarkar,Abhinav Khanna,Rajarshee Das,Umesh Kanala,Siddarth Khandelwal,Kumar Aman,Aish Dubey,Kaustubh Beedkar,Arjun Jain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CausalDriveBench，基于 Pearl 因果层级评估驾驶 VLA 的因果推理，发现现有模型在因果 QA 与反事实轨迹上均表现不足，流畅推理与观测场景轨迹精度并不等于因果理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶 VLA 会输出自然语言推理与预测轨迹，但其推理是否反映场景因果结构尚未被系统检验；需要区分感知显著性与因果相关性，并在关联、干预、反事实及因果发现层面评估因果理解。

Method: 构建基于 nuScenes 的因果场景图，区分因果活跃、休眠与干扰实体；按 Pearl 因果层级四层生成结构化视觉问答，并为高层级提供场景修改后的参考轨迹，实现推理级与动作级双重验证。

Result: 基准含 7,285 个经校验的因果 QA 对和 1,000 条反事实轨迹；评估 10 个驾驶专用 VLA 与 3 个通用 VLM。最佳模型 QA 准确率仅 70.6%，13 个模型中 4 个低于随机；驾驶微调相对同语言骨干通用 VLM 的因果 QA 代价为 2–34 个百分点；因果 QA 与轨迹准确率跨模型统计不相关，反事实提示下轨迹要么过度反应，要么退化为观测场景基线。

Conclusion: 现有驾驶 VLA 尚未证明具备可靠因果理解；流畅的自然语言理由和观测场景下的轨迹精度均不能作为因果理解的证据，需在干预与反事实设定下进行更严格评估。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving produce natural-language reasoning alongside predicted trajectories, but whether this reasoning reflects the causal structure of the scene remains untested. We introduce CausalDriveBench, an evaluation framework grounded in Pearl's Causal Hierarchy (PCH) that tests causal reasoning in driving-specific VLAs through structured visual question answering (QA) and alternative-trajectory prediction. To this end, we construct causal scene graphs over nuScenes that distinguish causally active, dormant, and distractor entities, separating perceptual salience from causal relevance. The benchmark spans all four rungs of PCH (association, intervention, and counterfactual along with causal discovery) for QA generation. For the higher rungs, we additionally provide reference trajectories under specified scene modifications, enabling action-level verification that complements reasoning-level evaluation. In total, the benchmark contains 7,285 verified causal QA pairs and 1,000 counterfactual trajectories derived from nuScenes. We evaluate 10 driving-specific VLAs and 3 general-purpose VLMs, and report three findings. First, the best model reaches only 70.6% QA accuracy, and 4 of 13 models score below random chance. Second, comparing each driving VLA to the general-purpose VLM that shares its language backbone, the cost of driving fine-tuning ranges from 2 to 34 percentage points on causal QA, with post-training design explaining the spread. Third, causal QA and trajectory accuracy are statistically uncorrelated across models: under counterfactual prompts, predicted trajectories either over-react or collapse onto the observed-scene baseline. Taken together, these results show that neither fluent rationales nor accurate observed-scene trajectories constitute evidence of causal understanding.

</details>


### [5] [Devol-ONE: One Autoregressive Mixture of Transformers to Unify Vision-Language-Action and Latent World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32193)
*Hongyi Cai,Yi Herng Ong,Tingshiuan C. Wu,Lim Chiew Hui,Hanxia Li,Kehong Guo,Sze Yuan Cheong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Devol-ONE，一种 Mixture of Transformers 架构，在单一自回归框架内统一视觉语言理解、潜在世界动态预测与动作生成，通过 V-JEPA 动力学流与视觉语言流逐层交叉注意力联合预测未来潜在状态，从而持续塑造动作专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型直接以当前视觉与语言上下文条件化动作，未显式建模场景在候选动作下的演化；而现有 WAM 虽尝试预测未来状态，却将预测与策略学习在架构上分离，仅通过预测输出（像素空间视频生成或独立于策略训练的潜在预测模块）相连，导致语义推理与物理动态未能真正融入动作生成。

Method: 提出 Devol-ONE，基于 Mixture of Transformers 的自回归框架：并行运行视觉语言流与 V-JEPA 预训练动力学流，在每一层通过注意力访问视觉语言 key-value 缓存，在语言引导下预测未来潜在状态；动作专家不再依赖预先计算的固定表示，而是由语义推理与预测的物理动态持续塑造。相较传统先编码 VL token 再送入动作专家的做法，实现逐层统一注意力。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-PLUS、RoboTwin2.0 及真实世界 Flexiv 单臂与双臂平台上进行了大量实验；消融研究表明动态流预测与逐层统一注意力均有效，验证了模型架构的一致性。

Conclusion: Devol-ONE 通过将世界动态预测与策略学习统一于单一自回归 Transformer 架构中，实现了语义理解、物理动态预测与动作生成的深度融合，实验与消融验证了其架构协同设计的有效性。

Abstract: Vision Language Action (VLA) models condition actions directly on current visual and language context, without an explicit account of how the scene evolves under candidate actions. World Action Models (WAM) attempt to address this limitation by predicting future states, but existing designs keep prediction and policy learning architecturally separate, connecting them only through the predicted output, whether through pixel space video generation or a latent forecasting module trained independently of the policy. We present Devol-ONE, a Mixture of Transformers architecture that unifies vision language understanding, latent world dynamics prediction, and action generation within a single autoregressive framework. Instead of encoding vision language tokens once and feeding them to the action expert, Devol-ONE runs autoregressive prediction jointly across a vision language stream and a V-JEPA pretrained dynamics stream, attending to the vision language key-value cache at every layer to forecast future latent states under language guidance. The action expert is in turn shaped continuously by semantic reasoning and predicted physical dynamics rather than by a fixed representation computed in advance. Extensive experiments are conducted on LIBERO, LIBERO-PLUS, RoboTwin2.0 along with real-world evaluation on Flexiv single-arm and dual-arm setups. Ablation studies show the effectiveness of dynamic stream prediction and layer-wise unified attention to validate our model architectural coherency.

</details>


### [6] [REMEDY: How Far Is Video Generation from Medical Education World Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32460)
*Lixing Tan,Yanghao Zhou,Qing Xia,Yuting Guo,Shuai Li,Aimin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出首个用于评估AI生成医学教学演示的基准REMEDY，涵盖12项任务、4类临床场景，通过900张真实演示首帧驱动5个开源视频生成模型生成4,500段视频，并从临床动作遵循、临床特征、视频质量、运动质量四个维度评估。结果显示，视觉逼真度和时序一致性并不能保证临床动作正确，即使最优模型MiniMax-H3在严格临床成功率上也仅达28.25%，细粒度临床动作仍是难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型已能生成逼真视频并有望成为世界模型基础，这为生成医学教学演示提供了可能；但医学教学既要求视觉质量，又要求程序性动作精确，而现有生成器是否能满足这些要求尚未被系统测量。

Method: 提出REMEDY基准：提供来自真实演示视频的900个首帧，覆盖手术室、影像、门诊与床旁、复苏四类场景下的12项任务；使用5个当代开源视频生成模型基于这些首帧生成4,500段视频；将任务特异性临床检查表与视频及运动质量指标结合，从临床动作遵循、临床画像、视频质量、运动质量四个维度进行评测。

Result: 现实外观和时序一致性并不确保正确的临床动作。即使最先进的MiniMax-H3在严格临床成功率上也仅达到28.25%，细粒度临床动作仍具有挑战性。

Conclusion: 这些发现为未来医学教育世界模型的开发奠定了基础并提供了路线图。

Abstract: Recent video generation models produce realistic videos and show potential as a foundation for world models. These advances create opportunities for generating medical teaching demonstrations, which requires both convincing visual quality and precise procedural actions. However, whether current generators can meet these requirements has not been measured. To address this problem, we introduce Readiness Evaluation of Medical Education Demonstration sYnthesis (REMEDY), to our knowledge, the first benchmark for AI-generated medical teaching demonstrations. REMEDY provides 900 first frames from real demonstration videos, covering 12 tasks across four scenarios: operating room, imaging, clinic and bedside, and resuscitation. Five contemporary open-source video generation models produce 4,500 videos from these frames. We combine task-specific clinical checklists with video and motion quality metrics. Evaluation covers four dimensions: clinical action following, clinical profiles, video quality, and motion quality. Our results show that realistic appearance and temporal consistency do not ensure correct clinical actions. Even the most advanced MiniMax-H3 achieves only 28.25% on strict clinical success rate, and fine-grained clinical actions remain challenging. These findings establish a foundation and roadmap for developing future medical education world models.

</details>


### [7] [JEPA Learns What the Mask Leaves Unrecoverable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32481)
*Peng Xie,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将JEPA对掩码几何的敏感性解释为一种线性测量与零空间现象：块掩码移除不可恢复的粗尺度内容，从而阻断低级先验捷径；散点/条带等可恢复掩码则留下捷径。作者在151次预训练中验证了该测量解释的可区分预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）对输入掩码方式异常敏感：块掩码通常有效，散点掩码无效，但既有解释多为经验观察，缺乏机制性刻画。

Method: 把掩码视为线性测量：在紧支撑小波基下，若原子支撑完全落入隐藏区域，则该原子处于测量的零空间，不会在可见数据中留下痕迹。JEPA损失仅要求编码上下文足以推断目标，因此任何可被低级先验恢复的目标都会形成捷径，而移动平均目标编码器可使该捷径自洽。真正消除捷径的是掩码造成的不可恢复粗尺度内容，并需保证每个目标可达范围内有足够上下文。作者在训练前对该不可恢复内容进行评分，并在151次预训练中检验由此得到的独特预测。

Result: ImageNet-100上，条带掩码与块掩码在面积和连续性上匹配但可恢复，其线性top-1为40.3%，随机掩码为40.8%，块掩码为64.3%；同一几何族内，留下不可恢复内容最少的放置比最多的低6.5个百分点（五组种子对）；像素目标跨度7个点，而潜变量目标跨度25个点；冻结目标时，随机与块掩码的差距（同种GPU上19个点）缩小到1.5，说明几何通过编码器自产目标起作用。UCF101上，掩码比例决定“内容”还是“可达性”成为约束；整帧移除在空间上不可恢复但可由相邻帧恢复，在两种比例下都最差；在V-JEPA自有掩码上，完整批处理与截断差别很小（36.0%对35.1%），而使块内100个目标token可见可提升到48.7%，隐藏块外100个上下文token则不能（33.7%）。

Conclusion: JEPA的有效性由掩码留下的不可恢复粗尺度内容与目标上下文可达性共同决定；掩码几何主要通过目标编码器为自身生成的目标起作用，而非仅由可见信息量决定。该测量视角为掩码设计提供了可预训练前评分的机制性解释。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures are unusually sensitive to how the input is masked: block masks work, scattered masks do not, and the explanations are empirical. We give a measurement account. A mask is a linear measurement, and in a compactly supported wavelet basis every atom whose support lies inside the hidden region falls in the measurement's null space and leaves no trace in the data. The JEPA loss asks only that the encoded context suffice for the target, so a target that a low-level prior can recover admits a shortcut, one the moving-average target encoder can make self-consistent. What removes the shortcut is the coarse-scale content the mask leaves unrecoverable, provided enough context stays within reach of each target. We score that content before training and test the account's distinctive predictions in 151 pre-training runs. On ImageNet-100, strip masks match blocks in area and contiguity yet are recoverable, and they land at 40.3% linear top-1, beside random masks at 40.8%, against 64.3% for blocks; within one geometry family, the placements that leave the least unrecoverable content lose 6.5 points to those that leave the most, over five seed pairs; pixel targets span 7 points where latent targets span 25; and against a frozen target the gap between random and block masks, 19 points on the same kind of GPU, closes to 1.5, so the geometry acts through the target the encoder produces for itself. On UCF101 the masking ratio decides which condition, content or reach, binds; removing whole frames, unrecoverable in space but recoverable from neighbouring frames, is worst at both ratios; and on V-JEPA's own masks, batching them intact instead of truncated changes little (36.0% against 35.1%), whereas making 100 target tokens inside the blocks visible lifts them to 48.7% and hiding 100 context tokens outside the blocks does not (33.7%).

</details>


### [8] [UnStep: Training-Free Acceleration of Causal Video Diffusion with Fewer Steps Than Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32518)
*Youssef Mansour,Enis Simsar,Fadime Sener,Markos Georgopoulos,Albert Pumarola,Ali Thabet,Edgar Schoenfeld*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UnStep：一种免训练推理包装器，通过减少DiT步数、限制KV缓存时间窗口，并结合重加噪+清洁缓存精修与注意力投影截断SVD，配合DiT/VAE运行时优化，使少步因果视频扩散模型在单H100达50 FPS、GB200达77 FPS且无质量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步因果视频扩散模型虽显著快于其蒸馏教师，但对实时流式视频生成仍不够快；需要在无需重训练的前提下进一步降低推理计算量。

Method: 提出免训练包装器UnStep：推理时使用少于蒸馏阶段的DiT步数，并限制注意力KV缓存保留的时间窗口。为补偿步数减少与注意力窗口化带来的质量损失，引入两种仅推理机制：将生成潜帧重加噪到接近干净水平，并复用现有clean-cache pass进行精修；对DiT注意力的value与output投影做截断SVD。同时采用保质量的DiT与VAE解码运行时栈：更高效的注意力调用与KV索引、带缓存系数的融合Triton RoPE、以及优化内存布局、精度与卷积核的VAE解码。

Result: 通过降低计算并优化运行时栈，UnStep大幅超越现有方法，建立因果视频扩散的新吞吐范式：单张H100达50 FPS，GB200达77 FPS，且无质量损失、无需重训练。

Conclusion: UnStep证明仅靠推理级优化即可显著加速少步因果视频扩散模型，实现高质量实时/流式视频生成。

Abstract: Distilling bidirectional multi-step video diffusion transformers into few-step causal models has become a common approach for streaming video generation. While these few-step students are significantly faster than the teachers they are distilled from, they remain slow for real-time generation. In this work we present UnStep, a training-free wrapper that accelerates few-step causal video models at inference by running them with fewer diffusion transformer (DiT) steps than during distillation and limiting the temporal window retained in the attention KV cache. We propose two inference-only mechanisms to recover quality lost by step reduction and attention windowing: renoising the generated latent frames to a near-clean level and reusing the existing clean-cache pass to refine them, and applying truncated SVD to the DiT attention value and output projections. We also accelerate inference with a quality-preserving runtime stack for the DiT and VAE decoder, including more efficient attention calls and KV indexing, fused Triton RoPE with cached coefficients, and VAE decoding with optimized memory layout, precision, and convolution kernels. By reducing computation and optimizing the runtime stack, UnStep sets a new throughput regime for causal video diffusion, by running substantially faster than current methods, reaching 50 FPS on a single H100 without quality loss, and 77 FPS on GB200, all without retraining.

</details>


### [9] [DraftAttention2: Fast Video Diffusion with Low-Resolution-Guided Mixed-Precision Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32628)
*Rui Ding,Haopeng Li,Weize Ma,Yufa Zhou,Yitong Li,Xiaoling Zhou,Jiashuo Cao,Liyang Li,Hua Geng,Jiuxiang Gu,Jun Lin,Enze Xie,Xuan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DraftAttention2 是一个免训练（training-free）的视频生成注意力加速框架：利用低分辨率“草稿”注意力图，联合决定注意力块的取舍与数值精度分配，并通过融合算子实现实际加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散 Transformer 提升了视频生成质量，但时空 token 上的注意力开销随分辨率与时长增长而急剧上升，现有高效视频生成方法的质量—效率权衡仍不够理想。

Method: 1) 用空间 2D 平均池化与最大池化的 query/key 捕捉互补的区域统计量，估计注意力块的重要性；2) 通过共享排序，在可配置预算下为重要块分配高精度、为次要但保留的块分配低精度、其余块跳过；3) 将稀疏化误差与注意力加权量化误差分离分析，给出“用低比特计算恢复被跳过交互可收紧输出误差上界”的条件，进而支持保留更多低精度交互、仅对注意力质量大的块使用高精度；4) 为把细粒度分配落地为速度收益，设计融合的操作数准备与单一注意力核，内含按精度划分的阶段，跨精度共享数据搬运、softmax 统计与输出累加。

Result: 实验显示该方法在质量—效率权衡上优于现有高效视频生成方法；优势在少步视频扩散中尤为明显，稀疏与 4/8 比特混合精度结合既显著提升生成质量，又保持可观加速。代码已开源。

Conclusion: DraftAttention2 以低分辨率草稿注意力为信号，统一调度注意力稀疏与混合精度，并借助融合内核实现免训练的实际加速，为视频扩散模型提供更优的质量—效率折中。

Abstract: Video generation has broad applications in content creation and entertainment. Diffusion transformers have advanced the quality of generated videos, but attention over spatiotemporal tokens becomes increasingly expensive as video resolution and duration increase. We present DraftAttention2, a training-free framework that uses the low-resolution draft attention map to jointly select attention blocks and assign their numerical precision. Specifically, spatial 2D average- and max-pooled queries and keys capture complementary regional statistics to estimate block importance, and a shared ranking assigns higher precision to important blocks, lower precision to less important retained blocks, and skips the rest under configurable budgets. Our analysis separates sparsification error from attention-weighted quantization error, establishing when recovering skipped interactions with low-bit computation tightens the output-error bound. This analysis motivates retaining more interactions at low precision while reserving higher precision for blocks with larger attention mass. To translate these fine-grained assignments into practical speedups, we further develop fused operand preparation and a single attention kernel with precision-specific phases, sharing data movement, softmax statistics, and output accumulation across precisions. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a superior quality-efficiency trade-off over existing efficient video generation methods. Notably, its advantage is particularly pronounced for few-step video diffusion, where jointly combining sparsity with 4- and 8-bit mixed-precision computation substantially improves generation quality while retaining significant acceleration. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [10] [RCVLA: 4D Radar-Grounded Semantic Reasoning and Trajectory Arbitration for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32681)
*Lianqing Zheng,Xiaokai Bai,Yixuan Luo,Runwei Guan,Minghao Liu,Zhiqiang Wei,Hui-liang Shen,Xichan Zhu,Zhixiong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了 Cap4DR 与 OmniHD-QA 两个大规模 4D 雷达-视觉-语言数据集，并提出 RCVLA 雷达-相机 VLA 框架（含语义推理模块 RCVLA-Sem 与轨迹仲裁模块 RCVLA-Phys），在驾驶问答与轨迹规划上显著优于 OmniDrive 等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D 雷达能提供互补于视觉语义的几何与运动线索，但将其集成进 VLA 模型面临两大挑战：一是需要雷达与语言的对齐以支撑语义推理，二是需要显式利用雷达测量来精炼与筛选轨迹。现有工作缺乏相应的大规模对齐数据与兼顾语义推理和物理轨迹优化的统一框架。

Method: 数据层面：构建 Cap4DR（86,016 条雷达-图像-文本样本，用于对齐预训练）与 OmniHD-QA（520,161 个问答对，覆盖场景描述、关键物体推理、占据理解与轨迹规划，用于指令微调）。模型层面：提出 RCVLA 框架。RCVLA-Sem 在相机与雷达 token 间进行门控双向交互，用于驾驶问答与参考轨迹生成，并用辅助头输出物体与占据查询；RCVLA-Phys 以这些查询和簇级雷达测量为条件，通过截断扩散精炼参考引导的轨迹候选，并利用雷达推导的碰撞时间（TTC）风险对候选评分进行校准。

Result: 在 OmniHD-QA 上，RCVLA-Sem 相比 OmniDrive 将 CIDEr 提升 9.92 分，关键物体速度误差降低 21.9%。RCVLA-Phys 进一步将平均 L2 误差从 0.348 m 降至 0.259 m，平均开环碰撞率从 0.576% 降至 0.175%。消融研究显示，语言对齐的雷达 token 提升了语义推理能力，簇级雷达测量与风险校准提升了轨迹仲裁性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过大规模雷达-语言对齐数据与显式雷达测量利用，可以同时增强 VLA 模型的语义推理与轨迹规划能力；语言对齐雷达 token 与簇级测量/风险校准分别在语义推理与轨迹仲裁中发挥关键作用。代码将开源。

Abstract: 4D radar provides geometric and motion cues that complement visual semantics, but integrating it into vision-language-action (VLA) models requires both radar--language alignment for semantic reasoning and explicit use of radar measurements for trajectory refinement and selection. To support these capabilities, we construct Cap4DR with 86,016 radar-image-text samples for alignment pretraining and OmniHD-QA with 520,161 question-answer pairs for instruction tuning across scene description, key-object reasoning, occupancy understanding, and trajectory planning. Building on these datasets, we propose RCVLA, a radar-camera VLA framework consisting of a radar-grounded semantic reasoning stage (RCVLA-Sem) and a trajectory arbitration stage (RCVLA-Phys). RCVLA-Sem performs gated bidirectional interaction between camera and radar tokens for driving question answering and reference trajectory generation, while auxiliary heads provide object and occupancy queries. RCVLA-Phys refines reference-guided trajectory candidates through truncated diffusion conditioned on these queries and cluster-level radar measurements, then calibrates candidate scores using radar-derived time-to-collision risk. On OmniHD-QA, RCVLA-Sem improves CIDEr by 9.92 points and reduces key-object velocity error by $21.9\%$ relative to OmniDrive. RCVLA-Phys further reduces average L2 error from $0.348$ to $0.259\,\mathrm{m}$ and average open-loop collision rate from $0.576\%$ to $0.175\%$ relative to RCVLA-Sem. Ablation studies further show that language-aligned radar tokens improve semantic reasoning, while cluster-level radar measurements and risk calibration improve trajectory arbitration. Code will be released.

</details>


### [11] [From Feed-Forward to Flow: Unifying Reconstruction and Generation Is Easier Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32761)
*Haoru Wang,Qianfan Shen,Kai Ye,Wenzheng Chen,Baoquan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个基于流的统一框架，用共享的干净目标预测器同时实现重建与生成：单步推理完成直接重建，多步流展开实现条件生成，并在外观与几何3D任务上实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重建与生成长期分属独立范式，且各有失效模式：前馈重建会把歧义平均成模糊，条件生成则会臆造合理但不符合场景的细节。受Atlas等空间世界模型统一重建与生成的成功启发，作者希望将两者统一到同一模型与骨干中。

Method: 提出一个统一的基于流的公式，使用共享的干净目标预测器：在单步端点处直接进行重建，并通过多步流展开实现条件生成。通过一个受控的玩具实验揭示机制，并将该公式实例化到外观与几何两类3D任务中（JiT-LVSM 用于新视角合成，JUSt3R 用于几何预测）。

Result: 玩具实验表明：单步时预测器坍缩到条件均值（类似前馈方法），偏向一致性而非多样性；多步推理时，生成细节的保真度随上下文丰富度提升，更近的观测减少歧义并得到更匹配的细节。JiT-LVSM 提升了新视角合成的感知与分布质量；JUSt3R 在保留有竞争力的单步几何预测的同时，增加多步推理能力，减少了面纱（veil）与飞点伪影，在更高测试时算力下产生更干净的表面结构。

Conclusion: 重建与生成可以共享同一公式与同一骨干，其行为由去噪配置（单步vs.多步）决定，说明这种统一出人意料地简单。

Abstract: Reconstruct where the images provide evidence, and generate where they do not: recent success of spatial world models such as Atlas (World Labs Team, 2026) highlights the value of unifying reconstruction and generation in one model. Yet the two have long lived in separate paradigms with distinctive failure modes: feed-forward reconstruction averages ambiguity into blur, while conditional generation invents plausible but scene-inconsistent detail. In this work, we present a unified flow-based formulation for reconstruction and generation, where a shared clean-target predictor performs direct reconstruction at its single-step endpoint and unfolds conditional generation through multi-step flow. A controlled toy study reveals the mechanism: with a single step, the predictor collapses to the conditional mean just like feed-forward methods, favoring consistency over diversity. With multi-step inference, the fidelity of generated details grows with context richness: closer observations reduce ambiguity and yield better-matched details. We further instantiate the formulation in appearance and geometry 3D tasks. JiT-LVSM improves perceptual and distributional quality in novel view synthesis, while JUSt3R retains competitive single-step geometry prediction with additional multi-step inference capabilities that reduces veil and flying-pixel artifacts, producing cleaner surface structure with greater test-time compute. Together, they show that reconstruction and generation can share both a formulation and a backbone, with their behavior governed by denoising configuration---making unification surprisingly simple.

</details>


### [12] [Train Together or Merge Later? Unifying VLA Experts via a Shared Action Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33125)
*Zhizhen Zhang,Yuxia Fu,Zijian Wang,Helen Huang,Yadan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PolicyWeave：通过合并兼容的后训练与上下文引导稀疏合并，将独立训练的任务专家组合成强多任务VLA策略，在RoboCasa365等基准上显著优于联合训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务VLA策略通常采用联合共同训练，但其性能往往不及各任务独立训练。模型合并是自然解法，但强单任务专家未必能合并出强策略，作者识别出任务特定动作接口（动作归一化与动作编码器/解码器）改变是造成不兼容的关键来源。

Method: 提出PolicyWeave：后训练阶段所有专家保留共享base策略的动作接口，任务适配仅限动作模型隐藏层的LoRA更新，以提升合并兼容性；部署时用初始视觉-语言上下文对专家LoRA更新打分，通过留一层的排序稳定性确定专家集合，形成固定的稀疏加权合并以抑制无关任务干扰。

Result: 在GR00T N1.5与RoboCasa365的18个任务上（仅用10%目标任务演示做SFT）：保留共享动作接口使四种静态合并方法的平均成功率从17.0%提升至52.8%；PolicyWeave达64.7%，任务特定RL后达74.1%，而联合RL仅60.7%。LIBERO-10与AgileX Piper机械臂实验进一步验证了长时程与真实世界操作中独立学习技能的部署能力。

Conclusion: 保留共享动作接口并配合上下文引导的稀疏合并，可在不做联合后训练的情况下有效融合独立专家，实现更强的多任务VLA策略。

Abstract: Co-training offers a straightforward way to build a multi-task vision-language-action (VLA) policy, but can fall short of the performance achieved by training each task independently. The challenge is to retain these task-specific gains in a multi-task policy without joint post-training. Combining independently trained experts through model merging is a natural approach, yet strong individual experts do not necessarily yield a strong merged policy. We identify one source of this incompatibility: task-specific changes to the action interface, comprising action normalization and the action encoder and decoder. We propose PolicyWeave, combining merge-compatible post-training with context-guided sparse merging. During post-training, all experts retain the common base policy's action interface, while task adaptation is restricted to LoRA updates in the hidden layers of the action model. This makes the experts more compatible with existing model merging methods. However, merging all experts can still introduce interference from unrelated tasks at deployment. PolicyWeave scores each expert's LoRA updates using the initial visual-language context, determines the expert set through leave-one-layer-out ranking stability, and forms a sparse weighted merge of the selected updates that remains fixed for current task. We evaluate PolicyWeave with GR00T N1.5 on 18 RoboCasa365 tasks, using only 10% of the target-task demonstrations for supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Preserving the shared action interface raises the average success rate across four static merging methods from 17.0% to 52.8%. PolicyWeave achieves 64.7% success with these SFT experts and 74.1% after task-specific reinforcement learning (RL), compared with 60.7% for joint RL. Further evaluations on LIBERO-10 and an AgileX Piper arm support the deployment of independently learned skills in long-horizon and real-world manipulation.

</details>


### [13] [DroneWAM: Efficient World Action Model for Drone Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33148)
*Liang Yao,Fan Liu,Hongbo Lu,Wei Xu,Jianyu Jiang,Yijun Shen,Chuanyi Zhang,Pai Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DroneWAM，一种基于 JEPA 架构的高效 world-action model，用于无人机视觉导航，通过隐空间预测、Resampler 压缩与自适应 rollout 在精度与效率间取得平衡，并构建 DroneNav-6D 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机视觉导航对 world-action model 的预测精度与计算效率要求苛刻：需要预判候选动作对观测的影响，但显式生成未来图像代价过高，且每个想象步都重复大量计算。

Method: 1) 采用 JEPA 架构直接在表征空间建模未来状态，避免显式未来图像生成；2) 使用预训练 Resampler 将稠密编码器特征压缩为少量 latent token，降低每步想象的计算量；3) 提出自适应 rollout，由偏好训练的 Gate 根据场景动态分配预测深度；4) 构建 DroneNav-6D 仿真数据集，包含同步 RGB 观测、6-DoF 飞行轨迹、控制指令与随机风扰。

Result: 在 DroneNav-6D 上，DroneWAM 在对比方法中取得最佳轨迹精度；自适应 rollout 将平均预测深度从 8 降至 4.58，同时提升轨迹精度。

Conclusion: 预测计算可跨场景更有效地分配，表明在隐空间建模未来状态并结合自适应预测深度，是无人机视觉导航中兼顾精度与效率的有效方案。

Abstract: World-action models give visual navigation agents a way to anticipate how candidate actions will change future observations and to act from the predicted consequences. For drones, this capability must operate under tight accuracy and efficiency constraints. We present DroneWAM, an efficient world-action model for drone visual navigation. DroneWAM adopts a JEPA-based architecture to model future states directly in representation space, avoiding the cost of explicit future image generation. A pretrained Resampler further compresses dense encoder features into fewer latent tokens, reducing the computation repeated at each imagined step. We also introduce adaptive rollout, where a preference-trained Gate adaptively allocates prediction depth according to the current scene. To support learning under richer aerial motion, we construct DroneNav-6D, a simulated visual navigation dataset with synchronized RGB observations, 6-DoF flight trajectories, control commands, and randomized wind disturbances. On DroneNav-6D, DroneWAM achieves the best trajectory accuracy among the compared methods. Adaptive rollout further reduces the average prediction depth from 8 to 4.58 while improving trajectory accuracy, demonstrating that predictive computation can be allocated more effectively across scenes. \href{ this https URL }{Codes and data} will be released.

</details>


### [14] [Scope-WM: Scoped Computation for Efficient Visual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33218)
*Chunzheng Li,Zesheng Jia,Hongda Zhang,Jiaying Tang,Yuntian Wang,Siao Liu,Jin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Scope-WM：一种面向机器人规划的稀疏视觉世界模型，通过动作条件选择器筛选预测相关token并复用高质量动作序列，在Push-T任务上实现6.97倍规划加速，显存和规划时间分别降至稠密DINO-WM的18.1%和14.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型通过预测未来观测支持机器人规划，但稠密潜状态传播和样本密集型轨迹优化带来高推理延迟与峰值显存占用，难以在资源受限平台上实时部署。现有稀疏世界模型加速方法存在不足：无引导的token稀疏化可能丢弃规划相关信息并限制可达稀疏度；或引入沉重辅助模块和多阶段训练流程。

Method: 提出Scope-WM，将计算范围限定于与预测相关的潜区域和有前景的动作序列。具体地，将预测相关性蒸馏到轻量级动作条件选择器中，仅对选中的紧凑token子集执行完整动力学预测；其余token通过前景状态及其变化的紧凑摘要进行更新，使背景无需昂贵的token-to-token交互即可感知前景动态。规划阶段，保留并复用初始MPC搜索中发现的高质量动作序列，在减少的rollout预算下聚焦后续搜索。整体流程仅需一次选择器蒸馏和一次稀疏世界模型联合训练。

Result: 在Push-T任务上，Scope-WM将峰值GPU显存和规划时间分别降至稠密DINO-WM的18.1%和14.3%，对应6.97倍规划加速，同时保持有竞争力的任务性能。在五个多样化视觉规划任务上的进一步评估表明其具有通用适用性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: Scope-WM通过动作条件选择器驱动的稀疏计算与动作序列复用，有效缓解视觉世界模型在规划中的延迟和显存瓶颈，实现实时部署潜力，并在多个视觉规划任务上验证了效率与性能的平衡。

Abstract: Visual world models enable robotic planning by predicting future observations, but dense latent-state propagation and sample-intensive trajectory optimization incur high inference latency and peak memory usage, limiting real-time deployment on resource-constrained platforms. Existing sparse world-model acceleration methods either rely on unguided token sparsification, which may discard planning-relevant information and restrict achievable sparsity, or introduce heavy auxiliary modules and cumbersome multi-stage training pipelines. In this work, we present Scope-WM, an efficient visual world model that scopes computation to prediction-relevant latent regions and promising action sequences. Scope-WM distills prediction relevance into a lightweight action-conditioned selector and applies full dynamics prediction only to a compact subset of selected tokens. It updates the remaining tokens using a compact summary of foreground states and their changes, allowing the background to perceive foreground dynamics without costly token-to-token interactions. During planning, Scope-WM preserves and reuses high-quality action sequences discovered during the initial MPC search, focusing subsequent search under reduced rollout budgets. The resulting pipeline requires only a one-off selector distillation followed by a single joint training stage for the sparse world model. On the challenging Push-T task, Scope-WM reduces peak GPU memory usage and planning time to $18.1\%$ and $14.3\%$ of those of dense DINO-WM, respectively, corresponding to a $6.97\times$ planning speedup, while maintaining competitive task performance. Further evaluations across five diverse visual planning tasks demonstrate the general applicability of Scope-WM. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [15] [VehDyn: A Driving World Model Benchmark for Vehicle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33264)
*Tianyi Wang,Wangsheng Du,Jiazhou Chen,Tianyi Zeng,Xiangyu Li,Jiseop Byeon,Yujin Wang,Yiming Xu,Yangyang Wang,Bingzhao Gao,Sikai Chen,Zhaomiao Guo,Junfeng Jiao,Christian Claudel,Alexandre Bayen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 VehDyn——面向自动驾驶视频世界模型的车辆动力学基准，基于 CARLA-CarSim 联合仿真构建 10,080 组全因子可控配置并同步记录车辆状态，通过分层评测框架（轨迹对齐、运动学一致性、动力学一致性）评测 12 个先进视频世界模型，发现轨迹指标已接近饱和、动力学一致性普遍不足，且视觉质量与动力学保真度仅弱相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型评测基准主要衡量视觉保真度与粗粒度物理合理性，难以判断生成的驾驶未来是否遵循真实的车辆运动学与动力学规律；同时缺少“车辆、道路、机动、速度条件可独立控制、且真值车辆状态与视频同步记录”的数据集，导致评测证据不足。

Method: 构建 CARLA-CarSim 协同仿真平台，将照片级渲染与经验证的多体动力学模型耦合；采用全因子设计生成 10,080 组配置，覆盖 5 种车型、4 个轮胎-路面摩擦系数、3 种机动动作、4 个目标车速、14 个场景、3 种光照，每组配对同步的位置、速度与姿态序列；在此基础上提出分层评测框架，分别度量轨迹对齐、运动学一致性与动力学一致性，并评测 12 个最先进视频世界模型；另用两套成熟协议评估视频质量并与其 VehDyn 得分做相关性分析。

Result: 轨迹级指标近乎饱和，12 个模型中有 10 个与真值差距在 20% 以内；但动力学一致性上无模型达到真值的 92%；视觉质量指标与车辆动力学保真度仅呈弱相关。模型排名上 DrivingWorld 的 VehDyn 得分最高，其后为 Cosmos 3 Nano 与 LTX-Video 2.5，且 VehDyn 得分与人类判断高度一致。

Conclusion: VehDyn 为开发兼具物理一致性与视觉真实性的驾驶世界模型提供了系统性基准基础，揭示了当前模型“视频像但动力学不准”的关键缺口。

Abstract: Video world models are emerging as data engines, action planners, and generative simulators for autonomous driving, but existing benchmarks primarily assess visual fidelity and coarse physical plausibility, providing limited evidence on whether generated driving futures obey realistic vehicle kinematics and dynamics. This limitation is further compounded by the lack of datasets in which vehicle, road, maneuver, and speed conditions are independently controlled, and ground-truth vehicle states are recorded in synchrony with videos. We introduce VehDyn, a driving world model benchmark for vehicle dynamics. VehDyn is built on a CARLA-CarSim co-simulation platform where photorealistic rendering is coupled with a validated multi-body dynamics model, and it contains 10,080 configurations from a full factorial design over five vehicle types, four tire-road friction coefficients, three maneuvers, four target speeds, 14 scenes, and three illuminations, each paired with synchronized position, velocity, and attitude sequences. Built on this dataset, VehDyn introduces a hierarchical evaluation framework that measures trajectory alignment, kinematic consistency, and dynamic consistency, and benchmarks 12 state-of-the-art video world models. We further assess the video quality using two established protocols and correlate it with the VehDyn score. Trajectory-level metrics are nearly saturated, with ten of twelve models within 20\% of ground truth, while no model reaches 92\% of ground truth on dynamic consistency, and visual-quality metrics are only weakly correlated with vehicle-dynamics fidelity. DrivingWorld achieves the highest VehDyn score, followed by Cosmos 3 Nano and LTX-Video 2.5, and the VehDyn score agrees closely with human judgment. VehDyn provides a systematic foundation for developing driving world models that are physically consistent and visually realistic.

</details>


### [16] [Resolving State-Representation Mismatch: State-Space Visual Reasoning for Open-Loop VLA Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33412)
*Junhao Xiao,Haoxiang Zhao,Menghao Fang,Jinkui Zhang,Jinghan Yu,Xinyu Huang,Zhiyu Wu,Kaiming Xu,Yi Chen,Youjun Bao,Zhiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SSVR 提出一种状态空间视觉推理范式，将静态视觉上下文、语言约束与循环隐状态解耦，用动作条件 GRU 更新隐状态，在开环规划任务上大幅超越现有方法，并实现高效多步推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的推理范式在开环规划中存在根本性的"状态表示失配"：仅给定初始观测，模型需在内部模拟动作条件下的状态转移，但文本空间、像素空间和隐空间推理分别面临有损的空间压缩、误差累积的视觉生成以及中间隐令牌被绕过的问题，从而损害可靠的长时程规划能力。

Method: 提出状态空间视觉推理（State-Space Visual Reasoning, SSVR），将静态视觉上下文、语言约束与一个循环隐状态三者解耦。SSVR 对初始图像和指令仅编码一次，随后每次动作预测均以隐状态为条件，并用动作条件 GRU 更新该隐状态。以 Qwen2.5-VL 作为骨干网络实现。

Result: 在 FrozenLake、Maze、MiniBehavior 上分别取得 99.5/99.6、96.3/98.0、83.9/90.6 的 EM/PR 指标，显著优于先前方法。在多种输入变换与迁移设置下验证了循环状态建模对 VLA 开环规划的有效性。通过复用静态视觉-文本上下文并更新紧凑的循环状态，SSVR 支持高效多步推理，在预构建前缀缓存的情况下，Maze 解码 rollout 速度最高比评估基线快 98.58 倍。

Conclusion: 循环状态建模能有效解决 VLA 开环规划中的状态表示失配问题，SSVR 在精度与效率上均取得显著提升，为长时程规划提供了可靠且高效的推理范式。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in vision-language-action (VLA) models, existing reasoning paradigms still face a fundamental \emph{state-representation mismatch} in open-loop planning. Given only an initial observation, models must internally simulate action-conditioned state transitions, whereas text-, pixel-, and latent-space reasoning can suffer from lossy spatial compression, error-accumulating visual generation, and bypass of intermediate latent tokens, respectively, undermining reliable long-horizon planning. We propose \textbf{State-Space Visual Reasoning} (SSVR), which decouples static visual context, language constraints, and a recurrent latent state. SSVR encodes the initial image and instruction once, then conditions each action prediction on the latent state and updates it with an action-conditioned GRU. Using Qwen2.5-VL as the backbone, SSVR achieves 99.5/99.6, 96.3/98.0, and 83.9/90.6 EM/PR on FrozenLake, Maze, and MiniBehavior, substantially outperforming prior methods. Extensive experiments support the effectiveness of recurrent state modeling for VLA open-loop planning across input transformations and transfer settings. By reusing static visual-textual context and updating a compact recurrent state, SSVR supports efficient multi-step inference, achieving up to $98.58\times$ faster Maze decoding rollouts than the evaluated baselines with the prefix cache prebuilt.

</details>


### [17] [ForeFly: A Dual-Horizon World Action Model for Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33581)
*Kunhui Wang,Xintong Zhang,Junyu Gao,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForeFly：一种双时域潜在世界动作模型，通过预测近程与路径关键远程未来并利用FGAR非对称融合，提升空中视觉语言导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中视觉语言导航（AVLN）要求无人机在复杂3D环境中沿长轨迹可靠地遵循指令。然而现有AVLN方法多为反应式或仅做单一时域预测，忽略了不同时间尺度上互补的未来线索，难以支撑长程导航。

Method: 提出ForeFly，一个双时域潜在世界动作模型：同时预测用于局部连续性的近程未来（proximal future）和用于长程引导的自适应路径关键未来（route-critical future）。采用时域特定的foresight queries，并以近期视觉记忆与路径关键视觉记忆进行初始化，为未来预测提供历史感知上下文。进一步设计Foresight-Guided Action Refinement（FGAR），非对称地利用近程前瞻增强局部动作，利用路径关键前瞻进行特征级校正与路径级引导。

Result: 在TravelUAV和UAV-ON基准上，ForeFly在seen与unseen设置下均持续超越强基线，验证了双时域前瞻与FGAR学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 双时域未来预测与非对称动作精炼能有效弥补现有AVLN方法反应式、单时域预测的不足，为复杂3D环境下的长程无人机语言导航提供了有效方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Aerial Vision-Language Navigation (AVLN) requires UAVs to maintain reliable instruction following over long trajectories in complex 3D environments. However, existing AVLN approaches are predominantly reactive or limited to single-horizon prediction, overlooking complementary future cues across different temporal horizons. To address this limitation, we propose ForeFly, a dual-horizon latent world action model that predicts both a proximal future for local continuity and an adaptive route-critical future for long-range guidance. Horizon-specific foresight queries are primed with recent and route-critical visual memories, providing history-aware context for future prediction. To exploit their distinct roles in action generation, we introduce Foresight-Guided Action Refinement (FGAR), which asymmetrically exploits proximal foresight for local action enhancement and route-critical foresight for feature-wise correction and route-level guidance. Experiments on the TravelUAV and UAV-ON benchmarks show that ForeFly consistently outperforms strong baselines across seen and unseen settings, validating the effectiveness of dual-horizon foresight and FGAR learning. The code is available at: this https URL

</details>


### [18] [GeoShrink: Accelerating Diffusion Transformers with Two Lines of Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33723)
*Haosen Li,Wenshuo Chen,Shaofeng Liang,Lei Wang,Bowen Tian,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoShrink 是一种免训练加速方法，在保留原求解器网格的前提下仅在预设锚点处评估模型，通过几何规则预测被跳过阶段的输出，在图像、视频、动作、音频与 3D 生成上实现约 5 倍加速并显著提升保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散 Transformer 沿采样轨迹反复进行模型评估，导致推理成本高昂。作者希望在无需重新训练、且不改变原有求解器网格的前提下，对采样过程进行加速。

Method: GeoShrink 保留原始求解器网格，但仅在规定的一组锚点处评估模型。在跳过的阶段，将最新观测到的"新息"（innovation）按几何保留的比例加到最近一次精确输出上，以此预测面向求解器的输出。该规则由弦切空间传输（chordal tangent transport）与往返线段投影推导得到，并给出在固定覆盖率与首段跨度下最小化最大相邻间隙扩张的几何锚点间距原则。分析刻画了预测误差的几何闭合与传播特性，且不假设能获知未来模型输出。

Result: 在约 5 倍加速下，GeoShrink 在 FLUX 上相对最强基线 PSNR 提升 3.10 dB；在 HunyuanVideo 上实现 4.99 倍加速，并使 ChronoMagic-Bench-150 PSNR 相对最强保真度基线提升 5.44 dB。在固定评估预算下，于动作、音频、音乐与 3D 生成任务上同样取得显著增益。实验覆盖图像、视频、动作、音频生成及适配的 3D 后端。

Conclusion: GeoShrink 通过几何化的锚点评估与创新外推策略，在不训练、不改网格的条件下有效降低扩散 Transformer 的推理成本，并在多模态生成任务上同时提升加速比与生成保真度。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers incur substantial inference cost through repeated model evaluations along a sampling trajectory. We introduce GeoShrink, a training-free acceleration method that retains the original solver grid while evaluating the model only at a prescribed set of anchors. At skipped stages, GeoShrink predicts the solver-facing output by adding a geometrically retained fraction of the latest observed innovation to the most recent exact output. We derive this rule from chordal tangent transport and round-trip line projection, and establish a geometric anchor-spacing principle that minimizes the largest adjacent gap expansion under fixed coverage and first span. The analysis characterizes the geometric closure and propagation of prediction errors without assuming access to future model outputs. Experiments cover image, video, motion, and audio generation, together with adapted 3D backends. At approximately $5\times$ acceleration, GeoShrink improves FLUX PSNR by 3.10 dB over the strongest listed baseline. On HunyuanVideo, it achieves a reported $4.99\times$ speedup and improves ChronoMagic-Bench-150 PSNR by 5.44 dB over the strongest listed fidelity baseline. Comparisons at fixed evaluation budgets further show substantial gains on motion, audio, music, and 3D generation.

</details>


### [19] [ReDrive: Shaping Representations with World Modeling for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33854)
*Yueting Zhu,Shaoyu Chen,Yuehao Song,Hui Sun,Qian Zhang,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReDrive：通过未来表征预测塑造面向规划的可视化特征，采用三阶段训练，无需推理期辅助模块或未来预测，在NAVSIM上实现91.0 PDMS（v1）和90.8 EPDMS（v2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端驾驶模型通常构建复杂的感知-规划流水线，或引入显式预测未来状态的世界模型，导致系统架构复杂。作者受通用视觉表征可迁移性的启发，认为足够强的视觉表征结合表征世界建模，无需复杂的推理期辅助模块即可支持有效规划。

Method: 提出ReDrive端到端驾驶框架，通过未来表征预测强化面向规划的可视化特征。采用三阶段训练流程：驾驶视频预训练、世界建模与规划的联合训练、规划器适配。最终得到强面向规划表征和高性能规划器，推理时无需辅助感知模块或未来预测，仅保留简单的编码器-规划器流水线。

Result: 在NAVSIM上表现强劲：NAVSIM v1达91.0 PDMS，NAVSIM v2达90.8 EPDMS。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅通过世界建模塑造表征即可实现高性能端到端规划，同时保持简单的编码器-规划器推理流水线。

Abstract: Driving policies require capabilities of scene understanding and future evolution prediction. To achieve this goal, current end-to-end models typically construct complex perception-planning pipelines or introduce world models that explicitly predict future states, resulting in a complex system architecture. Inspired by the transferability of general-purpose visual representations, we argue that combining sufficiently strong visual representations with representation world modeling can support effective planning without relying on complex inference-time auxiliary modules. Based on this insight, we present ReDrive, an end-to-end driving framework that strengthens planning-oriented visual features via future representation prediction. To achieve this, ReDrive adopts a three-stage training pipeline consisting of driving video pretraining, joint world-modeling and planning training, and planner adaptation. This yields a strong planning-oriented representation and a high-performance planner, while requiring neither auxiliary perception modules nor future prediction at inference time. Experiments on NAVSIM demonstrate strong performance, achieving 91.0 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 90.8 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2. These results show that shaping representations with world modeling is sufficient to enable high-performance end-to-end planning while retaining a simple encoder-planner inference pipeline.

</details>


### [20] [CAST: Reconstruction-Coupled Acceleration of Interactive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34144)
*Leyang Chen,Junyi Wu,Fanqing Kong,Shaoqiu Zhang,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAST：一种重建耦合的推理加速框架，通过交互敏感性与跨层覆盖选择锚点、频率与置信度感知的相位感知重建（PAR），并按下游重建责任协调历史KV路由，在保持接近Native视觉质量的同时实现最高3.48x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型必须在快速响应控制的同时保持场景一致性，但现有加速方法在恢复被跳过的特征时，难以应对异构的控制响应与空间传输问题，导致交互响应与空间一致性受损。

Method: 作者观察到交互引发的特征变化与近似误差相关，且低频插值误差对相位敏感、其相位演化更具可预测性。基于此提出CAST这一重建耦合推理框架：一是按交互敏感性与跨层覆盖来选择锚点（anchors）；二是用频率与置信度感知的相位感知重建（Phase-Aware Reconstruction, PAR）恢复被跳过的残差；三是依据下游重建责任来协调历史KV路由。

Result: 在Matrix-Game 3.0与HY-World 1.5上分别取得2.15x与3.48x加速，视觉质量接近Native（图1）；在所比较方法中取得最高VBench分数，并在WorldMark的13个维度中分别领先非Native基线7个和6个维度，体现了生成速度、视觉质量与实时控制下交互响应性的平衡。

Conclusion: CAST验证了利用交互敏感性与相位可预测性进行重建耦合推理的有效性，能够在实时控制下兼顾生成速度、视觉质量与交互响应性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Interactive world models must respond quickly to controls while preserving scene consistency. Existing acceleration methods can miss heterogeneous control responses and spatial transport when recovering skipped features. We observe that interaction-induced feature changes correlate with approximation error, while low-frequency interpolation errors are phase-sensitive and show more predictable phase progression. These findings motivate CAST, a reconstruction-coupled inference framework. CAST selects anchors by interaction sensitivity and cross-layer coverage, reconstructs skipped residuals with frequency- and confidence-aware Phase-Aware Reconstruction (PAR), and coordinates historical KV routing according to downstream reconstruction responsibility. On Matrix-Game 3.0 and HY-World 1.5, CAST achieves 2.15x and 3.48x speedups, respectively, while maintaining visual quality close to Native (Figure 1). It also attains the highest VBench scores among compared methods and leads non-native baselines on seven and six of thirteen WorldMark dimensions, demonstrating a balance of generation speed, visual quality, and interactive responsiveness under real-time control. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [21] [AGILE-GS: Anchor-Guided Fast Next-Best-View Selection for Active 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34176)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AGILE-GS 将 3DGS 的下一最佳视角选择拆分为“信息搜索”与“相机选择”，用虚拟锚点引导候选评分，再以 ridge-leverage 压缩候选集，从而在保持或超越基线效果的同时将选择延迟降低 1–2 个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射场重建通常需要数百个视角，视角数量与摆放位置同样重要；现有 3DGS 的 NBV 方法往往对候选池中每个视角逐一评分并只保留一个，计算开销大。论文指出“搜索信息”和“选择相机”本质上是可分离的两个问题，因此需要一种更高效的 NBV 框架。

Method: 提出 AGILE-GS：在 SE(3) 上优化一个虚拟锚点位姿，通过黎曼梯度上升最大化期望信息增益；该锚点无需可达、也不必存在于候选池中，仅用于标记模型最不确定的观察几何。随后依据锚点视角几何对候选视角评分，并用贪心 ridge-leverage 步骤在不渲染任何候选的情况下将候选池蒸馏为小而低冗余的短名单。AGILE-GS 直接取短名单首个视角作为下一视角；AGILE-GS+ 则对短名单内少量视角计算 Fisher 信息增益并选最优，从而把昂贵评估限制在少数视角上。

Result: 在标准基准和闭环具身采集任务中，AGILE-GS 与 AGILE-GS+ 均达到或超过现有基线，同时将视角选择延迟降低 1–2 个数量级；AGILE-GS+ 仅对短名单中的少量视角计算 Fisher 信息，避免了全候选池的昂贵评估。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将 NBV 中的信息搜索与相机选择解耦，可在不牺牲重建/采集性能的前提下显著提升 3DGS 主动视角选择的效率，为可扩展、低延迟的闭环三维采集提供了新思路。

Abstract: Radiance fields need hundreds of views, and their placement matters as much as their number. Next-best-view (NBV) selection for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) usually scores every candidate in the pool and keeps one. Searching for information and choosing a camera, however, are separable problems. We present AGILE-GS, an anchor-guided NBV method that separates the two. A virtual anchor pose is optimized on SE(3) by Riemannian gradient ascent on expected information gain. It need not be reachable or in the pool; it marks where the model is most uncertain. Candidates are scored against the anchor's viewing geometry, and a greedy ridge-leverage step distills the pool into a small, non-redundant shortlist without rendering any candidate. The shortlist can be used in two ways. AGILE-GS takes the first view on it as the next view, so no Fisher information is computed for any candidate. AGILE-GS+ computes the Fisher information gain of each shortlisted view and picks the best, so the expensive evaluation runs on a handful of views rather than the whole pool. On standard benchmarks and in closed-loop embodied acquisition, both match or exceed existing baselines while cutting selection latency by one to two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [22] [WorldGuide: Learning Success-Failure Boundaries in Latent World Models for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34206)
*Lin Liu,Lu Zhang,Ziying Song,Wu Yang,Yuzheng Zhuang,Yunzhi Zhuge,Shuai Tao,Wulong Liu,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldGuide 提出一种在潜空间中学习成功与失败交互差异的框架，结合预测预训练与对比学习，将学到的预测器作为可微奖励来指导策略与视觉编码器的联合优化，训练后丢弃预测器，部署时无需额外世界模型推理，在 LIBERO 100 和 SimplerEnv 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型能通过捕捉动作后果来改进 Vision-Language-Action（VLA）策略，但仅在专家演示上训练的模型对失败结果的暴露有限，难以区分视觉上相似的成功与失败交互，从而限制了 VLA 策略的可靠性。

Method: WorldGuide 在潜空间中学习成功与失败的区分表征：一是对成功和失败轨迹进行预测式预训练，二是在配对的成功—失败样本上施加对比学习。学习到的预测器作为可微奖励，指导策略与视觉编码器的联合优化。该预测器在训练后被丢弃，因此部署阶段无需额外的世界模型推理。

Result: 大量实验表明，WorldGuide 显著提升了 VLA 的可靠性，并在 LIBERO 100 与 SimplerEnv 上达到当前最优性能，分别取得 96.8% 与 72.0% 的成绩。

Conclusion: 通过在潜空间中刻画成功与失败的差异并将其转化为可微奖励，WorldGuide 在不增加部署开销的前提下有效提升了 VLA 策略的可靠性与性能；代码将公开。

Abstract: Latent world models offer a promising way to improve Vision-Language-Action policies by capturing the consequences of actions. However, models trained primarily on expert demonstrations have limited exposure to failure outcomes and may struggle to distinguish visually similar successful and failed interactions. We propose \textbf{WorldGuide}, a framework that learns these distinctions in latent space and uses them to guide policy training. WorldGuide combines predictive pretraining on successful and failed trajectories with contrastive learning on matched success--failure pairs. The learned predictor then provides a differentiable reward to guide joint optimization of the policy and visual encoder. The predictor is discarded after training, so deployment requires no additional world-model inference. Extensive experiments show that WorldGuide substantially improves VLA reliability and achieves state of the art performance on LIBERO 100 and SimplerEnv, reaching \textbf{96.8\%} and \textbf{72.0\%}, respectively. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [23] [WorldWeave: Growing Persistent Geometric Worlds for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34221)
*Yifan Huang,Lifan Jiang,Qingyue Hao,Cheng Chen,Boxi Wu,Xiaoxue Ren,Xiaofei He,Dehai Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldWeave 将世界状态维护与视觉渲染解耦，通过持续高程图生成、智能体场景组织与拼接构建可扩展显式3D世界，实现无边界的持续场景扩展与跨视角一致重访。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型缺乏显式、持久的结构化记忆，在持续场景扩展和跨视角重访时难以保持世界结构一致。

Method: 提出 WorldWeave 框架，解耦世界状态维护与视觉渲染。其地形模块基于扩散图像外推，在邻域条件与边界约束下生成连续度量高程图；智能体模块融合用户意图、地形证据与跨区域连通约束，通过分层语义规划、确定性几何编译与局部修正构建场景；视觉生成阶段，规划相机轨迹通过只读接口查询世界几何，生成深度序列引导视频合成，且不将生成结果写回世界状态。

Result: 结构记忆独立于短窗口视频生成，支持无预设地图边界的持续扩展，并为跨轨迹观测与重复访问提供一致几何基础。

Conclusion: WorldWeave 通过显式3D世界结构与只读查询机制，有效缓解世界模型结构记忆缺失问题，实现持续可扩展且几何一致的世界生成。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, world models still lack explicit, persistent structural memory, making it difficult to preserve consistent world structure during continual scene expansion and cross-view revisits. To address this limitation, we present WorldWeave, a world generation framework that decouples world-state maintenance from visual rendering. Specifically, WorldWeave combines continual elevation-map generation with agent-guided scene organization and stitching to build an expandable explicit 3D world that incrementally extends structural memory while preserving existing structure. First, its terrain module uses diffusion-based image outpainting to generate continuous metric elevation maps under neighborhood conditioning and boundary constraints. Next, an agent integrates user intent, terrain evidence, and cross-region connectivity constraints to construct scenes through hierarchical semantic planning, deterministic geometry compilation, and local revision. Finally, during visual generation, planned camera trajectories query world geometry through a read-only interface, producing depth sequences that guide video synthesis without writing the generated results back into the world state. As a result, structural memory remains independent of short-window video generation, enabling continual expansion without predefined map boundaries and providing a consistent geometric basis for observations across trajectories and repeated visits.

</details>


### [24] [Dexterous Tactile World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34286)
*Ziyao Zeng,Xiatao Sun,Hao Wang,Yueyang Pan,Zhengxiang Yu,Fengyu Yang,Tianyu Liu,Zhiwen Fan,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DTWM 是一个基于视频扩散 Transformer 的灵巧操作世界模型，将双手触觉信号通过零初始化残差注入预训练视频模型，实现以自我为中心的未来帧预测，并显著提升手部运动与感知预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作过程中的关键事件（如接触与释放）难以从视频中直接观测，而触觉更易感知。现有操作世界模型多仅依赖视频，忽略了触觉对未来动态预测的重要作用。

Method: 提出 Dexterous Tactile World Model（DTWM）：在预训练视频扩散 Transformer 上，将每只手的触觉信号通过零初始化残差注入视频 token 中对应的手部位置；同时使用因果掩码防止预测帧访问未来信息。模型以观察视频和双手触觉手套信号为条件，进行自我中心操作的未来帧预测。

Result: 与架构、参数量和训练匹配的纯视觉模型相比，DTWM 将手部运动低估从 23% 降至 9%，手部区域感知误差降低 7.4%（三次训练运行）。预测越靠后收益越大，后期预测块改进约为第一块的 4.1 倍。DTWM 在同设置下优于其他视觉-触觉世界模型；即使推理时无触觉，训练中使用触觉仍能提升未来帧预测。消融表明，力的大小和空间位置均有贡献：用二值接触状态（按手或按位置）替代触觉信号会增加预测误差。力的变化过程可指示交互将持续还是改变。

Conclusion: 触觉信号能有效增强视频世界模型对操作动态的预测能力，尤其有助于捕捉接触相关事件和长时预测；力的幅值与空间信息均为关键，触觉可作为训练期监督提升视觉预测，即使推理时不可用。

Abstract: World models for manipulation are typically trained from video, yet the events that determine how manipulation unfolds, such as making and releasing contact, are difficult to observe visually and are often easier to sense through touch. We present the Dexterous Tactile World Model (DTWM), a video world model for future-frame prediction of egocentric manipulation from both observed video and tactile signals from a glove worn on each hand. We condition a pretrained video diffusion transformer on each hand's tactile signal through a zero-initialized residual at the corresponding hand location in the video tokens, while a causal mask prevents predicted frames from accessing future information. Compared with a vision-only model matched in architecture, parameters, and training, DTWM reduces the underestimation of hand motion from 23% to 9%, while reducing the perceptual error in the hand region by 7.4% across three training runs per model. The benefit also increases over the prediction horizon, with the improvement in the later predicted chunks being about 4.1x larger than in the first. DTWM also outperforms other visual-tactile world models under the same setting, and training with touch improves future-frame prediction even when no touch is available at inference. Ablations show that the model benefits from both the magnitude and spatial location of force: replacing the tactile signal with binary contact states, either per hand or per location, increases prediction error. The observed course of the force indicates whether the interaction will persist or change.

</details>


### [25] [Text-Vision Synergistic Token Caching: A Training-Free Framework for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34319)
*Qianer Li,Chengjie Zhang,Jingwen Chen,Zanjia Tong,Jiyuan Zhang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TVCache，一种免训练的词元缓存框架，利用文本-视觉协同的归纳偏置（注意力头筛选与复用层选择）加速 VLA 模型推理，在保持精度的同时降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型可泛化机器人控制但计算开销大。现有的词元缓存虽为免训练即插即用方案，但未充分利用 VLA 的文本-视觉协同归纳偏置：未充分考虑注意力聚合中逐头可靠性，以及缓存复用中逐层稳定性，导致任务相关视觉定位不佳、缓存不稳定表示。

Method: TVCache 为免训练框架，包含两部分：(1) 基于文本-视觉信息聚焦度筛选注意力头，以提升任务相关且物理一致的视觉定位；(2) 引入由文本-视觉熵差引导的复用层选择机制，避免缓存不稳定表示并优化缓存资源分配。

Result: 在四个代表性 VLA 模型、两个仿真基准与真实机器人任务上验证有效性。相同词元保留率下，任务成功率持续优于现有 VLA 缓存，计算成本相当。在 OpenVLA-OFT 上于 12.5% 保留率时较 VLA-Cache 平均成功率提升最高 14.5 个百分点，同时相对全词元推理降低 FLOPs 2.45 倍。

Conclusion: TVCache 通过文本-视觉协同的词元缓存，有效提升 VLA 推理效率与任务成功率，具有良好的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable generalizable robotic control but remain computationally expensive. Token caching provides a training-free, plug-and-play acceleration alternative. However, existing VLA caching does not fully exploit a key inductive bias of VLA models: text-vision synergy, wherein textual semantics guide the precise visual grounding of task-relevant regions. In particular, existing designs insufficiently account for head-wise reliability in attention aggregation and layer-wise stability in cache reuse. To address this, we propose Text-Vision Synergistic Token Caching (TVCache), a training-free framework for efficient VLA inference. TVCache filters attention heads based on text-vision information focus to improve task-relevant and physically consistent visual grounding. Concurrently, we introduce a reuse-layer selection mechanism guided by text-vision entropy differences to avoid caching unstable representations and improve cache resource allocation. Extensive experiments across four representative VLA models, two simulation benchmarks, and real-world robotic tasks demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of TVCache. At matched token-retention ratios, TVCache consistently improves task success over existing VLA caching with comparable computational cost. On OpenVLA-OFT, it improves average success by up to 14.5 percentage points over VLA-Cache at 12.5% retention while reducing FLOPs by 2.45x relative to full-token inference.

</details>


### [26] [From Static to Dynamic: On-Policy Distillation from Image to Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34371)
*Bingqing Jiang,Li Luo,Zichao Yu,Yujin Han,Zhaolong Su,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MILD，一种运动保持的图像到视频潜空间蒸馏框架：用可学习线性连接器跨异构潜空间迁移图像专家知识，并用预训练预测约束与光流运动奖励保持视频动态与时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型的专业化通常依赖 on-policy distillation（OPD）与视频教师监督，但视频专家数据与训练成本高、查询延迟大；图像专家更易获得、查询更便宜，适合美学、OCR 等帧级、弱时间相关能力。然而图像与视频潜空间异构，无法直接监督学生中间状态；图像专家也缺乏跨帧运动监督，帧级提升易损害时间一致性。

Method: MILD 在 on-policy 蒸馏中引入可学习线性连接器，将学生的潜状态与预测更新对齐到图像专家对应表示，从而跨异构潜空间传递监督。同时将图像引导的修正约束在预训练学生预测附近，以保留视频动态；并加入基于光流的运动奖励，提升运动质量与时间一致性。

Result: 在多种专用图像专家和多个视频学生骨干上，方法持续优于以视频模型为教师的 OPD 基线；进一步实验表明其可跨不同连接器设计与异构架构有效迁移。

Conclusion: 图像到视频蒸馏是利用图像生成生态多样且演进能力来改进视频生成的有效路径。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) specializes pretrained video diffusion models through teacher supervision along the student's own generation trajectory. Although large video models are natural teachers, developing specialized video experts can require costly video data and training, while querying them incurs substantially higher latency than querying image experts. More readily available and cheaper to query, image experts offer a cost-effective alternative, particularly for largely temporal-agnostic capabilities such as aesthetics and OCR that admit frame-level supervision. However, heterogeneous image and video latent spaces prevent direct supervision of intermediate student states, while image experts lack cross-frame motion supervision, making temporal consistency vulnerable to frame-level improvements. In this paper, we propose MILD, a Motion-Preserving Image-to-Video Latent Distillation framework that transfers specialized image expertise while preserving pretrained video dynamics. MILD uses a learnable linear connector that aligns student latent states and predicted updates with those of image experts, enabling supervision transfer across heterogeneous latent spaces. We further constrain image-guided corrections around the pretrained student's predictions to preserve video dynamics and incorporate an optical-flow-based motion reward to improve motion quality and temporal consistency. Across specialized image experts and multiple video-student backbones, our method consistently outperforms video-teacher OPD baselines, with further studies demonstrating effective transfer across connector designs and heterogeneous architectures. These results establish image-to-video distillation as an effective route to improving video generation by drawing on the diverse and evolving capabilities of the image-generation ecosystem.

</details>


### [27] [Marathoner: Ultra-Long-Horizon Autonomous Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34378)
*Zhang Ruiyang,Ou Jinpeng,Xie Yifan,Zhou Jingang,Pan Lirui,Guo Qingpei,Zheng Zhedong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Marathoner——具备超长时程执行能力的自主智能体模型；通过系统化后训练管线（超长时程任务合成、拒绝采样微调、强化学习与 Later Stage Bonus Reward）显著提升性能，可连续工作 10+ 小时、完成 1000+ 次工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够为长期目标持续工作数月甚至数年，而现有智能体模型缺乏在挑战性任务上进行超长时程持续执行的能力。作者希望将这种能力通过后训练注入基座模型。

Method: 构建完整后训练管线：1）Ultra-Long-Horizon Task Synthesis：以 GitHub 多仓库中 1000+ 行新代码的大型 release PR 为主要来源，合成任务级数据；2）Multi-Task Chaining：将多个生成任务串联为更高难度任务，逼近前沿难度；3）拒绝采样微调：用强教师模型配合多种 harness 生成轨迹，并对基座模型做监督微调；4）强化学习：冷启动模型在独立沙箱中通过 harness 进行真实执行，并提出 Later Stage Bonus Reward，显式鼓励模型在执行后期做出有效操作。

Result: 在包含超长时程任务的 5 个基准上，Marathoner 相比基座模型取得一致且显著提升，甚至超过强专有模型的表现；分析显示其可在高难度任务上连续工作 10+ 小时，并进行 1000+ 次工具调用。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过大规模超长时程任务合成、拒绝采样监督微调、真实沙箱强化学习及后期阶段奖励设计，可有效赋予基座模型真实的超长时程执行能力，并在多项基准上验证了该后训练范式的有效性。

Abstract: Humans naturally possess the ability to work persistently toward long-term goals. Given a challenging task, humans can continuously work for months or even years to accomplish a specific objective. In this paper, we propose Marathoner, an autonomous agentic model possessing the ability of ultra-long-horizon execution. Specifically, we propose a comprehensive post-training pipeline to instill this critical capability into base model. For Ultra-Long-Horizon Task Synthesis, we leverage major release PRs containing 1000+ lines of new code from diverse GitHub repositories as the primary source for synthesizing challenging task-level data. Additionally, we introduce Multi-Task Chaining, which chains multiple generated tasks into a single more challenging task, enabling the synthesis of tasks with frontier-level difficulty. For rejection sampling finetuning, we combine strong teacher model with diverse harnesses to generate trajectories on our synthesized tasks and conduct supervised finetuning on base model with rejection sampled trajectories. For reinforcement learning, cold-started model performs real-world execution through harnesses in independent sandboxes during rollout process, effectively facilitating the acquisition of genuine ultra-long-horizon execution capability. We further propose a novel reward strategy, Later Stage Bonus Reward, which explicitly encourages model to perform meaningful maneuvers during later stages of execution. Through extensive evaluation on 5 benchmarks containing ultra-long-horizon tasks, Marathoner achieves consistent and substantial performance improvements over base model and even surpasses performance of strong proprietary model. Further analysis shows that Marathoner can consistently work for 10+ hours and conduct 1000+ tool calls on highly challenging tasks.

</details>


### [28] [CAR-VLA: Complexity-Aware and Risk-Adaptive Reasoning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34387)
*Xiaolei Chen,Zhuolin He,Yuxuan Liang,Xu Li,Haotian Chen,Shi Fan,Mengyang Zhao,Wenjuan Meng,Zisheng Chen,Zhihao Zhu,Zhounan Jin,Hengli Wang,Qingfan Wang,Jiamei Liang,Bin Li,Xiangyang Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAR-VLA，一个同时考虑场景复杂度与动态风险的驾驶VLA模型，将四类"复杂度–风险"组合映射到三种推理模式（快速直觉、慢思考、反射响应），并通过渐进监督学习与推理增强强化学习训练，在NAVSIM v1/v2与Navhard上取得有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向驾驶VLA模型的自适应推理方法主要关注"是否需要推理"，而忽略了"不同驾驶情境下应如何推理"。作者的核心洞见是：场景复杂度决定推理深度，但动态风险同样关键，它决定在时间紧迫情境下"如何推理"（紧迫性与关注点）。因此需要一种同时考虑复杂度与风险的统一推理引导机制。

Method: CAR-VLA将"复杂度–风险"划分为四个类别，并映射到三种推理模式：1）Fast Intuition（快速直觉）：简单低风险场景下直接生成轨迹；2）Slow Thinking（慢思考）：复杂低风险场景下进行深思熟虑的推理；3）Reflex Response（反射响应）：无论复杂度如何，在高风险场景下进行紧凑、以危险为中心的推理——并非单纯缩短思考，而是将推理聚焦于最关键的危害与即时安全响应。训练采用两阶段：先以渐进式监督学习串联场景评估、推理模式选择与轨迹生成；再用推理增强的强化学习提升驾驶质量与推理行为。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上达到91.1 PDMS，NAVSIM v2上达到90.3 EPDMS，Navhard上达到35.0 EPDMS，展现出有竞争力的驾驶性能。在navtest与自建高风险场景上的定性对比进一步表明模型具备风险感知推理与危害响应式轨迹生成能力。论文代码将公开。

Conclusion: CAR-VLA通过联合建模场景复杂度与动态风险来引导推理的深度、紧迫性与关注点，验证了"按情境选择如何推理"这一思路的有效性，在多个驾驶基准上取得有竞争力的结果，并展现出风险感知的推理与危害响应式轨迹生成能力。

Abstract: Existing adaptive reasoning methods for driving Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models primarily focus on whether to reason, overlooking how reasoning should differ across driving situations. Our key insight is that while scene complexity informs reasoning depth, dynamic risk is equally critical for deciding how to reason in time-critical situations. We therefore propose CAR-VLA, a unified driving VLA model that jointly considers scene complexity and dynamic risk to guide reasoning depth, urgency, and focus. CAR-VLA maps four complexity--risk categories to three reasoning modes: \textit{Fast Intuition} for direct trajectory generation in simple low-risk scenes, \textit{Slow Thinking} for deliberate reasoning in complex low-risk scenes, and \textit{Reflex Response} for compact, hazard-focused reasoning in high-risk scenes regardless of complexity. Rather than merely shortening deliberation, Reflex Response centers reasoning on the most critical hazard and the immediate safe response. We train CAR-VLA through progressive supervised learning that links scene assessment, reasoning-mode selection, and trajectory generation, followed by reasoning-augmented reinforcement learning to improve driving quality and reasoning behavior. Experiments on NAVSIM v1(91.1 PDMS), NAVSIM v2(90.3 EPDMS), and Navhard(35.0 EPDMS) demonstrate competitive driving performance. Qualitative comparisons on navtest and in-house high-risk scenarios further illustrate risk-aware reasoning and hazard-responsive trajectory generation. The code for this paper will be released publicly at: this https URL

</details>


### [29] [Precise Editing and Flexible Referencing for Interactable Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34470)
*Xinyao Liao,Xianfang Zeng,Zhu Liang,Zhoujie Fu,Qianxun Xu,Jiachi Liu,Gang Yu,Guosheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditWorld 是一个可交互视频世界模型，支持在自回归生成过程中流式接收编辑指令与参考图像，实现精确编辑和灵活引用；在 WBench-Editing 上取得总体 73.8、编辑 80.0 的最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型主要面向导航与探索，用户可以浏览生成的世界，但对已有世界内容的修改缺乏精细控制。论文希望将世界建模从“探索”扩展到“精确修改”。

Method: EditWorld 在自回归生成中流式注入编辑指令和参考图像；提出 Gated Causal Attention 以处理随时间变化的编辑条件与参考图像；提出 Sparse Context 机制维持有界历史上下文以支持长时推理；采用联合自回归与双向训练及退火自重采样；构建专门的数据合成与标注流水线以提供世界编辑监督；并提出 WBench-Editing 评测基准。

Result: 在 WBench-Editing 上，EditWorld 取得最佳综合性能：总体得分 73.8，编辑得分 80.0，在编辑相关指标上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: EditWorld 通过流式编辑条件、参考图像控制、长时上下文机制和专用训练/数据流程，实现了可交互世界中的精确修改与灵活引用，并建立了流式世界编辑的系统评测基准。

Abstract: We present EditWorld, a video world model for precise editing and flexible referencing in interactable worlds. Existing video world models primarily focus on navigation, letting users explore generated worlds but offering limited control over how existing world content is modified. EditWorld extends world modeling from exploration to precise modification by streaming editing instructions and reference images during autoregressive generation. To support these capabilities, EditWorld introduces Gated Causal Attention for temporally varying editing conditions and reference images, together with a Sparse Context mechanism that maintains a bounded historical context for long-horizon inference. We further adopt joint autoregressive and bidirectional training with annealed self-resampling, and construct a dedicated data synthesis and annotation pipeline that provides supervision for world editing. We also present WBench-Editing to systematically evaluate streaming world editing capabilities. EditWorld achieves the best overall performance on WBench-Editing with an overall score of 73.8 and an editing score of 80.0, substantially outperforming existing methods on editing-related metrics. this https URL

</details>


### [30] [WorldAttention: An Efficient Attention Architecture for Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34606)
*Zeyu Zhang,Jinyuan Mao,Dakai An,Wangbo Zhao,Hanfeng Lu,Jiasheng Tang,Yinghao Yu,Wei Wang,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WorldAttention，一种面向文本条件交互式视频世界模型的高效注意力架构，通过混合稀疏注意力与分层 KV 缓存协同设计，突破滑动窗口与全历史缓存的长时交互瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归扩散式交互视频世界模型需低延迟、长时长生成，但现有滑动窗口机制丢弃历史上下文，损害长程交互；全历史缓存又因注意力二次复杂度和 KV 缓存线性增长导致算力与显存不可承受。因此需在效率与长程一致性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出系统导向的 WorldAttention：1）混合稀疏注意力 HSA，将线性全局注意力与头自适应稀疏注意力结合；2）分层 KV 缓存 HKV，将历史 KV 对按语义索引分页，跨多级内存组织，实现细粒度检索与可控 GPU 驻留；3）配套定制内核，将理论效率转化为实际性能。

Result: 在 VBench-Long 与 InterVBench 上持续超越先前 SOTA，主体一致性得分分别达到 0.9472 与 0.9668。

Conclusion: WorldAttention 通过注意力内核与分层 KV 缓存协同设计，有效缓解长视频世界模型的计算与显存瓶颈，在保持长程上下文的同时提升生成效率与一致性，为交互式视频世界模型的可扩展长时生成提供系统级方案。

Abstract: Leveraging the paradigm of autoregressive diffusion, text-conditioned interactive video world models aim to simulate temporally coherent environments guided by textual instructions. While enabling low-latency, long-duration generation is pivotal for embodied AI and simulation-based planning, current frameworks primarily rely on sliding-window mechanisms to bound computational complexity. However, this approach inherently sacrifices historical context, undermining the long-range interactive capabilities. Conversely, maintaining a full-history cache remains computationally prohibitive and memory-intensive: the quadratic complexity of attention leads to excessive computational overhead, while the linear growth of the KV cache inevitably leads to GPU memory saturation. To overcome these limitations, we propose WorldAttention, a system-oriented attention architecture that achieves high efficiency through the co-design of specialized attention kernels and hierarchical KV cache management. First, we introduce Hybrid Sparse Attention (HSA), which integrates linear global attention supplemented with head-adaptive sparse attention. Additionally, we design a Hierarchical KV Cache (HKV) that organizes historical KV pairs into semantically indexed pages across multi-tier memory, enabling fine-grained retrieval and controlled GPU residency. These two designs are supported by tailored kernels to effectively translate their theoretical efficiency into real-world performance. Extensive experiments on VBench-Long and InterVBench demonstrate that WorldAttention consistently surpasses prior state-of-the-art methods, achieving subject consistency scores of 0.9472 on VBench-Long and 0.9668 on InterVBench, respectively.

</details>


### [31] [Unified Trajectory Matching Policy Optimization: Diverse T2I Generation and VLA Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34688)
*Zhiyuan Ma,Jiaming Li,Lingzhen Li,Yu Liu,Xuekai Zhu,Dingkang Liang,Kaiyan Zhang,Jianjun Li,Bowen Zhou,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Uni-TMPO，用轨迹匹配与正向 KL 优化替代奖励最大化式 RL，以缓解扩散/流策略在 T2I 与 VLA 后训练中的模式崩溃，并提升奖励、多样性与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励最大化式 RL 在扩散与流策略后训练中会导致策略模式崩溃：即便加入参考 KL 或熵正则，策略仍收缩到单一高奖励模式。在 T2I 中表现为图像同质化与 reward hacking；在 VLA 中则丢失多种可行策略，削弱任务与场景泛化。因此需要一种避免单一模式坍塌的统一 RL 后训练框架。

Method: 提出 Unified Trajectory Matching Policy Optimization（Uni-TMPO），统一面向扩散与流策略。核心做法：在每个轨迹组内将标准化奖励转换为目标分布，并从轨迹对数概率导出策略分布；随后用正向 KL 优化匹配目标分布与策略分布，而非直接最大化期望奖励。针对 T2I，采用进度条件化的由粗到细调度器高效构建轨迹；在统一框架下，针对 VLA 使用反馈条件采样，以更新后的观测构造轨迹。

Result: 大量实验表明，Uni-TMPO 在 T2I 奖励和 VLA 同分布（ID）任务成功率上优于最强基线；在 T2I 奖励—多样性—效率权衡以及 VLA 对留出任务与场景的泛化方面达到最佳表现。真实机器人评估显示，当较高奖励目标受阻时，多种动作策略具有实际价值。

Conclusion: Uni-TMPO 通过将奖励最大化转为轨迹分布匹配，有效缓解扩散/流策略的模式崩溃，在 T2I 与 VLA 后训练中同时提升奖励、多样性和泛化能力，并保留多种可行策略。

Abstract: Reward-maximizing reinforcement learning (RL) is widely used to post-train stochastic diffusion and flow policies for text-to-image (T2I) generation. However, reward-maximizing RL causes policy mode collapse even under reference KL or entropy regularization, reducing the policy to a single high-reward mode. In T2I, this produces similar images and reward hacking. When extended to vision-language-action (VLA) models, the same collapse removes alternative successful strategies and weakens task and scene generalization. To address this limitation, we introduce Unified Trajectory Matching Policy Optimization (Uni-TMPO), a unified RL post-training framework for diffusion and flow policies. First, Uni-TMPO converts standardized rewards into a target distribution within each trajectory group and derives the policy distribution from trajectory log probabilities. Then, forward Kullback-Leibler optimization matches the two distributions instead of maximizing expected reward. A progress-conditioned coarse-to-fine scheduler efficiently constructs T2I trajectories. Within the unified framework, feedback-conditioned sampling uses updated observations to construct VLA trajectories. Extensive experiments show that Uni-TMPO achieves higher T2I rewards and VLA ID success rates than the strongest baselines. More importantly, it achieves the best T2I reward-diversity-efficiency trade-off and VLA generalization to held-out tasks and scenes, while real-robot evaluation demonstrates the value of multiple action strategies when the higher-reward target is blocked.

</details>


### [32] [CoDrive: Cross-Vehicle World-Consistent Video Generation with Precise Trajectory Control for Cooperative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34749)
*Yu Meng,Baining Zhao,Junta Wu,Tengfei Wang,Rongze Tang,Haiyu Zhang,Wenqiang Sun,Chen Gao,Zhibo Chen,Xinlei Chen,Yong Li,Xiao-Ping Zhang,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CoDrive，一个跨车辆多视角驾驶视频生成框架，通过局部/全局自注意力、共享世界坐标系的投影相对位置编码和渐进混合任务训练，联合生成多车一致观测，并构建 CoDrive-Bench 进行系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实驾驶本质上是多智能体场景，但现有驾驶世界模型多从单一自车视角生成观测。简单扩展到多车无法保证不同智能体观察到一致共享世界，因此需要跨车辆、多视角且具有精确相机轨迹控制的一致性生成方法。

Method: CoDrive 交错使用局部自注意力和全局自注意力：前者建模每辆车各视角间的时空依赖，后者实现跨车辆信息交换与一致性建模。所有相机轨迹在共享世界坐标系中表示，并通过投影相对位置编码注入注意力层。训练采用渐进混合任务策略，结合大规模真实单智能体数据与合成跨智能体交互数据，以利用真实外观分布并学习跨智能体一致性。同时提出 CoDrive-Bench，覆盖真实与合成多车场景，评估轨迹可控性、场景几何一致性和实例级一致性。

Result: 实验表明，CoDrive 提升了轨迹可控性以及跨智能体几何一致性和实例级一致性，同时保持具有竞争力的视觉质量。

Conclusion: CoDrive 为多智能体驾驶世界建模提供了跨车辆、多视角联合生成方案，并通过专用基准验证了其在一致性控制与生成质量方面的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world driving is inherently multi-agent, yet most existing driving world models generate observations from a single ego vehicle. Independently extending them to multiple vehicles does not ensure that different agents observe a consistent shared world. We present CoDrive, a cross-vehicle, multi-view driving video generation framework that jointly generates observations of vehicles sharing the same dynamic scene with precise camera-trajectory control. CoDrive interleaves local self-attention, which models spatiotemporal dependencies among the views of each vehicle, with global self-attention, which enables information exchange and consistency modeling across vehicles. To explicitly encode their spatial relationships, all camera trajectories are represented in a shared world coordinate system and injected into the attention layers through projective relative positional encoding. We further adopt a progressive mixed-task training strategy that combines large-scale real-world single-agent data with synthetic cross-agent interaction data, allowing the model to benefit from real-world appearance distributions while learning cross-agent consistency from simulation. For systematic evaluation, we introduce CoDrive-Bench, a benchmark covering real and synthetic multi-vehicle scenarios and evaluating trajectory controllability, scene geometry consistency, and instance-level consistency. Experiments show that CoDrive improves trajectory controllability and cross-agent geometric and instance consistency while maintaining competitive visual quality.

</details>


### [33] [D$^2$-VLA: Dual-Memory Dual-Frequency Vision-Language-Action Model For Long Dynamic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34792)
*Zijian Ye,Chengqi Wei,Wei Huang,Anlin Zheng,Chunyu Zou,Liangyu Wu,Zikang Zhao,Zhenjie Peng,Yushuo Yang,Shuman Zhao,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: D²-VLA 通过 KV-cache 双记忆与双频控制，提升长时程操作中 VLA 对历史视觉线索和运动物体的响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作要求机器人记住已不在视野中的线索，并对运动物体作出反应；但现有 VLA 策略通常依赖最新观测，刷新视觉上下文又需额外昂贵的 VLM 前向计算。

Method: 在预训练 VLA 的 KV-cache 接口上引入双记忆与双频控制：使用块级因果 KV 缓存增量编码观测；依据不同时间注意力模式，为 VLM 与动作专家构建独立的历史 KV 读取视图；在周期性 VLM 更新之间，用门控适配器将新视觉特征注入最新历史条件 KV 块，并用短时快记忆队列支持动作重规划。

Result: 提出 DOMINO-Long 十任务基准，要求机器人在操作运动物体时利用早期视觉线索。D²-VLA 在 DOMINO 完整任务成功率 29.3%（π₀.₅ 9.6%，PUMA 17.2%），在 DOMINO-Long 达 60.0%（35.4%、20.6%）；在八项真机任务上提升成功率，LIBERO-Long 97.5%，RoboTwin 2.0 74.3%。

Conclusion: D²-VLA 有效缓解长时程操作中历史信息保持与实时视觉更新之间的权衡，在多项仿真与真机基准上显著优于对比方法。
