Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation requires robots to remember cues that are no longer in view while responding to moving objects. Yet vision-language-action (VLA) policies often rely on the latest observation, and refreshing their visual context typically requires another costly vision-language model (VLM) pass. We present D$^2$-VLA, which combines dual memory and dual-frequency control at the KV-cache interface of a pretrained VLA. D$^2$-VLA uses block-wise causal KV caching to encode observations incrementally and, guided by distinct temporal attention patterns, constructs separate historical KV read views for the VLM and action expert. Between periodic VLM updates, a gated adapter incorporates fresh visual features into the latest history-conditioned KV block, while a short fast-memory queue supports action replanning. We introduce DOMINO-Long, a ten-task benchmark requiring robots to use earlier visual cues when manipulating moving objects. D$^2$-VLA achieves complete-task success rates of 29.3\% on DOMINO, compared with 9.6\% for $\pi_{0.5}$ and 17.2\% for PUMA, and 60.0\% on DOMINO-Long, compared with 35.4\% and 20.6\%, respectively. It improves success rates on eight real-robot tasks and reaches 97.5\% on LIBERO-Long and 74.3\% on RoboTwin 2.0.

</details>


### [34] [WM-VLM: Probing Internal World Models for Interleaved Visual-Textual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34826)
*Yuheng Zha,Yilei Wang,Qiyue Gao,Junrong Chen,Yujia Wu,Zhengfeng Lai,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric P. Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 WM-VLM，为预训练视觉语言模型配备轻量级世界模型分支，使其在解决空间问题时同时用文本与生成的视觉状态进行推理，在2D/3D心理旋转任务上显著优于监督微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类常通过心理模拟视觉变换解决空间问题，而传统视觉语言模型主要依赖语言推理，缺乏对中间视觉状态的显式建模与利用。

Method: 提出 WM-VLM：在预训练 VLM 上增加轻量级世界模型分支以生成中间视觉状态；采用两阶段训练，先学习生成下一视觉状态，再学习利用该状态进行推理；并程序化构建具有可验证中间视觉状态的空间推理任务。

Result: 在2D和3D心理旋转任务上，WM-VLM 持续优于监督微调主干模型，最高提升39.25个百分点；消融实验表明性能提升依赖生成的视觉状态，移除或破坏这些状态会显著降低表现。

Conclusion: 结果表明，内部世界模型为视觉语言模型在语言与视觉空间中联合推理提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Humans often solve spatial problems by mentally simulating visual transformations. In contrast, conventional vision-language models (VLMs) reason primarily through language. We investigate whether VLMs can solve spatial problems by reasoning with both text and generated visual states. To this end, we introduce WM-VLM, which equips a pretrained VLM with a lightweight world model branch for generating intermediate visual states. Our two-stage training first teaches the model to generate the next visual state and then to use that state for reasoning. We programmatically construct spatial reasoning tasks with verifiable intermediate visual states. These tasks allow us to evaluate how well the model generates visual states and how much it relies on them to answer the question. On 2D and 3D mental rotation tasks, WM-VLM consistently outperforms the supervised fine-tuned backbone, with gains of up to 39.25 percentage points. Ablations suggest that these gains depend on the generated visual states, as removing or corrupting them sharply reduces performance. Together, these results suggest that internal world models offer a promising path toward VLMs that reason in both language and visual space.

</details>


### [35] [ECHO: Event-Augmented Context with Hindsight and Outlook for Wrist-Only Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34893)
*Xinyue Wang,Yicheng Jiang,Zesen Gan,Junhao He,Jiaxu Wang,Junhao Li,Jingtao Zhang,Tianlun He,Jianan Wang,Isabel Guan,Qiming Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ECHO——一种仅依赖腕部事件相机的潜在世界动作模型，通过历史事件流与未来事件预测查询，为策略提供时空上下文，在极端曝光下显著提升操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RGB相机的学习型操作策略在极端曝光下观测退化；事件相机虽具高动态范围，但固定相机漏掉静态场景内容，腕部相机运动又使已访问区域离开视野，存在时空局限。

Method: ECHO将腕部事件编码为紧凑运动表示：利用预训练事件编码器解释帧间视觉特征变化；hindsight模块以过去事件流保存夹爪轨迹，作为可寻址的离屏上下文；outlook模块引入可学习事件前瞻查询，监督其预测未来动作对应的事件窗口，使策略可预判场景变化。

Result: 在仅腕部RLBench任务中，正常光照下比RGB和RGB+事件基线分别高20.6和12.0个百分点；严重曝光下降下分别高14.6和11.3个百分点；并超过使用第三人称相机的RGB参考。真实世界腕部事件相机实验在正常与极暗光照的多任务中优于RGB-only和RGB+事件基线。

Conclusion: ECHO通过事件增强的回顾与前瞻时空建模，有效缓解腕部事件相机的时空局限，在极端曝光和真实场景中提升操作策略鲁棒性与性能。

Abstract: Learning-based manipulation policies relying on RGB cameras often suffer from degraded observations under extreme exposure. Event cameras mitigate this degradation by asynchronously detecting pixel-level intensity changes to offer a high dynamic range. However, their observations heavily depend on camera placement, as fixed cameras miss static scene content while wrist-mounted camera motion causes previously visited regions to leave the field of view. To address these spatial-temporal limitations, we present ECHO (Event-augmented Context with Hindsight and Outlook), a wrist-only latent world action model that encodes wrist events into compact motion representations to provide temporal and spatial context for policy reasoning. Specifically, ECHO utilizes a pretrained event encoder to explain visual-feature changes between frames. Its hindsight module preserves the gripper trajectory with past event stream as addressable off-camera context. Concurrently, the outlook module introduces learnable event foresight queries supervised to anticipate the event window for future actions, enabling the policy to predict upcoming scene changes. Evaluated on wrist-only RLBench tasks, ECHO outperforms RGB and RGB+event baselines by 20.6 and 12.0 percentage points under normal lighting, and by 14.6 and 11.3 points under severe exposure drops, respectively, while also surpassing RGB references using a third-person camera. Real-world experiments with a wrist-mounted event camera validate that ECHO outperforms RGB-only and RGB+event baselines across multiple tasks under both nominal and severely dark lighting. Project page is at this https URL .

</details>


### [36] [What Makes World Action Models Generalize? An Empirical Study of Test-Time Future Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34981)
*Renping Zhou,Zanlin Ni,Zihao Fan,Guohao Fu,Zeyu Liu,Hao Shi,Jie Zhang,Chi Bene Chen,Yang Yue,Xueyang Fu,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究世界动作模型（WAMs）在推理阶段是否需要生成未来帧的问题，发现潜在WAMs虽在分布内任务上表现相当，却丧失了泛化优势；分析表明收益几乎全部来自去噪第一步，即"准备"未来而非"生成"未来，据此提出Simple-WAM，仅用一次前向传播处理完全加噪的视频token并调整训练噪声调度，在仿真与真实任务上同时兼顾泛化性能与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）在训练时联合预测未来与动作，但视频去噪计算开销大，推理时是否必须生成未来存在争议：显式WAMs会逐步去噪出清晰帧，潜在WAMs则完全丢弃未来以加速。作者质疑潜在WAMs丢弃未来后是否仍保留促使其提出WAMs的泛化收益，因而需要系统检验这一取舍。

Method: 在匹配的骨干网络、训练数据与计算预算下进行受控对比，从环境扰动、数据效率、任务泛化三个维度评估显式与潜在WAMs的表现差异；通过分析去噪过程定位差距来源，发现泛化差距几乎完全由去噪第一步决定。基于此提出Simple-WAM：将未来建模简化为对完全加噪视频token的单次前向传播，并调整训练时的噪声调度以适配该推理行为。

Result: 当动作专家不再依赖未来表征时，三个泛化维度上均出现一致的性能退化；差距主要源于去噪第一步，说明收益来自对未来的"准备"而非"生成"。在仿真与真实世界任务中，Simple-WAM兼顾两端优势：泛化性能超越显式WAMs，同时效率与潜在WAMs相当。

Conclusion: WAMs的泛化收益并不依赖推理阶段的完整未来生成，而关键在去噪第一步对未来表征的"准备"。通过单次前向传播配合噪声调度适配，Simple-WAM在保持潜在WAMs效率的同时获得显式WAMs的泛化能力，为世界动作模型的高效推理设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) predict the future alongside actions during \emph{training}. Due to the heavy computation cost of video denoising, whether the future must still be generated during \emph{inference} is disputed: Explicit WAMs denoise it into clean frames along with every action chunk, whereas Latent WAMs discard it entirely for acceleration. We find that latent WAMs, despite matching explicit ones on in-distribution tasks, fail to retain the generalization benefits that originally motivated WAMs. To demonstrate this, we evaluate generalization along three axes: \emph{environmental perturbation}, \emph{data efficiency}, and \emph{task generalization}. Controlled comparisons with a matched backbone, training data, and budget reveal consistent degradation across all three axes when the action expert no longer conditions on future representations. Further analysis shows that the gap arises almost entirely from the first denoising step: the benefit comes from \emph{preparing} the future, not \emph{generating} it. We therefore propose \textbf{Simple-WAM}, which simplifies future modeling into a single forward pass of fully noised video tokens and adapts the training-time noise schedule to this inference behavior. Across simulation and real-world tasks, Simple-WAM achieves the best of both worlds, leading explicit WAMs in generalization performance with efficiency comparable to Latent WAMs. Project Page: \href{ this https URL }{\textcolor{panton}{\texttt{ this https URL }}}

</details>


### [37] [ActionUNet: Improving Robustness of VLA Models with Efficient Multi-scale Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34982)
*Di Zhu,Ziheng Yan,Fang Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ActionUNet，一种高效的多尺度微调框架，通过时间对齐的 U-Net 融合层次结构先验，并采用带二阶平滑约束的条件 SIREN 连续动作解码器，以低成本增强预训练 VLA 模型的泛化性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型通过将多模态语义映射为物理动作实现机器人操作，但粗粒度语义难以与细粒度时间执行对齐，导致其在杂乱环境中的泛化能力和鲁棒性受限。此外，多尺度建模可能破坏微观时间连续性并引发机械振荡，产生执行失败。

Method: ActionUNet 是一个高效的多尺度微调框架，仅需极小计算开销即可增强预训练 VLA 模型。首先在时间对齐的动作特征空间内构建轻量级时间 U-Net，融合层次结构先验以弥合语义与时间执行之间的尺度差距；随后针对多尺度建模可能破坏时间连续性的问题，引入条件 SIREN 作为连续动作解码器，并通过显式二阶平滑约束保证时间连续性、减少高频抖动，从而降低机械执行失败。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 与 LIBERO-Plus 基准及真实世界难例评测中，ActionUNet 将 π0.5 的成功率分别绝对提升 9.8%、6.1% 和 11.4%，并可泛化至基于回归的 OpenVLA-OFT 主干，体现出其作为微调策略的有效性与高效性。代码与实现细节已开源。

Conclusion: ActionUNet 通过多尺度结构先验融合与连续动作解码的联合设计，在保持基础 VLA 泛化能力与操作鲁棒性的同时，以低成本显著提升了操作成功率，是一种高效通用的 VLA 微调策略。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown great promise for robotic manipulation by mapping multi-modal semantics to physical actions. However, this mapping inherently struggles to align these coarse-grained semantics with fine-grained temporal execution. It leaves VLA models with limited generalization and insufficient robustness in cluttered environments. To overcome this issue, we propose ActionUNet, an efficient multi-scale fine-tuning framework that enhances pre-trained VLA models with minimal computational cost. ActionUNet first constructs a lightweight temporal U-Net within the temporal-aligned action feature space to fuse hierarchical structural priors, effectively bridging the scale gap between semantics and temporal executions. Recognizing that multi-scale modeling can disrupt microscopic temporal continuity and cause mechanical oscillations, ActionUNet then employs a conditional SIREN as a continuous action decoder. Equipped with explicit second-order smoothness constraints, this decoder guarantees temporal continuity and reduces high-frequency motion jitter. By smoothing temporal discontinuities from multi-scale fusion, this continuous formulation reduces mechanical execution failures while preserving the base VLA model's generalization and manipulation robustness. Extensive experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks, together with real-world hard evaluations, demonstrate that ActionUNet significantly improves {\pi}0.5 success rates by absolute 9.8%, 6.1%, and 11.4%, respectively, while also generalizing to the regression-based OpenVLA-OFT backbone, highlighting its effectiveness and efficiency as a fine-tuning strategy. Code and implementation details are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [38] [Proxy2World: Learning to Generate Worlds From Lightweight Proxies without Seeing Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35023)
*Hongli Xu,Weilong Yan,Anbang Wang,Chunyu Zou,Siyu Hong,Jingwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Proxy2World 是一种可控世界模型，仅用普通姿态 RGBD 视频即可训练，通过结合深度条件 RGB 生成与联合 RGBD 生成的跨模态流匹配，实现推理时的代理-相机混合去噪；同时提出 ProxyBench 基准。实验表明其在结构遵循与视觉质量间取得更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级场景代理可让创作者控制布局与运动，但外观、光照与特效留有想象空间。然而"合适的代理"定义并不唯一，导致成对的代理-视频数据难以大规模自动构建，从而限制了监督式世界模型的训练。

Method: 提出 Proxy2World：从普通姿态 RGBD 视频中学习互补能力，无需在人工制作的代理-视频对上训练。模型通过跨模态流匹配同时学习深度条件 RGB 生成与联合 RGBD 生成。学习这两种任务使推理时可采用代理-相机混合去噪，在遵循代理结构的同时生成自然、细节丰富的画面。此外构建了 ProxyBench，覆盖多样场景、相机轨迹与主体运动。

Result: 在 ProxyBench 上的实验显示，Proxy2World 在结构遵循度与视觉质量之间的平衡优于相机控制类与几何条件类方法，并获得定量指标、VLM 评估、人类评价及多样定性结果的支持。

Conclusion: 无需成对代理-视频数据，仅凭 RGBD 视频的跨模态联合学习即可支撑可控世界建模；代理-相机混合去噪是兼顾结构一致性与视觉真实感的关键，Proxy2World 与 ProxyBench 为该方向提供了方法与实践基准。

Abstract: Lightweight scene proxies let creators control scene layout and motion while leaving room for imagination in appearance, lighting, and visual effects. However, a suitable proxy is not uniquely defined, making paired proxy-video data difficult to construct automatically at scale. We present Proxy2World, a controllable world model that learns these complementary capabilities from ordinary posed RGBD videos, without training on authored proxy-video pairs. The model jointly learns depth-conditioned RGB generation and joint RGBD generation through cross-modal flow matching. Learning both tasks enables proxy-camera hybrid denoising at inference to follow the proxy structure while producing natural, detailed visuals. We further introduce ProxyBench to evaluate this capability across a diverse set of scenes, camera trajectories, and subject motions. Experiments on ProxyBench show that Proxy2World achieves a better balance between structural adherence and visual quality than camera-controlled and geometry-conditioned methods, supported by quantitative metrics, VLM assessments, human evaluations and diverse qualitative results.

</details>


### [39] [OPIS: An Input-Grounded Benchmark for Multi-Object Memory in Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35052)
*Hao Wang,Tao Yu,Liuzhou Zhang,HeXin Wang,Haopeng Jin,Yuxuan Zhou,Xinming Wang,Hongzhu Yi,Xinye Li,Yuanlei Wang,Ping Nie,Yan Huang,Yuxuan Zhang,Pengfei Zhou,Yanyan Zou,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OPIS——一个输入锚定的视频世界模型多物体记忆评测基准，通过固定初始观测中的物体实例来严格衡量模型的真实记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型需在时间上保持世界视觉状态的连续性，但现有评测协议常依赖生成历史、视频参考或选定的重访视角，会混淆对模型真实记忆能力的评估。因此需要一个严格锚定于初始观测中固定物体实例的评测方法来衡量多物体记忆。

Method: OPIS数据集涵盖真实世界、具身机器人和游戏世界三大领域共500个案例，为12,672个刚体、铰接体和可变形体实例提供密集的物体级标注。其以物体为中心的评估器结合关联推理与显式可见性推理，分层测量物体的存在性（Object Presence）、身份一致性（Identity）和结构（Structure），并根据物体运动学采用静态或动态评测轨道。

Result: 在八种图像到视频或相机条件化的世界模型上，OPIS得分介于48.65至56.01之间。当参考清单中的物体数量从少于20个增至40个以上时，存在性、身份和结构得分整体下降，其中平均身份得分从40.22降至23.11。

Conclusion: 结果表明，保持输入中特定物体实例的一致性，远比生成视觉上合理的元素要困难得多，凸显了当前世界模型在多物体记忆方面的显著不足。

Abstract: Video world models must preserve the visual state of the world over time, but existing evaluation protocols often rely on generated histories, video reference, or selected revisit viewpoints that can confound the assessment of a model's true memory capability. To address this, we introduce OPIS, an input-grounded benchmark that strictly anchors the assessment to a fixed set of object instances from the initial observation for evaluating multi-object memory in video world models. The OPIS dataset comprises 500 cases across real-world, embodied-robotic, and game-world domains, providing dense object-level annotations for 12,672 rigid, articulated, and deformable instances. Our object-centric evaluator combines association and explicit visibility reasoning to hierarchically measure Object (O) Presence (P), Identity (I), and Structure (S), utilizing static or dynamic evaluation tracks based on object kinematics. Across eight image-to-video or camera-conditioned world models, our proposed OPIS scores range from 48.65 to 56.01. As the reference inventory grows from less than 20 to more than 40 objects, the Presence, Identity, and Structure scores show an overall decline, with the average Identity score falling from 40.22 to 23.11. The results demonstrate that preserving the particular object instances in the input is considerably harder than generating plausible visual elements.

</details>


### [40] [RefineDrive: Reliable Failure-Guided Learning for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35078)
*Zhe Sun,Ziyi Luo,Yehao Lu,Lei Zhou,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefineDrive 是一个失败引导的后训练框架，通过可靠诊断、最小修正目标检索、修正SFT与带安全分层奖励的GRPO，从自生成失败中学习，在NAVSIM上显著提升VLA驾驶策略，且推理时无需显式诊断或修复阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA自动驾驶模型依赖专家成功示范，未充分利用模型自身失败；失败学习面临诊断不可靠、修正目标不匹配、奖励粗糙三类障碍。

Method: 从模拟器状态生成结构化可验证的碰撞与可行驶区域违规诊断；在聚类人类轨迹库中检索满足当前场景硬安全约束且尽量保留失败预测运动模式的最小修正目标；以驾驶上下文和失败轨迹为条件，将“诊断+修正”作为仅训练用辅助任务进行Correction SFT；再用GRPO与安全分层奖励优化，硬安全优先、保留连续安全反馈，仅在硬安全满足后奖励行驶进展；推理时策略直接预测轨迹，无需显式诊断或修复阶段。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上，RefineDrive将4B基础SFT策略从87.7 PDMS提升至91.7 PDMS；同一检查点无额外训练，在NAVSIM v2扩展指标下于原始NAVTEST场景达到89.4 EPDMS。受控消融验证了结构化诊断监督、检索修正目标与安全分层优化对直接规划的有效性。

Conclusion: 失败引导后训练可有效提升VLA自动驾驶策略的安全性与性能，并保持推理阶段直接规划，不引入额外诊断或修复开销。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving rely heavily on successful expert demonstrations, leaving model-specific failures underexploited. Learning from these failures is hindered by unreliable diagnoses, poorly matched correction targets, and coarse rewards. We propose RefineDrive, a failure-guided post-training framework that learns from self-generated failures through targeted supervision and safety-aware reinforcement learning. Reliable Diagnosis derives structured, verifiable feedback on collisions and drivable-area violations directly from simulator states. Minimum-Correction Target Retrieval searches a clustered human trajectory bank for nearby corrections that satisfy hard-safety constraints in the current scene, prioritizing preservation of the failed prediction's motion pattern. Conditioned on the driving context and failed trajectory, Correction SFT learns to generate the diagnosis followed by the retrieved correction as a training-only auxiliary task. We then apply GRPO with a Safety-Layered Reward that strictly prioritizes hard-safe trajectories, retains continuous safety feedback for both unsafe and hard-safe trajectories, and rewards driving progress only after hard safety is satisfied. At inference, the policy directly predicts trajectories from the driving context without an explicit diagnosis or repair stage. On NAVSIM v1, RefineDrive improves the 4B base SFT policy from 87.7 to 91.7 PDMS. Using the same checkpoint without additional training, RefineDrive achieves 89.4 EPDMS on the original NAVTEST scenes evaluated with NAVSIM v2 extended metrics. Controlled ablations support the benefits of structured diagnosis supervision, retrieved corrections, and safety-layered optimization for direct planning.

</details>


### [41] [RoGSW4RLD: Feed-Forward 4D Gaussian Lifting for Robot World Model Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35311)
*Jin Hyun Kim,Min Young Kim,Soohwan Song,Daekyum Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoGSW4RLD，一种前馈框架，将多相机机器人操作视频（含世界模型生成的预测视频）统一提升为可跨视角、跨时间查询的度量 4D 高斯场，并在 DROID、Cosmos 3 等数据上显著优于逐相机重建加标定融合的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件视频世界模型能从多相机预测机器人未来交互，但输出仍是彼此分离的视频集合，而非可在视角与时间上共享查询的度量场景。现有 4D 重建方法若独立重建各相机流再合并，无法保证跨视角一致性，尤其在使用运动机器人相机与固定外置相机组合时问题更严重。

Method: 提出 RoGSW4RLD 前馈框架，将同步多相机 rollout 提升为统一的、时间可查询的度量 4D 高斯场。它不学习独立的几何转移模型，而是直接重建已有世界模型生成的视觉未来。核心为两阶段架构：阶段 1 融合跨视角证据与机器人特有的关节几何和运动学，联合构建度量 4D 场；阶段 2 精修场的几何与外观，同时严格保持初始时间位移。

Result: 在 256 个留出的 DROID episode 上评估，RoGSW4RLD 显著优于带标定融合的逐相机重建：新视角 PSNR 提升 2.15 dB，深度 AbsRel 降低 47%，机器人位移误差降低 61%。这些增益同样扩展到动作条件的 Cosmos 3 rollout，表明预测的视频未来可成功转化为一致、可空间查询的 4D 度量表示。

Conclusion: RoGSW4RLD 通过前馈的两阶段 4D 高斯场重建，将多相机机器人视频及世界模型预测视频统一为跨视角一致、时间可查询的度量表示，为将预测视频未来转化为可空间查询的 4D 场景提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video world models predict future robot interactions from multiple cameras, yet their outputs remain disparate video collections rather than a shared metric scene queryable across viewpoints and time. While existing 4D reconstruction methods offer a path to spatialize these predictions, independently reconstructing and merging each camera stream fails to enforce cross-view consistency. This limitation is particularly detrimental when combining moving robot-mounted cameras with fixed external views. To address this, we introduce RoGSW4RLD, a feed-forward framework that lifts synchronized multi-camera rollouts into a unified, time-queryable metric 4D Gaussian field. Rather than learning a separate geometric transition model, RoGSW4RLD directly reconstructs the visual future generated by existing world models. Its core innovation is a two-stage architecture: Stage 1 jointly forms the metric 4D field by fusing cross-view evidence with robot-specific articulated geometry and kinematics, while Stage 2 refines the field's geometry and appearance while strictly preserving the initial temporal displacements. Evaluated on 256 held-out DROID episodes, RoGSW4RLD significantly outperforms camera-wise reconstruction with calibrated merging, improving novel-view PSNR by 2.15 dB, reducing depth AbsRel by 47%, and lowering robot displacement error by 61%. These robust gains extend to action-conditioned Cosmos 3 rollouts, demonstrating that predicted video futures can be successfully translated into consistent, spatially queryable 4D metric representations.

</details>


### [42] [Generative Uncertainty as a Self-supervised Signal for Semantic Similarity Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35341)
*Enrico Pallotta,Sina Raoufi,Lars Doorenbos,Gianni Franchi,Juergen Gall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种利用T2V扩散模型生成不确定性进行自监督视频语义相似度学习的方法，无需人工标注，并在OOD检测、视频检索等任务上优于预训练特征和基线特征选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语义相似度是计算机视觉基础问题，广泛用于OOD检测与视频检索；但视频具有复杂时空特性，定义与标注相似度困难且昂贵。

Method: 观察T2V扩散模型对熟悉概念生成一致、对专门概念生成方差大且不确定；据此在VideoMAE、V-JEPA等预训练表征上，仅用生成数据学习一个掩码，保留跨生成稳定的一致语义特征，丢弃与生成噪声或不确定性相关的特征，从而获得语义特征子空间。

Result: 在三个关键任务上，学到的特征子空间一致优于原始预训练特征和基线特征选择方法。

Conclusion: 生成式不确定性可作为自监督信号，用于无标注地学习视频语义相似度表征，并有效提升下游任务表现。

Abstract: Evaluating semantic similarity between videos is a fundamental challenge in computer vision, essential for tasks ranging from out-of-distribution (OOD) detection to video retrieval. However, defining and labeling video similarity is notoriously difficult and expensive due to the complex spatio-temporal nature. In this paper, we propose a novel self-supervised approach that leverages generative uncertainty from text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models to learn semantic similarity without human annotations. Our method is based on the observation that T2V models produce consistent outputs for familiar concepts but exhibit high variance and uncertainty when prompted with specialized concepts. We utilize this behavior to identify stable semantic features within existing pretrained representations, such as VideoMAE and V-JEPA. Specifically, we learn a mask over these embeddings using purely generated data, encouraging the model to retain features that remain consistent across generations of general concepts while discarding those associated with generative noise or uncertainty. Experimental results across three key tasks demonstrate that our learned feature subspaces consistently outperform original pretrained features and baseline feature selection methods.

</details>


### [43] [From Scores to Samples: Elastic Forcing for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35491)
*Chi Zhang,Yueyi Liu,Haoyang Shi,Ruichuan An,Haoyu Li,Yuhang Wu,Sen Cui,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需双向扩散教师和在线假分数模型的少步自回归视频生成后训练方法，直接从参考视频学习 rollout 分布，在冻结自监督视频表征空间中最小化 MMD，并借助混合 Nyström–蒙特卡洛估计器实现高效训练与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步自回归视频生成通常依赖 Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD)，需要双向扩散教师和在线假分数模型，导致训练/内存开销高，难以扩展到大规模模型。作者希望去除这些辅助分数模型，直接从参考视频学习 rollout 分布。

Method: 在冻结的自监督视频表示空间中最小化最大均值差异 (MMD)；使用 Nyström 与蒙特卡洛混合估计器，在近似偏差与采样方差之间取得平衡；采用内存高效的 replay 和梯度子采样使目标可实际训练；模型架构和初始化与 Self-Forcing 相同，但不使用辅助分数模型。

Result: 1.3B 模型将 VBench Total 分数从 83.80 提升至 84.64，并保持 17 FPS；移除辅助分数模型后，可在 8 张 H200 GPU 上对 14B 模型进行后训练；除蒸馏外，还能从参考视频中获取新的视觉风格、语义概念和空间先验，而不需要目标特定的扩散教师。

Conclusion: 该方法去除了对目标特定扩散教师和辅助分数模型的依赖，实现了高效且可扩展的少步自回归视频生成后训练，并支持风格、语义概念和空间先验的迁移学习。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive video generation commonly relies on Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD), requiring a bidirectional diffusion teacher and an online fake-score model. We instead learn the rollout distribution directly from reference videos, eliminating both score models during post-training. Our framework minimizes maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) in frozen self-supervised video representation spaces, using a hybrid Nyström--Monte Carlo estimator to balance approximation bias and sampling variance. Memory-efficient replay and gradient subsampling make this objective practical. Using the same architecture and initialization as Self-Forcing, our 1.3B model improves the VBench Total score from 83.80 to 84.64 while retaining 17 FPS. Removing auxiliary score models also enables 14B post-training on eight H200 GPUs. Beyond distillation, learning from reference videos enables the acquisition of new visual styles, semantic concepts, and spatial priors without a target-specific diffusion teacher.

</details>


### [44] [WorldPlay2: Extending Real-Time Interactive World Models in Control and Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35560)
*Haiyu Zhang,Wenqiang Sun,Tengfei Wang,Junta Wu,Jun Zhang,Yunhong Wang,Yu Qiao,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldPlay2 是一个交互式世界模型，通过因子化混合控制接口、压缩记忆共享和 Stable Forcing 蒸馏，兼顾异构控制、长时一致性与实时响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型需要实时响应多样化控制，并保持长时程一致性；但异构控制建模困难，上下文爆炸与蒸馏不稳定使长时一致性和实时响应难以兼得。因此，论文旨在解决异构控制学习、长时建模效率及蒸馏稳定性之间的耦合问题。

Method: 提出三项核心设计：1）因子化混合控制接口，将帧对齐的动作控制与结构化语义控制结合，显式解耦场景外观、角色身份和动态语义事件，以降低异构控制的学习难度；2）压缩记忆与稳定蒸馏协同设计，将历史上下文压缩为紧凑 memory tokens，由自回归学生模型与双向教师模型共享，从而以片段级、记忆条件化的 score evaluation 替代对完整长 rollout 的联合处理，显著降低蒸馏开销；3）Stable Forcing，通过少步策略初始化自回归学生模型，并利用 full-rollout replay 保持长时 rollout 质量，实现稳定鲁棒的蒸馏。

Result: 大量实验表明，WorldPlay2 具有较强泛化能力，并在性能上优于现有方法，验证了其在交互控制、长时一致性和效率方面的有效性。

Conclusion: WorldPlay2 通过“控制接口因子化 + 压缩记忆 + 稳定蒸馏”的协同设计，为交互式世界模型同时提升可控性、长时一致性与实时性提供了可行路径，并在实验中展现出较强泛化能力和优于现有方法的表现。

Abstract: Interactive world models require responding in real time to versatile controls and maintaining long-horizon consistency. However, modeling heterogeneous controls remains difficult, while explosive contexts and unstable distillation impede achieving both long-horizon consistency and real-time responsiveness. In this paper, we present WorldPlay2, an interactive world model that couples a factorized hybrid control interface with a co-design of compressed memory and stable distillation. 1) Our factorized hybrid control interface integrates frame-aligned action control with structured semantic control that explicitly disentangles scene appearance, character identity, and dynamic semantic events, thereby facilitating effective control learning. 2) To achieve efficient long-horizon modeling, we compress historical contexts into compact memory tokens shared by the autoregressive student and the bidirectional teacher. This design enables clip-wise, memory-conditioned score evaluation instead of jointly processing an entire long rollout, substantially reducing distillation overhead. 3) We further propose Stable Forcing, which initializes the autoregressive student via a few-step strategy and leverages full-rollout replay to preserve the quality of long-horizon rollouts, ensuring robust and stable distillation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the strong generalizability of our model and its superior performance compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [45] [PDMD: Projected Distribution Matching Distillation for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35768)
*Zimo Wang,Junkun Yuan,Angtian Wang,Haotian Yang,Canyu Zhang,Siyuan Yuan,Xingchang Huang,Bo Liu,Yizhi Wang,Yiding Yang,Chongyang Ma,Gordon Guocheng Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PDMD 通过投影去除 DMD 更新中与"学生-评论家端点残差"平行的分量，过滤评论家误差，从而稳定视频扩散蒸馏训练并提升样本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频扩散模型需在长时间跨度的时空 token 序列上执行数十次去噪评估，推理代价高。分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）虽能将函数评估次数（NFE）降至几次，但其样本在训练中会退化，出现渐进式过饱和与伪影。作者将这种不稳定归因于评论家（critic）误差——它在学生的连续更新中进入并随时间累积。

Method: 提出投影分布匹配蒸馏（PDMD）。核心做法是：在 DMD 更新中，投影掉与"学生-评论家端点残差"（student-critic endpoint residual）平行的分量，从而过滤评论家误差。理论分析表明：在固定含噪查询下，该残差是评论家端点误差的无偏估计；在高维假设下，该投影可去除常数比例的评论家误差，同时仅丢弃可忽略比例的理想 DMD 信号。实现上只需对 DMD 做一行代码修改，无需额外损失、网络、数据、模型前向或多阶段训练。

Result: 在 DMD 退化的场景下，该投影能稳定训练并提升样本质量，避免非自然纹理。使用 Wan2.1 时，PDMD 在 4 NFE 下取得 VBench 总分 83.73，比匹配的 DMD 高出 1.03 分。在 MiniMax-H3 视频-音频联合生成上，PDMD 取得 VideoGen-Eval 视觉总分 83.17，比最强蒸馏基线高 0.41 分，并在所比较的 4-NFE 模型中于全部六项音频指标上取得最佳表现。定性对比与用户研究在视觉质量、运动与音频质量上均偏好 PDMD 优于蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: PDMD 是一种低成本且即插即用的改进：仅需一行代码改动即可在少步（4 NFE）扩散蒸馏中抑制评论家误差累积，稳定训练并全面提升视频及视频-音频联合生成的质量。代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Modern video diffusion models require tens of denoising evaluations over long spatiotemporal token sequences. Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) reduces the number of function evaluations (NFE) to just a few. However, DMD samples can degrade during training, exhibiting progressive oversaturation and artifacts. We trace this instability to critic errors, which enter successive student updates and accumulate over time. We introduce Projected Distribution Matching Distillation (PDMD) to filter critic errors. PDMD projects out the component of the DMD update parallel to the student-critic endpoint residual. At a fixed noisy query, we prove that this residual is an unbiased estimate of the critic's endpoint error. Under high-dimensional assumptions, this projection removes a constant fraction of critic error while discarding only a vanishing fraction of ideal DMD signal. Empirically, the projection stabilizes training and improves sample quality where DMD degrades and develops unnatural textures. PDMD requires only a one-line code change to DMD, with no extra loss, network, data, model pass, or multi-stage training. With Wan2.1, PDMD achieves a VBench total score of 83.73 at 4 NFE, surpassing matched DMD by 1.03 points. On MiniMax-H3 joint video-audio generation, PDMD achieves a VideoGen-Eval visual total score of 83.17, 0.41 points above the strongest distilled baseline. PDMD also achieves the best performance on all six audio metrics among the compared 4-NFE models. Qualitative comparisons and user studies favor PDMD over the distilled baselines in visual quality, motion, and audio quality. Code and models are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [46] [DynaTokens: Teaching Dynamics to Camera-Controlled Video Models at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35704)
*Ma Ziqi,Chen Hongqiao,Gkioxari Georgia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DynaTokens：一组轻量级可学习场景专属 token，通过 cross-attention 将物体动态注入已冻结的相机控制世界模型，在保持相机控制的同时实现动态场景演化，在 VBench2 与 WorldScore 上取得优于 LoRA、分块微调等基线的动态-相机平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成包含两类运动：相机路径引起的全局运动与场景动态引起的运动。理想模型应同时支持两者——用户移动相机的同时场景动态持续演化。现有相机控制模型在静态场景中表现良好，但在动态场景中存在物体静止、运动错误或生成质量下降的问题。

Method: 核心观察是两类运动存在不对称性：相机运动全局影响生成视角，而物体动态是空间局部化的。据此提出 DynaTokens——一组轻量级可学习的场景专属 token。通过 cross-attention 机制，仅从少量示例轨迹中训练这些 token，同时保持基础世界模型冻结，从而在新查询相机路径下注入动态。该方法将可训练接口与学习目标的结构相匹配。

Result: 在 VBench2 和 WorldScore 评测中，DynaTokens 在动态与相机的同步权衡上优于 LoRA、block finetuning 以及专门的 trainable-layer 基线。token attention 分析、消融实验与运动时序性研究验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 将可训练接口匹配到学习目标的结构对于有效适配至关重要；DynaTokens 以轻量方式为相机控制世界模型注入场景动态，实现了更优的动态-相机权衡。

Abstract: Video generation must account for two sources of motion, one induced by the observer's camera path and the other caused by scene dynamics. An ideal camera-controlled video model should account for both motions: let users move the camera while evolving the scene dynamics. While current models handle camera-induced motion well in static settings, they struggle for dynamic scenes: objects are static, move incorrectly, or degrade in generation quality. We introduce DynaTokens, a lightweight set of learnable scene-specific tokens that teach dynamics to an existing camera-controlled world model. Our method is motivated by a simple asymmetry between the two sources of motion: whereas camera motion affects the generated view globally, object dynamics are spatially localized. Through cross-attention, DynaTokens trains the learnable tokens from a few example trajectories for a scene while keeping the base model frozen, and enables dynamics under new query camera paths. DynaTokens achieves a better simultaneous dynamics-camera tradeoff on VBench2 and WorldScore evaluations than LoRA, block finetuning, and specialized trainable-layer baselines. Analyses of token attention, ablations, and motion temporality suggest that matching the trainable interface to the structure of the learning target is important for effective adaptation. Project website: this https URL

</details>

### [47] [Typed Decision Models: An Early Evidence Audit and Evaluation Checklist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32160)
*Lijuan Tang,Yuemeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇面向"类型化决策模型（TDMs）"的早期证据综述。作者梳理了2026年9月19日至24日间发布的28篇论文，将其发现与标签概率分类、约束解码、重排序、校准和模型级联等既有工作进行关联。核心结论是：在此早期文献中，类型化读出（typed readout）本身尚未展现出相对同类标签概率读出的独立精度优势；Jev（TypeSafe于2026年9月15日发布的商业TDM）的明显收益主要体现在延迟和成本上，而在更难任务上仍存在精度差距。作者据此提炼出一份含14个条目的评估清单，并强调该综述应被视为早期证据地图而非对模型类别的定论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026年9月15日TypeSafe发布商业类型化决策模型Jev后，数日内即涌现出一批评估与复现工作，亟需对这些零散、快速产出的结果进行初步整合，以厘清类型化决策模型相对既有方法（标签概率分类、约束解码、重排序、校准、模型级联）的实际收益与局限，并为后续研究提供可操作的评估规范。

Method: 作者采用文献综述方法，检索并梳理2026年9月19日至24日期间发布的28篇相关论文，将各项发现与更早的标签概率分类、约束解码、重排序、校准以及模型级联等研究方向进行对照与关联；同时归纳这些研究中反复出现的共性问题（方法学弱点），据以设计评估工具。

Result: 在早期文献中，类型化读出本身并未显示相对于可比标签概率读出的独立精度优势；Jev最明确的收益体现在延迟与成本方面，而在更困难任务上仍存在精度差距。实际部署中，置信度常被用于决定何时将任务移交（defer）给更强的模型或人工处理。

Conclusion: 作者基于反复出现的研究弱点，提出了一份包含14个条目的TDM评估清单，供未来工作使用。由于所覆盖证据仅涉及单一托管模型发布后最初九天的情况，本综述应被理解为一幅早期证据地图，而非对类型化决策模型这一模型类别的成熟定论。

Abstract: Typed decision models (TDMs) return probability distributions over caller-defined options without generating text. TypeSafe released Jev, a commercial typed decision model, on 15 September 2026, and a small body of evaluation and replication work appeared within days. We review 28 papers posted between 19 and 24 September and relate their findings to earlier work on label-probability classification, constrained decoding, reranking, calibration, and model cascades. In this early literature, the typed readout itself has not shown an independent accuracy advantage over comparable label-probability readouts. Jev's clearest gains are in latency and cost, while accuracy gaps remain on harder tasks. In practical deployments, confidence is often used to decide when to defer to a stronger model or a human. We use recurring weaknesses in these studies to derive a 14-item evaluation checklist for future TDM work. Because the evidence covers only the first nine days after the release of one hosted model, the review should be read as an early evidence map rather than a settled assessment of the model class.

</details>


### [48] [From Knowing to Abstaining: Bridging the Representation-Action Gap in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32653)
*Jialuo He,Huangxun Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出VAD-R基准（通过捷径过滤与质量验证两阶段流程构建，标注逐步推理与因果证据缺口标签）来评估视觉语言模型的回避能力，并基于“隐藏状态可区分可回答性但未体现在输出中”的发现，提出表示到动作对齐方法Rep2Act，显著提升模型拒答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估VLM回避能力的基准存在两大缺陷：一是样本在图像或问题中包含捷径线索泄露可回答性，且显式的"unanswerable"选项阻碍了对自发回避能力的准确评估；二是训练数据仅提供二元标签，缺乏细粒度解释以进行更深层监督。

Method: 提出VAD-R基准，通过捷径过滤与质量验证的两阶段流程构建以防止答案可获取性泄露，每个样本标注逐步推理链与因果证据缺口标签。在此基础上，受"某些层的隐藏状态表征可有效区分可回答性但未体现在最终响应中"这一探针分析发现启发，提出Rep2Act——一种将潜在可回答性意识转化为显式回避决策的表示到动作对齐方法。

Result: 在VAD-R上，顶尖开源与闭源VLM的自发回避能力有限，平均召回率分别仅为11.4%和16.3%。Rep2Act将Qwen2.5-VL-3B的动作准确率从56.67%提升至86.33%，将Qwen2.5-VL-7B从59.33%提升至88.67%。在分布外TUBench上，仅用3B模型即达到53.3%的平均F1分数，分别超越闭源GPT-4 Turbo和GPT-4o达16.2%和1.1%。

Conclusion: VAD-R通过避免答案可获取性泄露并提供细粒度推理监督，实现了对VLM自发回避能力的更准确评估；Rep2Act证明将模型潜藏的可回答性表征对齐到显式回避动作是可行且高效的，能以较小模型超越大型闭源模型。

Abstract: The ability of vision-language models (VLMs) to abstain from unanswerable questions is as important as their ability to answer answerable ones accurately. Recently, several benchmarks have emerged to evaluate and improve VLM abstention, but they have substantial limitations. First, samples often contain shortcut cues in images or questions that reveal answerability, while an explicit "unanswerable" option further prevents accurate assessment of spontaneous abstention. Second, as training data, they generally provide only binary labels without fine-grained explanations for deeper supervision. To address these limitations, we introduce Visual Answerability Diagnosis with Rationales (VAD-R), a benchmark constructed through a two-stage pipeline of shortcut filtering and quality verification to prevent answerability leakage. Each example is annotated with step-by-step rationales and causal evidence-gap labels. Evaluation of state-of-the-art open- and closed-source VLMs on VAD-R reveals limited spontaneous abstention, with average recall rates of only 11.4% and 16.3%, respectively. Probing analyses show that hidden-state representations in certain layers can effectively distinguish answerability, yet this distinction fails to manifest in final responses. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Rep2Act, a representation-to-action alignment method that translates latent answerability awareness into explicit abstention decisions. Rep2Act improves action accuracy on VAD-R from 56.67% to 86.33% for Qwen2.5-VL-3B and from 59.33% to 88.67% for Qwen2.5-VL-7B. On the out-of-distribution TUBench, Rep2Act achieves an average F1 score of 53.3% with only a 3B model, surpassing the closed-source GPT-4 Turbo and GPT-4o by 16.2% and 1.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [49] [On the Behavioral Traits of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32776)
*Haokai Zhao,Jie Gao,Yunze Xiao,Xintao Wang,Weihao Xuan,Aditya Joshi,Mark Dredze,Jen-tse Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 A-B-D 框架，从真实交互轨迹的行为数据（B-data）自下而上推断 AI 智能体的"人格特质"，弥补自我报告（S-data）与实际行为脱节、LLM 评委评分（I-data）难以规模化且场景覆盖有限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户日益将不同 AI 智能体视为各有特点的"同事"，因此需要量化其类人"人格特质"。但现有测量手段存在双重缺陷：一是模型的自我报告（S-data）与其真实行为不一致；二是来自 LLM 评委的他人评分（I-data）规模化成本高、且覆盖的日常场景很少。因此需要一种可扩展、贴近真实行为的人格测量路径。

Method: 提出 A-B-D 方法，从现有交互轨迹的行为数据（B-data）中自下而上推断特质。数据规模为 345,667 条真实世界轨迹，覆盖 80 个模型、12 类任务、50 种运行框架（harness）。从中抽取 318 个候选特征，涵盖智能体每一步所采取的动作（功能性）与伴随的语言（语言性）。通过三重筛选（实例级稳定性、跨任务一致性、模型可区分性）保留 79 个特征，再对其做因子分析。

Result: 因子分析从 79 个特征中提取出六个稳定且可归因于模型的因子，其中两个为功能性、四个为语言性。例如 Kimi-K3 表现出最高的计划性（planfulness），而 GPT-5.5 与 GPT-5.6 的活力（energetic）最低。此外，量化了现实中的"知行差距"：这些因子与自我报告的大五人格得分仅呈弱相关，即便是概念上对应的配对（如外向性与 energetic）相关系数也仅为 r = 0.07（p = 0.58）。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解 AI 人格提供了新视角，对用户、开发者以及计算机科学与社会科学领域的研究者均具有启示意义。

Abstract: Users increasingly describe different AI agents as distinct colleagues to work with. AI personality research aims to quantify such impressions by attributing human-like "traits" to agents. However, existing measures fall short: models' self-reports (S-data) diverge from their actual behavior, while informant ratings from LLM judges (I-data) are costly to scale and cover few everyday scenarios. In this paper, we propose A-B-D to infer traits bottom-up from behavioral data (B-data), namely how agents act on their environment and communicate with users, as recorded in existing trajectories. From 345,667 real-world trajectories spanning 80 models, 12 tasks, and 50 harnesses, we extract 318 candidate features that capture both the actions an agent takes at each step (functional) and the language accompanying them (linguistic). We retain only features that show instance-level stability, cross-task consistency, and model discriminability. Factor analysis of the remaining 79 features uncovers six stable, model-attributable factors, two functional and four linguistic. For example, Kimi-K3 exhibits the most planfulness, whereas GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6 are the least energetic. Moreover, we quantify the "knowledge-action gap" in the wild: these factors correlate only weakly with self-reported Big Five scores, even for conceptually matched pairs such as extroversion and energetic (r = 0.07, p = 0.58). Our work offers a new lens for understanding AI personality, with implications for users, developers, and researchers from both computer science and social science.

</details>


### [50] [OpenTumorBoard: A Real-World Benchmark of Multidisciplinary Tumor Board Discussion Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32810)
*Anqi Li,Zhixuan Ge,Yixuan Duan,Jiarong Qian,Chi-Yu Chen,MingYu Lu,Huan-Yu Hsu,Yu Gu,Yue Guo,Sheng Wang,Wei Qiu,Hanwen Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 OpenTumorBoard 真实肿瘤委员会讨论基准，覆盖 611 例病例与 19,157 轮讨论，揭示当前 LLM 在多学科癌症决策中的明显不足，并表明真实讨论轨迹可用于模型适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多学科肿瘤委员会通过专家讨论整合多模态临床观察与纵向病史，但现有基准很少捕捉此类真实世界的决策轨迹，难以评估 LLM 在真实临床讨论中的表现。

Method: 构建 OpenTumorBoard 基准：从 YouTube 公开肿瘤委员会录制中转录 12,534 分钟内容，形成 611 例患者病例、19,157 个讨论轮次、10 类专家角色。设置两种评测任务：SPECIALIST TURN（对真实讨论中临床显著问题作答）与 BOARD SIMULATION（生成完整往复讨论，并就治疗建议、手术计划、下一步行动和临床试验匹配达成共识）。评估 14 个通用前沿与医学 LLM，并开展监督微调与强化学习实验；另由 3 名医学专家审核子集。

Result: 最佳模型在临床等效性上得分 3.43/5，与记录委员会结论一致性得分 2.78/5，显示现有模型能力有限。监督微调和强化学习可在留出测试集上提升性能，表明真实讨论轨迹可支持模型适配。3 名医学专家审核发现病例信息覆盖度高、事实性强，提取的共识结论保真度强。

Conclusion: 将发布 OpenTumorBoard 及其自动化整理流程，以支持面向多学科、个性化癌症决策的 LLM 开发与评估。

Abstract: Multidisciplinary tumor boards integrate multimodal clinical observations and longitudinal patient histories through specialist discussions, yet benchmarks rarely capture these real-world trajectories. We introduce OpenTumorBoard, a benchmark with 611 patient cases and 19,157 discussion turns across ten specialist roles, transcribed from 12,534 minutes of publicly available tumor board recordings on YouTube. The benchmark evaluates two settings: SPECIALIST TURN, in which an LLM responds to a clinically significant question posed during a real discussion, and BOARD SIMULATION, in which it generates an entire back-and-forth discussion and reaches a consensus on therapy recommendations, surgical plans, next actions and clinical trial matching. Evaluation of 14 general-purpose frontier and medical LLMs reveals substantial limitations: the best models score 3.43 out of 5 in clinical equivalence to specialist answers and 2.78 out of 5 in alignment with recorded board conclusions. Supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning improve performance on a held-out test set, suggesting that real-world discussion trajectories can support model adaptation. Three M.D. experts review a subset of the benchmark, finding high information coverage and factuality of patient cases and strong fidelity of extracted consensus conclusions. We will release OpenTumorBoard and its automated curation pipeline to support the development and evaluation of LLMs for multidisciplinary, personalized cancer decision-making.

</details>


### [51] [Beyond Calibration: Do a Typed-Decision Model's Probabilities Obey the Probability Axioms?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33209)
*Keyi Li,Yihao He,Quanyi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出用无需标注的"逻辑关联问题组"检验类型化决策模型（如Jev）的"连贯性"（coherence），即相关问题的概率是否逐项自洽。在ChaosNLI与PubMedQA的160个三选一项目上，Jev对"标签为X"与"标签非X"的概率平均缺失和为0.064，Qwen3.8-27B分别为0.293（首token）与0.122（言语化）；两系统均不连贯且失效模式不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型化决策模型以概率而非文本回答声明式的二选一或多选问题，现有评估仅报告准确率与校准度，二者都不要求模型对逻辑相关问题给出的概率逐项自洽；然而真正依据这些概率行动的系统正需要这种自洽性（连贯性）。因此需要一个无需标签、能直接检验连贯性的评测方法。

Method: 构建一组逻辑关联问题（如"标签是X吗""标签不是X吗""标签是另两个之一吗""是哪个标签"），无需标签即可检验连贯性。评测对象为TypeSafe的Jev与Qwen3.8-27B（官方BF16权重）。数据为ChaosNLI与PubMedQA的160个项目，各含三个互斥标签；共480个否定对。对Qwen3.8-27B分别考察首token概率与言语化概率两种读取方式，并做对照实验（两系统概率相近的对、去除双重否定标签、对Jev重复调用取平均）。

Result: 在480个否定对上，Jev"标签为X"与"标签非X"的概率平均之和偏离1达0.064（95% CI 0.055–0.072）；Qwen3.8-27B首token为0.293，言语化为0.122。首token读取的差距在两系统概率相近的对、无双重否定标签的对、以及Jev重复调用取平均后依然存在。Jev同样不连贯：其违例约为重复噪声的5倍，且过度支持单一标签的陈述，使三个单标签概率平均之和达1.14。两者失效模式不同：Qwen3.8-27B首token读取无论问题是否含"not"都低估某标签的补集，在480对中有196对同时拒绝一个陈述及其否定，且并不在更自信处变得更连贯；而Jev的违例则集中于其答案不确定之处。

Conclusion: 由于该检验无需标签，它能揭示只有在比较不同问题形式时才显现的偏差以及同一项目内部的不一致，从而暴露准确率和校准度评估所无法发现的连贯性缺陷。

Abstract: Typed-decision models such as TypeSafe's Jev answer a declared yes/no or multiple-choice question about a state with a probability instead of text, and their evaluations report accuracy and calibration. Neither requires that the probabilities a model gives to logically related questions fit together item by item, which is what a system that acts on those probabilities needs. We test this property, coherence, with a battery of logically linked questions that needs no labels. For 160 items from ChaosNLI and PubMedQA, each with three mutually exclusive labels, we ask whether the label is X, whether it is not X, whether it is one of the other two labels, and which label applies. On 480 negation pairs, Jev's probabilities for "the label is X" and "the label is not X" miss summing to one by 0.064 on average (95% CI 0.055 to 0.072). Qwen3.8-27B, run from its official BF16 weights, misses by 0.293 with first-token probabilities and by 0.122 with verbalized probabilities. The gap to the first-token readout persists on pairs where both systems give similar probabilities, without double-negation labels, and after averaging Jev's repeated calls. Jev is not coherent either: its violations are about five times its repeat noise, and it over-endorses statements about single labels, so that its three single-label probabilities sum to 1.14 on average. The two systems also fail differently. Qwen3.8-27B's first-token readout under-endorses the complement of a label whether or not the question contains "not", rejecting both a statement and its negation in 196 of 480 pairs, and it does not become more coherent where it is more confident, whereas Jev's violations concentrate where its answer is uncertain. Because the checks need no labels, they expose biases that appear only when question forms are compared, and inconsistencies within items.

</details>


### [52] [Does Learning to Predict the World Help Agents Act? Auditing World-Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33335)
*Xinyu Che,Hang Yan,Yanchen Liu,Haochen Liu,Ruifeng Li,Anran Shi,Heng Wang,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文质疑“世界模型式下一观测预测训练”的性能增益来源：通过错配观测目标和随机奖励训练发现，即使预测不准、奖励不含环境信息，智能体仍获得显著任务提升，表明增益可能更多来自优化过程对探索/行为的正则化，而非准确预测世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法要求智能体预测下一观测，并将预测转化为奖励或监督信号，即世界模型。但这类方法除学习预测环境外，还引入额外优化过程，因此性能增益究竟来自“更准确地预测世界”还是训练过程本身，尚不清楚。

Method: 在训练中用同分布但错配的观测替代真实下一观测目标；将基于预测的奖励替换为独立随机信号；在两个交互式文本环境和 VisualWebArena 中对比基模型与训练模型，并以 pass@64、预测准确率、候选动作数量和循环行为等指标评估。

Result: 错配目标使预测准确率相对真实目标下降 15.3%–61.6%，但仍保留显著任务增益；训练模型考虑更多候选动作且更少循环。随机奖励即使不含环境信息也能扩大任务覆盖（pass@64）。在 VisualWebArena 中，随机奖励训练使 pass@64 相对基模型提升 14.3%，且无需观测匹配奖励或外部多模态教师构建奖励。

Conclusion: 下一观测预测训练的收益并不能简单归因于准确的世界预测；优化过程本身可能带来探索增强、减少循环和任务覆盖提升。世界模型信息或预测奖励并非获得性能增益的必要条件，这对现有世界模型后训练范式提出挑战。

Abstract: Predicting how an environment will change before acting is a natural route to better decision making for agents. Recent post-training methods therefore require agents to predict the next observation and turn that prediction into a reward or a direct supervision signal, which is called world model. Existing next-observation training methods help the agent to learn the environmental content. However, they additionally involve an optimization process, which may introduce several effects other than learning to predict the world. Consequently, where the performance gain comes from during the training process remains an open question. We answer this research question through replacing true next-observation targets with in-distribution mismatched observations during the training process. Across two interactive text environments, mismatched targets lower prediction accuracy by 15.3-61.6% relative to ground-truth targets, yet retain substantial task gains over the base model. Compared with the base model, trained models consider more candidate actions and exhibit less looping. We also introduce a setting that replaces prediction-based rewards with independent random signals. This training expands task coverage (pass@64) even when the reward carries no environment information. We also generalize this finding to VisualWebArena, where random-reward training raises pass@64 by 14.3% relative to the base model, without observation-matching rewards or an external multimodal teacher for reward construction.

</details>


### [53] [E-CONAN (Entailment, CONtradition And Neutral) Diagnostics Dataset Investigating Linguistic Phenomena in Arabic Natural Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33530)
*Khloud AL Jallad,Nada Ghneim,Ghaida Rebdawi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对跨语言自然语言理解（NLU）错误分析缺乏统一分类体系的问题，提出跨语言 NLU 错误分析层级结构，并以阿拉伯语 NLU 为案例构建 NLI 层级框架，发布人工标注的 E-CONAN 诊断数据集，用于评估 9 个预训练语言模型和 5 个 LLM 的细粒度错误表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLU 在词汇歧义、高层推理等复杂语言现象上仍存在明显不足。系统分析不同语言现象下的错误，有助于全面评估模型局限与能力并优化泛化性能。现有诊断数据集虽可用于细粒度错误分析，但宏观/微观类别缺乏统一命名规范，也没有标准化的语言现象覆盖集合，导致跨研究、跨语言比较困难。

Method: 作者提出面向跨语言 NLU 错误分析的初始层级结构，并给出构建 NLI 层级框架的方法论；以阿拉伯语 NLU 为案例开展研究。进一步构建 E-CONAN 诊断数据集，基于所提出的阿拉伯语层级体系进行人工标注，涵盖粗粒度与细粒度类别。随后使用 E-CONAN 对 9 个预训练语言模型和 5 个 LLM 进行错误分析与性能评估。

Result: 实验表明：LLM 在世界知识与常识推理宏观类别上优于预训练模型，但在句法宏观类别上不如预训练模型。对所有模型而言，最难的语言现象均为 Reasoning；对全部预训练模型最容易的是 Syntactic，而对 LLM 最容易的是 Lexico-Syntactic。

Conclusion: 该工作为跨语言 NLU 错误分析提供了可复用的层级化分类框架和阿拉伯语诊断资源 E-CONAN，能够帮助 NLU 设计者开展细粒度错误分析与模型诊断。结果揭示了 LLM 与预训练模型在不同语言现象上的互补优势与薄弱环节，为后续模型改进和跨语言泛化研究提供了依据。

Abstract: Natural Language Understanding (NLU) plays a crucial role in various applications, yet its performance suffers from weaknesses in handling the complexities of human languages, ranging from lexical ambiguity to high-level reasoning difficulties. Analyzing errors across diverse linguistic phenomena is crucial for NLU improvement, as it will help humans get insights to comprehensively assess models' limitations and capabilities, so optimizing models' generalization. Notably, several benchmarks contain diagnostics datasets designed for investigation and fine-grained error analysis. When highlighting the gaps in the state-of-the-art, we noted that there is no naming convention for macro and micro categories or even a standard set of linguistic phenomena that should be covered. To overcome this gap, we propose an initial hierarchy for Cross-Lingual NLU error analysis. Moreover, we propose a methodology to create an NLI hierarchical framework and applied a case study on Arabic NLU. Moreover, this paper introduces E-CONAN diagnostics dataset, a freely available dataset manually-annotated with coarse-grained and fine-grained categories based on our proposed Arabic hierarchy. E-CONAN dataset helps NLU designers better understand their models by doing error analysis and in-depth investigation. We used E-CONAN to investigate the performance of 9 pretrained language models and 5 LLMs. Results indicate that LLMs outperform pretrained models in world knowledge and commonsense reasoning macro-category, and underperform pretrained models in syntactic macro-category. Moreover, the hardest phenomena for all models is Reasoning, and the easiest phenomena for all pretrained models is Syntactic, and the easiest for LLMs is Lexico-Syntactic.

</details>


### [54] [Jev Matches 7B Language Models for Speech-Neuroprosthesis Rescoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33538)
*Gabriele Cinà*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出用托管模型 Jev 替代语音神经假肢中需 GPU 的十亿级语言模型重打分器，将重打分建模为单次类型化决策并输出所有候选句概率，再与解码器得分融合；在 ALS 参与者 978 句测试集上，Jev 的词错误率略优于 OPT-6.7b 和 Qwen2.5-7B，且无需 GPU、成本低，但端到端延迟不优于本地 7B GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音神经假肢从脑活动解码尝试性语音后，需用数十亿参数语言模型对候选句重打分，而该组件是唯一依赖 GPU 的部分。直接换用更廉价模型存在困难：通用语言模型被要求从列表中选一句时，往往依据标签位置而非句子内容作答，产生位置偏差，难以可靠重打分。

Method: 将重打分形式化为单次类型化决策：一次调用返回每个候选句的概率；由托管模型 Jev 提供该服务，Jev 针对校准决策训练；随后将 Jev 概率与解码器自身得分结合。对比对象为 OPT-6.7b 与 Qwen2.5-7B，并在重调解码器权重后再次比较。

Result: 在 ALS 参与者 978 条留出句上，已发表解码器单独词错误率为 8.1%；Jev 达 7.5%，OPT-6.7b 与 Qwen2.5-7B 均为 7.8%。重调解码器权重后，Jev 为 6.9%，二者为 7.2% 与 7.4%。Jev 在四项比较中均领先，且在 95% 置信界上最多落后 0.2 个百分点。成本为每千句 0.07 美元，无需 GPU；专用 GPU 运行 7B 模型仅在利用率高于 43% 时每句更便宜，远超单用户产生量。端到端互联网延迟 262 ms，其中 62 ms 在服务商处；与本地 GPU 上 7B 模型的 27 ms 同量级，但不更快。

Conclusion: Jev 可作为语音神经假肢重打分的无 GPU、低成本替代方案，在词错误率上略优于 7B 级通用语言模型，并避免列表位置偏差；其经济性适合单用户低吞吐场景。局限是依赖外部托管服务与网络，端到端延迟未超过本地 GPU 7B 模型。

Abstract: A speech neuroprosthesis decodes attempted speech from brain activity and ends by rescoring the decoder's candidate sentences with a language model of several billion parameters, the only component that needs a GPU. Replacing that model with a cheaper one is hard: general language models asked to pick one sentence from a list answer from where a label sits in the list rather than from the sentence itself. We pose rescoring as a single typed decision, one call that returns a probability for every candidate, served by Jev, a hosted model trained for calibrated decisions, and combine it with the decoder's own score. On 978 held-out sentences from a participant with ALS, where the published decoder alone reaches 8.1% word error, Jev reaches 7.5% against 7.8% for both OPT-6.7b and Qwen2.5-7B; with the decoder's weight re-tuned, 6.9% against 7.2% and 7.4%. Jev is ahead in all four comparisons and at most 0.2 points behind at the 95% bound. It costs 0.07 USD per thousand sentences and needs no GPU; a dedicated GPU running a 7B model is cheaper per sentence only above 43% utilisation, far beyond what one user generates. End-to-end latency over the internet is 262 ms, of which 62 ms is spent at the provider, the same order as a 7B model on a local GPU (27 ms) but not faster.

</details>


### [55] [Do System One Decisions Add Up? A Study of Probabilistic Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33971)
*Saman Sarker Joy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究决策模型在"直接细粒度预测"与"经宽类目重构"之间的概率一致性（probabilistic coherence），在 Jev 与 English Laya 两个系统、TREC/CLINC150/MASSIVE 三个数据集上对比，发现二者存在显著不一致，且对准确率的影响方向相反，同时准确率提升可能伴随校准变差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型对每个问题给出的概率虽满足加和为 1，但当同一决策被拆分成更小的步骤时却可能自相矛盾，即缺乏概率一致性（probabilistic coherence）。这种不一致在真实应用工作流中可能带来隐患，因此需要专门研究。

Method: 在 Jev 与 English Laya checkpoint 两个系统上研究概率一致性，每个系统在 TREC、CLINC150、MASSIVE 三数据集上使用 2,500 个配对样本；共覆盖 72,000 个分类问题。比较三类预测：直接细粒度标签预测、宽类目概率、以及经由宽类目重构得到的预测；并用配对 bootstrap 区间等方法量化准确率变化与校准误差。

Result: 两系统均表现出显著不一致：类目层面的平均 total variation 对 Jev 为 0.219–0.349，对 Laya 为 0.424–0.689（0 表示完全一致）。后果差异明显：在 CLINC150 上，重构使 Jev 准确率下降 22.9 个百分点（配对 95% bootstrap 区间 [-24.9, -20.9]），使 Laya 提升 21.3 个百分点（[18.0, 24.5]）；该方向在三个数据集上均成立，全部六个未校正准确率变化区间均不含零。准确率提升亦可伴随置信度更不可靠：在 MASSIVE 上 Laya 准确率提升 9.2 个百分点，但期望校准误差（ECE）从 0.046 升至 0.124。误差分析同时识别出宽类目错误与类目内混淆。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，决策系统需要在应用所用的实际工作流中，对准确率、置信度校准与概率一致性进行联合评估，而不能仅看单一指标。

Abstract: A decision model can give probabilities that sum to one for every question yet disagree with itself when the same decision is broken into smaller steps. We study this form of probabilistic coherence in Jev and the English Laya checkpoint, using 2,500 matched examples per system across TREC, CLINC150, and MASSIVE. Across 72,000 classification questions, we compare direct fine-label predictions with broad-category probabilities and predictions reconstructed through those categories. Both systems show substantial disagreement: mean category-level total variation ranges from 0.219 to 0.349 for Jev and from 0.424 to 0.689 for Laya, on a scale where zero means exact agreement. The consequences differ sharply. On CLINC150, reconstruction reduces Jev's accuracy by 22.9 percentage points (paired 95% bootstrap interval: [-24.9, -20.9]) and improves Laya's by 21.3 points ([18.0, 24.5]). The same directions hold across all three datasets, with all six unadjusted accuracy-change intervals excluding zero. Improved accuracy can also accompany less reliable confidence: on MASSIVE, Laya gains 9.2 accuracy points while its expected calibration error rises from 0.046 to 0.124. Error analysis identifies both broad-category mistakes and within-category confusions. These findings show why decision systems need joint evaluation of accuracy, confidence calibration, and probability coherence in the workflow used by an application.

</details>


### [56] [AgentHop: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Agentic Multi-Hop Scientific Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34428)
*Chanhee Park,Jeongho Yoon,Sungbin Han,Hyeonseok Moon,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 AgentHop 诊断型基准：1,011 道多选题 + 受控七工具沙盒，在固定 token、轮次和工具调用约束下，将单一准确率拆解为检索、综合、工具调用、资源管理四个维度，并评估 19 个模型以揭示不同模型家族的失败模式与行为指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体任务需要大语言模型在多步交互中导航信息、收集证据，且受资源限制，失败来源多样；定位失败原因对诊断和改进智能体系统至关重要。但现有基准多只给出单一排行榜分数，失败模式不透明。

Method: 构建 AgentHop 诊断基准，包含 1,011 道多选题，并配备受控的七工具沙盒；在固定 token、轮次与工具调用次数约束下，将单个准确率分解为四个操作维度：检索、综合、工具调用和资源管理。对 19 个模型进行系统性评估与行为聚类分析。

Result: 模型行为按模型家族聚类，工具调用特征显示家族指纹：GPT 模型倾向提前提交；Anthropic 与 GLM 检查点先验证再提交；DeepSeek 与 Kimi 过度搜索；Gemini-3 Pro 保持平衡。分解维度还揭示家族内部结构：Claude Opus 4.6 与 Sonnet 4.6 准确率相差不到 1 个百分点，但在检索与综合侧重上分化，Opus 检索更多，Sonnet 综合更好。

Conclusion: AgentHop 通过多维度诊断而非单一分数，暴露智能体在检索、综合、工具调用和资源管理上的细粒度失败模式；作者发布完整基准集与测试框架，以支持诊断型智能体基准评测。

Abstract: Agentic tasks require a large language model to interact with the world, navigating information and gathering evidence across multiple steps with restricted resources. Due to this complexity, agentic task failures arise from various sources, and pinpointing these failure causes is essential to diagnose and improve agentic systems. Existing benchmarks, however, tend to focus on a single leaderboard score, leaving the underlying failure modes opaque. To fill this gap, we introduce AgentHop, a diagnostic benchmark of 1,011 multiple-choice questions paired with a controlled seven-tool sandbox under fixed token, turn, and tool-call constraints. AgentHop reveals model vulnerabilities by dissecting a single accuracy score along four axes of agent operation: retrieval, synthesis, tool-call, and resource management. Across 19 models, we find that behavior clusters by model family, with tool-call signatures revealing distinct family fingerprints: GPT models commit early, Anthropic and GLM checkpoints verify before committing, DeepSeek and Kimi over-search, and Gemini-3 Pro stays balanced. Decomposed axes further expose within-family structure: Claude Opus 4.6 and Sonnet 4.6 land within one accuracy point yet diverge on retrieval-versus-synthesis emphasis, with Opus retrieving more and Sonnet synthesizing better. We release the full benchmark set and the harness to support diagnostic agent benchmarking.

</details>


### [57] [Decide, Don't Generate: Competitive Dimensional ABSA with Jev's Typed Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35293)
*Yiqun Zhang,Peidong Wang,Zihan Wang,Shi Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文证明维度ABSA无需文本生成：用冻结模型Jev将SemEval-2026 Task III Track A三类任务分解为类型化问答/评分/是-否判断，仅用488个CPU拟合系数对齐标注，便在效价-唤醒回归上取得最低聚合RMSE 1.0645，并在三元组/四元组抽取上超过微调Llama-3.3-70B与GPT-OSS-120B基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ABSA大量转向文本生成范式，但作者质疑维度ABSA是否必须依赖生成式建模。研究动机是验证：仅靠冻结模型进行结构化决策与轻量校准，能否在无需文本生成、无需骨干微调的情况下，取得有竞争力的维度ABSA结果。

Method: 使用冻结模型Jev，通过回答类型化问题产生三类信号：评分标准分数、标签概率、是/否判断；将SemEval-2026 Task III Track A的三个任务统一分解为这些决策，并用488个系数在CPU上拟合，以对齐标注方案。整个流程不进行文本生成，也不调整骨干模型参数。

Result: 在六种语言、十个语料库的效价-唤醒回归上，系统达到1.0645 RMSE，为所有参赛系统中最低聚合误差。在三元组和四元组抽取上，分别达到52.09和44.06连续F1，高于微调Llama-3.3-70B和GPT-OSS-120B基线。消融分析显示：监督校准将原始回归误差约减半；即使精确效价-唤醒也仅给抽取增加约4.5 F1；抽取系统主要依赖跨度边界证据的学习组合，而非单一信号。

Conclusion: 竞争性维度ABSA不必依赖文本生成或大模型微调；冻结模型加少量监督校准即可有效对齐标注并完成回归与抽取任务。其中监督校准对回归性能至关重要，抽取性能则来自多种跨度边界证据的组合，而非任何单一线索。

Abstract: Aspect-based sentiment analysis (ABSA) has largely turned to text generation. We show that competitive dimensional ABSA does not need it. Using Jev, a frozen model that answers typed questions with rubric scores, label probabilities, and yes/no judgments, we decompose all three tasks of SemEval-2026 Task III Track A into such decisions and align them with the annotation scheme through 488 coefficients fitted on CPU, with no text generation and no backbone tuning. On valence-arousal regression over ten corpora in six languages, the system reaches 1.0645 RMSE, the lowest aggregate error of any participating system. On triplet and quadruplet extraction, it reaches 52.09 and 44.06 continuous F1, above fine-tuned Llama-3.3-70B and GPT-OSS-120B baselines. Analyses and ablations show where the accuracy comes from: supervised calibration roughly halves the raw regression error, exact valence-arousal would add only 4.5 F1 to extraction, and the learned combination of span-boundary evidence, not any single signal, carries the extraction systems.

</details>


### [58] [How Well Can LLMs Simulate Real Learner Evaluations of Educational Feedback?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35376)
*Momoka Furuhashi,Kouta Nakayama,Takashi Kodama,Saku Sugawara,Kyosuke Takami*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 用真实高中生生物学题目反馈评价数据，在群体和个体层面检验LLM模拟学习者主观评价的能力，并比较加入学习者画像/示例的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究探索用LLM模拟人类行为与偏好，但LLM能否模拟教育场景中真实学习者对反馈的主观评价尚不明确。

Method: 基于真实学习者对高中生物学问题反馈的评价数据，在群体与个体两个层面评估六个模型；对比是否提供学习者特定信息（如人格特质、评价示例）的模拟表现。

Result: LLM模拟学习者评价的能力仍然有限。提供学习者画像和示例可改善分数校准与个体层面模拟，但往往不能提升群体层面一致性。

Conclusion: 需要进一步研究哪些学习者信息与适应策略对学习者偏好模拟真正有效。

Abstract: While recent studies have explored human behavior and preference simulation using large language models (LLMs), it remains unclear how well LLMs can simulate subjective evaluations from real learners in educational settings. We investigate this question using real learner evaluation data on feedback for high-school biology questions at both the group and individual levels. We compare performance with and without learner-specific information, such as personality traits and evaluation examples, across six models. Our results show that LLMs still have a limited ability to simulate learner evaluations. Providing learner profiles and examples improves score calibration and individual-level simulation, but more often fails to improve group-level consistency. These findings highlight the need to investigate which learner information and adaptation strategies are effective for learner preference simulation.

</details>

### [59] [GT-PSSM: Unified Probabilistic Framework for Stochastic Dynamics Modeling and Dependency Learning in Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34161)
*Wonmo Koo,Jaeyeong Lee,Taeseong Yoon,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出GT-PSSM，将概率状态空间模型与图Transformer结合，统一建模随机性、长程时序依赖与变量间依赖，用于多元时间序列异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTAD方法多基于确定性模型和逐点重构/预测误差打分，但真实多元时间序列因测量噪声和系统固有随机性而具有随机性，大误差可能来自良性波动而非异常。概率状态空间模型（PSSM）虽能通过隐状态转移和发射模型量化不确定性，但现有PSSM类MTAD方法依赖对噪声敏感的循环结构，且缺乏显式的跨变量结构建模，难以捕捉长程时序依赖和变量间依赖。

Method: 提出Graph-Transformer-Enhanced Probabilistic State-Space Model（GT-PSSM）。该方法将PSSM对随机动态的概率建模与Graph Transformer对时序依赖和变量间依赖的学习紧密集成，在统一概率框架内联合建模随机性、长程时序依赖和变量交互。

Result: 摘要中未给出具体实验数据或定量结果，仅声称通过联合建模随机性、长程时序依赖与变量交互，GT-PSSM实现了更鲁棒的异常检测。

Conclusion: GT-PSSM通过融合PSSM的概率建模能力与Graph Transformer的结构化依赖学习，弥补了现有PSSM类MTAD方法在长程时序与跨变量依赖建模上的不足，为随机多元时间序列提供更鲁棒的异常检测框架。

Abstract: Multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTAD) is crucial for ensuring the safe and reliable operation of complex systems. Many existing methods learn normal patterns by training reconstruction or forecasting models on predominantly normal data. However, a large portion of these approaches rely on deterministic models and their associated point-wise output errors for anomaly scoring. Since real-world multivariate time series are inherently stochastic due to measurement noise and intrinsic system randomness, purely error-based scores can be unreliable, as large errors may arise from benign fluctuations rather than true anomalies. Probabilistic approaches address this limitation by quantifying uncertainty in model outputs. In particular, probabilistic state-space models (PSSMs) provide a principled framework by modeling stochastic system dynamics through latent state transitions and measurement noise via emission models. Despite this advantage, existing PSSM-based MTAD methods often struggle to capture long-range temporal dependencies and inter-variable dependencies, as they typically rely on noise-sensitive recurrent architectures and lack explicit cross-variable structure modeling. To address these limitations, we propose Graph-Transformer-Enhanced Probabilistic State-Space Model (GT-PSSM), a novel PSSM-based MTAD method that tightly integrates PSSM-based probabilistic modeling of stochastic dynamics with Graph Transformer-based learning of temporal and inter-variable dependencies. By jointly modeling stochasticity, long-range temporal dependence, and variable interactions within a unified probabilistic framework, GT-PSSM enables more robust anomaly detection.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

### [60] [P2P: Cross-View Population Denoising for Unpaired Single-Cell Perturbation Response Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34391)
*Haojie Yang,Ran Su*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: P2P 是一种面向 AI 虚拟细胞（AIVC）的扰动响应预测方法，以“随机细胞集合”为监督单元，学习可重复的群体转录组扰动效应，并在多个 Perturb-seq 数据集上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Perturb-seq 通过破坏性测序记录细胞对遗传扰动的转录响应，因此对照细胞与扰动细胞无法成对观测；同时，同一条件下的细胞仍存在异质性和噪声。若直接对单个细胞做回归，会把采样变异吸收进效应估计，但生物学解释需要的是可重复的群体效应。

Method: P2P 将同一条件下抽取的两个细胞集合视图作为监督单元：二者共享可重复的群体效应，仅差异来自视图特异变异。模型包含：置换不变的集合编码器用于汇总对照群体；结构化编码器用于表示扰动 token、细胞背景、剂量及组合交互；门控机制融合经验条件效应记忆与神经残差；异方差预测头输出群体均值和逐基因响应方差。

Result: 在统一协议和 5 个随机种子下，P2P 在 Adamson、Norman、Replogle K562、Replogle RPE1 上均相对 GenePert、LinearPert、SLIM、Scouter、scPILOT 取得最低表达 RMSE，以及最高 Effect Pearson、DEG F1 和 DEG 平均精度。在 Replogle K562 上，相比两项指标最强基线 Scouter，Effect Pearson 从 0.643 提升至 0.702，DEG F1 从 0.067 提升至 0.178。

Conclusion: P2P 通过细胞集合级监督有效建模可重复的群体扰动效应，缓解单细胞回归中的采样噪声问题，并在多项扰动响应预测指标上一致优于现有基线。

Abstract: AIVC (AI Virtual Cell) is a learned simulator of cellular behavior across conditions. Predicting how a cell population responds transcriptionally to a genetic perturbation is a core task. Perturb-seq records that response by destructive sequencing, so a control cell and a perturbed cell are never observed as a pair, and cells under one condition remain heterogeneous and noisy. Regression on individual cells absorbs sampling variation into the estimated effect, whereas interpretation requires the reproducible population effect. P2P (Perturbation-to-Perturbation) takes a stochastic cell-set view as its supervision unit. Two views drawn from the same condition share a reproducible population effect and differ by view-specific variation. A permutation-invariant set encoder summarizes the control population, a structured encoder represents perturbation tokens, cellular context, dose, and combination interactions, and a gate blends empirical condition-effect memory with a neural residual. A heteroscedastic head predicts the population mean and gene-wise response variance. Under one protocol and five seeds, P2P attains the lowest expression RMSE and the highest Effect Pearson, DEG F1, and DEG average precision on each of Adamson, Norman, Replogle K562, and Replogle RPE1 relative to GenePert, LinearPert, SLIM, Scouter, and scPILOT. On Replogle K562, Effect Pearson rises from 0.643 to 0.702 and DEG F1 rises from 0.067 to 0.178 relative to Scouter, the strongest baseline on both metrics.

</details>

### [61] [ViBR-WM: Visual Bayesian Regression for World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33844)
*Jifan Li,Ning Ning*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: ViBR-WM 是一种模块化视觉贝叶斯回归世界模型，通过可解释回归融合视觉特征、物理历史与已知协变量，结合视觉压缩与贝叶斯变量选择，递归预测联合视觉-物理状态并直接预测物理目标；在四类预测任务上，其平均总体物理目标误差均低于 Temporal Straightening、ConvLSTM、PredRNN 和 SimVP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型预测的核心挑战在于建模时序依赖与不确定性。现有方法需要在高维视觉表征、物理历史信息、已知协变量以及趋势/季节/周期等多尺度动态之间取得平衡，同时保持可解释性并量化参数不确定性与未来扰动。

Method: 提出 ViBR-WM：采用模块化架构，支持趋势、季节和周期动态；通过视觉压缩降低表征维度，通过贝叶斯变量选择降低活跃回归维度；后验预测以各预测子集的后验概率为权重进行组合，并纳入参数不确定性与未来扰动；模型递归预测联合视觉-物理状态，并直接预测物理目标。

Result: 在涵盖对象运动、植被绿度和太阳能发电的四类预测任务中，ViBR-WM 在所有任务上的平均总体物理目标误差均低于 Temporal Straightening、ConvLSTM、PredRNN 和 SimVP；重复拟合与重采样进一步支持其总体性能增益的稳健性。

Conclusion: ViBR-WM 表明，将可解释回归、视觉压缩、贝叶斯变量选择与后验概率加权集成相结合，可有效提升世界模型在多任务时序预测中的物理目标精度，并显式处理参数不确定性与未来扰动。

Abstract: Modeling temporal dependence and uncertainty is central to forecasting with world models. The Visual Bayesian Regression World Model combines visual features, physical histories and known covariates through interpretable regression, within a modular architecture supporting trend, seasonal and cycle dynamics. Visual compression reduces representation dimension, while Bayesian variable selection reduces active regression dimension. Posterior prediction combines forecasts across predictor subsets using their posterior probabilities as weights and accounts for parameter uncertainty and future disturbances. The model forecasts joint visual--physical states recursively and physical targets directly. Across four forecasting tasks spanning object motion, vegetation greenness and solar power, ViBR-WM achieves lower mean overall physical-target error than Temporal Straightening, ConvLSTM, PredRNN and SimVP on every task. Repeated fitting and resampling support these overall gains.

</details>

### [62] [Overview of the TREC 2025 Million Large Language Models track](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31921)
*Evangelos Kanoulas,Panagiotis Eustratiadis,Jamie Callan,Mark Sanderson,Yongkang Li,Jingfen Qiao,Gabrielle Poerwawinata,Vaishali Pal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向Agentic AI的“专家检索”新范式：不依赖静态元数据或人工描述，而是通过LLM的可观察行为动态推断其专长，并据此为用户查询排序候选专家模型。作者通过TREC Million LLM Track将传统文档检索目标转为专家LLM检索，构建了包含1000多个LLM查询、答案与log-probabilities的大规模基准，用于评估未见查询下的专家模型排序能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI生态中，智能体需要协作完成复杂任务，专家选择直接影响系统整体性能。现有方法通常假设可选模型数量少且文档完善，依赖静态元数据或手写描述来刻画能力；但现实中的模型专长高度多样，面向未来数百万个专用LLM时，这种预设能力声明难以准确、可扩展地反映模型真实专长。

Method: 核心方法是基于检索的专家选择范式：助手智能体通过考察模型的可观察行为来动态推断其专长，而非依赖预定义能力描述。具体实现上，TREC Million LLM Track将检索目标从文档转向专家LLM。参赛者获得由查询、答案及来自1000多个LLM的log-probabilities构成的发现集，需为每个模型学习有意义的专长表示；面对未见测试查询时，系统根据模型预期表现对候选LLM进行排序。

Result: 该工作将专家选择形式化为大规模专家检索任务，并提供了首个面向Agentic AI中专家检索的基准。数据集覆盖超过1000个LLM的行为信号，包括查询、答案和log-probabilities，支持在未见测试查询上进行模型排序评估。

Conclusion: 论文主张以行为驱动、检索式的方式替代静态能力描述，从而实现高效、自适应的专家选择，为未来百万级专用LLM生态中的Agentic AI协作提供可扩展的评估与研究方向。

Abstract: Agentic AI envisions ecosystems of intelligent agents collaboratively solving complex tasks with minimal human intervention. In such ecosystems, each agent possesses specialized expertise, making effective expert selection central to overall system performance. While most current approaches assume a small number of well-documented models, real-world expertise is far more diverse and cannot be adequately captured through static metadata or hand-written descriptions. We anticipate a future with millions of specialized language models (LLMs), each excelling in different domains or problem types. Rather than relying on predefined capability statements, we propose a retrieval-based paradigm in which an assistant agent infers expertise dynamically by examining models' observable behavior. Upon receiving a user query, the assistant ranks candidate LLMs based on demonstrated competence, enabling efficient and adaptive expert selection. The TREC Million LLM Track operationalizes this paradigm by shifting the retrieval target from documents to expert LLMs. Participants are given a discovery set consisting of queries, answers, and log-probabilities from more than one thousand LLMs and are challenged to infer meaningful expertise representations for each model. Given an unseen test query, systems must then rank the LLMs according to their expected performance, providing the first large-scale benchmark for expertise retrieval in agentic AI.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [63] [SAIL: Spatial Audio Intelligence with Large Language Models via Disentangled Acoustic-Spatial Encoding and Dual-Stream Q-Former](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34347)
*Zhengding Luo,Jinyang Wu,Haozhe Ma,Yanghao Zhou,Woon-Seng Gan,Wenwu Wang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出SAIL框架，通过解耦声学-空间特征与源级查询对齐，提升多源空间音频理解与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间音频大语言模型多依赖声学与空间特征的早期融合及源无关的token表示，难以在多源场景中保持单个声音事件与其空间属性的对应关系。

Method: SAIL包含：1）解耦空间音频Transformer，将Mel频谱与双耳相位差特征分别建模为声学流和空间流；2）源判别任务查询，为每个声源学习事件、方向和距离信息；3）双流Q-Former，按源槽组织声学与空间查询，实现与LLM的对齐。

Result: 与早期融合基线相比，SAIL在双源声音事件检测、方向与距离估计以及空间推理任务上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 结构化的、源判别性的音频表示对多源空间理解与推理至关重要。

Abstract: Spatial audio large language models (LLMs) enable embodied agents, wearable assistants, and immersive systems to recognize sound events, localize sources, and reason about their spatial relationships. However, existing spatial audio LLMs often rely on early fusion of acoustic and spatial features and source-agnostic token representations. These designs make it difficult to preserve the correspondence between individual sound events and their spatial attributes, particularly in multi-source scenes. To address this limitation, we propose SAIL, a Spatial Audio Intelligence framework with LLMs that preserves acoustic-spatial structure and source-level correspondence from audio encoding to LLM alignment. SAIL introduces a Disentangled Spatial Audio Transformer that represents Mel-spectrogram and interaural phase difference features as separate acoustic and spatial streams. Source-discriminative task queries further learn event, direction, and distance information for each source. A Dual-Stream Q-Former then aligns the two streams with the LLM using acoustic and spatial queries organized by source slots. Compared with the early-fusion baseline, SAIL achieves consistent improvements in dual-source sound event detection, direction and distance estimation, and spatial reasoning. These results demonstrate the importance of structured, source-discriminative audio representations for multi-source spatial understanding and reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [64] [Timed Rule-Based Supervision of an End-to-End Autonomous Parking Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31773)
*Kejia Gao,Liguo Zhou,Lei Yu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在固定 CARLA 停车场中，用人工规则式参数化安全护盾（PSS）监督端到端泊车策略的控制输出，闭环成功率从 85.16% 提升至 97.66%，但仅限仿真，缺乏形式化安全保证与跨场景泛化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端泊车策略在闭环运行中会反复出现边界越界、制动延迟、停滞或振荡等失效模式，需要在不再重新训练策略的前提下，通过运行时监督器进行纠正。

Method: 继承 Yang 等人的视觉 Transformer 端到端泊车架构，在其控制输出上叠加一个时序、基于规则的参数化安全护盾（PSS）。PSS 使用人工校准的速度、位置和持续时间阈值，对已观察到的失效模式进行干预。闭环评估设置：固定 CARLA 停车场、16 个留出目标车位、6 个初始位姿、4 轮重复，每种配置共 384 次尝试。

Result: 目标成功率从基线策略的 327/384（85.16%）提升至加入 PSS 后的 375/384（97.66%），增加 48 次成功、约 12.50 个百分点；成功尝试的平均位置误差为 0.21 m，平均朝向误差为 0.33°。

Conclusion: PSS 能有效纠正特定重复失效模式并提高闭环泊车成功率。但实验重复使用同一地图、车辆与传感器配置，且仅在仿真中验证，因此不能建立形式化安全保证，也不能证明对其他停车场或真实车辆的泛化能力。

Abstract: We study whether a manually specified runtime supervisor can correct recurring failures of an existing end-to-end parking policy in a fixed CARLA parking lot. The vision-based Transformer architecture is inherited from Yang et al.; our contribution is a timed, rule-based Parametric Safety Shield (PSS) applied to its control outputs. The PSS uses hand-calibrated speed, position, and duration thresholds to intervene in observed failure modes, including boundary exits, delayed braking, and stalled or oscillatory control. In the reported closed-loop evaluation, 16 held-out target slots and six initial poses are each evaluated in four rounds (384 attempts per configuration). Target success increases from 327/384 (85.16%) for the retrained policy to 375/384 (97.66%) with the PSS; mean position and orientation errors among successful attempts are 0.21m and 0.33 degrees. These results show an improvement within this simulator setup. The repeated attempts share one map, vehicle, and sensor configuration, and the PSS uses simulator world coordinates; thus the results do not establish generalization to other lots or real vehicles, or a formal safety guarantee.

</details>


### [65] [GT-VLA: Target-Conditioned Trace Guidance for Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31904)
*Ninghan Zhong,Jing-Chen Peng,Sriram Vishwanath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GT-VLA：通过外部通用VLM生成2D视觉轨迹，并以轨迹条件化动作生成，将VLM常识泛化能力注入VLA控制，从而提升未见任务、配置与长时程场景的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作上表现强，但易过拟合训练场景，难以泛化到未见任务、配置和长时程任务，且不能很好遵循新语言指令；通用VLM泛化强但无法直接控制机器人。作者希望结合VLM常识与VLA控制能力。

Method: 提出GT-VLA可引导框架：由通用VLM为当前技能识别语义引导，将其转换为2D视觉轨迹，并将动作策略条件化于轨迹渲染后的观测。架构分离语义目标获取、轨迹生成与底层动作执行；采用Mixture-of-Experts，为不同技能配备专门的轨迹模块与动作模块。

Result: 在LIBERO基准与真实机器人平台上评估，GT-VLA相较近期VLA基线在两种设置下均取得更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过轨迹条件化动作生成，可有效把外部通用VLM的高层语义引导传播到机器人动作，兼顾泛化与可执行性；代码与补充材料已公开。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong performance on robotic manipulation, but they often struggle to generalize to unseen tasks, configurations, and long-horizon settings. A key challenge is that VLAs overfit to training scenes and fail to follow novel language instructions. Off-the-shelf vision-language models (VLMs) often provide stronger generalization, but cannot directly control robot actions. To combine the common sense of VLMs with VLA control, we propose Guided Trace VLA (GT-VLA), a steerable framework that accepts guidance from an external generalist VLM through trace-conditioned action generation. GT-VLA uses a generalist model to identify semantic guidance for the current skill, converts this guidance into a 2D visual trace, and conditions its action policy on the resulting trace-rendered observation. This design separates semantic target acquisition, trace generation, and low-level action execution, allowing high-level guidance to propagate to robot actions. GT-VLA uses a Mixture-of-Experts architecture with skill-specific trace and action modules for robust execution. We evaluate GT-VLA on LIBERO and a physical robot platform, showing improved generalization over recent VLA baselines in both settings. The code and additional supplemental materials are available on our project website at this https URL .

</details>


### [66] [HapticWorld: an Interactive World Simulator with Real-time Torque Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31924)
*Shaoting Peng,Litian Liang,Yixuan Wang,Ming Yang,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Mark Cutkosky,James Jingxi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HapticWorld：一种可交互的世界模拟器，能预测关节力矩并将其实时反馈给操作者，闭合人类与世界模型之间的触觉回路，用于高效收集带力信息的数据并评估力/力矩条件下的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集型操作依赖力感知，而这种感知难以仅从视觉信号推断，无论是采集示范数据还是训练策略都是如此。然而，带力标注的数据难以大规模获取：真机采集受限于物理硬件；物理仿真器报告的接触力与真实测量存在系统性偏差；而学习型世界模拟器虽可扩展且逼真，却仅限视觉，导致操作者在采集时无触觉反馈，数据也缺乏力/力矩（F/T）标签。

Method: 提出 HapticWorld，一种可交互的世界模拟器，能够在预测观测的同时预测关节力矩，并将其实时渲染反馈给操作者，从而闭合人类与学习型世界模型之间的触觉回路。

Result: 在三个接触密集型任务上，力矩反馈使数据采集吞吐量平均提升 1.6 倍。基于 HapticWorld 生成的示范数据训练的策略在 60 次真机试验中成功 54 次，接近真机数据的 56/60 上界，并远超仅视觉基线的 19/60。此外，HapticWorld 内测得的成功率与真机评估高度吻合。

Conclusion: HapticWorld 可作为独立的、以力/力矩为条件的策略评估平台，在采集效率、策略迁移成功率和评估保真度上均表现优异。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation depends on force sensing that is hard to infer from visual signals alone, both for collecting demonstrations and for training policies. Force-annotated data, however, remains hard to obtain at scale: real-robot collection ties every demonstration to physical hardware, physics simulators report contact forces that deviate systematically from real measurements, and learned world simulators, though scalable and realistic, are vision-only, so operators feel nothing during data collection and the data carries no force/torque (F/T) labels. We present HapticWorld, an interactive world simulator that predicts joint torque together with observations and renders it back to the operator in real time, closing the haptic loop between a human and a learned world model. Across three contact-rich tasks, torque feedback raises data collection throughput by 1.6 times on average. Policies trained on HapticWorld-generated demonstrations succeed in 54/60 real-world trials, approaching the 56/60 upper bound of real-world data, and far exceeding the 19/60 success rate of the vision-only baseline. Moreover, the success rates measured inside HapticWorld closely match real-world evaluation, demonstrating that HapticWorld can serve as a stand-alone F/T-conditioned policy evaluation platform.

</details>


### [67] [Find Something You Can't Do: Agentic Real-World Reinforcement Learning for Self-Improving VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32069)
*Yuan Fang,Zechu Li,Haolei Tong,Puze Liu,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIND 是一个面向真实世界的智能体式强化学习框架，将场景理解、弱点感知练习、自我评估与策略改进在持续工作空间中闭环，使冻结的 VLA 策略能够自主持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型为机器人操作提供了强先验，但通常以冻结策略部署，无法从自身失败中改进。真实世界 RL 虽可带来持续提升，却受限于人工环境重置与任务成功监督，难以实现自主学习。
