Method: FIND 将自主练习重构为"场景条件化、性能感知"的任务选择问题：每次 rollout 后不恢复预设场景，而是利用当前场景决定下一步练什么。一个视觉-语言智能体从预定义任务库中识别可行任务，优先选择近期成功率较低的任务，并通过执行前后的配对观测评估结果。实例化方案采用冻结的 π0.5 VLA 加残差 off-policy RL。

Result: 在八个真实世界操作任务上，独立人工评估成功率从 55% 提升至 71.9%。一次代表性运行在 6 小时交互内完成 456 个自主回合，仅需 30 次场景恢复干预，在线学习过程中无需人工提供奖励标签。消融实验与系统评估进一步考察了关键设计选择、智能体评估准确率与人工干预需求。

Conclusion: FIND 实现了场景理解、弱点感知练习、自我评估与策略改进的闭环，在不依赖人工奖励标签与频繁重置的条件下，使冻结 VLA 策略在真实世界中获得显著且可验证的性能提升；网络资源已公开。

Abstract: Vision--language--action (VLA) models provide strong priors for robotic manipulation but are typically deployed as frozen policies, unable to improve from their own failures. Real-world reinforcement learning (RL) offers a path to continued improvement, yet manual environment resets and task-success supervision hinder autonomous learning. We introduce \textbf{FIND}, an agentic real-world RL framework that closes the loop between scene understanding, weakness-aware practice, self-evaluation, and policy improvement in a persistent workspace. FIND reframes autonomous practice as a scene-conditioned, performance-aware task-selection problem: instead of restoring a predefined scene after each rollout, it uses the resulting scene to determine what to practice next. A vision--language agent identifies feasible tasks from a predefined library, prioritizes those with lower recent success rates, and evaluates outcomes using paired pre- and post-execution observations. We instantiate FIND with a frozen $\pi_{0.5}$ VLA and residual off-policy RL. Across eight real-world manipulation tasks, the independent human-assessed success rate improves from $55\%$ to $71.9\%$. A representative run completes 456 autonomous episodes within 6 hours of interaction, requiring 30 scene-recovery interventions and no human-provided reward labels during online learning. Ablations and systematic evaluations further examine key design choices, agent evaluation accuracy, and human intervention requirements. Our website is made publicly available at: this http URL .

</details>


### [68] [RecastVLA: From Past Interaction to Future Control with Adaptive Policy States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32155)
*Wenbo Li,Jun Yang,Yiteng Chen,Wei Zhang,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RecastVLA 将动作生成过程本身作为持久策略状态，在流匹配 VLA 策略中通过共享快速权重实现动作侧测试时训练，在 LIBERO、RoboTwin、RoboDojo 及 12 个真实机器人任务上提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序操作要求机器人跟踪已经发生、但当前场景无法直接显示的历史信息。现有显式历史表征策略虽能把过去交互作为当前决策上下文，但尚不清楚动作生成本身能否构成用于后续控制的持久状态。

Method: 基于动作侧测试时训练，在流匹配 VLA 策略中维护自适应策略状态；该状态由共享快速权重表示，并在整次动作生成过程中保持固定。深度特定接口读取同一状态，跨深度与流评估的特征共同定义下一次策略调用的更新；后续动作损失通过反传穿越早期状态转移来训练初始化、接口与更新规则。部署时无需专家动作标签，仅使用策略自身的动作生成特征进行更新。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin、RoboDojo 与 12 个真实机器人任务上，RecastVLA 相较未使用测试时训练的匹配策略提升平均成功率，其中 RoboTwin Clean-to-Clean 提升 10.68 个百分点。RoboTwin 受控对比显示，保留状态可提升成功率，共享设计比独立训练的分层局部 TTT 高 2.58 个百分点。

Conclusion: 动作生成过程可作为持久策略状态，基于共享快速权重的动作侧测试时训练为顺序操作提供了有效且可部署的历史信息利用机制。

Abstract: Sequential manipulation requires a robot to track what has already happened, even when the current scene no longer reveals it. Policies with explicit history representations make past interactions available as context for current decisions. We ask how action generation itself can form a persistent state for subsequent control. Building on action-side test-time training, RecastVLA maintains an adaptive policy state within a flow-matching vision-language-action policy. The state is represented by shared fast weights and remains fixed throughout action generation. Depth-specific interfaces read the same state, while features across depths and flow evaluations jointly define one update for the next policy call. Subsequent action losses train the initialization, interfaces, and update rule by differentiating through earlier state transitions. At deployment, updates use the policy's own action-generation features without expert action labels. Across LIBERO, RoboTwin, RoboDojo, and twelve real-robot tasks, RecastVLA improves mean success over a matched policy trained without test-time training, including 10.68 percentage points on RoboTwin Clean-to-Clean. In controlled RoboTwin comparisons, retaining state improves success, and the shared design exceeds independently trained layer-local TTT by 2.58 points.

</details>


### [69] [RoboFFT: Finetuning generative robot policy via online reinforcement learning with forward process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32236)
*Yu Li,Shenghe Hu,Yuhan Wang,Yaoxiang Pu,Haotong Zhang,Yuanpei Chen,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboFFT：一种基于前向加噪与加权score/flow matching损失的强化学习微调框架，用PPO式代理比率更新生成式机器人策略，在仿真与真实任务中提升性能、稳定性与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式模型（扩散/流模型）能建模复杂、多模态的机器人动作分布，但仅靠模仿学习易受不完美示教与分布偏移影响，进一步提升通常依赖额外专家数据；强化学习可通过环境交互改进，但生成式策略的似然估计不可解，导致微调困难。

Method: 提出RoboFFT前向过程强化学习框架：对采样动作施加前向加噪，利用加权score/flow matching损失构造代理策略比率，并用于PPO式更新，从而在无需显式似然估计的情况下微调生成式机器人策略。

Result: 在代表性仿真基准上结合主流生成式机器人策略评估，包括长时程规划与稀疏奖励场景；实验与分析表明RoboFFT持续提升性能，并具有更好的稳定性与训练效率。进一步集成到真实世界RL框架中，在真实任务上验证有效。

Conclusion: RoboFFT为生成式机器人策略提供了一种可行的前向过程强化学习微调方案，将生成式模仿学习与强化学习有效衔接，并在仿真与真实场景中验证其性能、稳定性与效率优势。

Abstract: Generative models, such as diffusion and flow-based models, have shown strong promise for robot policy learning by capturing complex and multimodal action distributions from demonstrations. However, policies trained solely with imitation learning often suffer from imperfect demonstrations and distributional shifts, while further improvement typically requires additional expert data. Reinforcement learning offers a natural solution through environment interaction, but effectively finetuning generative robot policies remains challenging due to the intractability of likelihood estimation. In this work, we propose RoboFFT, a forward-process reinforcement learning framework for finetuning generative robot policies, which applies forward noising to sampled actions and uses the weighted score / flow matching loss to construct a surrogate policy ratio for PPO-style updates. We evaluate RoboFFT with popular generative robot policies on representative simulation benchmarks, including long-horizon planning and sparse reward settings. Extensive experiments and analysis demonstrate that RoboFFT consistently improves performance while achieving better stability and training efficiency. We further integrate RoboFFT into a real world RL framework and demonstrate its effectiveness in real world tasks. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [70] [Federated Subspace Guided Vision-Language-Action Policy Distillation for Non-IID Multi-Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32239)
*Biprodip Pal,Kaushik Roy,Yanming Zhu,Brendan Tidd,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FedDRMan：面向异构机器人操作任务的联邦子空间引导蒸馏框架，通过冻结教师蒸馏、兼容性聚类聚合与谱重平衡，缓解非IID导致表示漂移和更新冲突，在 LIBERO 上平均成功率峰值达 80.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可让多机器人无需集中训练数据即可联合提升操作策略，但任务与环境分布的非IID性会引发表示漂移和机器人策略更新互不兼容，导致朴素参数聚合具有破坏性。

Method: FedDRMan 在每轮通信中：服务器模型作为冻结教师指导本地行为克隆；采用低秩多模态子空间蒸馏与动作分布蒸馏，保留全局有用的表示几何与策略行为；针对异构聚合，按更新兼容性对客户端分组，并为每个簇维护持久模型；服务器对每个兼容聚合结果进行谱重平衡，以缓解较弱但任务相关的机器人策略更新方向被衰减。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上覆盖多种非IID设置、异构程度、客户端参与变化，并结合消融与聚合分析，FedDRMan 显著提升知识迁移，持续优于强联邦基线，峰值平均成功率达 80.7%，比最强评估联邦基线高 11.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: FedDRMan 能有效应对异构机器人操作中的非IID联邦学习问题，在表示保持、策略行为保持和异构更新聚合方面均具优势，是一种有效的联邦机器人操作策略学习方法。

Abstract: Federated learning offers a natural way for multiple robots to jointly improve manipulation policies without requiring centralized access to training demonstrations. However, non-IID task and environment distributions can induce representation drift and mutually incompatible robot-policy updates, making naive parameter aggregation destructive. We present FedDRMan, a federated subspace-guided distillation framework for heterogeneous robot manipulation. At each communication round, the server model provides a frozen teacher for local behavior cloning, while low-rank multimodal subspace and action-distribution distillation preserve globally useful representation geometry and policy behavior. To address heterogeneous aggregation, FedDRMan groups clients by update compatibility and maintains a persistent model for each cluster. The server then spectrally rebalances each compatible aggregate to mitigate attenuation of weaker task-relevant robot-policy update directions. Extensive experiments on LIBERO across diverse non-IID settings, heterogeneity levels, client participation variation, together with ablations and aggregation analyses, show that FedDRMan substantially improves knowledge transfer and consistently outperforms strong federated baselines achieving a peak mean success rate of 80.7%, 11.6 percentage points above the strongest evaluated federated baseline.

</details>


### [71] [DS-VLA: A Dendritic-inspired Vision-Language-Action Model for Robust Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32253)
*Yaxing Lyu,Jingyi Li,Mingkun Xu,Yujie Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 DS-VLA，将树突脉冲动力学引入视觉-语言-动作（VLA）控制：动作神经元配置多稀疏连接树突分支与异质可学习衰减因子，并加入神经元级抑制门控以选择性接纳多模态证据。在 LIBERO 四个套件的名义 rollout 与统一闭环动作扰动协议下，DS-VLA 取得 91.6% 名义成功率和 87.35% 扰动成功率，保留 95.4% 名义性能，显著优于 OpenVLA-OFT、FAST、π0、GR00T。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在语言条件操作任务的名义评估中表现良好，但当执行动作短暂受扰时，闭环鲁棒性不足；作者希望突破仅靠扩大视觉-语言骨干或生成式动作解码器的路径，引入脑启发的架构先验以提升具身智能的鲁棒性。

Method: DS-VLA 是一种树突启发的动作架构，将树突脉冲动力学融入 VLA 控制。其动作神经元具有多个稀疏连接的树突分支，每个分支使用异质、可学习的衰减因子，以实现模块化特征处理与时间信息整合；同时引入神经元级抑制门控，在胞体动力学之前自适应调节新多模态证据进入树突状态的过程，从而抑制不可靠状态更新并保留任务相关历史信息。评估采用 LIBERO 四个套件，在名义 rollout 和统一闭环动作扰动协议下进行，并辅以消融和神经动力学/扰动后轨迹分析。

Result: DS-VLA 在 LIBERO 上达到 91.6% 平均名义成功率与 87.35% 平均扰动成功率，保留 95.4% 的名义性能。相同扰动设置下，OpenVLA-OFT、FAST、π0、GR00T 分别仅为 39.45%、23.90%、28.55%、30.75%。受控消融隔离了神经元级共享抑制的贡献；神经动力学与扰动后轨迹分析表明，鲁棒性能与选择性证据抑制及有效行为恢复相关。

Conclusion: 将脑启发计算机制整合进 VLA 架构，可作为提升具身智能鲁棒性的有效架构先验，而不仅依赖缩放视觉-语言骨干或生成式动作解码器。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance in language-conditioned manipulation, yet success under nominal evaluation does not necessarily translate into robust closed-loop behavior when executed actions are transiently corrupted. We introduce DS-VLA, a dendritic-inspired action architecture that incorporates dendritic spiking dynamics into VLA control to address this limitation. Specifically, to enable modularized feature processing and temporal information integration, DS-VLA equips action neurons with multiple sparsely connected dendritic branches, each featuring heterogeneous, learned decay factors. Furthermore, to suppress unreliable state updates while preserving task-relevant historical information, we introduce a neuron-wise inhibitory gate that adaptively regulates the admission of new multimodal evidence into dendritic states prior to somatic dynamics. We evaluate DS-VLA on all four LIBERO suites under both nominal rollouts and a unified closed-loop action-perturbation protocol. DS-VLA achieves a 91.6\% average nominal success rate and an 87.35\% average perturbed success rate, retaining 95.4\% of its nominal performance. Under the same reported perturbation setting, OpenVLA-OFT, FAST, $\pi_0$, and GR00T achieve 39.45\%, 23.90\%, 28.55\%, and 30.75\%, respectively. A controlled ablation isolates the contribution of neuron-wise shared inhibition, while analyses of neural dynamics and post-perturbation trajectories associate robust performance with selective evidence suppression and effective behavioral recovery. Together, these results demonstrate that integrating brain-inspired computational mechanisms offers a promising architectural prior for robust embodied intelligence beyond merely scaling vision-language backbones or generative action decoders.

</details>


### [72] [WSM-Aware HRI: An IoT-Enhanced Framework for Early Detection and Norm-Guided Repair of Failures with LLM Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32336)
*Hanlin Zhang,Yuquan Wang,Tianwei Zhang,Zhenglong Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 WSM-Aware HRI：一个 IoT 增强的模块化框架，将人机交互失败统一建模为“世界状态不匹配”（WSM），借助 LLM 显式化人类指令中的隐含假设，并与机器人多模态感知和数字增强构建的 grounded world model 进行验证，从而在意图形成阶段主动检测即将发生的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互失败是机器人真实环境部署的主要障碍；既有工作多将失败视为孤立技术故障，或聚焦执行后的恢复行为，而许多崩溃实际源于人机双方对当前世界状态假设不一致。

Method: 提出 WSM-Aware HRI，一种 IoT 增强的模块化框架：将多样化 HRI 崩溃统一表示为人类指令隐含假设与机器人 grounded world model 之间的 World-State Mismatches（WSM）；机器人世界模型由多模态感知与数字增强构建。利用 LLM 将隐含假设显式化，映射到有限的不匹配类型，并指定用于对照机器人世界状态进行验证所需的证据。该方法将失败处理从执行时恢复前移至意图形成阶段的主动不匹配检测，并支持由安全、规范合规和多用户协调引导的干预及透明解释。

Result: 在十个涵盖视觉不匹配与潜在状态不匹配的日常案例上评估不匹配识别。系统能够准确产生预期输出结果；消融实验表明，可靠识别依赖于合适的 grounding 表示和面向验证的 refinement。

Conclusion: 将交互崩溃视为显式的世界状态不匹配，可实现更早检测即将发生的失败，并为将外部证据和社会约束整合进人机交互提供原则性机制。

Abstract: Human-robot interaction (HRI) failures remain a major barrier to deploying robots in real-world environments. Prior work often treats failures as isolated technical faults or focuses on post-hoc recovery behaviors. In practice, many breakdowns arise because humans and robots operate under inconsistent assumptions about the current world state. We propose WSM-Aware HRI, an IoT-enhanced modular framework that unifies diverse HRI breakdowns as World-State Mismatches (WSMs) between a human's instruction-implied assumptions and a robot's grounded world model built from multimodal perception and digital augmentation. A Large Language Model (LLM) is used to make implicit assumptions explicit, map them to a small set of mismatch types, and specify the evidence needed for verification against the robot's world state. WSM-Aware HRI shifts failure handling from execution-time recovery to proactive mismatch detection during intention formation, enabling interventions guided by safety, norm compliance, and multi-user coordination with transparent explanations. We evaluate mismatch identification in ten everyday cases spanning both visual and latent-state mismatches. The system can accurately produce the expected output results, and ablations show that reliable identification depends on appropriate grounding representations and verification-oriented refinement. These results indicate that treating interaction breakdowns as explicit world-state mismatches enables earlier detection of impending failures and offers a principled mechanism for integrating external evidence and social constraints into human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [73] [Think Fast, Plan Selectively: Adaptive Deliberation for Efficient Data-Driven MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32591)
*Yi Xian Goh,Sze Jue Yang,Hao Luan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Fast-TD-MPC，一种受人类双过程认知理论启发的轻量框架，自适应地在快速策略执行与测试时规划之间路由，仅在最需要的状态进行昂贵推理，在 103 个连续控制任务上保持竞争力并实现最多约 4 倍推理加速，外部扰动下选择性回退到规划以保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动 MPC 将学习到的世界模型与在线轨迹优化结合，在连续控制中性能优异，但每步需采样与评估数百条候选轨迹，单步代价高，限制了控制频率，远低于实时机器人需求。作者受人类认知双过程理论（System 1 快速直觉、System 2 缓慢审慎）启发，质疑是否每个决策都需要同等程度的计算审慎，从而提出按需分配计算资源的问题。

Method: 提出 Fast-TD-MPC 框架，采用自适应路由机制：在快速策略执行（System 1 式）与测试时规划（System 2 式）之间动态切换，把昂贵的审慎推理保留给最需要的状态；通过这种选择性规划降低平均推理开销，同时在困难或受扰状态下回退到完整规划以维持鲁棒性。

Result: 在 103 个连续控制任务上取得与原方法相当的任务性能，推理速度最高提升约 4 倍；在存在外部扰动时，Fast-TD-MPC 能选择性地回退到规划，鲁棒性与原始规划器相当。

Conclusion: 并非所有决策都需要同等计算审慎度，通过双过程启发的快慢自适应路由，可以在几乎不损失性能与鲁棒性的前提下显著降低 MPC 的推理开销，为实时机器人控制中的高效测试时规划提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Data-driven model predictive control (MPC) combines learned world models with online trajectory optimization, achieving strong performance in continuous control. However, the per-step cost of sampling and evaluating hundreds of candidate trajectories restricts deployment to control frequencies well below what real-time robotics demands. Motivated by the dual-process theory of human cognition, which distinguishes between fast, intuitive processing (System 1) and slower, deliberative reasoning (System 2), we ask whether every decision requires the same degree of computational deliberation. We propose Fast-TD-MPC, a lightweight framework that adaptively routes between fast policy execution and test-time planning, reserving costly deliberation for states where it is most needed. Fast-TD-MPC delivers competitive task performance across 103 continuous control tasks while achieving up to ~4x faster inference. Under external disturbances, Fast-TD-MPC selectively falls back to planning, maintaining robustness comparable to the original planner.

</details>


### [74] [World SLAM Model: Joint World Modeling for SLAM and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32626)
*Minghui Qin,Yijun Yuan,Weicheng Zheng,Kenan Li,Weibang Wang,Chang Sun,Junhao Huang,Anmin Liu,Yicheng Yao,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WSM将SLAM核心机制（增量状态更新、持久记忆、后端优化）直接嵌入导航任务，端到端联合训练导航与SLAM目标，在保持几何估计精度的同时提升长程导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法仅将SLAM视为提供位姿、地图或token的上游模块，未将其状态维护与误差优化机制用于下游导航本身，导致长程交互中世界状态难以保持一致。

Method: 提出World SLAM Model（WSM）统一框架：给定当前观测与导航目标，预测未来视觉状态并联合估计相机运动与稠密几何；以增量状态更新和持久记忆维护持续演化的空间世界状态，并通过后端优化累积误差；该状态作为动作生成与闭环导航的基础。模型以导航—SLAM联合目标进行端到端训练。

Result: 实验表明，WSM在提升导航性能的同时保持了较强的SLAM定位与建图精度。

Conclusion: SLAM可作为长程世界建模与具身交互的内在机制，而非仅上游模块；将SLAM范式直接引入下游导航具有显著潜力。

Abstract: We introduce World SLAM Model (WSM), a unified framework that brings the SLAM paradigm directly into downstream navigation. Rather than treating SLAM merely as an upstream module that provides poses, maps or tokens, WSM adopts its core mechanisms, including incremental state updates with persistent memory and backend refinement of accumulated errors, to maintain a consistent world state during interaction. Given the current observation and a navigation goal, WSM predicts future visual states and jointly estimates their camera motion and dense geometry, grounding visual prediction in an evolving spatial world state. This spatial state is continuously updated as new observations arrive and provides the basis for action generation and closed-loop navigation. WSM is trained end-to-end with a joint navigation--SLAM objective, enabling downstream navigation to benefit directly from SLAM-style state maintenance and refinement while preserving accurate geometric estimation. Experiments demonstrate improved navigation performance together with strong SLAM accuracy, highlighting the potential of SLAM as an intrinsic mechanism for long-horizon world modeling and embodied interaction.

</details>


### [75] [PF-RL: Progress Field Reinforcement Learning via Goal-Conditioned Value Geometry for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32634)
*Yunpeng Qing,Yilun Kong,Sixu Lin,Ming Zhou,Yiming Fei,Shuang Luo,Yixiao Chi,Haoming Gu,Jingyuan Liu,Changxu Wei,Zhi Hou,Changqing Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Progress Field Reinforcement Learning (PF-RL)，通过在预训练 VLA 特征上学习结构化的目标条件进度表示，将几何距离转化为稠密信用反馈，实现细粒度的转移级信用分配，提升长时程操作策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化微调（RFT）在改进视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略方面具有潜力，但稀疏的任务级结果难以为中间转移提供有效信用，在长时程操作中尤为突出。现有进度感知方法虽架构各异，却普遍将任务进度建模为显式的标量预测，缺乏刻画中间观测与任务目标关系的结构，从而可能阻碍有效的转移级信用分配。

Method: PF-RL 在预训练 VLA 特征上学习结构化的目标条件进度表示，并将其转化为用于策略优化的稠密信用。具体而言，一个轻量级的共享 Progress Field 头将当前表示与目标表示映射到紧凑的进度空间，其中几何距离诱导出目标条件价值；同时辅以时间目标与目标结构目标来塑造所学几何结构。转移级价值变化自然产生稠密的进度优势，从而支持离线策略改进与在线强化微调中的细粒度信用分配。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin2.0 及真实世界双臂操作任务上的大量实验表明，PF-RL 相较强监督微调、强化微调以及进度感知基线，均能稳定提升策略性能。

Conclusion: 将进度建模为结构化的目标条件进度场（而非显式标量预测），可有效提供稠密的转移级信用，PF-RL 因此成为改进长时程 VLA 策略的有效范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning~(RFT) has emerged as a promising paradigm for improving Vision-Language-Action~(VLA) policies, yet sparse task-level outcomes provide limited credit for intermediate transitions, especially in long-horizon manipulation. A natural approach is to model intermediate task progress and use it as dense feedback for policy improvement. Despite their architectural differences, existing progress-aware methods commonly formulate task progress as an explicit scalar prediction, providing limited structure for modeling how intermediate observations relate to the task goal, which may hinder effective transition-level credit assignment. We introduce Progress Field Reinforcement Learning (PF-RL), which learns a structured goal-conditioned progress representation over pretrained VLA features and converts it into dense credit for policy optimization. A lightweight shared Progress Field head maps current and goal representations into a compact progress space, where geometric distance induces goal-conditioned value, while complementary temporal and goal-structure objectives shape the learned geometry. Transition-level value changes naturally yield dense progress advantages, enabling fine-grained credit assignment for both offline policy improvement and online reinforcement fine-tuning. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin2.0, and real-world bimanual manipulation tasks show that PF-RL consistently improves policy performance over strong supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement fine-tuning, and progress-aware baselines.

</details>


### [76] [SEES: A Self-Evolving Embodied System via Failure-Guided VLA Policy Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32698)
*Ziwen Li,Hanlue Zhang,Zhenyang Ren,Tianyu Huang,Runqi Lin,Haoyu Wang,Zhengqing Gao,Yandong Guo,Fakhri Karray,Tongliang Liu,Chris Russell,Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SEES（自进化具身系统）通过从失败中学习、自动定位瓶颈技能并在仿真中构建定制强化学习任务，无需专家额外演示即可持续提升 VLA 策略的长时程任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在短时程任务上泛化性良好，但在长时程任务上并不可靠。部分原因在于大规模训练数据偏向演示成本更低的单阶段操作任务，导致单个薄弱的原子技能可能在多个多阶段任务中引发连锁失败。现有改进方法通常依赖专家识别瓶颈并提供额外演示，成本高昂且在部署后往往难以实施。

Method: SEES 将以长时程任务分解为原子任务，并路由至对应的“技能族”策略，每个技能族由共享同一 VLA 适配器的相关原子技能组成。执行过程中系统自动监测原子任务结果，定位失败频率最高的原子技能作为当前瓶颈。为突破瓶颈，SEES 在仿真中通过恢复到此前经历过的状态，并借助 LLM 生成任务特定的成功判据，从而构造定制化的强化学习任务；随后通过在线强化学习更新共享的技能族适配器，促进相关原子技能之间的正向迁移与多轮进化中的累积提升。

Result: 大量实验表明，SEES 可与不同的 VLA 骨干模型集成，并逐步提升其长时程任务性能；同时观察到在未见任务上仍能持续改进，证明其泛化能力超出进化训练设定。

Conclusion: SEES 提供了一种无需专家演示、可从失败中自我进化的具身系统方案，通过瓶颈定位、仿真 RL 任务构造与技能族适配器在线更新，实现长时程任务性能的持续提升与跨任务迁移。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action (VLA) policies demonstrate promising generalization across diverse short-horizon tasks. However, they remain unreliable on long-horizon tasks, partly because the large-scale training data is biased toward single-stage manipulation tasks that are cheaper to demonstrate. A single weak atomic skill can cause failures across multiple multi-stage tasks. To address such failures, existing methods often require experts to identify the bottleneck and provide additional demonstrations, making the improvement costly and potentially impractical after deployment. To this end, we present a Self-Evolving Embodied System (SEES) that learns from failures and improves the VLA policy without additional expert demonstrations. SEES decomposes long-horizon tasks into atomic tasks and routes them to corresponding family policies. Each family consists of related atomic skills that share one VLA adapter. During execution, the system automatically monitors atomic-task outcomes to identify the most frequently failing atomic skills as the current bottlenecks. To overcome these bottlenecks, SEES constructs tailored RL tasks in simulation by restoring previously encountered states and generating task-specific success criteria with an LLM. Online RL updates the shared family adapters to promote positive transfer among related atomic skills and cumulative improvement across evolution rounds. Extensive experiments show that SEES can be integrated with different VLA backbones to progressively improve their long-horizon performance. We also observe continued improvement on unseen tasks, providing evidence of transfer beyond the evolution settings.

</details>


### [77] [An Empirical Study on What Matters for Viewpoint-Generalizable Policies in Visual Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32762)
*Mino Nakura,Sriram Krishna,Yufei Wang,Shubham Tulsiani,Zackory Erickson,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作通过受控实证研究发现：在视觉模仿学习中，保留密集视觉 token 并让动作头参与几何推理，可显著提升视觉运动策略的视角泛化能力；该策略在覆盖广泛相机位姿的仿真任务中保持性能，并能以随机相机配置实现从仿真到真实世界的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模仿学习是训练机器人操作策略的重要方向，但现有策略对视角扰动十分脆弱，难以部署到相机位姿多变的真实环境。因此，作者希望系统性地厘清哪些设计选择能够促进视觉运动策略的跨视角泛化。

Method: 作者开展受控实证研究，在覆盖广泛相机位姿的仿真任务套件上，对不同视觉运动策略设计选择进行比较。重点考察两类因素：是否保留密集视觉 token，以及动作头是否参与几何推理。

Result: 实验表明，当保留密集视觉 token 且动作头参与几何推理时，策略的视角泛化能力提升；在一系列相机位姿变化较大的仿真任务中，采用这些设计选择的策略仍保持良好性能。进一步地，该策略在随机相机配置下可从仿真零样本迁移到真实世界。

Conclusion: 保留密集视觉表征并使动作头参与几何推理，是提升视觉运动策略跨视角泛化的重要设计选择。该结论为视觉模仿学习策略在多样视角环境中的部署及零样本 sim-to-real 迁移提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Visual imitation learning is a promising approach to training robot manipulation policies capable of completing a wide variety of tasks. However, policies today remain brittle to viewpoint perturbations, making deployment in diverse environments a challenge. We present a controlled empirical study of which design choices allow visuomotor policies to generalize across viewpoints. We find that viewpoint generalization improves when dense visual tokens are retained and the action head participates in geometric reasoning. On a suite of simulated tasks that span a wide range of camera poses, we show that these design choices yield a policy that remains performant across viewpoints. As a practical consequence, a policy trained with these design choices also transfers zero-shot from simulation to the real world under random camera configurations.

</details>


### [78] [CLAP: Closed-Loop Alignment with Pressure for Precise Suction Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32767)
*Yixian Zou,Chongyang Xu,Yuling Xin,Ziliang Feng,Fanman Meng,Shuaicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLAP通过压力模块感知吸盘真空状态，将其替代吸力命令融入VLA策略本体感知并与视觉特征融合，同时终止开环执行以重新推理，在真实Unitree Z1上实现96.67%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 堆叠与码垛需要精确放置，一层残留的误差会被下一层继承，而平整垫面缺乏引导错误位姿归位的特征。自上而下吸力适合密集排列，且吸力已被引入视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略。但已有工作未报告基于实测真空信号条件的策略，也未利用该信号中止正在进行的动作。视觉在此无法解决问题，因为关键时刻杯体与其所持表面相互遮挡。

Method: 提出CLAP：接入真空管路的压力模块使吸附状态可观测。解码后的读数替代策略本体感知中的吸力命令，与视觉特征融合，并终止开环执行窗口，使策略基于新观测重新推理。数据方面，在物理机器人上记录目标状态拆解，离线反转关节状态序列，无需仿真回放；定向阶段演示仅占训练帧的8.3%，覆盖吸力转换及中断抓取留下的构型。

Result: 在真实Unitree Z1上，单一多任务检查点在两种颜色设置下均达96.67%平均成功率，分别高出最强基线16.67和10.00个百分点；单色四块堆叠成功率平均误差为15.92 mm。四个消融设置分别落后5.00至13.33个百分点。

Conclusion: CLAP通过可观测的真空压力信号增强了吸力VLA策略的鲁棒性与精度，显著优于基线并验证了各组件贡献，作者将开源代码与训练权重。

Abstract: Stacking and palletising demand precise placement: error left in one layer is inherited by the next, and a flat pad offers no feature to funnel a wrong pose into the right one. Top-down suction suits such dense arrangements, and suction has already been brought into vision-language-action (VLA) policies. What that work does not report, however, is a policy conditioned on a measured vacuum signal, or one that uses it to abandon an action already under way. Vision does not settle the question here, because at the moment it matters the cup and the face it holds occlude each other. We present CLAP, which makes the attachment state observable through a pressure module tapped into the vacuum line. The decoded reading replaces the suction command in the policy's proprioception, is fused with the visual features, and terminates the open-loop execution window so that the policy re-infers from a fresh observation. For data, we record goal-state disassembly on the physical robot and reverse the joint-state sequence offline, without a simulation replay. Targeted phase demonstrations, 8.3% of the training frames, cover the suction transitions and the configurations an interrupted grasp leaves behind. On a real Unitree Z1, one multi-task checkpoint reaches 96.67% average success in both colour settings, 16.67 and 10.00 points above the strongest baseline, its monochromatic four-block successes averaging 15.92 mm of error. Four ablation settings fall 5.00 to 13.33 points short. We will release code and trained weights.

</details>


### [79] [Copper-Policy: Focus on the Representation for Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32779)
*Zexin Feng,Yixu Feng,Lingyu Xiao,Shang Su,Kexin Zheng,Chang Xu,Mengkai Shi,Shuo Feng,Xintao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Copper-Policy 提出与策略联合学习紧凑世界表示：通过时序联合嵌入预测未来观测嵌入，而非重建像素，从而在无需具身预训练的情况下取得强控制性能，并显著提升训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 World Action Models（WAMs）通常依赖像素空间或潜空间预测未来场景演化，以获取行为先验，但预测细节成本高昂。近期证据表明，协同训练收益在测试时无需生成也能保留，因此核心问题变为：WAM 究竟需要学习什么才能改善控制？论文希望避免预定义目标空间和高成本生成，直接学习对控制有用的紧凑世界表示。

Method: 提出 Copper-Policy：不再依赖预定义目标空间，而是让紧凑世界表示与策略联合学习。方法采用时序联合嵌入预测，在任务意图条件下预测未来观测嵌入，不进行像素重建；该预测目标与动作解码共同塑造表示，同时策略仍可访问当前帧空间细节以支持执行。由于预测目标紧凑，每样本训练 token 减少，2B 参数模型可在 8×RTX 5090 GPU 上 9.67 小时完成训练，在匹配 A100 GPU 上比 Fast-WAM 快 6 倍。表示分析显示，所学特征能更好区分任务驱动变化与扰动，并为控制提供互补信息。

Result: Copper-Policy 在 RoboTwin 上优于所有未具身预训练的对比方法；在 LIBERO-Plus 上达到 80.85%，超过若干具身预训练的 VLA 模型；在三个具有挑战性的真实机器人任务上，性能与 π0.5 相当，且平均分更高。同时训练效率显著提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，WAM 不必依赖像素重建或预定义目标空间；通过紧凑世界表示与策略的联合学习及未来观测嵌入预测，即可提升控制性能。Copper-Policy 兼顾强控制表现与高效训练，为 WAM 设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) acquire behavioral priors by modeling future scene evolution, but predicting detailed futures in pixel or latent space incurs substantial cost. Recent evidence that co-training gains persist without test-time generation raises a question: what must a WAM learn to improve control? We introduce Copper-Policy, which learns a compact World representation with the policy rather than relying on a predefined target space. Through temporal joint-embedding prediction, it predicts future observation embeddings conditioned on task intention without reconstructing pixels. This prediction and action decoding shape the representation jointly, while the policy retains access to current-frame spatial detail for execution. Representation analyses show that the learned features better separate task-driven change from perturbations and provide complementary information for control. Compact prediction targets reduce training tokens per sample, enabling a 2B-parameter model trained in 9.67 hours on 8$\times$ RTX 5090 GPUs and 6$\times$ faster than Fast-WAM on matched A100 GPUs. Copper-Policy outperforms every compared method without embodied pretraining on RoboTwin and several embodied-pretrained VLAs on LIBERO-Plus (80.85%). On three challenging real-robot tasks, it performs comparably to $\pi_{0.5}$ and attains a higher average score. Together, these results show that Copper-Policy combines strong control performance with efficient training.

</details>


### [80] [Scanning While Imagining: A Scene-Graph World Model for Robotic Ultrasound Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32837)
*Xuesong Li,Shuai Chen,Feng Li,Zhongliang Jiang,Nassir Navab,Yuan Bi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SonoGraph-WM：一种动作与目标条件下的世界模型，以场景图（SG）表示解剖结构并联合预测未来SG与探头位姿，实现前瞻性超声探头导航；利用CT标签图生成对齐的SG–位姿训练数据，在CT病例与机器人-体模实验中验证了高预测精度与导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声采集高度依赖操作者解读解剖结构并预判探头运动所致视野变化的能力；然而现有多数机器人超声导航方法在选择动作时并未显式预测这些解剖变化。同时，带追踪与解剖标注的真实超声序列数据稀缺，限制了监督学习。

Method: SonoGraph-WM将解剖表示为场景图，编码可见结构、其几何与空间关系，而非合成超声图像。以SG历史与探头位姿为输入，用统一Transformer联合预测未来SG与位姿。采用滚动时域规划器：递归想象候选轨迹，选择抵达目标图的最短预测路径，并在短执行时域后基于新观测重规划。为减少对追踪标注超声的依赖，从CT标签图沿表面约束的探头轨迹生成对齐的SG–位姿训练数据。

Result: 在四个留出CT病例上，空间关系F1在20步预测内保持93%以上；使用标注派生SG的闭环导航在胆囊与胰腺上成功率分别为77.50%与75.00%。在机器人-体模实验中，使用标签图派生SG的规划器在73.7%的试验中抵达目标视图。

Conclusion: 结果支持以CT监督的解剖世界建模用于探头规划，并强调频繁观测更新对可靠导航的重要性。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) acquisition depends on the operator's ability to interpret anatomy and anticipate how the view will change with probe motion. Many robotic US navigation methods select actions without explicitly predicting these anatomical changes. We propose SonoGraph-WM, an action- and goal-conditioned world model for anticipatory probe navigation. The model represents anatomy as scene graphs (SGs), capturing visible structures, their geometry, and spatial relationships without synthesizing US images. Given a history of SGs and probe poses, a unified Transformer jointly predicts future SGs and poses. A receding-horizon planner recursively imagines candidate trajectories, selects the shortest predicted path reaching a goal graph, and follows it over a short execution horizon before replanning from new observations. To reduce reliance on tracked and anatomically annotated US sequences, we generate aligned SG--pose training data from computed tomography (CT) label maps along surface-constrained probe trajectories. On four held-out CT cases, spatial relation F1 remains above 93% over 20 prediction steps, and closed-loop navigation achieves 77.50% and 75.00% success for the gallbladder and pancreas, respectively, using annotation-derived SGs. In robot--phantom navigation experiments with label-map-derived SGs, the planner reached the target view in 73.7% of trials. These findings support CT-supervised anatomical world modeling for probe planning and highlight the importance of frequent observation updates for reliable navigation. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [81] [FINE: Future-Informed Navigation Encoding for Data-Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32855)
*Khang H. Nguyen,Hoang Pham Quang Nguyen,Ha Phuong Nguyen,Khanh Dinh Binh,Xuan Ha Nguyen,Vien Ngo,Duy Ho Nguyen Minh,Huan Nguyen,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FINE 框架，从已有 VLN 示范轨迹的未来观测中挖掘隐式监督，通过显式地标 token 与隐式未来 token 两类辅助表示增强导航策略，在示范数据受限时显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将视觉语言导航（VLN）策略适配到新环境成本高昂，因为每条新增路线与指令都需具身示范。而标准“观测到动作”的训练仅利用了轨迹中很小一部分信息：未来观测其实已揭示智能体将遇到、且与指令相关的地标，包括其外观与三维空间排布。

Method: FINE（Future-Informed Navigation Encoding）为 VLN 主干网络配备两种互补的辅助表示：1）显式地标 token，按指令指定的有序地标序列，在语义 2D patch 特征空间与视角相关的 3D 几何特征空间中预测未来地标区域；2）隐式未来 token，通过视频世界模型生成同一场景下的合理反事实未来，学习区分真实到达的地标状态与反事实未来状态。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE 的 val-unseen 上，完整训练数据下 FINE 将 InternVLA-N1 的成功率分别提升 2.6 与 4.5 个百分点；在示范数据受限时收益更大：70% 示范预算下成功率提升 6.8 个百分点，约恢复因减少训练示范而损失性能的三分之一。

Conclusion: FINE 能有效从现有示范轨迹中提取未来信息作为辅助监督，提升 VLN 策略性能，且在低示范预算场景下具有更强的数据效率优势。

Abstract: Adapting vision-language navigation (VLN) policies to new environments is expensive because every additional route and instruction requires an embodied demonstration. Yet standard observation-to-action training uses only a small fraction of the information already contained in each trajectory. In particular, future observations reveal the instruction-relevant landmarks that the agent will encounter, including what they look like and how they are arranged in 3D. We introduce FINE, a Future-Informed Navigation Encoding framework that extracts this latent supervision from existing demonstrations. FINE equips a VLN backbone with two complementary auxiliary representations. First, explicit landmark tokens follow the ordered landmarks specified by the instruction and are trained to predict future landmark regions in both semantic 2D patch-feature space and viewpoint-dependent 3D geometric feature space. Second, an implicit future token learns to distinguish the landmark state that is actually reached from plausible same-scene counterfactual futures generated by a video world model. On R2R-CE and RxR-CE val-unseen, FINE improves InternVLA-N1 by 2.6 and 4.5 success-rate points, respectively, at full training data. More importantly, as demonstrations become limited, the benefit grows: at a 70% demonstration budget, FINE improves success rate by 6.8 points, recovering roughly one-third of the performance lost by reducing the training demonstrations. Project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [82] [RoboFoundry: System-as-Policy Evolution for Self-Learning Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32862)
*Jingsong Liang,Shuhao Liao,Shizhe Zhang,Diyuan Hou,Yuxin Cai,Xinjian Deng,Chengyang He,Wenhui Huang,Runjia Tan,Zhidong Wang,Lan Yu,Xuesong Tian,Guillaume Sartoretti,Jie Luo,Yao Mu,Wenjun Wu,Wanhua Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboFoundry，将具身智能体的支撑系统统一建模为策略，并通过自我演化实现系统级持续改进；在 EmbodiedBench、RoboMemArena、LIBERO-PRO 及真实机器人部署中均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常孤立地优化智能体栈中的单个组件（如记忆、上下文、技能或动作接口），没有把支撑系统本身视为统一策略；同时，仅靠交互并不能带来自我改进，除非将执行经验转化为持久且经过验证的系统级变更。

Method: 提出 Self-Evolving System-as-Policy 框架 RoboFoundry：诊断决策与记忆管理中的能力缺口，将执行轨迹转化为经过验证的任务特定系统更新，并进一步提升为通用系统改进。演化作用于两个互补层面：上下文系统（管理主动内部上下文与持久文件系统记忆）和分层技能系统（组织原子技能、可复用组合与失败条件恢复）。共享语义接口分离具身无关决策与具身特定执行，使演化后的系统能力可跨异构机器人迁移。

Result: 在 EmbodiedBench 上达到 SOTA，使 GPT-5.5 提升 27.8%；使 Qwen3.7-Plus 接近 GPT-5.5（70.3% vs. 72.7%）。在长时程记忆任务 RoboMemArena 上，至少超过所有基线 39.0%，即使对照由外部基础模型辅助的方法。在 LIBERO-PRO 上，在所有扰动类型中超过 Cap-Agent0 达 243.8%–679.7%。真实部署中展示了跨机器人和任务的零样本迁移与在线演化。

Conclusion: RoboFoundry 验证了“系统即策略”的自我演化范式可跨基础模型带来稳定增益，并显著增强长时程记忆、扰动鲁棒性与真实环境迁移能力，展现出实现全自主具身智能体的潜力。

Abstract: A foundation model should not act in isolation as an embodied agent. Yet, existing methods often optimize individual components of the agent stack, such as memory, context, skills, or action interfaces, rather than treating the supporting system itself as a unified policy. Moreover, interaction alone does not yield self-improvement unless execution experience is converted into persistent, validated system changes. We therefore propose RoboFoundry, the first embodied agentic framework that formulates this process as Self-Evolving System-as-Policy. RoboFoundry diagnoses capability gaps in decision-making and memory management, converts execution traces into validated task-specific system updates, and promotes recurring improvements to the general system. Evolution operates over two complementary surfaces: a context system that manages active internal context and persistent file-system memory, and a hierarchical skill system that organizes atomic skills, reusable compositions, and failure-conditioned recovery. A shared semantic interface separates embodiment-invariant decisions from embodiment-specific execution, allowing evolved system capabilities to transfer across heterogeneous robots. On EmbodiedBench, RoboFoundry achieves state-of-the-art performance, notably improving GPT-5.5 by 27.8%. It also brings Qwen3.7-Plus to near parity with GPT-5.5 (70.3% vs. 72.7%), showing consistent gains from system-as-policy evolution across foundation models. For long-horizon memory, RoboFoundry outperforms all baselines on RoboMemArena by at least 39.0%, even against methods assisted by external foundation models. On LIBERO-PRO, it further outperforms Cap-Agent0 by 243.8%-679.7% across all perturbation types. In real-world deployments, RoboFoundry demonstrates zero-shot transfer and online evolution across robots and tasks, highlighting its potential for fully autonomous embodied agents.

</details>


### [83] [TriDrive: Joint Driver, Vehicle, and Road Modeling for Forecasting and Driver Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33000)
*Yuhang Wang,Jingxin Yang,Chuheng Wei,Yuechen Guo,Jinghan Xu,Zhao Han,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TriDrive是首个统一联合预测驾驶员运动学、车辆动力学与道路需求的框架，通过自动化条件转移模型与多模态编码器、有向残差连接实现实时车载评估，在AIDE、BATON及comma four部署实验中均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将舱内活动建模与交通条件下的驾驶员运动建模割裂处理，缺乏对驾驶员、车辆、道路三者交互与协同演化的联合建模，也缺少实时车载评估。

Method: 提出TriDrive统一框架，采用自动化条件转移模型联合预测驾驶员运动学、车辆动力学和道路需求。模态特定编码器包括：驾驶员锚定运动学表示、因果CAN总线动力学、冻结V-JEPA 2道路潜变量及结构化道路边界。通过有向残差连接，使驾驶员与道路上下文细化车辆预测。为实时部署，蒸馏道路编码器，在comma four与外部8 GB GPU上运行；轻量当前状态警告探针以5 Hz更新，p95延迟177 ms，同时联合模型并发预测。

Result: 在公开AIDE基准上，运动学编码方案取得已发表基线中的全量SOTA，All-MPJPE为48.05对71.47。在197.2小时自然驾驶BATON子集上，有向连接与道路边界将辅助介入PR-AUC提升：转向起始+0.084，碰撞时间下降+0.286。实时探针在人工标注手动驾驶警告上AUROC为0.725，优于openpilot基线0.563；14名驾驶员的配对道路研究中，其警告在合适性(+1.79)和及时性(+2.67)上评价优于openpilot驾驶员监控系统。

Conclusion: TriDrive验证了驾驶员-车辆-道路联合建模的可行性与实时车载部署能力，在公开基准、自然驾驶预警和真实道路人因评价中均优于现有基线。

Abstract: Predicting how drivers, vehicles, and road scenes interact and evolve together is central to driver monitoring. Prior work models in-cabin activity or traffic-conditioned driver motion in isolation, motivating joint driver, vehicle, and road modeling with real-time on-vehicle evaluation. We introduce TriDrive, to our knowledge the first unified framework that jointly forecasts driver kinematics, vehicle dynamics, and road demands through an automation-conditioned transition model. Modality-specific encoders (an anchored kinematic representation of the driver, causal CAN-bus dynamics, and frozen V-JEPA 2 road latents with structured road margins) are connected by directed residual connections through which driver and road context refine vehicle forecasts. We evaluate TriDrive on three downstream tasks. On the public AIDE benchmark, its kinematic encoder recipe sets a new full-set state of the art (SOTA) among published baselines (48.05 versus 71.47 All-MPJPE). On 197.2 hours of naturalistic BATON subset, directed connections and road margins raise assistance-engaged PR-AUC by 0.084 for steering onset and 0.286 for time-to-collision drops. For real-time use, we distill the road encoders and run TriDrive on a comma four with an external 8 GB GPU, where a lightweight current-state warning probe updates at 5 Hz with 177 ms p95 latency while the joint model forecasts concurrently. The probe is above an openpilot-based baseline on human-labeled manual-driving warnings (AUROC 0.725 versus 0.563), and in a paired on-road study 14 drivers rate its warnings as more appropriate (+1.79) and timely (+2.67) than those of openpilot's driver-monitoring system.

</details>


### [84] [SwingRL: Adaptive Observation Reinforcement Learning with World-Model Prediction for Cable-Suspended Hoisting Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33053)
*Guangming Wang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Yucheng Xin,Wanli Ma,Jiucai Liu,Yunxiang Ma,Joe Ingham,Haibing Wu,Yixiong Jing,Olaf Wysocki,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SwingRL，一种残差强化学习框架，用于缆索悬挂吊装中的精准插入任务，通过融合时序对齐世界模型、经典抗摆先验与循环残差策略，应对反馈延迟/丢失与不确定动力学，仿真中大幅超越基线，并在真实平台上实现90%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆索悬挂吊装广泛用于搬运重物或大件（如起重机辅助施工），但基于柔性缆索的机器人吊装存在三大挑战：负载运动欠驱动、外部扰动多变、视觉观测延迟或丢失导致感知状态在执行时已过时。在将悬挂负载的插孔端精确套入钢筋销钉这一常见施工任务中，这些问题尤为关键，因此需要同时解决"过时反馈"与"不确定动力学"两个耦合问题。

Method: 提出SwingRL，一种残差强化学习（RL）框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）age-aware世界模型——利用已执行的命令将最近收到的负载观测传播到当前控制步，在延迟且有损的感知下提供时序对齐的状态估计；（2）经典抗摆先验——提供名义跟踪与摆动阻尼；（3）循环残差策略——学习对先验的有界修正（而非完整控制律），以补偿系统参数变化、外部扰动与残余状态估计误差。

Result: 在涵盖系统参数变化、风扰、感知退化与强阵风的分级难度阶梯上对比经典与学习基线：在最困难设置下，SwingRL取得69.5%严格成功率与77.3%宽松成功率，分别领先所有基线至少60个百分点。世界模型消融实验支持时序对齐状态估计在观测丢失增加时维持插入成功的作用。此外，无需真实机器人微调，SwingRL在物理平台上达到90%成功率。

Conclusion: 通过将时序对齐的世界模型、经典抗摆先验与残差策略相结合，SwingRL有效解决了延迟/有损感知与不确定动力学下的悬挂负载精准插入问题，在仿真与真实物理平台均表现优异，且具备良好的从仿真到现实的迁移能力。

Abstract: Cable-suspended hoisting is widely used to move heavy or bulky payloads that cannot be handled conveniently by rigid pick-and-place systems, for example in crane-assisted construction. Robotic hoisting using flexible cables is challenging because payload motion is underactuated, external disturbances vary, and delayed or lost visual observations can make the perceived payload state stale at control execution. These effects are particularly critical during precise insertion of a suspended payload's sockets onto rebar pins, which is a very common task in construction environments. We present SwingRL, a residual reinforcement-learning (RL) framework that combines an age-aware world model, a classical anti-swing prior, and a recurrent residual policy to address two coupled problems: stale feedback and uncertain dynamics. The world model propagates the newest received payload observation to the current control step using the executed commands, providing a time-aligned state estimate under delayed and lossy sensing. The prior supplies nominal tracking and swing damping. The residual policy learns bounded corrections to the prior rather than the complete control law, compensating for system-parameter variation, external disturbances, and remaining state-estimation errors. We evaluate SwingRL against classical and learning-based baselines across a cumulative difficulty ladder covering system-parameter variation, wind disturbance, degraded sensing, and strong gusts. Under the most difficult setting, SwingRL achieves 69.5% strict and 77.3% broad success, exceeding all baselines by at least 60 percentage points, respectively. World-model ablations support the role of time-aligned state estimation in maintaining insertion success as observation loss increases. Finally, without real-robot fine-tuning, SwingRL achieves 90% success on the physical rig.

</details>


### [85] [VPTwin: Real-Sim-Real Video Prediction for Robotic Manipulation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33104)
*Zhenghao Xiao,Minting Pan,Nantian He,Dongzhan Zhou,Yunbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VPTwin 提出 Real-Sim-Real 视频预测框架，以真实演示构建数字孪生，并通过 Isaac Sim 在随机物理配置与候选动作下生成多组前向动力学推演，作为上下文参考来融合仿真物理合理性与真实视频中未建模接触交互，从而提升长时程动作条件视频预测与操作规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的动作条件视频预测在机器人世界模型中常出现误差累积与物理不合理幻觉，长时程 rollout 会严重损害下游动作规划，因此需要引入物理仿真先验来约束真实未来预测。

Method: 提出 VPTwin，一个 Real-Sim-Real 视频预测框架。针对目标操作任务，先由 VLM 从真实演示片段重建可执行数字孪生；针对未观测物理属性估计的病态性，在 Isaac Sim 中对候选动作轨迹和随机化物理配置进行多组前向动力学 rollout。再以这些 rollout 作为上下文参考，在真实视频预测中融合两个域：用仿真动力学保证物理合理性，同时从真实视频中捕捉未建模的接触交互。进一步构建基于 VPTwin 的预测规划闭环，用于视觉验证 VLM 提出的动作并指导可靠的真实世界执行。

Result: 评估显示，VPTwin 在视频预测中显著减少了物理幻觉，并在操作规划性能上取得明显提升。

Conclusion: 通过真实-仿真-真实的孪生锚定与仿真 rollout 上下文参考，VPTwin 有效缓解了纯数据驱动视频预测的复合误差和物理不合理问题，并能与 VLM 动作提议结合形成可验证、可执行的机器人操作规划闭环。

Abstract: While action-conditioned video prediction provides an intuitive world model for robotics, purely data-driven predictors often suffer from compounding errors and physically implausible hallucinations in long-horizon rollouts, severely undermining downstream action planning. We propose VPTwin, a Real-Sim-Real video prediction framework that anchors real-world future prediction using real-synchronized simulation twins. For a target manipulation task, a VLM reconstructs an executable digital twin from a real demonstration episode. To accommodate the ill-posed estimation of unobserved physical properties, Isaac Sim simulates multiple forward dynamic rollouts across randomized physical configurations under candidate action trajectories. Using these rollouts as in-context references, VPTwin harmonizes both domains, using simulation dynamics to enforce physical plausibility while capturing unmodeled contact interactions from real video. Furthermore, we establish a predictive planning loop using VPTwin to visually verify VLM-proposed actions and guide reliable real-world execution. Evaluations show substantial reductions in physical hallucinations during video prediction and marked improvements in manipulation planning performance.

</details>


### [86] [TimelyDAgger: Timing-Aware Expert Querying for VLA Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33157)
*Zhixuan Zhao,Peiyan Li,Enhao Zhang,Yueran Tao,Hao Wang,Chenghao Yue,Lei Lv,Wentao Zhao,Jiahao Chen,Xin Liu,Kangyao Huang,Yu Luo,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TimelyDAgger，通过监控 VLA 内部特征并结合专家反馈自适应调整接管阈值，以优化机器人门控 DAgger 中的专家接管时机，从而提升策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人门控 DAgger 主要关注“是否需要帮助”的检测，但专家接管的时机同样会决定示范数据的质量及其对策略学习的价值；因此需要把接管时机纳入门控与评估框架。

Method: 提出 TimelyDAgger：使用 Bridge-PCA 监控视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的内部特征，并基于专家行为进行反馈引导的阈值自适应，以改进专家接管时机；同时构建评估框架，将故障检测、接管时机与策略改进联系起来，并提出 Target-Aligned Supervision Ratio（TASR），用于在不重新训练的情况下评估监督质量。

Result: 实验表明接管时机确实影响策略学习；在匹配专家动作预算下，TimelyDAgger 在多数评估设置中实现了有竞争力的故障检测性能，并取得更高的训练后成功率。

Conclusion: 机器人门控 DAgger 中的接管时机是影响示范价值与策略学习的关键因素；通过内部特征监控与专家反馈驱动的阈值自适应，TimelyDAgger 能在控制专家成本的同时提升最终策略表现。

Abstract: DAgger improves robot policies by aggregating expert supervision from states visited during policy execution. Robot-gated DAgger automates expert queries, allowing the robot to decide when to request expert takeover. While existing gates emphasize detecting the need for assistance, takeover timing also shapes the content of these demonstrations and their value for policy learning. We propose TimelyDAgger, combining Bridge-PCA monitoring of internal vision-language-action (VLA) features with Feedback-guided Threshold Adaptation based on expert behavior to improve takeover timing. We introduce an evaluation framework linking failure detection, takeover timing, and policy improvement, including Target-Aligned Supervision Ratio (TASR) for assessing supervision quality without retraining. Experiments show that takeover timing affects policy learning, with TimelyDAgger achieving competitive failure detection and higher post-training success in most evaluated settings under matched expert-action budgets.

</details>


### [87] [Dynamic Manipulation with World-Action Models via Counterfactual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33172)
*Sunwoo Park,Wonbin Lee,Seonghyun Jin,Youngmin Kim,Jangho Park,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动态预测规划（DPP）框架，通过反事实规划解决世界-动作模型（WAM）在动态目标操作中的目标响应坍缩问题，无需动态数据额外训练即可在单张消费级GPU上实现实时动态操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于静态演示训练的WAM即使具备所需操作技能，也常无法操作移动目标。作者将其归因于“目标响应坍缩”：随着执行推进，策略越来越偏向延续已学习的行为，对目标位置变化的响应能力下降。核心矛盾在于模型所学内容与基于当前上下文可生成内容之间的差距。

Method: 将动态操作建模为反事实规划，解耦“用于计划生成的上下文”与“用于执行的物理状态”。DPP首先利用WAM的预测性滚动估计交互发生时刻，并结合观测到的目标运动预测目标未来交互位置；随后构造反事实观测，将预测目标位置置于机器人熟悉的上下文中，从而调用已有操作技能，而非在不熟悉的机器人-目标构型下生成恢复行为；最终在执行时将计划连接到机器人实际状态。方法无需在动态数据上额外训练，可在单张消费级GPU上实时运行。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人上，针对多种目标运动均取得一致提升；仿真性能超过所有评估基线，包括额外使用动态数据训练的方法。

Conclusion: DPP通过反事实规划与上下文-执行解耦，有效缓解目标响应坍缩，使WAM无需动态数据训练即可实现实时动态目标操作，验证了将熟悉技能迁移至动态场景的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: World-Action models (WAMs) trained on static demonstrations often fail to manipulate moving targets even when they possess the required manipulation skills. We attribute this failure to target-response collapse: as execution advances, the policy becomes increasingly biased toward the learned continuation of its ongoing behavior and less responsive to target relocation. To bridge the gap between what the model has learned and what it can generate from the current context, we formulate dynamic manipulation as counterfactual planning by decoupling the context used for plan generation from the physical state used for execution. Our framework, Dynamic Predictive Planning (DPP), first uses the WAM's predictive rollout to estimate when an interaction is expected to occur, and combines this timing estimate with observed target motion to predict the target's future interaction position. DPP then constructs a counterfactual observation that places this predicted target position in a familiar robot context, allowing the model to invoke an existing manipulation skill rather than generate a recovery behavior from an unfamiliar robot-target configuration. The resulting plan is connected to the robot's actual state during execution. DPP enables real-time dynamic manipulation on a single consumer GPU without additional training on dynamic data. Experiments in simulation and on a real robot demonstrate consistent improvements across diverse target motions, with simulation performance surpassing all evaluated baselines, including methods additionally trained on dynamic data. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [88] [DeltaWAM: Change-Centric Visual Foresight via Delta Tokens for an Efficient World-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33177)
*Tianyun Jiang,Wenrui Bao,Bingxin Xu,Yu Tian,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeltaWAM 提出以“变化增量令牌（delta token）”为核心预测单元的世界-动作模型，避免像素级重建整幅未来场景；基于 DeltaWorld 自回归预测增量令牌，并用流匹配动作专家生成动作块，在 LIBERO 上取得 92.8% 平均成功率，且推理高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有像素空间世界-动作模型为机器人操作提供视觉前瞻时，需要反复重建完整未来场景，计算开销大且存在显著时空冗余。物理操作中相邻帧大部分视觉上下文相同，真正需要动作策略预测的是帧间变化。

Method: DeltaWAM 将连续稠密 DINO 特征图之间的变化编码为单个向量，即紧凑的 delta token，并以其作为未来预测的基本单位。模型基于大规模视频预训练的潜在世界模型 DeltaWorld，自回归地为每个未来帧预测一个 delta token；随后由 flow-matching 动作专家以预测到的状态转移和当前 DINO 特征作为空间锚点，生成动作块。

Result: 模型参数量 0.725B，在两块 H100 上训练 256 GPU 小时；LIBERO 平均成功率达 92.8%，在 LIBERO-Pro 的程序化扰动下仍具稳健泛化能力。推理时每个动作块耗时 142.1 ms，峰值显存 3.86 GB。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，以帧间变化而非完整像素未来为预测中心，可显著降低世界-动作模型的计算与时空冗余，同时在机器人操作任务上保持高成功率与良好泛化，并具备较高推理效率。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) offer visual foresight for robotic manipulation, but pixel-space models repeatedly reconstruct entire future scenes, incurring high computational cost and spatio-temporal redundancy. In physical manipulation, consecutive frames often share most of their visual context; the changes between them are what an action policy needs to anticipate. We introduce DeltaWAM, a change-centric WAM that makes a compact delta token the unit of future prediction. Each token is a single vector encoding changes between consecutive dense DINO feature maps. DeltaWAM builds on DeltaWorld, a latent world model pretrained on large-scale videos, to autoregressively predict one delta token per future frame. A flow-matching action expert then conditions on the predicted transitions and current DINO features, which serve as spatial anchors, to generate action chunks. Trained for 256 GPU hours on two H100 GPUs, DeltaWAM has 0.725B parameters and achieves 92.8% average success on LIBERO. It also shows robust generalization under procedural perturbations on LIBERO-Pro. Inference takes 142.1 ms per action chunk with 3.86 GB peak memory.

</details>


### [89] [TAO-DA: Towards Autonomous Operation--A Dual-Arm Vision-Language-Action Model for Coordinated Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33197)
*Yongsheng Zhao,Han Gao,Baoping Cheng,Jingyao Tang,Dian Zhou,Deng Liang,Ji Ge,Xuanzhang Wen,Lei Zhao,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出对称双臂专家(DAE)架构，在共享VLM骨干上构建解耦的臂专属专家塔，通过两阶段双臂意图路由（显式语言指令+隐式视觉语义）缓解跨臂干扰，并引入任务进度预测模块支持多机器人协同调度，验证了双臂意图路由、跨臂解耦及技能泛化/迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏显式机制区分双臂状态与意图，导致不必要的跨臂干扰，降低任务执行成功率；同时缺少任务进度同步机制，难以支撑双臂乃至多机器人协同调度。

Method: 1) 对称双臂专家(DAE)架构：共享VLM骨干+解耦的臂专属专家塔；2) 两阶段双臂意图路由：第一阶段依据显式语言指令路由，第二阶段依据隐式视觉语义路由；3) 轻量级任务进度预测模块：对pre-chunk时序特征与本体感知、视觉观测的语义表示做交叉注意力，估计逐帧任务完成进度，实现进度同步以支持协同调度。

Result: 实验证明模型在双臂意图路由与跨臂干扰解耦方面有效；初步显示单臂→双臂（及反向）任务的涌现式技能泛化，以及跨臂运动域技能迁移。

Conclusion: 通过解耦双臂专家与两阶段意图路由显式处理双臂状态/意图，并借助任务进度预测实现协同调度，DAE在双臂机器人操作中有效缓解跨臂干扰，并展现技能泛化与迁移潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a unified framework for grounding high-level semantic information into low-level robot actions, enabling scalable robotic manipulation across diverse tasks. However, existing VLA models lack explicit mechanisms to disentangle the states and intents of the two arms, leading to unintended cross-arm interference that degrades task execution success. To address this issue, we propose a symmetric Dual-Arm Expert (DAE) architecture built upon a shared Vision-Language Model (VLM) backbone with decoupled, arm-specific expert towers. Expert selection is carried out through a two-stage dual-arm intent routing scheme, in which experts are routed either by explicit language instructions in the first stage or by implicit visual semantics in the second stage. Moreover, we introduce a lightweight task progress prediction module that leverages cross-attention between the pre-chunk temporal features and semantic representations of proprioceptive and visual observations to accurately estimate frame-wise task completion progress. This module facilitates task progress synchronization to support coordinated scheduling for collaborative multi-robot tasks. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our model in dual-arm intent routing and the disentanglement of cross-arm interference, and further provide preliminary evidence of emergent skill generalization from single- to dual-arm tasks (as well as the reverse), together with cross-arm motion-domain skill transfer.

</details>


### [90] [ActionGround: Training-Free Runtime Refinement of Frozen VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33256)
*Namai Chandra,Madhur Thareja,Shriram Damodaran,Addison Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActionGround 是一种免训练、神经符号的运行时控制层，可包裹冻结的 VLA 策略，在不重训练、不微调、不访问权重的情况下，通过符号相态状态机和惯性加权欧拉-拉格朗日动力学项修正机器人动作，提升抓放任务的成功率、稳定性与轨迹效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Vision-Language-Action（VLA）模型直接将视觉观测和语言指令映射为机器人动作，但未显式建模操作任务的阶段结构，也未显式纳入执行过程中的刚体动力学，导致控制稳定性和轨迹效率受限，且难以在不重训练模型的情况下改善。

Method: 提出 ActionGround：一种神经符号、免训练的运行时层，包裹冻结 VLA 策略，无需重训练、微调或权重访问，每控制步开销小于 1 ms。其包含两部分：1）符号化、相态感知的有限状态机，识别操作阶段（approach、grasp、transport、place），并施加阶段特定的规则修正；2）始终开启的惯性加权欧拉-拉格朗日项，将机器人运动方程纳入控制步，同时将其动力学残差记录为一致性诊断，而非用作门控。评估覆盖 OpenVLA、OpenVLA-OFT、Force-VLA、Generalist-VLA，在 10 个 LIBERO-Spatial 抓放任务上使用 7-DoF Franka Panda，并保持跨任务和主干网络的固定参数；另进行 Robosuite 噪声扫描，以及与真实 Agilex Piper 试验匹配种子的仿真对照。

Result: 在固定参数设置下，ActionGround 将成功率提升最高 6 个百分点，稳定性提升最高 19.3 个百分点，轨迹效率提升最高 15%。在 Robosuite 注入动作噪声的扫描中，轨迹抖动鲁棒性约提升 10 倍；在与真实 Agilex Piper 匹配种子的 Robosuite 仿真对照中，基线成功率从 35% 提升至 95%。物理硬件实验仅为定性部署展示，真实机械臂的逐试验定量成功率留待未来工作。评估范围限于刚体抓放操作。

Conclusion: ActionGround 表明，无需训练即可通过符号相态与动力学感知的运行时修正显著增强冻结 VLA 策略的鲁棒性、稳定性与效率，具备低开销、即插即用特点；但当前验证局限于刚体 pick-and-place 任务，真实硬件定量评估尚不充分。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map visual observations and language instructions directly to robot actions, but they do not explicitly represent the phase structure of manipulation tasks or the rigid-body dynamics governing execution. We present ActionGround, a neuro-symbolic, training-free runtime layer that wraps a frozen VLA policy without retraining, fine-tuning, or weight access, adding less than 1 ms of overhead per control step. A symbolic phase-aware finite-state machine identifies the manipulation phase (approach, grasp, transport, or place) and applies a phase-specific rule-based correction. In parallel, an always-on, inertia-weighted Euler-Lagrange term incorporates the robot's equations of motion into each control step, while its dynamics residual is logged as a consistency diagnostic rather than used as a gate. We evaluate ActionGround across OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, Force-VLA, and Generalist-VLA on ten LIBERO-Spatial pick-and-place tasks using a 7-DoF Franka Panda. With fixed parameters across tasks and backbones, ActionGround improves success rate by up to 6 percentage points and stability by up to 19.3 percentage points, while improving trajectory efficiency by up to 15%. In a separate Robosuite noise sweep, ActionGround provides approximately a 10x improvement in trajectory-jerk robustness under injected action noise. In a matched-seed Robosuite simulation companion to a real Agilex Piper trial, simulated baseline success increases from 35% to 95%. The physical-hardware experiment is presented as a qualitative deployment demonstration; quantitative per-trial success on the real arm is left for future work. Our evaluation is limited to rigid-object pick-and-place manipulation.

</details>


### [91] [AquaWAM: A Dynamics-aware World Action Model for Underwater Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33299)
*Cunhao Zhu,Yifeng Wang,Dongliang Xu,Yunzhong Hou,Yue Yao,Chi Harold Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个面向水下具身智能体的世界行动模型 AquaWAM，通过建模动作条件与被动物理动力学，用紧凑导航状态替代高维视觉预测，在 USIM 基准上取得更高成功率与更快推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界行动模型主要预测动作条件下的视觉观测，未显式建模水下机器人特有的被动动力学（惯性、浮力、水动力阻力、持续漂移等），而这类动力学在动作结束后仍持续影响载体，导致水下决策与预判不准确。

Method: AquaWAM 不预测未来图像，而是联合建模动作条件动力学与被动物理动力学，包括推进器死区、指令结束后仍持续的惯性滑行以及环境水流；感知输入以 DVL、IMU、压力传感器和关节编码器为主，相机仅提供理解目标与目标位姿的语义信息；通过建模紧凑导航状态而非高维视觉观测，显著降低模型规模与计算开销。

Result: 在 USIM 基准的 20 项水下任务中，AquaWAM 任务成功率达 72.6%，优于现有方法；在 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin 上比 U0 做出动作决策快 2.7 倍；在部分机载传感器不可用时仍有效，例如缺少 DVL 速度测量时成功率为 61.6%，而 U0 为 39.4%。

Conclusion: AquaWAM 表明，面向水下具身智能体的世界行动模型应显式纳入被动物理动力学，并以紧凑状态建模替代高维视觉预测，从而在精度、鲁棒性与计算效率之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) are becoming increasingly important and useful for embodied intelligence, as they enable robots to anticipate the consequences of candidate actions before interacting with the physical environment. However, underwater robots are usually subject to passive dynamics, such as inertia, buoyancy, hydrodynamic drag, and persistent drift, which can continue to affect the vehicle even after an action is completed. Existing WAMs, which primarily predict action-conditioned visual observations, are not explicitly designed to capture such passive motion dynamics. In this paper, we present AquaWAM, the first World Action Model designed for underwater embodied agents. Instead of predicting future images, AquaWAM models both action-conditioned and passive physical dynamics, including the thruster dead band, the inertial glide that outlasts each command, and ambient currents. Specifically, it senses through the DVL, IMU, pressure sensor and joint encoders, while cameras supply only semantics for understanding goals and target pose. By modeling compact navigation states rather than high-dimensional visual observations, AquaWAM substantially reduces the model size and computational cost compared with conventional WAMs. Experimentally, AquaWAM achieves a 72.6% task success rate across 20 underwater tasks on the USIM benchmark, outperforming existing methods while making action decisions 2.7x faster than U0 on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. Our model also remains effective when some onboard sensor measurements are unavailable. For example, without DVL velocity measurements, our method still achieves a 61.6% success rate, compared with 39.4% for U0.

</details>


### [92] [Q-WAM: 4-Bit Quantization of World Action Models with Action-Subspace Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33269)
*Arash Akbari,Arman Akbari,Jingwu Luo,Yuhao Lei,Yi Gao,Weiwei Chen,Xuan Zhang,Zhenman Fang,Geng Yuan,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Q-WAM：面向世界动作模型（WAMs）的 4-bit 权重-激活后训练量化方法，通过动作可观测性格拉姆矩阵（AOG）与动作子空间保护（ASP）保护动作生成精度，在仿真与真实机器人上显著降低内存并接近 16-bit 模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAMs 通过迭代扩散联合生成视频与机器人动作，在机器人操作中表现强，但计算与内存开销过大，难以部署。现有 PTQ 方法（如平滑、旋转）不足以维持动作生成精度，因此需要针对 WAMs 动作生成特性的专用量化方案。

Method: 提出 Q-WAM，一种 4-bit 权重-激活量化方法。核心包括：1）动作可观测性格拉姆矩阵（AOG），度量某层输入通道加权组合的舍入误差经全部去噪步骤后对最终动作的影响；2）动作子空间保护（ASP），将少数最动作敏感的通道组合保留在 16-bit 低秩分支中，其余权重和激活量化为 4-bit，并实现为 GPU 上高效的稠密矩阵乘法；3）通过聚合各专家层中 AOG 导出的动作质量，识别对生成动作最重要的专家，仅对这些专家应用 ASP，以最小开销保持动作质量。

Result: 在三个 WAMs 上进行了仿真和真实部署评估。在 RoboTwin 2.0 上达到 89.6–93.0% 平均成功率，与 16-bit 模型差距在 1.1 个百分点以内，同时量化块内存降低 3.1–3.4 倍；比最强基线 SVDQuant 高 2.5–8.7 个百分点。在 Unitree G1 人形机器人和双臂 UR3 机器人上，相比 SVDQuant 成功率提升 12.8–17.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: Q-WAM 通过动作敏感度感知的 4-bit 量化与稀疏专家保护，在保持 WAMs 动作生成质量的同时显著降低内存，并在仿真和真实机器人任务中优于现有 PTQ 基线，验证了面向动作生成进行量化保护的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly generate video and robot actions through iterative diffusion and perform strongly in robotic manipulation. However, their prohibitive compute and memory costs pose substantial deployment challenges. Post-training quantization (PTQ) can reduce these costs, but existing PTQ methods such as smoothing and rotation are insufficient to maintain the precision of action generation. To overcome this limitation, we propose Q-WAM, a new 4-bit weight-activation quantization for WAMs that preserves the actions the model generates. Specifically, we introduce the \textit{Action Observability Gramian (AOG)}, which measures how much rounding errors in each weighted combination of a layer's input channels change the final action through all denoising steps. We also develop Action-Subspace Protection (ASP), which keeps the few most action-sensitive channel combinations in a tiny 16-bit low-rank branch and quantizes the complementary weights and activations to 4 bits, both as dense matrix multiplications that run efficiently on GPUs. Finally, to preserve action quality with minimal overhead, we identify the experts that matter most for the generated action by aggregating the AOG-derived action mass across the layers of each expert and apply ASP only to those experts. We evaluate Q-WAM on three WAMs, both in simulation and in real-world deployment. On the RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark, it reaches 89.6--93.0\% average success rate, within 1.1 percentage points of the 16-bit models, while reducing the memory of the quantized blocks by 3.1--3.4$\times$. Our method outperforms the strongest baseline, SVDQuant, by 2.5--8.7 percentage points. On a Unitree G1 humanoid and a bimanual UR3 robot, it improves success over SVDQuant by 12.8-17.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [93] [Recursive Harness Distillation across Agents for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33378)
*Seungyeon Kim,Junhoo Lee,Minkyu Kim,Baekseung Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出“递归执行框架蒸馏”（Recursive Harness Distillation），将强智能体在VLA策略执行中的干预经验蒸馏为轻量智能体可复用的“操作手册”（playbook），并利用轻量智能体的执行反馈递归精炼该手册，从而在不更新模型参数的情况下提升跨任务、跨环境的机器人操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型虽具备广泛操作能力，但在执行中遇到失败时，往往难以诊断问题并自适应调整行为；而强智能体可通过与环境交互发现有效干预策略。因此，核心动机是：如何把强智能体积累的干预经验沉淀下来，并让不同能力的智能体在无需参数更新的情况下复用。

Method: 方法为Recursive Harness Distillation。强智能体先将自身交互经验蒸馏成面向轻量智能体的playbook；随后根据轻量智能体执行反馈递归完善playbook。该playbook作为可复用指导，使智能体在新任务实例中直接调用积累的干预知识，而非微调VLA模型参数。

Result: 真实世界操作任务中，框架将成功率从37.3%提升至64.0%。在SimplerEnv Bridge上，使用playbook的轻量智能体成功率为66.7%，仅用GR00T的基线为41.7%，且优于无playbook的强智能体；同一playbook也能提升强智能体，使其成功率达到79.2%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，面向机器人学的harness distillation可行：干预经验可以被积累、通过执行过程精炼，并在不同智能体间复用，从而普遍提升操作性能。

Abstract: A central goal in robotics is to enable manipulation across changing tasks and environments. Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide broad manipulation capabilities but can struggle when execution requires diagnosing failures and adapting behavior. Strong agents can discover effective interventions through interaction with these policies. We propose Recursive Harness Distillation to accumulate this experience as reusable guidance across agents. A strong agent distills its experience into a playbook for a light agent, then recursively refines the playbook using the light agent's execution feedback. The resulting playbook enables agents to reuse accumulated intervention knowledge in new task instances without updating model parameters. In real-world manipulation, the harness improves success from 37.3% to 64.0%. On SimplerEnv Bridge, the light agent with the playbook achieves 66.7% success, compared with 41.7% for the GR00T-only baseline, and outperforms the strong agent without a playbook. The same playbook also benefits the strong agent, which reaches 79.2% success. These results demonstrate the feasibility of harness distillation for robotics: intervention experience can be accumulated, refined through execution, and reused across agents to improve manipulation.

</details>


### [94] [VIDEAS: Distilling Explicit Action Semantics from Demonstration Videos for World Models via Prior-Guided Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33464)
*Jianan Wang,Haoquan Zhai,Siyang Zhang,Bin Li,Juan Chen,Jingtao Qi,Zhuo Zhang,Enze Wang,Haoxiang Jin,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VIDEAS 数据蒸馏框架，将操作视频中的连续物理动态转化为显式动作语义，并构建 8B/9B 语言世界模型 VIDEAS-WM，在高层次具身动作语义推理上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型虽能预测未来状态并辅助动作规划，但难以真正内化底层因果物理规律，无法显式推理动作前置条件与后续状态转移。

Method: VIDEAS 将视觉演示解构为离散动作轨迹，利用先进 VLM 提取包含动作前置条件和效果的结构化知识；引入基于文本环境的先验引导轨迹仿真机制验证物理一致性；加入负轨迹以提升知识完整性与数据多样性，缓解认知偏差；基于 AgiBot-World 数据集构建 34K 高质量样本，训练 8B/9B 参数的语言世界模型 VIDEAS-WM。

Result: VIDEAS-WM 在高层次具身动作语义推理任务上取得 SOTA 表现，展现出深刻的物理理解能力，并对未见场景具有稳健泛化性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过数据蒸馏将视频动态转为显式动作语义并配合物理一致性验证，可有效提升基础模型的具身因果物理推理与世界建模能力。

Abstract: World models learn internal representations of environment dynamics to predict future states, enabling agents to optimize action plans without physical interactions. However, developing world models that genuinely internalize underlying causal physical laws to explicitly reason about action preconditions and subsequent state transitions remains an open challenge. In this paper, we propose VIDEAS, a data distillation framework that transforms continuous physical dynamics from operational videos into explicit action semantics for foundation models. Specifically, it deconstructs visual demonstrations into discrete action trajectories and utilizes advanced vision-language models (VLMs) to extract structured knowledge encapsulating action preconditions and effects. To ensure physical consistency, we introduce a prior-guided trajectory simulation mechanism grounded within a text-based environment to rigorously validate the extracted knowledge. Notably, we incorporate negative trajectories to enrich knowledge completeness and enhance data diversity to mitigate cognitive bias. Furthermore, we present VIDEAS-WM, an 8B/9B-parameter suite of language-based world models trained on 34K high-quality samples derived from AgiBot-World dataset. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VIDEAS-WM establishes state-of-the-art performance in high-level embodied action semantic reasoning, exhibiting profound physical understanding and robust generalization across unseen scenarios.

</details>


### [95] [SLIP-VLA: Single-Step Latent Imagination for Policy Learning in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33575)
*Tianfu Li,Haoxuan Xu,Wenbo Chen,Haitian Li,Changchuan Yang,Xinhu Zheng,Jun Ma,Yuan Liu,Lujia Wang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SLIP-VLA 是一个 VLA 策略学习框架，用单步去噪获得时序稠密的未来潜表征，并通过几何/语义对齐、动作条件潜世界模型与逆动力学建模提升感知与控制充分性，在仿真与真实任务上达到 SOTA，单步潜想象仅需 12 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型多直接从当前观测预测动作，缺乏对未来场景演化的显式建模；引入未来预测的方法中，稠密未来建模常依赖昂贵的迭代去噪，而单步替代方案性能又不如多步方法。因此需要兼顾高效未来建模与强动作性能。

Method: 提出 SLIP-VLA：（1）通过单次去噪更新获得时序稠密的未来潜表征（Single-Step Latent Imagination）；（2）将中间隐变量与未来几何特征和语义特征对齐，提升感知充分性；（3）采用动作条件潜世界建模与逆动力学建模，将潜状态转移与机器人动作显式耦合，提升控制充分性。

Result: 在多种仿真基准与真实机器人操作任务上取得 state-of-the-art 性能，单步潜想象耗时仅 12 ms。

Conclusion: SLIP-VLA 以单步潜想象实现高效且时序稠密的未来建模，并通过感知与控制两方面的充分性增强，证明了其在效率与动作性能上兼具优势的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models are increasingly effective for robotic manipulation, yet most predict actions directly from current observations without explicitly modeling future scene evolution. Recent methods introduce future prediction to improve action generation, but dense future modeling often requires expensive iterative denoising, while one-step alternatives can underperform their multi-step counterparts. To reconcile efficient future modeling with strong action performance, we present SLIP-VLA, a policy learning framework that equips VLA models with a Single-Step Latent Imagination for future-aware action prediction. SLIP-VLA obtains temporally dense future latent representations with a single denoising update, and we improve the perceptual sufficiency of these representations by aligning intermediate latents with future geometric and semantic features. We further improve their control sufficiency through action-conditioned latent world modeling and inverse dynamics modeling, explicitly coupling latent transitions with robot actions. SLIP-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse simulation benchmarks and real-world manipulation tasks, while its single-step latent imagination takes only 12 ms.

</details>


### [96] [Achieve What You Imagined: Learning to Align Actions with Visual Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33832)
*Yuheng Qiao,Ziran Wei,Xiaohan Wang,Daqiang Guo,Yichen Luo,Zhibo Pang,Peng Zhou,Sichao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将WAM生成的视觉预测视为目标条件视觉提议而非可直接执行计划，利用冻结的动作条件世界模型预测动作后果，基于两种未来预测的一致性及其与终态目标的契合度构造反馈，并用Flow Policy Optimization优化WAM动作头，从而在无需在线机器人交互和任务奖励模型训练的情况下提升真实UR5操作任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAM）可联合预测未来视觉观测与机器人动作，但其视觉预测与生成动作所隐含的后果之间可能存在不一致；WAM常先给出视觉上合理的任务完成结果，却未必生成能可靠达成该结果的动作序列。因此需要利用这种跨模型预测差异来改进动作策略。

Method: 方法上，将WAM生成的视觉预测作为目标条件视觉提议，而非直接可执行计划；使用冻结的动作条件世界模型预测动作条件下的未来后果；根据两个未来预测之间的一致性以及与终态目标的对齐程度构造反馈；最后采用Flow Policy Optimization（FPO）优化WAM的动作头。该框架避免了在线机器人交互和额外训练任务特定奖励模型。

Result: 在四个真实世界UR5操作任务上，该方法将平均成功率从43.4%提升至75.1%，而对比方法π_{0.5}为61.4%。结果表明，在所评估的操作任务中，跨模型预测差异可为改进机器人策略提供有效反馈。

Conclusion: 跨模型预测差异可作为机器人策略优化的有效反馈信号；通过FPO优化WAM动作头，能够在不依赖在线交互与任务奖励模型的情况下显著提升真实操作任务成功率。

Abstract: World-action models can jointly predict future visual observations and robot actions. However, discrepancies may exist between their visual predictions and the consequences implied by generated actions. We observe that WAMs can often generate visually plausible task-completion outcomes before producing action sequences that reliably achieve them. Consequently, we treat the WAM-generated visual prediction as a goal-conditioned visual proposal rather than a directly executable plan. We use a frozen action-conditioned world model to predict action-conditioned consequences and construct feedback based on consistency between the two future predictions and alignment with the terminal goal. Leveraging this feedback, we employ Flow Policy Optimization (FPO) to optimize the action head of the WAM. This framework avoids online robot interaction and additional training of task-specific reward models. Across four real-world UR5 manipulation tasks, our method increases the mean success rate from 43.4% to 75.1%, compared with 61.4% for $\pi_{0.5}$. These results show that cross-model prediction discrepancy can provide useful feedback for improving robot policies under the evaluated manipulation tasks. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [97] [Beyond One-Step Accuracy: State-Affine Latent Transition for Reliable Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33595)
*Boyuan Zhang,Yingjun Du,Xiantong Zhen,Ling Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出一步预测精度无法反映递归规划中的误差传播，提出状态仿射潜在转移模型SALT，以递归多步展开监督训练，在一步误差更大的情况下显著提升闭环规划成功率并降低执行后模型预测代价骤升导致的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合嵌入世界模型通常以编码状态上的一步预测为目标训练，但规划时会将学习到的转移递归地作用于自身预测。因此，一步预测精度不能刻画预测误差在递归展开中的传播，训练目标与规划使用方式不匹配。

Method: 将多步展开误差分解为各步引入的误差及其后续转移传播；证明状态仿射动力学恰为雅可比与状态无关的可微转移，从而消除非线性传播残差，使误差传播算子仅依赖动作序列。据此提出SALT（State-Affine Latent Transition），动作同时调制状态变换与加性更新；采用递归多步展开监督，将每个预测潜在状态反馈回转移中训练。

Result: 在四个视觉规划环境中，SALT的一步预测误差比匹配的LeWM基线高1.48–2.19倍，但闭环成功率平均提升10.0个百分点；在OGBench-Cube上，执行后模型预测代价骤升的失败回合比例从23.3%降至2.0%。

Conclusion: 递归规划性能更取决于多步误差传播特性而非一步预测精度；通过状态仿射参数化与递归展开监督，可牺牲一步精度换取更优的闭环规划表现和更稳健的执行后模型预测一致性。

Abstract: Joint-embedding world models enable visual planning by learning action-conditioned dynamics in latent space. Yet they are commonly trained for one-step prediction on encoded states, while planning recursively applies the learned transition to its own predictions. One-step accuracy therefore does not capture how prediction errors propagate under recursive rollout. We decompose multi-step rollout error into the errors introduced at individual steps and their propagation through subsequent transitions. We show that state-affine dynamics are precisely the differentiable transitions with state-independent Jacobians, eliminating the nonlinear propagation residual and making the error propagation operators depend only on the action sequence. Guided by this result, we introduce SALT (State-Affine Latent Transition), an action-conditioned state-affine dynamics model in which the action modulates both the state transformation and the additive update. We train SALT through recursive multi-step rollout supervision, feeding each predicted latent state back into the transition so that training matches how the model is used during planning. Across four visual planning environments, SALT exhibits $1.48$--$2.19\times$ higher one-step prediction error than the matched LeWM baseline, yet improves closed-loop success in every environment by $10.0$ percentage points on average. On OGBench-Cube, the fraction of episodes that fail with a sharp rise in model-predicted cost after execution decreases from $23.3%$ to $2.0%$.

</details>


### [98] [AD-E2E-JEPA: A Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture For End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34085)
*Haoran Zhu,Wancong Zhang,Yann LeCun,Anna Choromanska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文系统评测了现有 JEPA 世界模型在端到端自动驾驶中的表现，发现"精度高则算力贵、算力省则规划弱"的权衡，并提出 AD-E2E-JEPA：通过 SIGReg 正则化的可学习投影器将规划 patch 数减少 16 倍、嵌入维度减少 4 倍，实现 100 倍推理加速且保持规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主驾驶需要能够理解物理世界、进行推理与规划并安全运行的世界模型。现有动作条件化的 JEPA 世界模型（LeWM、DINO-WM、JEPA-WM）在端到端自动驾驶（E2EAD）中要么对驾驶任务准确但计算昂贵，要么计算高效却不足以支撑规划，存在明显的效率—性能权衡，亟需一种兼顾两者的方案。

Method: 1) 采用目标条件零样本规划设定，用真实未来观测作为目标、不训练任何驾驶策略，从而将世界模型质量与策略学习解耦；2) 系统评估 LeWM、DINO-WM、JEPA-WM 三类动作条件化 JEPA 世界模型；3) 提出 AD-E2E-JEPA，在投影后的 patch 嵌入上引入 SIGReg 正则化的可学习投影器，降低规划所需的 patch 数量与嵌入维度；4) 验证该自监督预训练投影器对下游模仿学习的增益。

Result: 投影器将规划 patch 数减少 16 倍、嵌入维度减少 4 倍，实现 100 倍推理加速，8 帧 rollout 在 256 条候选轨迹上运行仅需 0.8 秒。在不训练驾驶策略的情况下，世界模型自身对平均 20 米远的目标，位移分别达到 4.0/2.8 米（对应 256/8,192 条候选轨迹词汇表）；在 NAVSIMv2 上目标条件零样本规划达到 67.3/72.9 EPDMS（含乘性安全指标）和 84.1/86.5 EPDMS†（不含安全指标）。自监督预训练投影器将下游模仿学习从 80.2 提升至 85.4 EPDMS。

Conclusion: 现有 JEPA 世界模型在 E2EAD 中存在效率与规划能力的权衡；AD-E2E-JEPA 通过在投影嵌入上施加 SIGReg 正则化的可学习投影器，在大幅压缩计算成本的同时保持规划性能，并能提升下游模仿学习效果，验证了高效且可用于规划的世界模型可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires \textit{world models} that can understand the physical world, reason and plan, and operate safely. In this paper, we first systematically evaluate existing action-conditioned joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) world models, including LeWM, DINO-WM, and JEPA-WM for end-to-end autonomous driving (E2EAD). To isolate world-model quality from policy learning, we employ a goal-conditioned zero-shot planning setting that evaluates these models using ground-truth future observations as goals, without training any driving policy. We find that existing JEPA-based world models are either accurate for driving but computationally expensive, or computationally efficient but insufficient for planning. To address this trade-off, we propose \textbf{AD-E2E-JEPA}, which introduces a SIGReg-regularized learnable projector applied to projected patch embeddings. The projector reduces the number of planning patches by $16\times$ and the embedding dimension by $4\times$, achieving a $100\times$ inference speedup while retaining planning performance, with a 0.8-second runtime for an 8-frame rollout over 256 candidate trajectories. \textit{Without} training any driving policy, the world model itself reaches the goals located 20 meters away on average within the displacement of respectively 4.0/2.8 meters, using world-model rollouts over trajectory vocabularies of respectively 256/8,192 candidates. On the NAVSIMv2 benchmark, it achieves 67.3/72.9 EPDMS with multiplicative safety metrics and 84.1/86.5 EPDMS$^{\dagger}$ without them in goal-conditioned zero-shot planning. Experiments further show that the self-supervised pretrained projector improves downstream imitation learning performance from 80.2 to 85.4 EPDMS. The source code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [99] [InfraVLA: Extending Vision-Language-Action Navigation with Infrastructure Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33647)
*Lukas Vierling,Benjamin Ramtoula,Luke Robinson,Ronald Clark,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InfraVLA 将预训练导航 VLA 适配到静态基础设施摄像头（CCTV）视角，通过两阶段训练显著提升仓库导航成功率，并在真实四足机器人上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多机器人室内环境（仓库、办公室、医院）已安装摄像头，可观测机器人当前视角外的区域，但现有导航策略（包括 VLA 模型）未利用这些信息；关键决策信息往往仅基础设施摄像头可见。

Method: 提出端到端方法 InfraVLA：用 CCTV 编码器将每个外部视图转为输入序列 token，使预训练导航 VLA 利用基础设施视角。由于外部视图仅在稀疏决策点重要，直接微调无效，因此采用两阶段训练：先在带过采样反事实数据的示范上训练，再在恢复数据上训练。

Result: 在两个仿真仓库任务（寻找指令指定物体、绕行堵塞通道）上，分布内成功率为 100% 和 100%，无 CCTV 基线分别为 34.0% 和 73.6%；分布外测试集为 88.2% 和 88.9%。真实四足机器人用少于 10 分钟示范微调后达到 83.3%，仅机载基线为 29.2%。

Conclusion: 利用既有基础设施摄像头可显著增强导航 VLA 策略，尤其在决策信息不在机载视野时；两阶段训练有效促使策略使用外部视角，并具备向真实机器人迁移的能力。

Abstract: Many indoor environments in which robots operate, such as warehouses, offices, and hospitals, already have cameras installed. They observe parts of the building that the robot cannot see from where it stands, yet navigation policies, including recent vision-language-action (VLA) models, do not use them. We propose InfraVLA, an end-to-end method that adapts a pretrained navigation VLA to such static infrastructure views: a closed-circuit television (CCTV) encoder turns each external view into tokens of the input sequence. Because the views matter only at rare decision points, fine-tuning alone did not make the policy use them in our experiments; we therefore train in two stages, on demonstrations with upsampled counterfactual data and then on recovery data. We evaluate on two simulated warehouse tasks, finding an object named in the instruction and rerouting around blocked aisles, where the deciding information is often visible only to the infrastructure cameras. Tested in distribution, InfraVLA reached a success rate of 100% on both, against 34.0% and 73.6% for a baseline without CCTV input. On out-of-distribution test sets it reached 88.2% and 88.9%.
