On a real quadruped fine-tuned with under 10 minutes of demonstrations, the policy reached 83.3% against 29.2% for the on-board-only baseline.

</details>


### [100] [WAM-OPD: Sharpening World Action Models via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34250)
*Panjun Liu,Xiaohan Lei,Shiqi Zhang,Yikun Wang,Yongxin Zhang,Mingyi Hu,Shida Sun,Jiateng Shou,Wengang Zhou,Jiajun Deng,Zhiwei Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 WAM-OPD：将 on-policy distillation 用于预训练 world action models，并用 prefix-weighted trajectory replay 避免蒸馏期间重复环境 rollout，从而在提升目标任务性能的同时保持预训练能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练 world action models 具备跨任务通用操作能力，但要在目标任务上达到专家水平且不破坏预训练技能仍很困难。直接拟合狭小任务数据分布会偏离学生策略诱导分布；而标准 on-policy distillation 在闭环操作中需随策略演化反复 rollout，仿真中昂贵、真实机器人上常不可行。

Method: WAM-OPD 采用 on-policy distillation 思路，将任务特定教师知识在学生自身诱导分布下迁移。为免重复环境交互，提出 prefix-weighted trajectory replay：使用固定轨迹池，主要由初始学生 rollout 组成，并补充任务特定教师 rollout 扩大覆盖。对池中每条轨迹，按存储的连续观测历史条件化当前策略，生成新的去噪路径，并由任务特定教师提供监督。由于历史分布随策略演化发生偏移，PWTR 用每个决策前缀上累计路径分数导出的代理重要性权重，对逐决策蒸馏损失重加权。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，蒸馏期间无需额外环境交互即可实现任务适应；WAM-OPD 提升目标任务性能，同时在未参与适应的任务上保持接近初始模型的性能。

Conclusion: WAM-OPD 表明，通过固定轨迹池 replay 与前缀加权重加权，可在不反复环境 rollout 的情况下将 OPD 有效用于 WAM，实现目标任务的专家级适配并缓解灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Pretrained world action models (WAMs) provide generalist capabilities across diverse robotic manipulation tasks, yet improving target-task performance to an expert level without degrading pretrained skills remains challenging. We explore on-policy distillation (OPD) for WAMs and introduce WAM-OPD. WAM-OPD inherits the advantage of OPD methods that transfer task-specific teacher knowledge under the student's own induced distribution, rather than directly fitting the student to a narrow task-specific data distribution. However, in closed-loop manipulation, the observation histories change as the student policy evolves, requiring fresh environment rollouts to remain on-policy. Applying OPD to WAMs entails repeated data collection, which is costly even in simulation and often impractical on real robots. To avoid repeated environment rollouts during distillation, we introduce prefix-weighted trajectory replay (PWTR). PWTR uses a fixed trajectory pool composed primarily of initial-student rollouts, supplemented with task-specific teacher rollouts to broaden trajectory coverage. For each trajectory replayed from this pool, PWTR conditions the current policy on successive stored histories to generate fresh denoising paths, along which the task-specific teacher provides supervision. Although these denoising paths are refreshed as the policy evolves, the replayed environment trajectories remain fixed. PWTR therefore reweights per-decision distillation losses using proxy importance weights derived from path scores accumulated over the trajectory prefix preceding each decision to mitigate the resulting shift in the history distribution. Simulated and real-world experiments demonstrate task adaptation without additional environment interaction during distillation. In both settings, WAM-OPD improves target-task performance while retaining near-initial performance on tasks excluded from adaptation.

</details>


### [101] [MomWorld: Momentum-Aware Latent World Model for Long-Horizon Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33737)
*Ziying Song,Shengkai Zhang,Lei Yang,Haozhuang Chi,Yuchen Liu,Jiangtao Su,Lin Liu,Ziyang Liu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动量感知潜在世界模型 MomWorld 及其轨迹精炼模块 MoFlow，用于自动驾驶长时域规划，通过传播潜在动量并联合预测未来构型与动量状态，提升长时域规划一致性并降低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域规划需预判场景演化与潜在风险，但现有方法难以将观测历史中的运动趋势有效传播到未来；基于单一潜在状态的长滚动会衰减有用动态、保留陈旧运动模式，并破坏近期可靠规划。

Method: MomWorld 从历史到当前观测中提取场景运动趋势，将潜在动量传播至未来时域，并联合预测未来构型与动量状态。其包含三个关键机制：可学习动量持续机制以保持稳定趋势，场景条件动量更新以适应未来动态，场景自适应重置门以在突变下抑制陈旧动量。进一步提出 MoFlow，一种动量条件流匹配模块，仅用少量积分步将基础轨迹精炼以对齐预测的未来场景演化，并采用时域感知残差融合，在保持近期规划稳定性的同时允许更强的长程修正。

Result: 在 NAVSIM、nuScenes 与 Bench2Drive 上的大量实验表明，MomWorld 提升了长时域规划一致性，并在 6 秒规划时域内相较 MomAD 将平均碰撞率降低 12.2%。

Conclusion: 动量感知的潜在世界模型能够有效传播历史运动趋势并适应未来场景变化，结合动量条件轨迹精炼，可提升自动驾驶长时域规划的一致性与安全性。

Abstract: Long-horizon planning enables autonomous vehicles to anticipate scene evolution and potential risks, supporting safe and stable decisions in complex interactions. However, existing methods struggle to propagate motion trends from observed history into the future. Long rollouts based on a single latent state may further attenuate useful dynamics, retain stale motion patterns, and disrupt reliable near-term plans. We introduce MomWorld, a momentum-aware latent world model for long-horizon planning. MomWorld extracts scene motion trends from historical-to-current observations and propagates latent momentum into future horizons, jointly predicting future configuration and momentum states. A learnable momentum persistence mechanism preserves stable trends, scene-conditioned momentum updates adapt future dynamics, and a scene-adaptive reset gate suppresses stale momentum under abrupt changes. We further propose MoFlow, a momentum-conditioned flow-matching module that refines a base trajectory to align with the predicted future scene evolution in only a few integration steps, with a horizon-aware residual fusion that preserves near-term planning stability while permitting stronger long-range corrections. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM, nuScenes and Bench2Drive demonstrate that MomWorld improves long-horizon planning consistency and reduces the average collision rate by 12.2% relative to MomAD over a 6-second planning horizon.

</details>


### [102] [When World Models Lie: Adaptive Safety Analysis Under Wrong Imaginations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34300)
*John Cao,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应潜空间安全滤波器：利用直接观测到的世界模型误差，通过自适应保形推断（Adaptive Conformal Inference）构建在线不确定集，并在该集合上对学习到的 Hamilton-Jacobi 安全值函数进行悲观最小化，从而在模型准确时保持低保守性、在模型失配时提高谨慎性，并提供有限时间覆盖保证。仿真与硬件实验表明，该方法相较现有潜空间安全滤波器显著减少失败，同时保持任务完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型可为高维机器人系统提供安全推理基础，但其预测可能存在偏差、失准或“自信地错误”。潜空间安全滤波通常在世界模型动力学上学习 Hamilton-Jacobi 安全值函数；若世界模型不准确，值函数会继承其误差并产生过度自信的安全估计。现有方法多依赖集成分歧或值目标一致性残差等辅助信号进行自适应，但这些信号在世界模型预测偏离观测时仍可能很小，难以可靠反映模型失配。

Method: 提出自适应潜空间安全滤波方法：以预测潜状态与由观测推断的潜状态之间的差异为直接误差信号，利用 Adaptive Conformal Inference 在线构建不确定集，并给出自适应不确定半径的有限时间覆盖保证；安全评估时，在所构建不确定集上对已学习的安全值函数取最小值，即进行悲观评价。该机制使滤波器在世界模型准确时保持最小保守性，而在观测揭示模型失配时自动增加保守性。

Result: 通过仿真和硬件实验验证：与当前最先进的潜空间安全滤波器相比，所提方法显著降低失败率，同时保持任务完成能力；并能在模型准确时减少不必要的保守性，在模型失配时提高安全裕度。

Conclusion: 直接利用观测到的世界模型误差来校准潜空间安全推理是有效且必要的。将 Adaptive Conformal Inference 的在线不确定集与 Hamilton-Jacobi 安全值函数的悲观优化结合，可在有限时间覆盖保证下实现自适应安全滤波，提升世界模型不完美条件下的机器人安全性与任务保持能力。

Abstract: World models offer a powerful substrate for safety reasoning in high-dimensional robotic systems, but they are also fallible: their predictions can be biased, miscalibrated, or confidently wrong. This creates a central challenge for latent-space safety filters, which often learn Hamilton-Jacobi safety value functions on the dynamics of a world model. If the world model is incorrect, the resulting value function can inherit its errors and produce overconfident safety estimates. Existing latent safety filters often rely on auxiliary signals such as ensemble disagreement or value-target consistency residuals for adaptation, but these signals can remain small even when the world model's predictions deviate from observations. We propose an adaptive latent safety filter that calibrates safety reasoning using directly observed world-model error. Our method uses Adaptive Conformal Inference to construct online uncertainty sets from discrepancies between predicted and observation-inferred latent states, then evaluates safety pessimistically by minimizing the learned value function over these sets. This allows the filter to remain minimally conservative when the world model is accurate, while becoming more cautious when observations reveal model mismatch. We provide a finite-time coverage guarantee for the adaptive uncertainty radius. Through simulation and hardware experiments, we show that our method significantly reduces failures relative to state-of-the-art latent safety filters while preserving task completion.

</details>


### [103] [AnyStep-WAM: Budget-Aligned Distillation and Adaptive Inference for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33748)
*Rui Wang,Xiangyu Wang,Donglin Yang,Yibo Li,Canyang Chen,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AnyStep World Action Model（AnyStep WAM）：通过预算对齐的教师轨迹蒸馏训练区间条件流映射，并配合轻量风险-收益调度器，实现从单步到多步的可调去噪预算与场景自适应计算分配；在三个 WAM 上平均减少 49.8%–85.28% 去噪步数并保持成功率，同时显著提升单步预算下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAMs）通常依赖固定步数的迭代去噪进行动作生成。然而机器人操作任务中的动作块对生成误差敏感度不一：关键动作需高精度、多步细化，非敏感动作可用更少步数快速生成。固定去噪步数因此造成计算浪费或精度不足，缺乏可调预算与按场景分配计算的能力。

Method: 1）预算对齐的教师轨迹蒸馏（budget-aligned teacher-trajectory distillation）：利用显式冻结教师转移与共享低秩适配器训练区间条件流映射，使同一模型支持从单步预测到多步细化的动作生成。2）轻量风险-收益调度器：基于单步预览预测教师曲率难度与各预算下的师生保真度，选择满足风险自适应保真度要求的最小去噪预算。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 上对 Motus、FastWAM、LingBotVA 三个 WAM 评估：平均去噪步数分别减少 60.2%、49.8%、85.28%，且任务成功率保持基线水平。单步去噪预算下，AnyStep 训练使任务成功率分别提升 7.07%、12.08%、8.94%。六个真实机器人操作任务进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: AnyStep WAM 实现了可调预算预测与场景相关计算分配，在不损失成功率的前提下大幅降低去噪步数，并增强单步生成性能，兼具仿真与真实操作任务的有效性。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) couple predictive visual modeling with action generation, typically relying on iterative denoising with a fixed denoising steps. However, manipulation tasks contain actions chunks with varying sensitivity to generation errors: critical actions require precision, while less sensitive actions allow faster generation with fewer denoising steps. Here we introduce AnyStep World Action Model, a general framework for tunable-budget prediction and scene-dependent computation allocation. Our budget-aligned teacher-trajectory distillation trains interval-conditioned flow maps using explicit frozen-teacher transitions and shared low-rank adapters, supporting action generation from one-step prediction to multi-step refinement. Building on this capability, a lightweight risk-benefit scheduler predicts teacher-curvature-based difficulty and budget-specific student-teacher fidelity from a single one-step preview, selecting the smallest budget predicted to satisfy risk-adaptive fidelity requirements. We evaluate our framework on three widely used WAMs Motus, FastWAM, and LingBotVA using RoboTwin 2.0. Our method reduces average denoising steps by 60.2%, 49.8%, and 85.28%, respectively, while maintaining baseline task success rates. In particular, our AnyStep training substantially improves model performance under a one-step denoising budget, increasing task success rates by 7.07%, 12.08%, and 8.94% on Motus, FastWAM, and LingBotVA, respectively. Experiments on six real-world manipulation tasks further validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [104] [Principal Steering Subspaces for Online Adaptation of Frozen Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33765)
*Jialeng Ni,Nathan Zhao,Kunpeng Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出主导向子空间（PSS）：用有限差分探测解码器响应，构造固定低维控制基，仅用SAC控制主要响应方向，其余正交补按高斯先验重采样，从而在线适配冻结的扩散/流匹配生成式机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器人策略提供强行为先验，但通过在线交互更新大型扩散或流匹配模型代价高昂；潜空间强化学习虽可避免更新预训练生成器，仅控制初始采样噪声，但高维噪声对解码动作的影响具有强各向异性，直接全潜空间控制效率低且不稳定。

Method: PSS作为前向查询接口：对冻结解码器施加有限差分扰动，估计各噪声方向对输出动作的响应，选取响应最强的若干方向构成固定低维控制基；SAC仅在该子空间内学习控制，正交补方向在每次查询时独立地从高斯先验重采样。方法兼容扩散策略与流匹配策略；并进一步集成到冻结的闭源3B参数VLA人形学习系统中，采用同步转移采集、重置时间优化与延迟感知异步部署。

Result: 在RoboMimic三个任务、扩散与流匹配策略上，响应谱呈显著集中性。五组匹配的任务-生成器组合中，PSS通常比全潜空间控制收敛更快、后期训练更稳定，整体最终性能更强。Controlled Diffusion-Square消融表明，同等维度下主导响应方向优于随机子空间和低响应子空间。在探索性螺丝刀放置评估中，冻结VLA策略成功2/10次，SAC+PSS适配后成功6/10次。

Conclusion: 解码器响应几何可作为冻结生成式机器人策略在线适配的实用控制基；PSS以低维、前向查询方式提升样本效率与稳定性，并展示出适配闭源大规模VLA策略的可行性。

Abstract: Generative robot policies provide expressive behavior priors, but updating a large diffusion or flow-matching model through online interaction is costly. Latent-space reinforcement learning avoids updating the pretrained generator by controlling its initial sampling noise, yet high-dimensional noise can have strongly anisotropic effects on decoded actions. We introduce Principal Steering Subspaces (PSS), a forward-query interface that constructs a fixed low-dimensional control basis from finite-difference decoder responses. Soft Actor-Critic controls the leading response directions, while the orthogonal complement is independently resampled from the Gaussian prior at each query. On three RoboMimic tasks with diffusion and flow-matching policies, response spectra reveal substantial concentration. Across five matched task-generator pairs, the training curves indicate that PSS generally converges faster and exhibits more stable late-training behavior than full-latent control, while achieving stronger final performance overall. Controlled Diffusion-Square ablations further show that leading-response directions outperform random and least-responsive subspaces of equal dimension. We further integrate PSS with a frozen, closed-source 3B-parameter vision-language-action (VLA) policy in a humanoid learning system with synchronous transition collection, reset-time optimization, and latency-aware asynchronous deployment. In an exploratory screwdriver-placement evaluation, success is observed in 2/10 trials for the frozen VLA policy and 6/10 after SAC+PSS adaptation. These results support decoder-response geometry as a practical basis for online adaptation of frozen generative robot policies.

</details>


### [105] [ArticulateArena: A Metric for Articulated Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33931)
*Yumeng He,Yongfei She,Huanyu Chen,Chun Yuan,Peihao Li,Joseph Masterjohn,Yin Yang,Ying Jiang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ArticulateArena：一种表示不变的关节运动度量，并发布ArticulateArena-20K数据集，用于更公平地评估铰接物体重建中的运动学预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有铰接物体重建方法可预测几何与关节结构，但几何评估容易，关节/运动学评估困难。因为关节定义的是运动而非形状，且缺乏运动间统一距离；现有协议分别评估关节类型、轴方向、原点、运动范围，忽略这些参数共同描述同一物理运动，同一运动可有不同等价编码，导致等价编码可能得分极差，部分分量误差在准确预测处病态或未定义。

Method: 提出ArticulateArena，作为关节的表示不变Chamfer距离对应物：不比较编码参数，而比较单自由度关节诱导的运动。用李代数端点twist的无序对表示关节，证明商距离是度量；统一固定、旋转、移动和螺旋关节；通过紧化将连续关节纳入同一评分；按质量分布加权后可解释为运动部件以米为单位的RMS运动。进一步提出运动感知树编辑距离，将度量提升到完整运动学树，用同一运动单位惩罚多余/缺失关节等结构错误；在固定内积下，该距离在重标号意义下仍是树上的度量。同时发布ArticulateArena-20K，包含19,977个经运动学验证的铰接物体，并用新度量重新评估已发表的重建方法。

Result: ArticulateArena在理论上被证明为度量，可统一处理多种关节类型并扩展到运动学树；树编辑距离在重标号意义下保持度量性质。发布ArticulateArena-20K，含19,977个铰接物体及验证运动学，并基于该度量对公开重建方法进行重新评估。

Conclusion: 该工作为铰接物体运动学预测提供了表示不变、具有度量性质且物理可解释的评估框架，并配套大规模验证数据集，推动该类任务的标准化与公平比较。

Abstract: Modern methods reconstruct or generate simulation-ready articulated objects, predicting not only their geometry but also how their parts are connected and allowed to move. Evaluating the geometry is straightforward, but evaluating the predicted articulation is not, because articulation specifies a motion rather than a shape, and there is no agreed distance between two motions. More specifically, existing protocols score joint type, axis direction, origin, and motion limits separately, although these parameters jointly describe a single physical motion, and the same motion can be written as different parameter values. As a result, a joint can score maximally wrong against an equivalent encoding of itself, and several component errors are ill-conditioned or undefined exactly where predictions become accurate. We propose ArticulateArena, a representation-invariant counterpart of Chamfer distance for articulation that compares the motions one-DOF joints induce rather than the parameters that encode them. It represents each joint by the unordered pair of its Lie-algebra endpoint twists, and we prove that the resulting quotient distance is a metric. It unifies fixed, revolute, prismatic, and helical joints, brings continuous joints into the same score through a compactification, and reads as the RMS motion of the moving part in meters when weighted by its mass distribution. A motion-aware tree edit distance lifts the metric to full kinematic trees, pricing structural errors such as spurious or missing joints in the same motion units as joint errors, and for a fixed inner product it remains a metric on trees up to relabeling. Alongside the metric we release ArticulateArena-20K, a new suite of 19,977 articulated objects with verified kinematics, and we re-evaluate published reconstruction methods on it under the new metric. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [106] [Efficient World Action Model Inference with Adaptive Intermediate States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34608)
*Zhinnan Liu,Haozhi Han,Ruge Zhang,Teng Ma,Tao Ma,Zheng Liu,Yifeng Chen,Yunquan Zhang,Ting Cao,Yunxin Liu,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 WAMACHINE，通过对推理状态进行保留与自适应，在闭环重规划、去噪步与Transformer层三个层面加速世界动作模型（WAM）推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 通过联合建模动作与环境动力学实现未来感知控制，但迭代扩散/流推理带来大量去噪延迟。虽可利用前次推理状态加速，然而规划上下文、观测及中间表示的变化会使其迅速失效，因此需对推理状态进行"自适应"而非简单复用。

Method: WAMACHINE 为免训练框架，包含三项机制：(1) Trajectory Remapping——在闭环重规划间映射前一重规划状态以初始化下一次重规划，减少冗余轨迹生成；(2) Observation Rebinding——在动作执行期间进行预判推理，待一致性校验通过后，将保留的去噪状态重绑到真实观测以继续推理，降低控制环暴露的延迟；(3) Residual Rescaling——跨Transformer层选择性重标定保留的层状态，并在探针校验失败时通过完整计算中间层进行刷新，减少重复的Transformer计算。

Result: 在 LIBERO 与 RoboTwin 2.0 上对三种代表性 WAM 架构评测：观测到动作延迟提速 1.47-3.05 倍，每次重规划 GPU 推理时间提速 2.23-3.27 倍，同时保留原生 WAM 任务成功率的 96.69%-99.54%。

Conclusion: 通过状态复用与自适应而非重新计算，WAMACHINE 在不训练的前提下显著加速 WAM 推理且几乎不损失任务成功率，验证了推理状态可被有效保留与适配以实现高效精确的连续控制。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable future-aware control by jointly modeling actions and environment dynamics. However, iterative diffusion or flow inference incurs substantial denoising latency. Prior inference state offers a natural opportunity for acceleration, yet changing planning contexts, observations, and intermediate representations can quickly render retained state stale. Preserving useful computation therefore requires adapting inference state rather than reusing it as-is. To this end, we present $\mathrm{WAM}{\scriptstyle\mathrm{ACHINE}}$, a training-free framework that accelerates WAM inference by preserving and adapting inference state for efficient and accurate continuation as the control loop evolves. Across closed-loop replans, Trajectory Remapping remaps replan state from the preceding replan to initialize the next replan, reducing redundant trajectory generation. Across denoising steps, Observation Rebinding performs anticipatory inference during action execution and rebinds retained denoising state to the real observation for continuation when consistency checks pass, reducing latency exposed to the control loop. Across Transformer layers, Residual Rescaling selectively rescales retained layer state and refreshes it through full computation of the middle layers when probe checks fail, reducing repeated Transformer computation. Evaluations of three representative WAM architectures on LIBERO and RoboTwin 2.0 show that $\mathrm{WAM}{\scriptstyle\mathrm{ACHINE}}$ achieves 1.47-3.05$\times$ speedups in observation-to-action latency and 2.23-3.27$\times$ speedups in GPU inference time per replan, while preserving 96.69-99.54% of native WAM task success.

</details>


### [107] [ReSync: Re-Aligning the Two Clocks of Asynchronous World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33944)
*Xi Lin,Feihong Zhang,Yulong Shi,Yanghong Mei,Zuxing Lu,Xiaofan Zhu,Zihao Liang,Zhirui Gao,Zhaowen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究世界-动作模型异步推理中动作流与视频流“双时钟”不同步的问题，提出可从采样计划直接读取的“承诺-证据差距”，并据此提出 ReSync：在支持窗口内只推进世界流后恢复原生去噪，从而在不改参数、不比候选的情况下提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型通常联合生成未来视频与动作，但对两条流采用不同去噪步数：动作流少步快速解码以保持控制速度，视频流多步运行以保持未来预测清晰。该设计虽有意为之，却使两流处于不同“时钟”，导致动作可能已可执行，而支撑该动作的未来视频仍远未解析。作者将其形式化为异步推理的双时钟视角，并试图量化这种不同步带来的决策质量问题。

Method: 作者定义“承诺-证据差距”（commitment-evidence gap），该量可直接从模型自身的采样计划读取，而非通过搜索测量。基于该差距，提出 ReSync：将计算放在有用区间内，即保持动作状态不变，仅在受支持窗口内推进世界流，然后恢复原生去噪。该方法不改变参数，也不比较候选样本。

Result: 差距具有预测性：差距扩大时，候选效用更难识别，额外候选采样收益下降，而推进世界流收益上升，两者出现交叉。因此增加世界计算并非简单越好，有用区间两端闭合且均可在 rollout 前从计划读出。在冻结的 paired RoboCasa 面板上，ReSync 将成功率提升 4.48 个百分点；等计算量但不推进世界的等待控制组无提升；同一规则无需调参即可迁移到第二个基准和第二个骨干网络。

Conclusion: 世界-动作模型的异步推理存在可诊断的承诺-证据差距，额外世界计算存在有效区间而非单调增益。ReSync 通过在该区间内推进世界流并保持动作状态，实现了稳定、免调参且可迁移的性能提升，为世界-动作模型推理调度提供了原则性方法。

Abstract: Jointly generating future video and actions has become a standard recipe for world-action models, and the strongest systems denoise the two streams on separate schedules: actions are decoded in few steps so control stays fast, while the video stream runs longer to keep the predicted future sharp. The design is deliberate, but it leaves the two streams on different clocks, and an action can become executable while the future that should justify it is still largely unresolved. We formalize this as a two-clock view of asynchronous inference and introduce the commitment-evidence gap, a quantity read directly from a model's own sampling schedule rather than measured by search. The gap is predictive: as it widens, candidate utility becomes harder to identify and extra candidate sampling buys less, while advancing the world stream buys more, and the two cross. Spending more world computation is therefore not simply better. The useful interval is closed at both ends, and both ends can be read off the schedule before any rollout. ReSync places the computation inside it: hold the action state, advance only the world within the supported window, then resume native denoising. No parameters change and no candidates are compared. On a frozen paired RoboCasa panel this improves success by 4.48 points, while an equal-compute control that waits without advancing the world does not move, and the same rule transfers to a second benchmark and a second backbone without retuning.

</details>


### [108] [Don't Throw Away the Tail: Action Upcycling for Policy Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34911)
*Taesung Kwon,Jangho Park,Sunwoo Park,Youngmin Kim,Seonghyun Jin,Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的Action Upcycling算法，通过复用被丢弃的动作延长执行视界，在保持成功率的同时将策略调用次数减少1.2–1.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人策略从单次观测预测一段未来动作，仅执行前缀后即丢弃其余部分并重新规划。执行视界长度的选择面临反应性与效率的权衡：短视界保持反应性但需频繁调用策略。现有测试时方法虽能自适应选择视界，但要么读取模型内部信号（需针对每种架构选择信号），要么额外采样（增加成本）。

Method: 作者提出Action Upcycling，一种免训练算法，在不访问模型内部、不额外采样的情况下，复用策略本会丢弃的动作。核心观察是：只要动作速度保持平滑，被丢弃的动作就与其重新规划的版本保持接近。因此算法将执行视界延长至速度开始出现波动的临界点。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务上的大量实验表明，Action Upcycling在多种视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）乃至一个世界动作模型（WAM）上，将策略调用次数减少1.2–1.7倍，且成功率无损失。

Conclusion: Action Upcycling适用于任意分块策略，开销可忽略，且与少步采样、流式动作解码等策略加速方法正交，为策略加速开辟了新维度。

Abstract: Modern robot policies predict a chunk of future actions from a single observation, execute only a prefix, and discard the rest before replanning. Choosing the length of this prefix, the execution horizon, poses a trade-off between reactivity and efficiency. A short horizon keeps the policy reactive to the environment, but requires frequent policy calls. Recent test-time methods adaptively select the horizon for each chunk, but they either read model internals, where the signal must be chosen for each architecture, or draw extra samples, which adds cost. We propose *Action Upcycling*, a training-free algorithm that reuses actions the policy would otherwise discard, without accessing model internals or drawing extra samples. We find that discarded actions stay close to their replanned versions as long as the action velocity remains smooth. Action Upcycling therefore extends the execution horizon up to the point where the velocity begins to fluctuate. Extensive experiments on simulated and real-world manipulation tasks show that Action Upcycling reduces policy calls by 1.2--1.7$\times$ with no loss in success rate, across multiple Vision-Language-Action Models (VLAs) and even a World Action Model (WAM). It applies to any chunked policy at negligible cost and is orthogonal to other policy acceleration methods such as few-step sampling and streaming action decoding, opening a new axis for policy acceleration.

</details>


### [109] [Test-Time Spatial Reasoning for Robot Manipulation Using Generative Real-to-Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33982)
*Ivan Kapelyukh,Yafei Hu,Ran Gong,Brandon May,Tushar Kusnur,Laura Herlant,Karl Schmeckpeper,Edward Johns,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练、测试时框架 Simify：从单张 RGB-D 图像重建可仿真资产，利用大规模并行物理仿真与进化搜索进行显式空间推理，并在真实机器人上实现未见物体的复杂重排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理是通用机器人智能的基础，长时程、多物体交互任务需要机器人理解并优化物体间空间关系；现有基础模型在精确空间推理及仿真到现实迁移方面仍有不足。

Method: Simify 无需训练，在测试时运行：输入单张场景 RGB-D 图像，结合 3D 生成模型与视觉语言模型重建仿真就绪资产；给定由奖励函数指定的任务（如搭建最高塔），并行启动数千次仿真 rollout，并通过进化搜索优化物体排列，通常在数秒内收敛。

Result: 在真实机器人硬件上进行定量实验，端到端执行未见物体的复杂物体重排任务；结果优于面向空间推理的基础模型 prior work，表明推理阶段大规模并行仿真能有效提升性能，并凸显完整、准确几何对成功 sim-to-real 迁移的重要性。

Conclusion: Simify 验证了免训练测试时大规模并行物理仿真在机器人空间推理与物体重排中的潜力；有效的仿真到现实迁移依赖高质量场景重建与几何精度。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is fundamental to general robot intelligence, as it enables robots to complete long-horizon tasks involving multi-object interaction. We introduce Simify, a training-free, test-time framework that performs explicit spatial reasoning via massively parallel physics simulation. From a single RGB-D image of a scene, Simify reconstructs simulation-ready assets leveraging 3D generative models and vision-language models. Then given a task specified by a reward function (e.g., build the tallest tower), Simify launches thousands of parallel rollouts in simulation and performs an evolutionary search to optimize object arrangements, typically converging within seconds. We conduct quantitative experiments on real-robot hardware to demonstrate the ability of our framework to execute complex object rearrangement tasks end-to-end with previously unseen objects. Results show that our framework outperforms prior work on foundation models for spatial reasoning by effectively exploiting large-scale parallel simulation during inference, and also highlight the importance of complete and accurate geometry for successful sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [110] [Quantile Head for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34061)
*Xuan Wang,Yinan Wu,Haoran Duan,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出Quantile Head，通过统一回归与流匹配目标并推导分位数目标，在单次前向中预测中位数与正间隙形成有序边际动作分位数，实现无需重训练的多策略采样，并在多个基准与真机上取得最优成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的动作头存在明显局限：点回归只能给出动作分布的点估计，表达能力不足；标准流匹配采样器则需昂贵的迭代采样，推理开销大。因此需要一种兼顾分布表达与单步高效推理的动作头设计。

Method: 作者将回归与流匹配统一到同一目标下，并进一步扩展推导出分位数目标。基于该目标设计Quantile Head：一次前向预测中位数及正间隙，构成有序的边际动作分位数。这些分位数支持多种采样策略且无需重新训练，并通过联合监督训练默认的中位数策略。对联合监督的局部分析表明，在邻近分位数校准、间隙固定且校正速度匹配时，直接中位数更新的方差低于仅中位数监督。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、LIBERO-Pro以及两个真机任务上，联合监督的中位数策略在所比较方法中取得最高平均成功率，并在匹配的LIBERO基线中实现最短平均回合时间。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 统一回归与流匹配的分位数视角为动作头设计提供了新思路，Quantile Head以单步推理实现分布级建模，兼顾成功率与效率，且支持灵活的采样策略切换，具有良好的实用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models integrate pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) with action heads for robot control. Common action heads have distinct limitations: point regression provides only a point estimate of the action distribution, while standard flow-matching samplers require costly iterative sampling. To address these limitations, we unify regression and flow matching under a shared objective and extend it to derive a quantile objective. This quantile objective guides the design of our Quantile Head, which predicts a median and positive gaps to form ordered marginal action quantiles in one forward pass. These quantiles support multiple sampling strategies without retraining and are jointly supervised to train the default median policy. Our local analysis of this joint supervision shows that, with calibrated nearby quantiles, fixed gaps, and matched correction speed, direct median updates have lower variance than under median-only supervision. Experiments show that this jointly supervised median policy achieves the highest average success rates among the compared methods on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, LIBERO-Pro, and two real-robot tasks, together with the shortest mean episode time among matched LIBERO baselines; code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [111] [RoboFL: Federated Expert Assembly for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34968)
*Rongyu Zhang,Ruizhi Fan,Yunfan Lou,Hengyu Fang,Shenli Zheng,Chenrui Wu,Yili Jin,Li Du,Dan Wang,Yuan Du,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboFL 提出 MoSAIC（Mixture of Slotted Adapters）用于联邦世界-动作学习：将本地训练的 LoRA 适配器直接作为服务器 MoE 的专家分支，结合 FARD 路由蒸馏与 PCEA 专家聚合，实现高效个性化联邦微调，在仿真与真机任务上优于集中式 PEFT 及 MoE 联邦 VLA 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）与世界-动作模型受限于物理交互数据稀缺、机构数据孤岛和任务异构。联邦学习结合参数高效微调（PEFT）可避免交换完整模型更新，但适配器联邦聚合存在难点：朴素聚合会纠缠不兼容更新；引入 MoE 式路由又可能稀释专家专精性并导致专家选择不稳定。

Method: 提出 RoboFL，实例化 MoSAIC 框架。MoSAIC 将各客户端本地训练的 LoRA 适配器直接安装为服务器 MoE 的专家分支，使专家携带先验知识；服务器端路由器学习 token 分配，并联合优化路由与专家参数。进一步引入 Foresight-to-Action Routing Distillation（FARD）对齐模型三条路径的路由；Path-Consensus Expert Aggregation（PCEA）将完整专家更新转换为紧凑全局适配器，用于个性化再分发。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0、RLBench 以及真实 Franka 机械臂上验证。RoboFL 通过结构化专家组装取得更优性能：在 Franka 机械臂上比集中式 PEFT InternVLA-A1 高 12.23%；相对基于 MoE 的联邦 VLA 基线，每轮客户端通信量最高降低 86.81%。

Conclusion: MoSAIC 面向联邦世界-动作学习，有效将本地适配器整合为服务器 MoE 专家，通过路由蒸馏与路径共识专家聚合兼顾专家专精、路由稳定与通信效率，为异构物理交互数据下的 VLA 协作训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action and world-action models are increasingly popular, yet remain bottlenecked by physical interaction data that is scarce, institutionally siloed, and task-heterogeneous. A natural federated solution is to let each client adapt a shared foundation model through parameter-efficient fine-tuning, avoiding the exchange of full-model updates. However, federating these adapters is nontrivial, as naive aggregation can entangle incompatible updates, while incorporating MoE-style routing into federated aggregation may dilute specialization and destabilize expert selection. We present RoboFL, which instantiates MoSAIC (Mixture of Slotted Adapters) for federated world-action learning. MoSAIC directly installs locally trained LoRA adapters as the expert branches of a server MoE. Server-side routers learn token assignments over these prior-informed branches while jointly refining routing and expert parameters. Foresight-to-Action Routing Distillation (FARD) aligns routing across the model's three paths, while Path-Consensus Expert Aggregation (PCEA) converts complete expert updates into a compact global adapter for personalized redistribution. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0, RLBench, and a real-world Franka robot arm show the superiority of RoboFL with structured expert assembly, as it outperforms centralized PEFT InternVLA-A1 by 12.23% on the Franka arm, while reducing per-round client communication by up to 86.81% relative to MoE-based federated VLA baselines.

</details>


### [112] [RAVEL: Asynchronous Rolling Inference for Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34170)
*Yuhan Chen,Ke Yu,Pengfei Liu,Shuxun Wang,Yi Yang,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAVEL 是一种异步 VLA 推理框架，通过滚动缓冲与解耦 VLM 编码来降低流匹配动作生成的延迟，实现低延迟、高频闭环机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在通用机器人操作中表现优异，但依赖计算昂贵的 VLM 编码和多步迭代动作生成，造成显著推理延迟，使机器人难以在动态环境中快速响应。

Method: RAVEL（Rolling Asynchronous VLA Enabling Low-Latency Control）针对 VLM 主干与动作专家的计算瓶颈进行异步推理：1）为减少多步动作去噪延迟，允许近期动作在单步去噪后即执行，并将部分去噪的未来动作放入滚动缓冲区继续推进；2）将 VLM 编码与滚动动作生成解耦，动作专家使用最新可用的 VLM 上下文连续运行，同时通过轻量级 Fast Observation Pathway（FOP）以当前观测直接条件化动作专家。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务中，RAVEL 在保持底层 VLA 任务能力的同时，持续实现显著更低的响应延迟，支持高频且响应迅速的闭环控制。

Conclusion: RAVEL 通过异步推理与滚动生成有效缓解 VLA 的编码和去噪计算瓶颈，在不牺牲任务性能的前提下提升实时性，适用于动态环境中的机器人操作。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) models are highly effective for generalist robot manipulation, yet their reliance on computationally expensive VLM encoding and multi-step iterative action generation imposes a significant latency bottleneck. The resulting inference latency makes it difficult for robots to respond quickly, especially in dynamic environments. We address this limitation with RAVEL (Rolling Asynchronous VLA Enabling Low-Latency Control), an asynchronous inference framework that addresses the computational bottlenecks of both the VLM backbone and the action expert. To reduce the delay from multi-step action denoising, RAVEL allows near-term actions to be executed after a single denoising step by carrying partially denoised future actions forward in a rolling buffer. To avoid blocking on slow VLM encoding, RAVEL decouples VLM encoding from rolling action generation, allowing the action expert to operate continuously using the latest available VLM context, while a lightweight Fast Observation Pathway (FOP) directly conditions the action expert on current observations. Across simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, RAVEL consistently achieves substantially lower response latency while maintaining the task capability of the underlying VLA, enabling high-frequency and responsive closed-loop control.

</details>


### [113] [Learning to Act under Visual Interruptions with Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35003)
*Mingle Jiang,Rui Xu,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出MAIL-Bench基准，用于评估VLA模型在任务执行中相机中断/缺失视觉输入时的闭环操作能力，并提出MINT方法，通过训练鲁棒策略并结合光流外推或动作条件世界模型在推理时补全缺失观测，在π0.5、GR00T N1.5及AgiBot G2实机上提升相机丢失下的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常在任务执行全程所有相机流可用的假设下开发与评估；一旦某个相机在任务中途停止输出帧，策略必须在缺少该视角后续观测的情况下继续行动。该问题具有实际重要性，但视觉中断如何影响闭环操作尚缺乏系统理解与评估手段。

Method: 1) 提出MAIL-Bench：在每条成功参考轨迹的多个阶段中断不同相机，衡量策略在视觉输入不可用时的能力保持情况；2) 提出MINT：先训练VLA策略使其在缺失视觉输入下仍可运行；推理时利用光流外推或动作条件世界模型选择性补充缺失观测，并在预测视图不可靠时撤回预测。

Result: 在π0.5和GR00T N1.5上的实验表明，MINT在相机丢失条件下显著提升任务成功率，优于原始模型；在AgiBot G2上的实验进一步验证了相机丢失条件下的真实机器人部署可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作将VLA视觉中断问题形式化为可评估的基准任务，并给出训练与推理阶段相结合的MINT方案，证明在相机缺失时保持/恢复策略能力是可行且有效的，为鲁棒VLA操作提供了基准与方法基础。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, but they are typically developed and evaluated with all camera streams available throughout task execution. When a camera stops delivering frames during task execution, the policy must continue acting without access to subsequent observations from the missing view. Despite its practical importance, how such interruptions affect closed-loop manipulation remains insufficiently understood. To investigate this problem, we introduce MAIL-Bench, a benchmark that evaluates visual interruptions with VLA models. By interrupting different cameras at multiple stages of each policy's successful reference trajectory, MAIL-Bench measures how well policies retain their capabilities when visual inputs become unavailable. Building on this benchmark, we propose MINT, which first trains VLA policies to remain functional under missing visual inputs. At inference time, MINT selectively supplements missing observations using optical-flow extrapolation or an action-conditioned world model, and withdraws predicted views when they become unreliable. Experiments on $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.5 show that MINT significantly improves task success under camera loss over the original models. Experiments on AgiBot G2 further demonstrate the real-robot deployment under camera loss. The benchmark is available at this https URL

</details>


### [114] [FailPatch: Failure Residual Patching for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34175)
*Peng Yu,Jiacheng Wang,Ziheng Zhang,Xuchong Zhang,Baoting Li,Zhuoyuan Yu,Yuxiang Chen,Tiancai Wang,Hongbin Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FailPatch：一种失败驱动的残差修补框架，将动作监督与执行可靠性监督解耦，在冻结VLA策略的动作隐空间中引入Null门控残差专家库，以极少可训练参数提升长时程与真实任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略多依赖成功示范进行适配，只能提供直接动作监督，难以覆盖易失败状态；部署失败虽能暴露这些状态，却缺少传统监督学习所需的纠正动作。因此需要一种能利用失败部署轨迹、同时保持原策略可靠行为的方法。

Method: FailPatch将监督分为两部分：成功示范约束策略应如何动作，部署轨迹指示行为何时不可靠。观察到动作隐表示中可靠状态与失败状态具有明显线性可分性，且直接参与动作生成。基于此，在冻结VLA策略的动作隐空间中加入Null-gated Residual Expert Bank，并采用统一的Preserve–Redirect–Trust目标：可靠状态保留原策略，失败相关状态选择残差专家，并在有界干预下将表示从失败区域重定向至成功相关区域。

Result: 仅需0.52%可训练参数，FailPatch在RoboTwin四个长时程任务上干净评估成功率提升11.0个百分点，clean-to-random泛化提升9.5个百分点；在三个真实世界任务上相较基线提升16.7个百分点。

Conclusion: FailPatch表明，将失败部署轨迹用于可靠性监督、并在动作隐空间进行有界残差修补，可在极低参数开销下显著提升VLA策略的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are typically adapted using successful demonstrations, which provide direct action supervision but rarely cover failure-prone states. Deployment failures expose these states, yet lack the corrective actions needed for conventional supervised learning. We propose FailPatch, a failure-driven residual patching framework that decouples action supervision from execution-reliability supervision. Successful demonstrations ground how the policy should act, while deployment trajectories indicate when its behavior becomes unreliable. We further observe that action hidden representations exhibit clear linear separability between reliable and failure-associated states while directly conditioning action generation. Building on these insights, FailPatch introduces a Null-gated Residual Expert Bank into the action hidden space of a frozen VLA policy. A unified Preserve--Redirect--Trust objective retains the original policy in reliable states, selects residual experts in failure-associated states and redirects representations from failure regions toward success-associated regions under bounded intervention. With only 0.52% trainable parameters, FailPatch improves success rates by 11.0 percentage points on four long-horizon RoboTwin tasks under clean evaluation, 9.5 percentage points under clean-to-random generalization, and 16.7 percentage points over the baseline across three real-world tasks. Project and code: this https URL .

</details>


### [115] [EMPIRIC: Experiment-Driven Learning of Residual World Models for Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35047)
*Yichao Liang,Amber Li,Dat Nguyen,Emily Bunnapradist,Michelangelo Naim,Sreela Kodali,Matteo Merler,Bowen Li,Kiran Gopinathan,Yiyun Liu,Nikhil Pimpalkhare,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Adrian Weller,Zenna Tavares,Tom Silver,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EMPIRIC：以物理引擎为基座、学习“残差世界模型”的智能体，通过程序合成缺失机制并用贝叶斯推断估计参数/隐状态，从而预测、主动实验与假设修正；在五个仿真域和真实机器人上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需通过实验学习陌生物体行为并据此规划；物理引擎虽提供运动与接触知识，但会遗漏胶水固化、水加热、风等完整机制，因此不能完全依赖引擎或从零学习。

Method: EMPIRIC 学习残差世界模型：在物理引擎上扩展可表示新力、约束与隐状态的程序代码；用贝叶斯推断从带噪观测中估计程序参数与隐状态；模型用于预测动作结果、选择信息量高的实验，并在预测失败时修正假设。

Result: 在五个仿真域中，EMPIRIC 学到可解释、可复用的模型，并以更少环境交互解决更多任务，优于三个基线；在真实机器人上，它学会风力与多米诺质量以完成操作任务。

Conclusion: 将物理引擎先验与可学习残差程序结合，能有效补齐缺失机制并支持主动实验与在线修正，展示出可解释、样本高效、可迁移到真实机器人的世界模型学习能力。

Abstract: A robot should be able to learn through experiments how unfamiliar objects behave and interact, then plan with that knowledge. It need not start from scratch: physics engines supply knowledge of motion and contact, but can omit entire mechanisms, such as glue curing, water heating, or wind. We present EMPIRIC, an agent that learns a residual world model: a physics engine extended with code for the missing mechanisms. The learned programs can introduce new forces, constraints, and hidden state, and Bayesian inference estimates their parameters and states from noisy observations. The resulting model lets the agent predict the outcomes of actions, choose informative experiments, and revise its hypotheses when predictions fail. Across five simulated domains, EMPIRIC learns interpretable, reusable models, and solves more tasks with fewer environment interactions than all three baselines. On a physical robot, it learns wind forces and domino masses to solve a manipulation task. Website and code: this https URL

</details>


### [116] [WB-WAM: Heterogeneous Body-Hand Pre-training for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34199)
*Chuan Qin,Shaoting Zhu,Siyuan Luo,Siqiao Huang,Hongyu Zhao,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 WB-WAM（World Action Model），将显式全身动作监督引入生成式视频预训练，结合异构数据与人体运动迁移，实现数据高效的人形机器人移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人移动操作需要身体与手部协调，但传统机器人预训练数据对全身运动覆盖有限，机器人数据稀缺。

Method: 构建共享物理动作空间，整合身体、根节点与灵巧手标注，从1880.2小时部分标注的视频与运动数据中联合学习视频与动作；经 PICO 中训练并辅以前向运动学监督适配机器人任务；构建 WB-Datasets（重定向的第一人称人类演示与机器人轨迹）。

Result: 仿真中 HumanoidArena 达 81.9%；真实世界五项任务平均成功率 84.0%；任务对齐的 PICO 中训练提升下游性能并减少真机演示需求。

Conclusion: 异构全身预训练与人体运动迁移是人形机器人移动操作实现数据高效学习的可行路径。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation demands coordinated body and hand behavior, while conventional robot pre-training data provide limited coverage of such whole-body motion. We present WB-WAM, a World Action Model that incorporates explicit whole-body action supervision into generative video pre-training. A shared physical action space integrates body, root, and dexterous hand annotations from heterogeneous sources, enabling joint video and action learning from 1880.2 hours of partially annotated video and motion data. The resulting priors are refined through PICO mid-training and adapted to robot tasks with auxiliary forward kinematics supervision. We construct WB-Datasets to support these stages with retargeted egocentric human demonstrations and robot trajectories, allowing task-aligned human motion to supplement limited robot data. Evaluations in simulation demonstrate strong whole-body task performance with 81.9% in HumanoidArena, while real-world experiments further validate WB-WAM with 84.0% mean success across five tasks. Moreover, task-aligned PICO mid-training improves downstream task performance while reducing the need for real-robot demonstrations. These results support heterogeneous whole-body pre-training and human motion transfer as a practical route to data-efficient humanoid loco-manipulation.

</details>


### [117] [Spatial Grafting: Grounding 3D Features for Flow-Matching Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35249)
*Dingsheng Liu,Yangzheng Wu,Mahboubeh Asadi,Zhiyuan Li,Jinbang Huang,Yixin Xiao,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Spatial Grafting，一种轻量通用空间模块，将冻结的空间重建特征绑定到机器人相对度量几何，并以交叉注意力注入 flow-matching 动作专家；不修改宿主感知通路，在多种 VLA/WAM、仿真与真实平台上提升操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人操作策略（VLA 或 WAM）将交互相关的度量几何隐式化；空间重建特征虽可靠，但仅描述局部形状，未说明其相对机器人的位置。如何将这些特征有效传递给预训练策略仍未解决。

Method: Spatial Grafting 构建具有度量基础的空间 token，并通过 cross-attention 注入 flow-matching 动作专家；不修改宿主感知路径，从而保留预训练收益。同一 graft 架构无需针对不同宿主重新设计，可适配 2 个 VLA 和 2 个 WAM。

Result: 在 4 个仿真基准（短时程操作、视觉鲁棒性、杂乱场景、长时程移动操作）和 3 个真实机器人平台（单臂/双臂）上评估。RoboTwin 2.0 双臂基准上，graft 提升所有宿主；grafted π0.5 在 clean/randomized 场景分别提升 11.3% 和 15.6%，达到 94.0% 和 92.4%，超过最强已发表 3D 条件策略 WAM4D（93.8% 和 89.9%）。在 BEHAVIOR-1K 长时程双臂移动操作中，六个任务里五个超过 2025 挑战赛冠军，最多提升 0.47 Q-score，并在三项共同报告任务上平均超过 map-conditioned spatial policy。

Conclusion: Spatial Grafting 是一种通用、轻量且有效的空间模块，可将重建几何可靠注入预训练操作策略，跨宿主、跨基准和跨平台一致提升性能，且在长时程任务中增益更明显。

Abstract: Pretrained robot manipulation policies such as vision-language-action models (VLAs) or world-action models (WAMs) leave interaction-relevant metric geometry implicit. Recent breakthroughs in spatial reconstruction can supply the necessary geometry reliably, but their features describe local shape without stating where it lies with respect to the robot. How best to deliver these features to a pretrained policy remains unresolved. We propose Spatial Grafting, a versatile, lightweight spatial module that binds frozen reconstruction features to metric, robot-relative geometry. Spatial Grafting constructs metric-grounded spatial tokens and injects them into the flow-matching action expert through cross-attention, without modifying the host's perceptual pathway, so the host retains the full benefit of its pretraining. We evaluate it more broadly than any geometry-aware policy we compare against: one graft architecture, with no per-host redesign, on two VLAs and two WAMs, across four simulation benchmarks that span short-horizon manipulation, visual robustness, clutter and long-horizon mobile manipulation, and on three real-robot platforms with single- and dual-arm configurations. On RoboTwin 2.0, a dual-arm manipulation benchmark, the graft improves every host across VLAs and WAMs. Grafted $\pi_{0.5}$ gains 11.3% and 15.6% on clean and randomized scenes, reaching 94.0% and 92.4%, above the strongest published 3D-conditioned policy, WAM4D (93.8% and 89.9%). The margin widens as the horizon lengthens: on tasks from BEHAVIOR-1K, a dual-arm mobile manipulation challenge scored by average task progress, it surpasses the 2025 challenge winner on five of six tasks,by up to 0.47 Q-score, and exceeds a map-conditioned spatial policy on average across the three tasks both report.

</details>


### [118] [RoboICL: Embodied In-Context Learning with GPT-6 Astra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34261)
*Fangcheng Liu,Yeqing Shen,Anda Cheng,Weishi Mi,Chao Tang,Chenyuan Liu,Yushun Xiang,Tingguang Li,Yong-Lu Li,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboICL 是一种无需机器人专用参数更新、也无需学习 VLA 的上下文机器人控制框架，通过分离演示上下文与交互记忆，提升通用视觉语言模型在高精度与长时程任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉语言模型可用于零样本机器人控制，在开放式、语言或图像条件操作中表现突出，但在高精度和长时程任务上明显较弱。本文希望在不进行机器人特定参数更新、不训练专用 VLA 的前提下缩小这一差距。

Method: RoboICL 将“演示上下文”（有记录示例时提供）与“交互记忆”（累积模型自身动作和观测结果）分离，二者共享“观测—动作—回执—观测”语法。为跨任务阶段保留经验，方法将采样演示块与有界锚定记忆结合：固定锚点保留早期 rollout 交互用于上下文学习，最新交互支持即时纠错。另可选使用 Jev 门控动作复用。

Result: 在 30 个 RoboDojo 任务上，Open 类别用零样本、其余用单演示，RoboICL 较官方零样本 GPTAstra 在每个类别提升 20–27 个进度分；在 Memory 和 Open 上领先排行榜基线，Precision 与最强基线相当，Long-Horizon 具有竞争力。30 任务 Overall 得分为 50.64，最强基线为 33.68。十任务子集得分 60.60，距 π0.5 + GPTAstra 混合方法 2.00 分以内。三个真机任务平均进度从零样本 14.45 提升至单演示 63.33、三演示 78.89。两个开发任务中，Jev 门控动作复用减少 GPTAstra 调用 33–48%。

Conclusion: RoboICL 通过上下文学习与锚定交互记忆，在不微调模型的情况下显著提升通用视觉语言模型的机器人控制性能，并在仿真与真实机器人任务中均取得收益，同时可降低模型调用次数。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language models offer a promising way to zero-shot robot control: \gptastra{} excels at open-ended and language- or image-conditioned manipulation but remains substantially weaker on high-precision and long-horizon tasks. We introduce \emph{RoboICL}, an in-context robot-control framework that narrows these gaps without robot-specific parameter updates or a learned VLA. RoboICL separates \emph{demonstration context}, which provides recorded examples when available, from \emph{interaction memory}, which accumulates the model's own actions and observed outcomes. Both use a shared observation--action--receipt--observation grammar. To preserve experience across task stages, RoboICL combines sampled demonstration blocks with bounded anchored memory. Fixed anchors keep earlier rollout interactions available for in-context learning, while the latest interaction supports immediate error correction. Across 30 RoboDojo tasks, using zero shot for Open and one demonstration elsewhere, RoboICL improves on official zero-shot \gptastra{} by 20--27 progress-score points in every category. It leads the leaderboard baselines on Memory and Open, achieves comparable performance to the strongest Precision baseline, and remains competitive on Long-Horizon. Its 30-task Overall score is 50.64, versus 33.68 for the strongest baseline. On a separate ten-task subset, RoboICL scores 60.60, within 2.00 points of the $\pi_{0.5}$ + \gptastra{} hybrid approach. On three real-robot tasks, mean progress rises from 14.45 at zero shot to 63.33 at one shot and 78.89 at three shots. On two development tasks, optional Jev-gated action reuse reduces \gptastra{} calls by 33--48\%. Code is available at \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [119] [mmHRI: Towards Privacy-Preserving Human-Robot Interaction with Millimeter-Wave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34220)
*Junqiao Fan,Yuxuan Hu,Bofan Lyu,Yanshuo Lu,Pengfei Liu,Jiarui Zhang,Fangqiang Ding,Lihua Xie,Gen Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: mmHRI：首个毫米波雷达引导的多模态隐私保护HRI机器人操作框架，联合估计人体动作与3D姿态并驱动VLA策略实现闭环操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRI系统多依赖RGB摄像头持续观测人类以响应手势等非语言指令，在病房、餐厅等隐私敏感环境中受到人类直接拍摄限制，存在隐私泄露问题。因此需要一种无需视觉成像的隐私保护HRI方案。

Method: 采用毫米波(mmWave)雷达透过遮挡感测人体运动。提出mmHRI框架，包含两项关键设计：1）双流架构，联合学习未滤波的原始雷达张量与雷达点云，同时估计人体动作与3D姿态；2）基于记忆的状态空间模型(MSSM)，保留历史雷达特征以缓解雷达数据稀疏与时间不一致问题，减少姿态/动作的突变。估计出的人体状态被转换为结构化文本机器人指令，用于控制视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略，实现闭环机器人操作与人类感知反应。

Result: 在隐私保护帘幕场景下，mmHRI动作识别准确率达85.09%，优于现有基于雷达的替代方案。机器人试验证明在视觉遮挡下可成功完成递送与取回，且在未见过的受试者、杂乱配置与环境变化下任务性能稳定。

Conclusion: mmHRI验证了基于毫米波雷达的隐私保护HRI在机器人操作中的可行性与鲁棒性，能在无视觉成像的条件下实现可靠的人体动作/姿态感知与闭环操作。

Abstract: Assistive robots increasingly operate in many human-centered environments and perform various human-robot interaction (HRI) tasks, such as object delivery. However, most existing HRI systems rely on RGB cameras that continuously observe humans to respond to non-verbal commands, such as hand gestures. This raises privacy concerns in privacy- critical environments, such as hospital wards or restaurants, where direct camera observation of humans is restricted. To develop privacy-preserving HRI, we leverage millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar, which can sense human motion through privacy barriers without identifiable imagery. We propose mmHRI, the first multi-modal robot manipulation framework that achieves mmWave radar-guided privacy-preserving HRI. mmHRI introduces two key designs to mitigate the sparsity and temporal inconsistency of radar data in cluttered robot manipulation environments. First, we propose a dual-stream architecture that jointly learns from unfiltered raw radar tensors and radar point clouds to estimate both human actions and 3D poses. To mitigate signal inconsistency, mmHRI further incorporates a memory-based state-space model (MSSM) that retains historical radar features to reduce abrupt changes in pose/action. These estimated human states are then converted into structured textual robot instructions, which control a vision-language-action (VLA) policy for closed-loop robot manipulation and human-aware reactions. Our evaluation covers human action recognition and closed-loop delivery and retrieval. In the privacy-preserving curtain setting, mmHRI achieves 85.09% action-recognition accuracy, outperforming existing radar-based alternatives. Robot trials further demonstrate successful delivery and retrieval under visual occlusion, with stable task performance across unseen subjects, clutter configurations, and environments.

</details>


### [120] [From Pixel to Poses: Object-centric Tool Manipulation Learning from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35375)
*Bangjun Wang,Longyan Wu,Yukun Wei,Shenghe Shao,Chaoyi Huang,Wenze Cui,Zetong Xu,Hanlin Wu,Long Chen,Yi Ma,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: P2P-T 是一个数据高效、以物体为中心的工具操作框架，直接从人类演示中学习工具使用，通过物体中心世界模型提取姿态先验并接入姿态感知底层策略，绕过人-机器人配对数据，在复杂真实工具操作任务上比此前 SOTA 执行性能提升 73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作规模化主要受限于真实机器人数据稀缺；现有利用人类视频演示的方法计算开销大，且仍需配对的人-机器人数据做域对齐；当前 SOTA 擅长长时序任务，但在复杂工具操作所需的精细、精确控制上表现不足。

Method: 提出 P2P-T（from Pixel to Poses for Tool Manipulation），一个数据高效、以物体为中心的框架，直接从人类演示学习工具使用。采用两阶段方法：1）预训练以物体为中心的世界模型，提取稳定的姿态先验；2）将这些先验集成到高效、姿态感知的底层策略中。借助现代基础模型驱动的鲁棒自动化数据处理流水线，完全无需人-机器人对齐数据。

Result: 大幅降低整体训练开销；仅需少量逐任务微调，在复杂真实世界工具操作任务上执行性能较此前 SOTA 提升 73%，而这些任务对标准大规模预训练模型仍难以完成。

Conclusion: P2P-T 通过物体中心世界模型与姿态感知策略，绕开人-机器人配对数据依赖，缓解真实机器人数据稀缺问题，并在精细工具操作任务上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Scaling up robotic manipulation is primarily bottlenecked by the scarcity of real-world robot data. While recent approaches leverage human video demonstrations to mitigate this shortage, they remain computationally expensive and still rely on paired human-robot data for domain alignment. Although current state-of-the-arts excel at long-horizon tasks, they struggle with the delicate and precise control required for complex tool manipulation. To overcome these limitations, we introduce P2P-T, from Pixel to Poses for Tool Manipulation, a data-efficient, object-centric framework that learns tool use directly from human demonstrations. P2P-T bridges the cognitive and physical execution gap through a two-stage approach. First, pretraining an object-centric world model to extract stable pose priors; second, integrating these priors into an efficient, pose-aware low-level policy. By utilizing a robust automated data processing pipeline powered by modern foundation models, P2P-T completely bypasses the need for human-robot aligned data. This reduces overall training overhead drastically. With minimal per-task fine-tuning, our framework achieves a 73% improvement over the previous state of the art in execution performance on complex, real-world tool manipulation tasks that currently remain out of reach for standard large-scale pretrained models.

</details>


### [121] [Natural State-Prediction Accuracy can Hide Weak Controlled Responsiveness in VLA Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34684)
*Hyungjoon Kim,Wonbin Son,Mi Young Lee,Jun Young Lee,Seungmin Rho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估框架，将预测准确率、目标状态响应性与上下文稳定性分离，证明VLA模型内部表征的高自然轨迹准确率可能伴随弱目标状态响应性，并表明三者提供独立诊断信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭从VLA模型内部表征中准确解码物体状态，并不能证明预测忠实反映目标物理状态的变化；在自然观测中，物体状态、机器人构型、遮挡与任务进度共同变化，上下文线索可能混入预测，导致准确率被高估。

Method: 构建评估框架：使用经物理验证的观测，将目标坐标与机器人上下文交叉，从而分离出预测准确率、目标状态响应性（controlled target-state responsiveness）与上下文稳定性三项指标；并针对表征、读出器与训练数据进行对比与干预实验。此外，在基于初始状态误差和物理变量的失败预测器中加入响应性与上下文敏感性。

Result: 在固定表征—读出器组合中，高自然轨迹准确率可与弱的目标状态响应性并存；三项属性提供互不相同的诊断信息；在失败预测器上加入响应性与上下文敏感性后，相比指定基线降低了新初始化下的策略失败预测误差，而同观测下的受控MAE也具有信息量。

Conclusion: 应当在自然预测准确率之外，同时评估目标状态响应性与上下文稳定性，并进一步考察它们与实际策略行为和任务结果之间的关系。

Abstract: Accurately decoding object states from the internal representations of vision-language-action (VLA) models does not establish that the predictions respond faithfully to changes in the target physical state. In natural observations, object state, robot configuration, occlusion, and task progress vary together, allowing contextual cues to contribute to prediction. In this paper, we introduce an evaluation framework that separates prediction accuracy, target-state responsiveness, and context stability using physically validated observations that cross target coordinates with robot contexts. We demonstrate that high natural-trajectory accuracy can coexist with weak controlled target-state responsiveness in fixed representation-readout pairs. Comparisons and interventions involving representations, readouts, and training data show that the three properties provide distinct diagnostic information. Furthermore, adding responsiveness and context sensitivity to a failure predictor based on initial state error and physical variables reduces policy-failure prediction error on new initializations relative to the specified baseline while same-observation controlled MAE is also informative. These findings motivate evaluating target-state responsiveness and context stability alongside natural prediction accuracy, and examining their relationship to actual policy behavior and task outcomes.

</details>


### [122] [GAE: General Action Expert for Real-Time Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34233)
*Yuefan Wang,Huaicheng Zhou,Xiao He,Zhijie He,Mingchuan Yang,Huayi Zhang,Li Chai,Jinxin Liu,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出通用动作专家GAE，一个面向人形全身遥操作的低延迟统一学习框架，通过大规模异构人体动作数据、两阶段策略训练与延迟条件预判机制，实现多样动态动作的实时人机同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形化身可拓展人类物理存在，使远程机器人参与社交、服务与劳动活动；但现有遥操作系统难以同时实现多样动态的全身行为与响应式人机同步，尤其在真实遥操作中存在噪声、具身不匹配和端到端延迟问题。

Method: GAE从视频、动画和动作捕捉等异构来源构建大规模人体动作数据集，经标准化与增强处理。训练采用两阶段范式：先训练特权生成器策略，在仿真中跟踪人体动作参考并生成可行的人形轨迹；再训练可部署执行器策略，在课程域随机化下跟踪生成轨迹。为提升同步性，引入延迟条件预判机制，自适应补偿实时遥操作中的端到端延迟。

Result: 在Unitree G1和Westlake O1机器人上的仿真与真实实验表明，GAE使人形机器人能平滑模仿多样、敏捷且富有表现力的人体行为。

Conclusion: GAE验证了统一学习框架在通用、低延迟人形全身遥操作中的有效性，可缓解动作数据噪声、具身差异与延迟问题，为人形化身参与远程活动提供可行路径。

Abstract: Humanoid avatars extend human physical presence beyond the body, enabling people to participate in social, service, and labor activities through remotely operated robots. This requires teleoperation systems capable of realizing diverse and dynamic whole-body behaviors while maintaining responsive human-robot synchronization. We present General Action Expert(GAE), a unified learning framework for general-purpose, low-latency humanoid whole-body teleoperation. To cover diverse human behaviors, GAE builds a large-scale human motion dataset from heterogeneous sources, including videos, animations, and motion capture, followed by standardization and augmentation. GAE then addresses the noise and embodiment mismatch in human motions with a two-stage training paradigm: a privileged generator policy first tracks human motion references in simulation and rolls out feasible humanoid trajectories; a deployable executor policy then learns to track these generated trajectories under curriculum domain randomization. For responsive human-robot synchronization, GAE introduces a latency-conditioned anticipation mechanism that adaptively compensates for end-to-end delay during real-time teleoperation. Simulation and real-world experiments on Unitree G1 and Westlake O1 robots demonstrate that GAE enables humanoids to smoothly mirror diverse, agile, and expressive human behaviors. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [123] [Rethinking Causal Action Tokenization with Conditional Annealing in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35469)
*Chenyu Zhang,Yuhang Cao,Daru Du,Yingxi Lu,Jing Shao,Ruoqu Chen,Jiajun Liu,Liu Cao,Yicheng Liu,Hang Zhao,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CATok因果动作分词器，将动作分词重构为因果结构化生成过程：通过条件退火流匹配提取因果动作令牌，并用基于MMDiT的令牌条件流匹配解码器重建连续动作块，在多个仿真基准与真实机器人操作任务中提升重建-压缩权衡、推理效率以及VLA任务成功率与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归VLA模型虽具可扩展性，但其动作分词器将分词视为压缩问题，产生的表示与自回归骨干在语义上不对齐；同时，高层语义推理与低层运动执行的分离通常依赖显式注意力掩码。

Method: CATok将分词重新定义为因果结构化生成过程。其条件退火机制通过逐步退火流匹配过程提取动作令牌：每个令牌以所有前序令牌为条件，并编码特定噪声水平下的残差重建信号，从而形成由粗到细、生成语义与自回归建模结构对齐的因果令牌空间。基于多模态扩散Transformer（MMDiT）的令牌条件流匹配解码器，从离散令牌重建连续动作块，达到混合扩散头架构的重建精度。离散瓶颈在设计中实现知识隔离，无需显式注意力掩码即可分离高层语义推理与低层运动执行。

Result: 在三个仿真基准和真实世界机器人操作任务上的广泛评估表明，CATok在重建保真度-压缩权衡和推理效率方面持续优于现有分词方法，同时提升VLA任务成功率和训练效率。

Conclusion: CATok为纯自回归VLA系统建立了高性能、可扩展的基础，表明因果结构化动作分词是连接离散自回归建模与连续机器人动作控制的有效方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a scalable path to robot learning, yet existing action tokenizers treat tokenization as a compression problem, producing representations that are semantically misaligned with the autoregressive backbone. We propose CATok, a causal action tokenizer that reframes tokenization as a causally structured generative process. CATok introduces a conditional annealing mechanism that extracts action tokens by progressively annealing a flow-matching process: each token is conditioned on all preceding tokens and encodes the residual reconstruction signal at a specific noise level, establishing a coarse-to-fine causal token space whose generative semantics are structurally aligned with autoregressive modeling. A token-conditioned flow-matching decoder built on Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (MMDiT) reconstructs continuous action chunks from these discrete tokens with the precision of hybrid diffusion-head architectures. This discrete bottleneck enforces knowledge insulation by design, cleanly separating high-level semantic reasoning from low-level motor execution without requiring explicit attention masking. Extensive evaluations across three simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that CATok consistently surpasses existing tokenization methods in both reconstruction fidelity-compression tradeoff and inference efficiency, while improving VLA task success rate and training efficiency, establishing a high-performance, scalable foundation for purely autoregressive VLA systems.

</details>


### [124] [UMR: Universal Manipulation Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34256)
*Song Liu,Linyi Li,Yanshun Zhao,Rxuan Li,Xinrui Xu,Yi Ju,Yahui Deng,Senge Zhang,Guoyu Liu,Yixuan Li,Wuyang Zhang,Yao Li,Congcong Zhu,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出统一动作表示 UMR，将操作分解为世界坐标系下的 World Flow 与相对末端的 Ego Trajectory，并实例化为 0.5B 参数的 WEPVLA 策略，实现从人类演示到异构机器人的零样本技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用具身操作依赖跨本体、易扩展的统一动作表示。然而现有策略多采用本体特定的动作空间，导致跨本体演示数据难以大规模利用，并限制了向新本体和空间变化的迁移能力。

Method: 提出 Universal Manipulation Representation（UMR），将操作分解为两个功能不同但几何耦合的分量：与本体无关的 World Flow（描述世界坐标系中任务相关的物体运动）和 Ego Trajectory（表示当前位姿相对的末端执行器运动），二者通过 SE(3) 共轭耦合。将其实例化为 WEPVLA——一个紧凑的 0.5B 参数策略，通过双流 Point Action Adapter 与统一 Point Action Expert 在统一几何动作空间中学习。此外提出 Data-Efficient Strategy（DES），通过阶段感知的点云编辑在保持演示接触几何的前提下丰富物体配置。

Result: 仿真中，WEPVLA 在 LIBERO 上达到 97.5% 平均成功率，在 10 任务 RLBench 基准上达到 85.7%。真实实验中，单策略经 DES 增强人类演示后可在多样部署条件下零样本迁移；每任务约 10 分钟人类演示且无机器人演示，六种评测设置平均成功率达 91.7%，对比 HumanEgo 的 60.8%。

Conclusion: UMR 提供了本体无关且几何统一的动作表示，使 WEPVLA 能高效利用人类演示实现零样本跨本体迁移，在仿真与真实场景中均显著优于基线，验证了统一动作表示在通用具身操作中的价值。

Abstract: General-purpose embodied manipulation hinges on a unified action representation that generalizes across embodiments and scales readily. Yet existing policies rely on embodiment-specific action spaces, making cross-embodiment demonstrations difficult to leverage at scale and limiting transfer to new embodiments and spatial variations. To this end, we introduce Universal Manipulation Representation (UMR), a unified action representation that enables zero-shot skill transfer from human demonstrations to heterogeneous robots. UMR decomposes manipulation into two functionally distinct yet geometrically linked components: embodiment-agnostic World Flow, which describes task-relevant object motion in the world frame, and Ego Trajectory, which represents end-effector motion relative to the current pose. We instantiate UMR as World--Ego Point VLA (WEPVLA), a compact 0.5B-parameter policy that learns in the unified geometric action space through a dual-stream Point Action Adapter and a unified Point Action Expert, with an $SE(3)$ conjugation coupling the two components. To improve data efficiency, we complement UMR with a Data-Efficient Strategy (DES) that diversifies object configurations through stage-aware point-cloud editing while preserving demonstrated contact geometry. In simulation, WEPVLA achieves average success rates of 97.5\% on LIBERO and 85.7\% on the 10-task RLBench benchmark. In real-world experiments, a single policy trained on human demonstrations augmented by DES transfers zero-shot to diverse deployment conditions. With about 10 minutes of collected human demonstrations per task and no robot demonstrations, it achieves 91.7\% average success across six evaluation settings, compared with 60.8\% for HumanEgo. Code and additional materials are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [125] [F4R: Failure-Driven Recognition, Reconstruction, Refinement, and Redeployment for Continual Robot Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35575)
*Zhuoyuan Yu,Jiacheng Wang,Tianle Liu,Yihua Ren,Peng Yu,Chen Bai,Ziheng Zhang,Yufei Jia,Jindou Jia,Yuhang Zhang,Xinrui Zhang,Shang Yujing,Yuxiang Chen,Chuhao Zhou,Tiancai Wang,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出F4R：一种失败驱动的real-to-sim-to-real闭环学习框架，将真实世界失败自动诊断并重建为交互式物体中心环境，通过失败条件仿真-现实协同训练与强化学习迭代提升策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作模型受限于专家演示覆盖不足和对物理交互理解不足；通过收集新失败的真实演示来改进代价高、效率低、可能不安全且难以扩展。

Method: F4R利用智能体从rollout中自动识别与诊断失败；将每个失败重建为保留任务相关空间与物理条件的交互式、物体中心桌面环境；随后进行失败条件化的sim-real协同训练，并在重建环境中做针对性强化学习；改进策略重新部署，新失败持续反馈进入下一轮重建与学习闭环。

Result: 在四个真实机器人操作任务上评估，F4R取得93.75% In-Distribution和90.0% Out-of-Distribution成功率；在OOD条件下，在未额外收集真实纠正演示的情况下，比预算匹配的Targeted BC基线高18.75个百分点。

Conclusion: F4R表明可将真实失败转化为有针对性的策略改进，在不增加真实纠正演示收集成本的情况下提升分布内与分布外操作性能，为real-to-sim-to-real闭环学习提供了有效路径。

Abstract: The real-world performance of current vision-language-action models is fundamentally constrained by the limited coverage of expert demonstrations and their insufficient understanding of physical interactions. A common remedy is to collect additional real-world demonstrations of newly encountered failures. However, this process is costly, inefficient, potentially unsafe, and difficult to scale. To address this challenge, we propose Failure for Rising (F4R), a failure-driven real-to-sim-to-real closed-loop learning framework that converts real-world failures into targeted policy improvement. F4R first uses an agent to automatically identify and diagnose failures from rollouts. It reconstructs each failure as an interactive, object-centric table-top environment that preserves the task-relevant spatial and physical conditions. The policy is then refined through failure-conditioned sim-real co-training followed by targeted reinforcement learning in the reconstructed environments. The improved policy is subsequently redeployed, while newly observed failures are continuously fed back into the next reconstruction and learning cycle. Real-world evaluations on four manipulation tasks show that F4R achieves 93.75% In-Distribution and 90.0% Out-of-Distribution (OOD) success, outperforming the budget-matched Targeted BC baseline by 18.75 percentage points under OOD conditions without collecting additional real-world corrective demonstrations.

</details>


### [126] [FutureDuet: Decoupling Observation Access from Future Supervision in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34362)
*Jie Wu,Yuzhi Huang,Junqi Liu,Weichen Zhang,Haibin Huang,Yin Chen,Jingyan Jiang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FutureDuet 提出一种双视角未来监督的世界动作模型：主视角与腕部视角分别采用不同的未来预测目标，以更好支持机器人动作生成，且辅助预测模块仅训练期使用，不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型将主相机和腕部相机观测融合为单一视觉流，并用相同的未来视频目标训练，但二者视觉动态差异显著：主相机稳定，反映场景级任务演化；腕部相机随末端执行器运动，包含局部交互变化、视角偏移与自遮挡。统一目标难以同时适配两种不同预测需求。

Method: 提出 FutureDuet，保留双视角用于控制，但为每个视觉流设计不同未来目标。主视角使用未来 RGB 建模任务演化，并引入交互掩码与机器人骨架，将监督聚焦于任务物体和机器人运动；腕部流使用未来潜变量预测，建模短时程交互变化，无需像素级重建。ActionDiT 联合读取由此得到的 Task State 与 Interaction State，融合场景级进度与近距交互证据。所有辅助预测模块仅在训练阶段使用，推理时无额外开销。

Result: 在 RoboTwin50 上取得 94.2% 干净场景成功率和 94.1% 随机化成功率，在 LIBERO 上平均成功率为 99.2%。在六个需要精细交互的 RoboTwin50 任务上提升最明显，相比 Fast-WAM 在干净和随机化设置下平均提升 9.2% 和 12.8%。

Conclusion: 分离腕部路径并为双视角分别设计未来监督可带来互补收益，说明差异化未来目标能有效提升机器人动作生成，尤其有利于精细交互任务。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot action generation with future visual supervision. Existing WAMs commonly fuse main and wrist observations into one visual stream and train both with the same future-video objective, despite their different visual dynamics. A stable main camera reveals scene-level task evolution, whereas wrist cameras move with the end effector, mixing local interaction changes with viewpoint shifts and self-occlusion. These contrasting predictive demands suggest that the two views may benefit from different future objectives. We introduce FutureDuet, which retains both views for control, while allowing each visual stream to receive a different future objective. For the main view, future RGB models task evolution, while interaction masks and robot skeletons focus supervision on task objects and robot motion. For the wrist stream, future latent prediction models short-horizon interaction changes without requiring pixel-level reconstruction. ActionDiT jointly reads the resulting Task State and Interaction State, combining scene-level progress with close-range interaction evidence. All auxiliary prediction modules are training-only, adding no inference overhead. FutureDuet achieves 94.2% clean and 94.1% randomized success on RoboTwin50 and 99.2% average success on LIBERO. The improvements are most pronounced on six RoboTwin50 tasks that require precise interaction, averaging gains of 9.2% and 12.8% over Fast-WAM in clean and randomized settings. Controlled studies further show complementary gains from separating the wrist pathway and designing future supervision separately for the two views.

</details>


### [127] [RoboIRGBench: Benchmarking Implicit Referential Grounding in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34384)
*Aernaer Akelijiang,Jiannan Li,Zhineng Chen,Jingjing Chen,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出“隐式指称 grounding” (IRG) 能力评估基准 RoboIRG-Bench，基于 RoboMME 构建 11 个任务、40 个变体，覆盖直接、推理中介、空间和语境指称四类挑战；评测代表性 VLA 模型发现隐式指令下存在显著指称鲁棒性缺口，且真实机械臂 Franka Research 3 上同样存在，外部更强 VLM 亦不能消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在机器人操作中表现良好，但已有基准通常假设任务相关信息在指令中被显式给出；现实中人类常隐式提及对象、数量和关系，要求机器人从语言与感知语境中恢复目标，这一能力（IRG）尚未被系统评估。

Method: 构建 RoboIRG-Bench：基于 RoboMME，包含 11 个任务、40 个变体；覆盖四类挑战：直接指称、推理中介指称、空间指称、语境指称。对具有不同记忆机制的代表性 VLA 模型进行评测，并在 Franka Research 3 机械臂上做真实世界验证。

Result: 发现明显“指称鲁棒性缺口”：显式指令下表现好的模型在需从语境恢复同一任务相关信息时性能急剧下降；推理中介与空间指称尤其困难；使用外部 VLM 的模型鲁棒性更好但仍显著失败；换用更强外部 VLM 不能消除缺口；真实机械臂上缺口持续存在，表现为指称 grounding 错误与下游执行失败。

Conclusion: IRG 是可靠机器人指令跟随中一个独立且尚未充分探索的能力，亟需能稳健整合语言、感知、推理与动作的 VLA 模型。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, yet existing benchmarks typically assume that task-relevant information is explicitly specified in the instruction. In practice, however, humans frequently refer to objects, quantities, and relations implicitly, requiring robots to recover the intended target from linguistic and perceptual context. We study this capability as Implicit Referential Grounding (IRG) and introduce RoboIRG-Bench, a manipulation benchmark designed to systematically evaluate it. Built upon RoboMME, RoboIRG-Bench contains 40 variants derived from 11 tasks and covers four challenges, including direct, reasoning-mediated, spatial, and contextual referential grounding. As IRG often requires retaining and retrieving previously established context, we evaluate representative VLAs spanning different memory mechanisms. Our evaluation reveals a noticeable referential robustness gap. Models that perform well under explicit instructions can degrade sharply when the same task-relevant information must be recovered from context. Reasoning-mediated and spatial references are particularly challenging, while models using external VLMs show greater robustness but still exhibit significant failures. Moreover, replacing the external VLM with a stronger model does not eliminate these gaps. We further validate these findings on a Franka Research 3 robot arm, where the gap persists under real-world manipulation and manifests as both incorrect referent grounding and downstream execution failures. These results establish IRG as a distinct and underexplored capability for reliable robotic instruction following and highlight the need for VLAs that can robustly integrate language, perception, reasoning, and action.

</details>


### [128] [From World Models to World Action Models: Rethinking Next-State Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34414)
*Tingyu Yuan,Ziming Ji,Biaoliang Guan,Wen Ye,Wenrui Tian,Zhaopeng Gu,Feihong Zhang,Xu Yang,Yan Huang,Zhaowen Li,Chaoyang Zhao,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CF-WAM动态下一状态预测框架，通过采样同一未来的视觉、语义、几何与交互投影并统一为视频形式来监督WAM，增强动作学习与跨具身泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Models的核心范式是预测下一状态，强调预测保真度。随着其演进为World-Action Models (WAMs)，现有方法在训练前将下一状态固定为RGB、单一潜特征或预定义目标的静态组合，从而将动作学习限制在特定表征所保留的归纳偏置内，制约了性能与泛化。

Method: 提出CF-WAM框架：动态采样同一未来状态的视觉、语义、几何和交互四种投影，将它们标准化为统一的视频形式，并跨这些投影监督统一的WAM。这些投影所暴露的动作相关约束在训练步中累积，迫使WAM捕捉支撑同一动作条件未来的多种投影的底层状态转移结构。该动态机制还为人类与机器人学习提供了自然的跨具身动力学参考系，使异构经验可绕过外观差异直接贡献于共享的状态转移学习。

Result: 实验表明CF-WAM提升了训练效率与最终控制性能，并有效将人类经验转化为策略收益。在RoboCasa-GR1上达到SOTA，平均成功率82.50%；在LIBERO-Plus上达82.65%；真实世界评估中最高达84.00%。

Conclusion: 通过跨不同下一状态参数化联合学习，CF-WAM突破了固定表征的归纳偏置限制，实现了跨具身泛化，并在多个基准和真实环境中取得领先性能。

Abstract: Predicting the next state is a core paradigm of World Models for modeling physical dynamics, emphasizing prediction fidelity. As World Models evolve into World-Action Models (WAMs), existing methods still fix the next state before training as RGB, a single latent feature, or a static combination of predefined targets, thereby constraining action learning to the inductive biases preserved by a particular representation. To address this limitation, we propose CF-WAM, a dynamic next-state prediction framework that samples visual, semantic, geometric, and interaction projections of the same future, standardizes them into a common video form, and supervises a unified WAM across these projections. The action-relevant constraints exposed by these projections accumulate across training steps, forcing WAM to capture the underlying state-transition structure that supports multiple projections of the same action-conditioned future. This dynamic mechanism also provides a natural cross-embodiment dynamics reference frame for Human and Robot learning. By jointly learning across different next-state parameterizations, heterogeneous Human and Robot experience can bypass appearance differences and directly contribute to shared state-transition learning, improving cross-embodiment generalization. Experiments show that CF-WAM improves both training efficiency and final control performance, while translating Human experience effectively into policy gains. CF-WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on RoboCasa-GR1 with an average success rate of 82.50%, while reaching 82.65% on LIBERO-Plus and up to 84.00% in real-world evaluations.

</details>


### [129] [Revision, Not Restart: Revisable Visual Plans for Closed-Loop World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35439)
*Pengyiang Liu,Junbo Niu,Wenhao Zheng,Xinchen Chen,Canyu Li,Zhongyue Shi,Jiahao Xie,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可修订时序规划（RTP），将预测视觉未来作为持续动作条件，并通过学习到的修订桥接在反馈后调整视觉生成，从而支持闭环机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型常用预测的视觉未来来条件化机器人动作，但执行反馈可能使预测的部分内容失效，而其中任务结构仍可能有效，因此需要一种能在反馈后修订视觉规划而非完全重规划的方法。

Method: RTP维护视觉未来作为持续动作条件；核心是学习到的修订桥接：从视觉生成过程中保存的中间状态恢复，并依据当前观测调整其后续生成。视觉与动作监督将修订与后续控制连接；时间感知历史提供观测证据；自适应策略在新噪声基础上选择保留、桥接修订或全新重规划，再解码下一动作。

Result: 在RoboMME和RMBench上，RTP任务平均成功率分别为48.6%和84.8%。匹配比较支持学习到的续接；估计的检查点来源效应与动作前缀效应为正，但精度较低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，反馈驱动的视觉规划修订与闭环任务性能之间存在联系，为世界-动作模型中的可修订时序规划提供了有效路径。

Abstract: World-action models use predicted visual futures to condition robot actions, yet execution feedback can invalidate parts of a prediction while leaving its task structure useful. We propose Revisable Temporal Planning (RTP), which maintains the visual future as a persistent action condition and revises it after feedback. Its central mechanism is a learned revision bridge: it resumes an intermediate state saved during visual generation and adapts its continuation to current observations. Visual and action supervision connect this revision to subsequent control. Time-aware history supplies observed evidence, and an adaptive policy selects retention, bridge revision, or fresh replanning from new noise before decoding the next action. On RoboMME and RMBench, RTP achieves task-averaged success rates of 48.6% and 84.8%, respectively. Matched comparisons support learned continuation; estimated checkpoint-source and action-prefix effects are positive but less precisely resolved. These results connect feedback-driven visual-plan revision to closed-loop task performance. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [130] [Gaze Prompts: Temporally Dense Human Attention for Vision-Language-Action Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34550)
*Yihan Zhou,Rui Yan,Mingcong Li,Zheyuan Huang,Xu Yang,Xueyang Guo,Yilin Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出“眼动追踪监督的注视提示”（eye-tracker-supervised gaze prompting），利用VR遥操作中记录的注视点作为帧级视觉引导，注入VLA微调，并在部署时用轻量预测器替代眼动仪，从而在六项真实双臂操作任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA微调虽在每一步将图像与动作配对，但语言指令通常只提供任务级信息，导致每一时刻的视觉相关性是隐含的、未被显式监督。作者希望为VLA提供帧级、时刻对应的视觉引导，以提升策略对关键区域的关注与操作成功率。

Method: 在VR遥操作过程中采集操作者注视数据；训练时将记录的注视位置以十字准星（crosshairs）形式渲染到机器人头部相机图像上，作为帧级视觉提示进行VLA微调。部署阶段不依赖眼动仪，也不改变策略架构，而是训练一个轻量预测器，根据近期图像和语言指令估计注视位置，生成同类型视觉提示。方法以π0为基座实例化。

Result: 在六项真实世界双臂操作任务中，平均成功率从26.3%提升至56.0%；当用单一策略同时训练全部六项任务时也观察到性能增益。作者还发布了GazeMani数据集，包含1,200条带同步注视信息的遥操作轨迹。

Conclusion: 注视提示能够以低成本、可部署的方式为VLA微调引入帧级视觉监督，显著提升真实双臂操作性能；部署时无需眼动仪且无需修改策略结构，并提供了配套数据集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) fine-tuning pairs images with actions at every step, yet typically provides only a task-level language instruction, leaving moment-to-moment visual relevance implicit. We introduce \emph{eye-tracker-supervised gaze prompting}, which uses gaze recorded during VR teleoperation to provide frame-level visual guidance for VLA fine-tuning. During training, recorded gaze locations are rendered as crosshairs on the robot's head-camera images. At deployment, a lightweight predictor estimates gaze locations from recent images and the instruction, supplying the same type of visual prompt without an eye tracker or changes to the policy architecture. Instantiated with $\pi_0$, gaze prompting increases mean success from $26.3\%$ to $56.0\%$ across six real-world bimanual manipulation tasks, with gains also observed when a single policy is trained on all six tasks. We release \textsc{GazeMani}, a dataset of $1{,}200$ teleoperated trajectories with synchronized gaze.

</details>


### [131] [Where Memory Belongs: Ledger, an Object Ledger for Memory-Augmented VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34554)
*Tanguy Dieudonné,Jack B. Jedlicki,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Ledger：按记忆类型将机器人操作记忆分为策略内短期感知记忆与策略外长期物体记忆，在 RoboMME 上以单组权重取得四套件最优平均成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程、部分可观测的机器人操作依赖记忆，例如物体被放入哪个抽屉、移动了谁的杯子、经过多少动作周期。现有 VLA 将记忆直接嵌入策略，但基准显示没有单一策略内机制能覆盖所有时空维度，且大幅落后于 oracle 方法。

Method: 作者主张记忆类型决定记忆位置：短期感知记忆（重复、计时、回溯）应位于策略内部，长期物体记忆（持久空间状态、容器关系、事件历史）应外置为显式可读记录。Ledger 在单个微调 π0.5 策略上实现该分离：策略内使用帧采样记忆，策略外构建“ledger”时空物体记忆，由 SAM3 跟踪器和 VLM 描述器从演示中生成，并由 LLM 规划器在步骤边界读取和决策。

Result: 在 RoboMME 上，Ledger 取得所评估方法中最高的四套件平均成绩 64.3%，最强先前方法在相同评测下为 45.9%；物体指代 60.7% 对 40.3%，物体永存 86.7% 对 56.2%，且仅使用单组权重。运行时根据指令和记录选择记忆来源，无需任务级路由器。

Conclusion: 将短期感知记忆保留在策略内、把长期物体记忆外置为可读 ledger，并由 LLM 规划器在步骤边界读取，可有效覆盖不同时空记忆维度，显著提升长时程机器人操作的记忆能力，同时避免任务级路由。

Abstract: Memory is essential for long-horizon, partially observed robotic manipulation: a robot must remember which object was placed in a drawer, whose cup it moved, or how many action cycles have elapsed. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models embed memory directly inside the policy, but benchmarks show no single in-policy mechanism covers all spatio-temporal dimensions, trailing oracle methods by a wide margin. We argue that memory type dictates where memory should reside: short-term perceptual memory (repetition, timing, retracing) belongs inside the policy, while long-term object memory (persistent spatial state, containment, event history) belongs outside as an explicit, readable record. We present Ledger, a harness that realizes this split over a single fine-tuned $\pi_{0.5}$ policy by pairing an in-policy frame-sampling memory with an external spatio-temporal object memory, the ledger, built from a SAM3 tracker and a VLM captioner of the demonstration and read by an LLM planner that decides at step boundaries. On RoboMME, Ledger reaches the highest four-suite average among the evaluated methods, 64.3% (vs. 45.9% for the strongest prior method under identical evaluation), leading object reference (60.7% vs. 40.3%) and object permanence (86.7% vs. 56.2%) using a single set of weights. Choosing the memory source at runtime, from the instruction and the record, removes the need for a task-level router.

</details>


### [132] [Aerial GRIPPER: A Gradient-based Real-time Inverse-game Predictor and Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34594)
*Zeshuai Chen,Meng Wang,Jindou Jia,Xiang Yu,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GRIPPER框架，将空中抓取非合作目标建模为不完全信息下的广义和追逃博弈，通过在线推断目标代价参数并迭代求解开环纳什均衡，结合基于梯度的逆博弈求解器实现>50Hz实时规划，配合抗扰控制器完成精确跟踪与抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确捕获非合作目标是一项极具挑战性的任务。此类目标具有自主决策能力且其意图/代价参数未知，传统方法难以在对抗性、高度动态的场景中实现实时、鲁棒的预测与规划，因此需要一种能够在线推断目标策略并快速求解博弈均衡的框架。

Method: 1) 将交互建模为不完全信息下的广义和追逃博弈(general-sum pursuit-evasion game)；2) 在线推断目标的底层代价参数，并在滚动时域(receding-horizon)循环中迭代精化开环纳什均衡(OLNE)策略；3) 开发计算友好的基于梯度的逆博弈求解器，避免显式计算Hessian逆，借助隐式微分和快速Hessian-向量乘积实现>50Hz的实时求解；4) 设计抗扰控制器，克服不确定负载与夹爪驱动的扰动，实现规划轨迹的精确跟踪与目标抓取。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，GRIPPER在计算效率和任务性能上均优于对比方法；系统成功完成对非合作目标的捕获与运送任务，验证了框架在高对抗场景下的鲁棒性、适应性与实时性。

Conclusion: GRIPPER通过将非合作目标抓取问题转化为可实时求解的逆博弈框架，实现了高效、鲁棒且高频率的规划与抓取，为对抗性场景下的空中操作提供了可行且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate capture of non-cooperative targets is critical.
