In an attempt to tackle this intractable challenge, an aerial gripper system integrated with a Gradient-based Real-time Inverse-game Predictor and PlannER (GRIPPER) framework is proposed. The interaction is formulated as a general-sum pursuit-evasion game under incomplete information. Specifically, underlying cost parameters of the target are inferred online, and the open-loop Nash equilibrium (OLNE) strategy is iteratively refined within a receding-horizon loop. To ensure high-frequency execution, a computationally friendly gradient-based inverse-game solver is developed. Without explicit computation of the Hessian inverse, the optimized solution is updated (> 50 Hz) based on implicit differentiation and fast Hessian-vector products. Meanwhile, an anti-disturbance controller is developed to overcome disturbances of uncertain payload and gripper actuation, enabling precise tracking of the planned trajectory and accurate grasping of the target. Simulations and real-world experiments illustrate the superior computational efficiency and task performance of GRIPPER. The task of capturing and delivering a non-cooperative target is accomplished, highlighting the robustness, adaptability, and real-time performance of the framework in highly adversarial scenarios.

</details>


### [133] [NavJev: Efficient Vision-Language Navigation via Action-Centric Visual Compression and Discriminative Action-Semantic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34969)
*Kai Sheng,Liuyi Wang,Jinlong Li,Haojie Dai,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavJev 将零样本 VLN 从“每步调用 MLLM 做多模态自回归推理”改为“视觉压缩 + 轻量类型化动作选择”，在 R2R-CE 上以 0.65 秒/步达到 27.0% SR 和 22.4% SPL，显著降低推理延迟与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本 VLN 方法依赖 MLLM 对视觉观测、导航指令和候选动作进行多模态推理，但每个导航步都重复自回归生成，导致推理延迟高、具身智能体响应性受限。因此需要一种更高效的在线导航范式，在保留多模态语义信息的同时避免逐步重型生成。

Method: 提出 NavJev 框架：1）Action-Centric Visual Compression（ACVC）将 waypoint 几何信息、BLIP 标题和 RAM 语义标签融合为候选动作的紧凑表示；2）Discriminative Action-Semantic Memory（DASM）过滤共享语义，并在导航步间维护具有判别性的动作特定证据；3）基于上述表示，Jev 直接在可用动作集合上进行结构化概率决策，将在线导航转化为紧凑视觉压缩加轻量类型化动作选择。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 数据集上，NavJev 取得 27.0% SR 和 22.4% SPL，每步仅需 0.65 秒，相比基于 MLLM 的 VLN 方法大幅降低推理延迟与成本。

Conclusion: NavJev 通过动作中心视觉压缩与判别性动作语义记忆，将零样本 VLN 的在线推理从重复多模态生成重构为结构化动作选择，在保持竞争性导航性能的同时显著提升推理效率，为具身导航的实时响应提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Recent zero-shot Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) methods increasingly rely on multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to reason over visual observations, navigation instructions, and candidate actions. Although effective, repeatedly invoking autoregressive multimodal reasoning at every navigation step introduces substantial inference latency, limiting the responsiveness of embodied agents. We propose NavJev, an efficient VLN framework that reformulates online navigation from repeated multimodal generation into compact visual compression followed by lightweight typed action selection. Specifically, Action-Centric Visual Compression (ACVC) integrates waypoint geometry, BLIP captions, and RAM semantic tags into compact representations of candidate actions, while Discriminative Action-Semantic Memory (DASM) filters shared semantics and maintains discriminative action-specific evidence across navigation steps. Based on these representations, Jev directly performs structured probabilistic decisions over the available action set. Experiments on R2R-CE show that NavJev achieves 27.0% SR and 22.4% SPL with only 0.65 s per navigation step, while substantially reducing inference latency and cost compared with MLLM-based VLN methods. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [134] [Do Not Cut When Uncertain: Rejectable and Calibrated Decision Heads for VLA Policies in Robotic Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35039)
*Heng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出可拒绝且校准的决策头（RCDH），通过在冻结的VLA骨干网络上附加类型化、可拒绝、可校准的输出接口，使模型在遮挡导致单帧决策歧义时能够表达“不知道/不应行动”，从而避免高置信度且不可逆的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略（基于行为克隆或流匹配）被优化为输出动作轨迹，无法表达拒绝或弃权；在机器人采摘中，叶片遮挡使相同像素可能对应可切割茎或不存在茎，单帧决策本质歧义，迫使模型强行承诺，导致高置信度错误和不可逆后果。作者认为VLA的失效模式主要由输出接口决定，而非骨干网络规模。

Method: 提出RCDH（Rejectable and Calibrated Decision Heads），一种类型化、可拒绝、可校准的输出接口，可附加到冻结的VLA骨干上，无需重训练或新特征。其包含：(i) 显式拒绝与有序、条件分解的决策模式；(ii) 面向风险感知弃权的校准过程。在具有可控叶片遮挡的机器人采摘平台上，对比生成式、枚举式、校准式和可拒绝式接口，并检验拒绝空间排序是否关键。

Result: 仅替换输出头即可在遮挡条件下恢复分布外可用性，同时保持分布内性能；进一步验证拒绝空间排序的重要性。结果表明，在不确定性下实现可靠操作，关键缺失接口是“拒绝的权利”，而非更大的模型。

Conclusion: VLA的可靠性瓶颈在于输出接口能否表达拒绝与校准的不确定性；RCDH以即插即用方式为冻结VLA提供风险感知弃权能力，在遮挡采摘场景中显著改善OOD可用性且不牺牲ID性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies trained with behavior cloning or flow matching are optimized to output an action trajectory, but they cannot express "I don't know" or "I should not act." In robotic harvesting, occlusion makes single-frame decisions fundamentally ambiguous: identical pixels can correspond either to a cuttable stem or to no stem at all. Existing VLAs are forced to commit, leading to high-confidence errors with irreversible consequences. We argue that the failure mode of a VLA is determined not by backbone scale but by its output interface. We propose Rejectable and Calibrated Decision Heads (RCDH), a typed, rejectable, and calibrated output interface that can be attached to a frozen VLA backbone without retraining or new features. RCDH introduces (i) a decision schema with explicit rejection and ordered, conditional decomposition, and (ii) a calibration procedure for risk-aware abstention. We evaluate RCDH on a robotic harvesting platform with controllable leaf occlusion, comparing generative, enumerated, calibrated, and rejectable interfaces. We show that replacing only the output head restores out-of-distribution usability under occlusion while preserving in-distribution performance. We further test whether the ordering of the rejection space is critical. Our results suggest that the right to refuse, rather than a larger model, is the missing interface for reliable manipulation under uncertainty.

</details>


### [135] [QuadHand: A Compact Quadrotor Aerial Manipulator with MRC-SDF-Based Whole-Body Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35094)
*Rui Jin,Ruiyang Liu,Xinhang Xu,Haotian Jin,Yi Wang,Yizhuo Yang,Lihua Xi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出紧凑型四旋翼空中机械臂QuadHand，集成3自由度机械臂、夹爪和电池辅助被动重心补偿模块，并设计MRC-SDF与时空全身轨迹优化框架，实现复杂环境中的安全可执行空中操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人空中机械臂（UAM）可进行三维物理交互，但扩大工作空间会加剧机械臂引起的扰动；现有几何表示在近距离交互中难以兼顾几何保真度与计算效率。

Method: 硬件上，QuadHand由四旋翼、3-DoF机械臂、夹爪及电池辅助被动CoG补偿模块组成，以抑制主要臂致扰动。算法上，提出MRC-SDF（多关节机器人中心符号距离场），在可计算前提下保留精细几何细节；并提出时空全身轨迹优化框架，联合优化四旋翼与机械臂，生成安全且可执行的轨迹。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，该方法能够在复杂环境中实现安全且可执行的空中操作。

Conclusion: QuadHand结合MRC-SDF与时空全身轨迹优化，可有效缓解臂致扰动、提升近距离交互的几何表示与轨迹生成能力，为复杂环境下的安全空中操作提供可行方案。

Abstract: Uncrewed aerial manipulators (UAMs) integrate robotic arms with aerial platforms for three-dimensional physical interaction. However, enlarging the workspace increases arm-induced disturbances, while existing geometric representations face a trade-off between geometric fidelity and computational efficiency in close-proximity interaction. This paper presents QuadHand, a compact quadrotor aerial manipulator with a 3-DoF arm, gripper, and battery-assisted passive CoG compensation module to reduce dominant arm-induced disturbances. We further propose MRC-SDF, a Multi-articulated Robot-Centric Signed Distance Field that preserves fine geometric detail with tractable computation, and a spatiotemporal whole-body trajectory optimization framework that jointly optimizes the quadrotor and manipulator for safe and executable trajectory generation. Simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate safe and executable aerial manipulation in complex environments.

</details>


### [136] [Zero-Shot Reactive Obstacle Avoidance for Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35231)
*Weihang Guo,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的避障方法NUDGE，将符号距离场的梯度在推理时注入基于扩散或流匹配的机器人策略，实时引导其远离障碍物，同时保留原有任务分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略（如扩散策略、视觉-语言-动作模型）虽具备强大任务能力，但缺乏对障碍物的显式避让机制，需要一种通用、免训练、可实时反应式避障的手段，且不破坏策略原有任务分布。

Method: NUDGE在推理阶段将符号距离场（SDF，返回每个点到最近障碍物的距离）的梯度注入到任意基于扩散或流匹配的机器人策略中，通过可微关节轨迹解码器支持多种动作参数化（绝对/相对关节位姿、末端执行器位姿），从而在不重新训练的前提下实现避障。

Result: 实验表明NUDGE能够保留策略的任务分布，并可实时、反应式地运行。

Conclusion: NUDGE是一种通用、免训练的推理时避障方案，可无缝集成到扩散策略与视觉-语言-动作模型中，在不牺牲任务性能的前提下实现实时反应式避障。

Abstract: We propose NUDGE (Nudge Update via Differentiable GEometry), a training-free obstacle-avoidance procedure that can be incorporated in any robot policy based on diffusion or flow matching, including diffusion policies and vision-language-action models. Our work injects gradients from a signed distance field, a function returning each point's distance to the nearest obstacle, into the policy at inference time to steer it away from obstacles. It supports any common action parameterization, from absolute or relative joint poses to end-effector poses, through a differentiable joint-trajectory decoder. Experiments show that NUDGE preserves the policy's task distribution and runs reactively in real time.

</details>


### [137] [DexAgent: An Agentic Human2Sim2Robot Framework for Dexterous Manipulation with Self-Evolving Tool Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35318)
*Youhui Wang,Yunzhu Li,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Huang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexAgent：一个智能体式 Human2Sim2Robot 框架，从单段第一视角人类视频与任务提示生成物理合理的机器人轨迹，用于策略训练，在11个真实任务中将成功率提升至基线3.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频是可扩展的灵巧操作演示来源，但现有 Human2Sim2Robot 流程依赖预定义程序，难以适应多样化的物体属性与交互（尤其涉及铰接、可变形物体），泛化性与长时序任务处理能力不足。

Method: 提出智能体式框架 DexAgent，包含四个阶段：人类视频语义理解、基于物体属性的仿真重建、机器人轨迹优化、机器人数据生成。每阶段通过工具库选择合适技能或按需开发新技能以适配任务与物体属性；采用属性特定的验证器评估各阶段输出的物理有效性与任务要求，并反馈用于迭代精炼，防止误差在工作流中传播。最终阶段在仿真中变化物体与机器人状态以生成多样轨迹，并对渲染观测重贴图以促进 sim-to-real 迁移。新技能与验证器保留于工具库，实现自进化与能力复用。

Result: 在11个真实世界任务中，使用 DexAgent 生成数据训练的策略成功率较竞争基线高出3.5倍；随处理的人类视频增多，处理时间下降（能力可累积复用）。

Conclusion: DexAgent 通过智能体化、验证引导的自进化流程，克服了传统 Human2Sim2Robot 预定义程序的局限，可处理多样物体与长时序任务，并高效生成多样化机器人轨迹以提升策略的 sim-to-real 迁移与任务成功率。

Abstract: Human videos offer a scalable source of demonstrations for dexterous robot manipulation. However, existing human-to-simulation-to-robot (Human2Sim2Robot) pipelines rely on predefined procedures that struggle to accommodate diverse object properties and interactions, particularly those involving articulated and deformable objects. We introduce DexAgent, an agentic Human2Sim2Robot framework that converts a single egocentric human video and a task prompt into physically grounded robot trajectories for policy training. It operates through four stages: semantic understanding of human videos, property-based simulation reconstruction, robot trajectory optimization, and robot data generation. At each stage, DexAgent adapts its approach to the task and object properties by selecting suitable skills from its tool library or developing new ones when needed. Property-specific verifiers assess stage outcomes for physical validity and task-specific requirements and provide feedback for refinement, preventing error propagation through the workflow. This adaptive, verification-guided process allows DexAgent to process diverse objects and long-horizon tasks. In the final stage, DexAgent varies object and robot states in simulation to generate diverse robot trajectories from a single human video, then retextures the rendered observations to facilitate sim-to-real transfer. Newly developed skills and verifiers are retained in its tool library, making it self-evolving to accumulate reusable capabilities. This reduces processing time as DexAgent encounters more human videos. Across eleven real-world tasks, policies trained with DexAgent-generated data achieve a 3.5x higher success rate than competing baselines. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [138] [Self-Evolving Coding Agents: From Digital Programs to Physical-World Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35432)
*Hongcheng Gao,Jingjing Zhou,Zelin Zheng,Shijia Ge,Jay Zhu,Yazhe Wang,Jianshu Zeng,Xuan Shangguan,Di Wu,Lingyu He,Zhiqi Jia,Sihang Wu,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出“Physical Coding”范式，用代码显式表示物理任务状态与执行过程，并构建 HexaAnything 智能体，在真实与仿真机器人任务中验证其泛化、长程执行与自演进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA/WAM 模型将观测与指令直接映射为动作，导致策略与训练分布强绑定：布局或视角微变即失败，指令泛化差；根因是任务需求、条件、进度与失败恢复被隐式编码在动作序列中，难以检查与修正。数字编码智能体通过“调用工具—验证结果—依据反馈修改代码”提供了可借鉴的工作模式。

Method: 提出 Physical Coding：将任务状态与执行表示为代码。其中 Code as World 记录对象、关系、约束与进度；Code as Policy 组织规划、验证、恢复与执行。实现 HexaAnything，调用感知、规划、控制工具（含 VLA/WAM 策略），并基于外部反馈进行在环决策；经验证的执行轨迹转化为数据与记忆，推动从工具与 Harness 到模型权重、架构乃至硬件与任务设计的演进。

Result: 在 RoboCasa365 上，HexaAnything 相比 XR-1 VLA 提升 Composite-Unseen 与整体成功率；其 Harness 训练的 HexaModel 在所有 split 上优于基座，表明代码轨迹可内化物理执行。在 PhyBench 与双臂 AgileX 机器人上，智能体能自主完成物理实验及多数桌面任务，常快于已发表结果。观察到数据、模型与工具的自演进现象。

Conclusion: 代码化表示可提升物理任务的可检查性、可修正性与泛化能力，并使物理经验以可复用程序、记忆或证据的形式回流。未来工作聚焦权重内化、架构/语言/表示/任务的自主重设计，以及制造与科学场景部署。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action (WAM) models map observations and instructions directly to robot actions. This directness ties a policy to training: minor layout or viewpoint changes cause failure, and instructions generalize poorly. The root cause lies in representation: task requirements, conditions, progress, and failure recovery are implicitly encoded in action sequences, making them difficult to inspect or revise. Digital coding agents offer a precedent: LLMs call tools, verify results, and revise from feedback as executable code. The same working pattern of explicit state, manageable execution, and revisable procedures underlies generalization and long-horizon execution in the physical world, letting physical experience return as reusable programs, memory, or evidence. We propose Physical Coding, representing task state and execution as code. Code as World records objects, relations, constraints, and progress; Code as Policy organizes planning, verification, recovery, and execution. We build HexaAnything, which calls perception, planning, and control tools, including VLA/WAM policies, and makes in-the-loop decisions from external feedback. Verified traces become data and memory, enabling evolution from tools and Harness to model weights, architectures, and ultimately hardware and task design. On RoboCasa365, HexaAnything improves Composite-Unseen and overall success over XR-1 VLA, and its Harness-trained HexaModel beats the base on every split, indicating code traces internalize physical execution. On PhyBench and a dual-arm AgileX robot, the agent autonomously completes physics experiments and most tabletop tasks, often faster than published results. We observe data, model, and tool self-evolution; future work targets weight internalization, autonomous redesign of architectures, languages, representations, and tasks, and deployment in manufacturing and science.

</details>


### [139] [Uni-VLaT: Whole-Body Tactile Adaptation of VLA Policies for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35450)
*Zihao Wang,Shutong Liu,Siqi Zheng,Liu Cao,Ruoqi Chen,Rundong Liu,Yanchao Yang,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Uni-VLaT，将全身分布式触觉信息以预测式潜在状态的方式融入视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，用于人形机器人接触丰富型运动-操作任务，在五项真机任务上取得 75% 平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理接触往往决定人形机器人应如何响应，但仅靠视觉与本体感觉难以刻画物理交互，尤其是接触区域被遮挡时；相比在预设区域获取稀疏力/力矩，分布式触觉能保留全身空间分辨的接触模式，因此需要研究如何将全身触觉信息整合进 VLA 策略以实现接触丰富控制。

Method: 提出 Uni-VLaT，引入一条触觉通路，其潜在状态不仅用于动作生成，还被训练以预测未来的触觉、本体感觉和视觉表征；该预测目标构建了以触觉为锚点的多模态上下文，从而形成对物理世界更结构化的理解。

Result: 在五项真机任务（触觉触发运动、持续物理交互、人机接触、运动-操作）上评估，Uni-VLaT 平均成功率为 75%，比无触觉输入基线高 43 个百分点，比有触觉但无预测监督的基线高 7 个百分点；在两个预训练 VLA 骨干上，Table Sweeping 均提升 30 个百分点，Back-Tap Walking 提升 85–90 个百分点；消融表明上下文化触觉预测与绝对未来目标对性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 结果表明，预测式触觉学习为将预训练 VLA 策略扩展到全身物理交互提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Physical contact often determines how a humanoid should respond during loco-manipulation, yet vision and proprioception alone are often insufficient to characterize physical interaction, especially when the contact region is occluded. Unlike sparse force or torque measurements at predefined regions, distributed tactile sensing preserves spatially resolved contact patterns across the robot body. We therefore study how to integrate such whole-body tactile information into vision-language-action (VLA) policies for contact-rich control. Our approach, Uni-VLaT, introduces a tactile pathway whose latent state is trained not only for action generation, but also to predict future tactile, proprioceptive, and visual representations. This predictive objective builds a tactile-anchored multimodal context, encouraging a more structured understanding of the physical world. We evaluate Uni-VLaT on five real-robot tasks covering tactile-triggered locomotion, sustained physical interaction, human-robot contact, and loco-manipulation. Uni-VLaT achieves a 75% average success rate, outperforming a baseline without tactile input by 43 points and a tactile-input baseline without predictive supervision by 7 points. Across two pretrained VLA backbones, our method improves Table Sweeping by 30 points on both backbones and Back-Tap Walking by 85-90 points. Ablations further show that contextualized tactile prediction and absolute future targets are critical to performance. These results indicate that predictive tactile learning provides an effective route for extending pretrained VLA policies to whole-body physical interaction.

</details>


### [140] [MM-ABC: Towards Generalist Mobile Manipulation via Seeing, Coordinating and Imagining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35652)
*Qiwei Liang,Guangyu Chen,Shaolong Zhu,Zikuan Xiao,Jinxuan Lu,Yifan Xie,Renjing Xu,Wenbo Ding,Tianxing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MM-ABC是一个面向移动操作的基础模型，通过"感知—协调—想象"三部分（稀疏多层级VLM特征、训练期世界想象未来分支、掩码联合注意力臂-基座协调）实现跨动作流协作，在多个基准和真实任务上取得领先成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作使可达工作空间本身可控，带来两大挑战：连续自身运动下的空间感知，以及异构机械臂与基座动作的协调控制。现有方法或用显式3D表征/预测世界模型强化几何，或将移动与操作解耦为独立动作流。作者认为高效移动操作不仅需要解耦，还需要支持跨流高效协作的表征。

Method: 提出MM-ABC，围绕Seeing、Coordinating、Imagining Arm-Base Collaboration构建。关键组件：(1) 稀疏多层级VLM特征用于空间感知；(2) 仅训练期的未来分支，利用世界想象与几何意图作为额外监督，强化感知与操作意图预测；(3) MM-APT，通过掩码联合注意力与clean-action x-prediction协调分离的操作与移动流。预训练使用5000+小时异构机器人数据，覆盖40万+回合、12个数据集、17种本体。

Result: 消融显示：以速度预测替代clean-action预测使RoboCasa365复合已见任务成功率从32.8%降至29.2%，移除未来监督或多层级条件导致更大下降。实验覆盖EBench、RoboCasa365、ManiSkill-HAB、LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus及真实移动操作。EBench达44.71%，RoboCasa365达61.2%，LIBERO达99.1%，无扰动训练的LIBERO-Plus达82.8%，五个真实任务平均成功率达83%。

Conclusion: MM-ABC证明移动操作不仅需要动作流解耦，更需要支持跨流协作的表征；结合多层级VLM感知、世界想象未来监督与掩码联合注意力协调，能在仿真基准与真实任务上取得强性能，验证各组件设计的有效性。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation extends robot interaction beyond a fixed kinematic workspace by making the reachable region itself controllable. This flexibility introduces two central challenges: spatially grounded perception under continuous ego-motion and coordinated control of heterogeneous arm and base actions. Existing approaches strengthen geometry through explicit 3D representations or predictive world models, and often decouple mobility and manipulation into separate action streams. We argue that effective mobile manipulation requires not only decoupling, but also representations that support efficient cross-stream collaboration. We present MM-ABC, a foundation model built around Seeing, Coordinating, and Imagining Arm-Base Collaboration. MM-ABC combines sparse multi-level VLM features for spatial perception; a training-only future branch that uses world imagination and geometric intent as extra supervision, strengthening perception and manipulation-intent prediction and improving the overall learning signal; and MM-APT, which coordinates separate manipulation and mobility streams through masked joint attention and clean-action x-prediction. In controlled ablations, replacing clean-action prediction with velocity prediction lowers success on RoboCasa365 composite-seen tasks from 32.8% to 29.2%, and removing future supervision or multilevel conditioning causes larger drops. We pretrain MM-ABC on 5,000+ hours of heterogeneous robot data spanning 400K+ episodes, 12 datasets, and 17 embodiments. Experiments cover EBench, RoboCasa365, ManiSkill-HAB, LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and real-world mobile manipulation. MM-ABC achieves 44.71% success on EBench, 61.2% on RoboCasa365, 99.1% on LIBERO, 82.8% on LIBERO-Plus without perturbation training, and 83% mean success on five real-world tasks.

</details>


### [141] [Humanoid Loco-Manipulation With Discrete VLA Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35709)
*Wenxin Shao,Siqi Chai,Kun Li,Kerou Zhang,Xinzhou Jiang,Wei Xu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Holo-M 是首个面向人形机器人全身 loco-manipulation 的离散 VLA 模型：通过统一动作分词器将全身动作空间分解为四个身体部位相关 tokenizer，并将动作 token 加入语言模型词表，结合分组离散扩散解码实现实时控制，在 SIMPLE 基准上取得通用与专项评测最优成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基于离散动作 token 的 VLA 模型在机械臂操作上有效，但人形机器人全身动作空间（腿、躯干、臂、手）维度更高且异构，带来分词、训练和实时推理挑战，现有 VLA 方法未有效解决；同时，使用独立连续动作专家的模型存在知识隔离问题。

Method: 提出 Holo-M：1）扩展语言模型词表，将动作 token 原生纳入语言模型；2）设计统一动作分词器，将人形动作空间分解为四个身体部位特定 tokenizer——末端执行器、身体、手和运动学，以支持跨异构本体与数据源训练，包括人形遥操作、第一人称人类视频和仿真；3）为满足实时控制，采用分组离散扩散解码按身体部位解码动作 token，而非对动作 token 自回归生成。

Result: 在 SIMPLE 人形 loco-manipulation 基准上进行了大量实验，Holo-M 在通用（generalist）和专项（specialist）评测中均取得最高成功率，并显著领先第二名。

Conclusion: Holo-M 表明通过将动作 token 融入语言模型词表并结合部位分组离散扩散解码，可有效应对人形机器人高维异构全身动作空间的分词、跨本体训练与实时推理挑战；作者将开源全部代码与模型权重。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models using discrete action tokens have proven effective for controling robotic arms on manipulation tasks. For a humanoid, however, the whole-body action space -- legs, torso, arms, and hands -- is far higher-dimensional and heterogeneous, raising tokenization, training, and real-time inference challenges that the previous VLA models do not address. We present Holo-M, to our knowledge the first discrete VLA model for humanoid loco-manipulation that intrinsically exploits the language model by extending its vocabulary with action tokens. In this model, we devise a unified action tokenizer that decomposes the humanoid action space into four body-part-specific tokenizers -- end-effector, body, hand, and kinematics -- enabling training across drastically different embodiments and data sources, including humanoid teleoperation, ego-centric human video, and simulation. By extending the language model's vocabulary with these action tokens, we avoid the knowledge-insulation problem inherent to the models that use separate continuous action experts. To meet real-time control requirements, we decode each body part's action tokens through grouped discrete diffusion decoding, rather than using autoregression on the action tokens. We have conducted extensive experiments on the SIMPLE humanoid loco-manipulation benchmark, in which Holo-M achieves the highest success rates in both the generalist and specialist evaluations, leading the second best by significant margins. We will release all the code and model weights.

</details>


### [142] [DexRoam: Learning Mobile Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation from Egocentric Whole-Body Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35761)
*Rui Zhou,Yibo Yuan,Junkai Zhao,Fangyuan Zhao,Xiaoguang Zhao,Shanghang Zhang,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexRoam 提出从人类第一人称演示学习移动双臂灵巧操作，保留连续耦合的全身运动；用消费级 VR 头显与头戴立体相机无追踪器采集，并通过具身、动作语义、时间三阶段对齐映射到机器人动作空间，使标准 VLA 策略可联合学习人类与机器人演示。实验显示人类演示显著提升策略成功率，并减少机器人演示需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动双臂灵巧操作需要在一个轨迹中持续协调移动、全身运动与手指级灵巧性，导致真实机器人演示采集瓶颈严重。第一人称人类演示可扩展，但已有方法常简化人体运动，丢弃了任务依赖的细粒度耦合结构。

Method: 开发无追踪器的人类全身操作演示采集系统，仅使用消费级 VR 头显和头戴立体相机，无需外部相机或动作追踪器。提出三阶段显式对齐：具身对齐、动作语义对齐、时间对齐，将捕获的人类运动映射到机器人动作空间，保留细粒度全身运动，并支持标准 VLA 策略联合学习人类与机器人演示。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，人类演示在不同 VLA 骨干和训练范式下持续提升策略学习：GR00T N1.7 平均成功率从 29% 提升至 56%；pi0.5 从 32% 提升至 57%；仅用一半机器人演示即可匹配纯机器人训练。消融实验确认每个对齐阶段均必要。

Conclusion: 保留细粒度运动结构的人类演示，有望为可扩展的全身移动操作提供有效路径，缓解机器人演示瓶颈，并增强 VLA 策略学习。

Abstract: Mobile bimanual dexterous manipulation requires continuous coordination of locomotion, whole-body motion, and finger-level dexterity within a single trajectory, creating a severe robot demonstration bottleneck. Egocentric human demonstrations offer a scalable alternative, but prior approaches ease the transfer by simplifying human motion, discarding exactly the fine-grained, coupled structure such tasks depend on. We present DexRoam, a complete system for learning mobile bimanual dexterous manipulation from human demonstrations, in which whole-body motion remains continuous and coupled throughout the human-to-robot transfer process. To enable scalable collection of whole-body human manipulation demonstrations, we develop a tracker-free capture system using only a consumer VR headset and a head-mounted stereo camera, without external cameras or motion trackers. We then perform three explicit alignment stages---embodiment, action-semantic, and temporal---to map captured motion into the robot action space, preserving fine-grained whole-body motion and allowing human and robot demonstrations to be jointly learned by standard VLA policies. Real-world experiments with different VLA backbones show that human demonstrations consistently improve policy learning across training paradigms, raising average success from 29% to 56% on GR00T N1.7 and from 32% to 57% on pi0.5, while matching robot-only training with half the robot demonstrations. Ablations confirm that each alignment stage is necessary. These results highlight the potential of human demonstrations for scalable whole-body mobile manipulation with preserved fine-grained motion structure.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [ConflictVLA-Bench: Benchmarking Behavioral Responses of Vision-Language-Action Models to Premise Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31792)
*Liyu Hou,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ConflictVLA-Bench 基准，通过将冲突前提任务与前提一致的参照任务配对，同时评估结果与执行过程，揭示 VLA 模型在无效任务前提下普遍存在“失败坚持”（Failed Persistence）现象：即使任务失败，模型仍持续趋近原始目标、保留早期轨迹结构，说明仅凭终端失败无法判定行为脱离或拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对 VLA 模型的评估多聚焦于终端任务结果，但任务失败本身无法区分“行为脱离”（放弃原目标）与“持续追求后再出现执行错误”（Failed Persistence）。这一空白导致对模型如何响应无效任务前提的理解不足，需要能同时刻画结果与执行过程的评估方法。

Method: 构建 ConflictVLA-Bench 基准：基于 LIBERO，包含 2,826 个由提示词条件化的冲突任务，覆盖四类冲突家族、四种结构配置和两种提示条件；将冲突 rollout 与前提一致的参照 rollout 配对，联合评估任务结果与执行过程。评估 8 个 VLA 模型，并分析轨迹是否趋近原始目标、是否保留早期轨迹结构、动作幅度是否被抑制，以及显式前提检查能否带来选择性、协调性的行为变化。

Result: 在所有 8 个 VLA 模型上，无效前提使原始目标完成率至少下降 17.3 个百分点，OpenVLA 的下降达 56.2 个百分点。更关键的是，即便模型在前提一致任务上成功、在匹配的冲突任务上失败，它们仍常继续趋近原始目标、保留早期轨迹结构，且动作幅度抑制有限。显式前提检查并未稳定地产生选择性且协调的行为改变。Failed Persistence 在各评估模型中反复出现。

Conclusion: 终端失败既不能证明行为脱离，也不能证明拒绝；仅依赖结果指标不足以评估 VLA 模型。研究强调需要将执行过程纳入 VLA 评估，以识别 Failed Persistence 等现象。实验数据与更多细节见项目页面。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models perform strongly on manipulation tasks, their responses to invalid task premises remain underexplored. Existing evaluations of premise conflicts often focus on terminal task outcomes, yet task failure alone cannot distinguish behavioral disengagement from continued pursuit followed by an execution error. We call the latter pattern Failed Persistence. To study this phenomenon, we introduce ConflictVLA-Bench, which pairs conflict rollouts with premise-consistent reference rollouts and evaluates both outcomes and execution processes. Built on LIBERO, the benchmark contains 2,826 prompt-conditioned conflict tasks spanning four conflict families, four structural configurations, and two prompt conditions. Across all eight VLAs, invalid premises reduce original goal completion by at least 17.3 percentage points, with the reduction reaching 56.2 percentage points for OpenVLA. Crucially, even when models succeed on premise-consistent tasks and fail on their matched conflict tasks, they often continue to approach the original targets, retain early trajectory structure, and show limited action magnitude suppression. Failed Persistence therefore recurs across the evaluated models. Explicit premise checking does not consistently produce selective and coordinated behavioral changes. These findings show that terminal failure alone establishes neither behavioral disengagement nor refusal and that outcomes alone are insufficient for VLA evaluation. Experimental data and additional details are available on the project page: this https URL

</details>


### [144] [DriveHierarchy: A Benchmark for Diagnosing VLM Driving Capabilities from Open-Loop Understanding to Closed-Loop Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31814)
*Chengkai Xu,Jiaqi Liu,Yicheng Guo,Peng Hang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DriveHierarchy：将VLM自动驾驶评测组织为感知定位、上下文记忆、心智推理、闭环执行四个层级的分层基准，包含76,798条QA（84,279帧）的开环基准与含100个场景的闭环仿真平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM自动驾驶的评测因"驾驶能力是复合性的"而困难：一个合格系统需定位交通参与者与风险、跨视图与跨时间整合上下文、推理未来演化并在闭环交互下采取行动。现有基准要么只测开环理解，要么只测闭环驾驶，难以解释这些能力如何组织、如何关联，也无法为模型诊断与改进提供结构化依据。

Method: 提出DriveHierarchy分层基准，将VLM自动驾驶分为四个层级：感知定位（perceptual grounding）、上下文记忆（contextual memory）、心智推理（mental reasoning）、闭环执行（closed-loop execution）。实现方式为：整合多个开源自动驾驶数据集，构建统一开环基准，含76,798条问答对、覆盖84,279帧；并开发基于真实道路网络、支持交互式场景构建的闭环仿真平台，从中精选100个驾驶场景用于具身评测。

Result: 在15个VLM上的实验表明，DriveHierarchy能捕捉结构化但非冗余的能力差异，将开环理解与闭环驾驶相关联，并为诊断与基准引导的优化提供实用依据。

Conclusion: DriveHierarchy可作为评估与改进VLM自动驾驶系统的统一框架。

Abstract: Evaluating VLM-based autonomous driving remains difficult because driving competence is composite, where a capable system must ground traffic participants and hazards, integrate context across views and time, reason about future evolution, and act appropriately under closed-loop interaction. Existing benchmarks usually assess either open-loop understanding or closed-loop driving but provide limited structure for explaining how these abilities are organized, how they relate, and how they may inform model diagnosis and improvement. We present \textsc{DriveHierarchy}, a hierarchical benchmark that organizes VLM-based autonomous driving into four ranks, spanning perceptual grounding, contextual memory, mental reasoning, and closed-loop execution. To instantiate this hierarchy, we integrate multiple open-source autonomous-driving datasets into a unified open-loop benchmark with 76,798 question-answer pairs over 84,279 frames and develop a closed-loop simulation platform with interactive scenario construction on a real-world road network, from which 100 driving scenarios are curated for embodied evaluation. Experiments on 15 VLMs show that \textsc{DriveHierarchy} captures structured but non-redundant capability variation, relates open-loop understanding to closed-loop driving, and provides a practical basis for diagnosis and benchmark-guided optimization. \textsc{DriveHierarchy} therefore serves as a unified framework for evaluating and improving VLM-based autonomous driving systems. An anonymized project has been released on this https URL

</details>


### [145] [COUNTERMEM: World-Model Verified Counter-Factual Memory for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31874)
*Hongji Pu,Ruixiang Tang,Yongfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 COUNTERMEM：一个强化学习框架，通过可执行世界模型（测试、证明器、求解器）在失败动作后评估局部反事实，构建并复用"经校验的反事实记忆"，并用学习到的记忆使用策略在任务成功与交互成本间权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆框架主要通过智能体与真实世界交互来构建记忆（如记住动作反馈以改进未来任务），但很少在记忆构建时提出"what if"问题：若采取不同动作，反馈是否会变化，以及该反馈如何转化为有用记忆。直接在主动环境中获取此类反馈代价高昂，且会改变进行对比所需的状态。

Method: COUNTERMEM 在失败动作后，从原始状态的副本或重置出发，利用可执行世界模型（如测试、证明检查器、求解器）评估局部替代方案；将改进连同原始动作与纠正动作、经检查的结果及复用条件一同存储。通过一个学习到的记忆使用策略选择检索到的记录或跳过记忆，以平衡任务成功与交互成本，而基础 LLM 保持固定。在留出评估期间，记忆与策略均被冻结。

Result: 在六个领域的 12 个基准设置上评估。使用 gpt-oss-120b 时，COUNTERMEM 在全部 12 个基准上同时改进 ReAct 和 Reflexion，平均较未增强版本提升 12.6 个百分点。在两个骨干模型的四领域对比中，任务运行 token 减少 7.7–42.0%（不含离线选择器训练成本）。进一步分析表明：移除校验或持久化存储会削弱增益，而将校验后的纠正应用于不适宜的决定可能使增益反转。

Conclusion: COUNTERMEM 通过在失败后利用可执行世界模型构建经校验的反事实记忆，并配合学习到的记忆使用策略，在多个领域稳定提升 ReAct/Reflexion 的任务表现并降低交互 token 成本；消融显示校验与持久化存储是关键，且错误地复用纠正会导致性能反转。代码将在接收后发布。

Abstract: Existing agent memory frameworks mainly create memory through an agent's interaction with the factual world, e.g., remembering feedback from actions taken to improve performance on future tasks. However, these frameworks seldom ask the "what if" question during memory construction: what if a different action had been taken, would the feedback have changed, and how could this feedback become useful memory? Obtaining such feedback directly in an active environment can be expensive and can alter the state needed for comparison. In this work, we introduce COUNTERMEM, a reinforcement-learning framework for constructing and using verified counterfactual memory across tasks. After a failed action, COUNTERMEM evaluates local alternatives from a copy or reset of the original state using executable world models, such as tests, proof checkers, and solvers. It stores improvements with the original and corrected actions, checked outcomes, and conditions for reuse. A learned memory-use policy selects a retrieved record or skips memory to balance task success and interaction cost, while the base LLM remains fixed. Both memory and policy are frozen during held-out evaluation. We evaluate COUNTERMEM on 12 benchmark settings across six domains. With gpt-oss-120b, COUNTERMEM improves both ReAct and Reflexion on all 12 benchmarks across six domains, averaging a gain of 12.6 percentage points over their unaugmented versions. In the four-domain comparison across two backbones, task-run tokens decrease by 7.7-42.0%, excluding offline selector-training costs. Further analyses show that removing verification or persistent storage weakens the gains, while applying verified corrections to unsuitable decisions can reverse them. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [146] [Agentsensus: Consensus-Compressed Shared Memory for Multi-Agent Story Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32297)
*Yu Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Agentsensus：用统一长期记忆替代每个角色独立的私有记忆流，将同一事件的记录合并为所有见证者共有，并链接语义相关记录；在多世界多轮模拟中显著减少条目数，同时实现记忆共享与链接，且质量不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准智能体故事世界为每个角色配置私有记忆流，导致同一共享事件按见证者重复存储，产生严重的存储冗余。作者希望用统一、可合并、可链接的记忆机制解决这一问题。

Method: Agentsensus 是一个故事世界模拟框架，采用统一长期记忆：同一事件的记录合并为一条、由所有见证者共同拥有；语义相关的记忆记录之间建立链接。评估覆盖四个世界（两部中国古典小说、Hamlet、一个真实冲突时间线），运行 40–80 轮，并在等粒度协议下与三种按角色记忆设计对比。

Result: Agentsensus 比最接近的基线少写 22–44% 条目；是唯一实现记忆共享（14–28% 记录被多个角色持有，部分被 10 个角色持有）和链接（94–99%）的设计；模拟质量与基线相当甚至更好。消融实验表明合并机制是关键：禁用后存储膨胀 3.1 倍，共享率降为 0。共享还随模拟时长累积，在 10、20、40 轮下从 6% 升至 9% 再升至 14%。

Conclusion: 统一且可合并、可链接的长期记忆能有效降低共享事件重复存储，形成跨角色的记忆共享与语义链接，并随模拟周期增长而持续获益，同时不损害模拟质量。

Abstract: A agentic story world is a dynamic system simulating who learned what, when, and from whom -- yet the standard design gives each character a private memory stream. A shared event is therefore stored once per witness, large duplication will be incurred in terms of storage. We present Agentsensus, a story-world simulation framework in which there is an unified long-term memory. Records of the same event merge into one owned by all its witnesses, and semantically relevant memory records are linked. We evaluate on four worlds -- two classical Chinese novels, Hamlet, and a real-world conflict timeline -- run for 40 to 80 rounds against three per-character memory designs under an equal-granularity protocol. Agentsensus writes 22-44% fewer entries than the closest baseline and is the only design whose memory becomes shared (14-28% of records held by more than one character, some by 10) and linked (94-99%), at judged simulation quality indistinguishable or even better than the baselines. An ablation attributes this to the merge itself: disabling it multiplies the store by 3.1x and takes sharing to exactly zero. Sharing also compounds with the horizon rather than saturating early, rising 6% to 9% to 14% as one world is re-run at 10, 20 and 40 rounds.

</details>


### [147] [Carnator: Fast Text-to-Video Generation with Generation-Native Compatibility-Guided Cross-Request Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32420)
*Xingkun Yin,Xuebin Tang,Mingkun Xu,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Carnator 提出一种面向视频扩散 Transformer 的跨请求加速框架：通过轻量早期探针从模型内部扩散状态构建 Early Signature，以风险感知的兼容性决策判断复用有效性，并据此定位目标特定计算，联合复用历史潜轨迹与稀疏注意力连接。在三个文生视频骨干上实现最高 2.17× 端到端加速，同时保持有竞争力的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散 Transformer 虽能生成高质量视频，但迭代去噪带来显著推理延迟，限制交互式与大规模服务。现有加速方法多面向单个请求，仅能利用单条生成轨迹内部的冗余；跨请求复用虽提供额外节省，但现有方法常以粗粒度语义相似度推断可复用性，将“语义相关”与“生成级计算兼容”混为一谈：激进的复用可能接受不兼容的历史计算，保守的复用又错失大量加速机会。

Method: 提出 Carnator 跨请求加速框架，直接从模型不断演化的内部状态中提取生成原生的兼容性证据。具体而言：先执行轻量早期探针，从内部扩散状态构建 Early Signature；再通过风险感知的兼容性决策评估复用有效性；并用同一证据刻画复用范围，定位目标特定计算，指导历史潜轨迹与稀疏注意力连接的联合复用。

Result: 在三个文本到视频骨干模型上，Carnator 在缓存接受更选择性条件下，仍持续获得比所评估的跨请求基线更高的缓存命中端到端加速，最高达到 2.17× 加速，并保持有竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: 以生成原生兼容性证据替代粗粒度语义相似度，可更准确判断跨请求复用的有效性与范围，从而实现更选择性但更高效的跨请求复用，在显著加速视频扩散推理的同时维持生成质量。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers produce high-quality videos, yet iterative denoising incurs substantial inference latency, limiting interactive and large-scale serving. Most existing acceleration methods focus on individual requests, thereby restricting efficiency gains to redundancy within a single generation trajectory. Recent cross-request reuse offers an additional source of savings, but existing approaches often infer reusability from coarse semantic similarity. This conflates semantic relatedness with generation-level computational compatibility, so aggressive reuse may accept incompatible historical computation while conservative reuse leaves substantial acceleration unrealized. We present \emph{Carnator}, a cross-request acceleration framework that addresses this challenge by extracting and using generation-native compatibility evidence directly from the model's evolving internal states. Specifically, \emph{Carnator} performs a lightweight early probe to construct an Early Signature from internal diffusion states, assessing reuse validity through risk-aware compatibility decisions. The same evidence characterizes reuse scope by localizing target-specific computation and guiding joint reuse of historical latent trajectories and sparse attention connectivity. Across three text-to-video backbones, Carnator consistently achieves higher cache-hit end-to-end acceleration than the evaluated cross-request baselines despite more selective cache acceptance, reaching up to 2.17$\times$ speedup while maintaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [148] [Are Vision-Language-Action Models Robust to One-Step Observation Perturbations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32550)
*Shojiro Yamabe,Jun Sakuma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作研究VLA模型对"单步观测扰动"的鲁棒性，发现扰动影响与动作块执行长度相关，并提出CARE：根据与历史预测动作块的一致性动态选择执行长度，在低计算开销下提升鲁棒性并保持干净性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型要在物理世界部署，必须理解其安全风险。现有安全研究主要关注在整个episode中持续施加的观测扰动，而仅在episode内短暂出现的"瞬时观测损坏"（momentary observation corruption）仍是被忽视的安全威胁，因此需要补足这一空白。

Method: 作者考察每个episode仅在单个时间步施加一步扰动（one-step perturbation）的鲁棒性，并分析其影响与动作块执行长度（action chunk execution length）的关系；据此提出CARE，通过判断当前预测动作块与先前预测动作块的一致性，动态选择执行长度：仅在检测到扰动时采用较短的执行长度。

Result: 实验表明，单步扰动会显著降低VLA模型性能，且其影响程度取决于动作块执行长度。CARE能够以较低计算开销提升鲁棒性，同时在无扰动（干净）条件下保持原有性能。

Conclusion: 瞬时单步扰动是VLA部署中不可忽视的安全威胁；通过一致性驱动的动态执行长度选择，CARE在低开销下实现鲁棒性与干净性能之间的权衡，为VLA安全部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Understanding the safety risks of vision-language-action (VLA) models is essential for their deployment in the physical world. Existing safety research has mainly considered persistent perturbations that are applied continuously to observations throughout an episode. However, momentary observation corruption, in which observations are severely perturbed only briefly within an episode, remains an underexplored safety threat. To address this gap, this work investigates robustness to one-step perturbations applied at a single time step per episode. Our experiments reveal that these perturbations substantially degrade VLA performance and that their impact depends on the action chunk execution length. Based on them, we propose CARE, which dynamically selects the execution length based on consistency with the previously predicted action chunk. CARE improves robustness with low computational overhead while preserving clean performance by selecting shorter execution lengths only under perturbations.

</details>


### [149] [World Models with Predictable Long-Horizon Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32657)
*Yuhao Du,Shunian Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出“单步预测准确”并不能保证世界模型长程 rollout 保持数据分布；作者通过固定高斯参考分布的显式解码器与行为平均转移约束，构建可精确保持参考占用的联合状态—动作转移，并在像素控制任务上验证其长程分布稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型常以单步预测精度为优化目标，但即使单步准确，模型多步 rollout 仍可能偏离数据分布，导致长程采样不稳定、链条发散。因此需要区分并显式控制三类性质：模型趋近的分布、趋近速率，以及所学条件动力学。

Method: 作者将解码后的平稳分布显式化：学习一个固定高斯参考的解码器，并约束行为平均转移保持该参考分布。针对受控系统，联合转移通过条件动作图（conditional action chart）保持行为占用，而不要求每个固定动作下不变。利用联合状态—动作旋转和平行高斯噪声，得到可精确保持参考分布且条件密度可处理的转移。理论方面，从有限初始化样本库推导绝对收敛界，并用条件动作空间散度度量对参考分布的偏离。

Result: 在 12 个控制任务、216 个拟合像素检查点上，带参考混合的占用模型在全部 36 个“任务—随机种子”单元中均使每条评估链在 10^5 步内保持；rollout 与参考分布的能量统计差为 -0.0002±0.0003（训练种子标准误）。四个非保持性对照臂均出现链丢失，尽管高斯对照臂在 10 步短期预测上更准确；离线 DreamerV3 参考基线也取得更好的短时程精度。

Conclusion: 结果强调，世界模型评估不能只看短时程预测误差：应分别考察其趋近的目标分布、趋近速率以及条件动力学质量。所提占用保持机制能显著改善长程 rollout 的分布稳定性，即使短期精度并非最优。

Abstract: Accurate one-step predictions do not ensure that a world model's rollouts retain the data distribution. We make the model's decoded stationary law explicit by learning a decoder of a fixed Gaussian reference and constraining the behaviour-averaged transition to preserve that reference. For controlled systems, a joint transition uses a conditional action chart to preserve behaviour occupancy without requiring invariance at each fixed action. Joint state--action rotations and parallel Gaussian noise give an exactly preserving transition with a tractable conditional density. We derive an absolute convergence bound from finite initialization banks and control departure from the reference through conditional action-space divergence. Across $216$ fitted pixel checkpoints on twelve control tasks, the occupancy model with a reference mixture retains every evaluated chain at $10^5$ steps in all $36$ task--seed cells, with a rollout-minus-reference energy-statistic difference of $-0.0002\pm0.0003$ (training-seed standard error). Each of the four nonpreserving comparison arms loses chains, although the Gaussian arm is more accurate at ten steps. An offline DreamerV3 reference also achieves better short-horizon accuracy. These results distinguish three properties of a world model: the distribution it approaches, the rate of approach, and the conditional dynamics it learns.

</details>


### [150] [World Agent: Can Language Models Keep a World Running?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32692)
*Weixing Chen,Weipeng Zhang,Nan An,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"世界智能体任务（world agent task）"，将世界生成的评测点从"交付时刻"后移到"持续运行"阶段：模型不负责生成世界，而负责让世界持续运转，即协调事件并将其后果向后续演化传递。作者据此构建 WorldAgent-Benchmark，包含维护（maintenance）与推演（deduction）两条互补赛道，用 LLM 语义判断结合程序化校验、以及纯程序化评测来度量世界的持续运行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型正从生成逼真画面转向生成可玩世界，但"交付的世界能否持续运行"尚无评测。现有评测分别止步于生成、交付或单步转移，各停在不同环节；局部状态转移或中间结果正确，并不能保证因果事件流被正确组织。

Method: 提出 world agent task：模型需保持世界运行，协调事件并将其后果向前传递以约束后续演化。在 WorldAgent-Benchmark 中实例化为两条赛道：（1）维护赛道——将连续叙事中的事件落地为显式世界状态的正确转移，并满足因果、时序与并发约束；（2）推演赛道——在部分可观测下预测世界演化并朝目标行动。维护赛道结合 LLM 辅助语义判断与程序化校验和打分，推演赛道完全程序化评测；每条判断可对照世界状态与执行日志审计，分数可由保存的判断与执行记录重算。

Result: 在 8 个模型上，随着世界中被移除的预置结构增多，得分持续下降；因果关联检查是所有模型最薄弱的环节。

Conclusion: 该基准使"世界持续运行"变得可度量，并能区分"局部完成"与"事件组织失败"；代码与数据集将随论文发布。

Abstract: World models are moving from generating realistic frames to generating playable worlds, yet whether a delivered world can keep running is not tested anywhere. Existing evaluations stop at generation, at delivery, or at single-step transitions, and each stops at a different point along the way. Correct local state transitions or intermediate outcomes do not guarantee a correctly organized causal event flow. We propose the world agent task, which moves the evaluation point of world generation from the moment of delivery to the continued operation that follows. In this task, a model is not asked to generate a world. It is held responsible for keeping the world running, which requires coordinating events and carrying forward their consequences to constrain subsequent evolution. We instantiate the task in WorldAgent-Benchmark with two complementary tracks. In the maintenance track, the model must ground the events of a continuous narrative into correct transitions of the explicit world state while respecting causal, temporal, and concurrency constraints. In the deduction track, the model must predict how the world will evolve under partial observations and act toward a goal. The maintenance track combines LLM-assisted semantic judgments with programmatic validation and scoring, while the deduction track is evaluated entirely programmatically. Individual judgments are auditable against world states and execution logs, and scores can be recomputed from the saved judgments and execution records. Across 8 models, scores decline steadily as pre-built structure is removed from the world, and causal-relation checking is the weakest component for every model. The benchmark makes the continued operation of a world measurable and distinguishes local completion from failures in event organization. Code and dataset will be released on this https URL .

</details>


### [151] [Mandela-Bench: Multimodal Models Remember Canonical Images Instead of Seeing Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32763)
*Yicheng Bao,Zhenkun Gao,Xiahui Guo,Mingqian Yang,Xueheng Li,Bangwei Liu,Mingang Chen,Lijun Li,Xuhong Wang,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Mandela-Bench 基准，用于检验多模态模型能否用自身世界知识识别"知识型伪造"的经典图像；结果显示 36 个模型普遍存在"识别偏置"——模型倾向相信记忆中的经典图像而非实际观察到的编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图像编辑已能通过单条指令无缝篡改经典照片，往往不留像素级痕迹，唯一线索可能是"图像所描绘的事实"本身。现有基准依赖生成器伪影、图文不一致、视觉不合理性或外部参照，无法检验模型能否运用自身世界知识去验证一张被识别的图像，因此存在评测空白。

Method: 构建 Mandela-Bench，包含对经典图像的 1,507 处编辑：1,359 处"纯知识型伪造"（每处违反一个可验证事实）、148 处无锚点对照（保留编辑过程但不引入事实矛盾），以及 474 张未改动原图。评测同时关注模型是否检测出伪造，以及其解释是否能指出被插入的实体或所违反的事实。在 36 个多模态模型（从 0.8B 参数到前沿规模）上进行测试，并开展多项对照实验（隔离展示替换人脸、提供真实事件与日期、裁剪掉可识别构图后再提供信息、显式验证提示等）。

Result: 发现一致的失败模式：当从熟悉照片中移除某公众人物时，模型在高达 72.7% 的回复中仍会说出该人物。部分模型在孤立展示时能区分替换人脸与原始人脸，却仍将完整编辑后的照片判为真实。提供真实事件与日期不能提升基于知识的检测，而在裁剪掉可识别构图后再提供相同信息则可以。即使在显式验证提示下，36 个模型中也仅有一个能在至少一半伪造图像上满足知识型识别（KGR）标准。对照设定（无锚点对照与未改动原图）用于排除单纯感知或流程因素。

Conclusion: 这些失败不能仅用知识缺失或感知不足来解释，而与"识别过程将验证偏向记忆中的经典图像、而非实际观察到的编辑"这一机制相符。即模型的世界知识被用于"回忆"而非"核验"，构成了知识型伪造检测中的系统性偏置。

Abstract: Historical photographs and other canonical images can now be edited seamlessly with a single instruction, often leaving no reliable pixel-level trace. In such cases, the only evidence of manipulation may be a fact about what the image depicts. Existing benchmarks instead rely on generator artefacts, image-caption inconsistencies, visual implausibilities, or external references, and therefore do not test whether a model can use its own world knowledge to verify a recognized image. We introduce Mandela-Bench, containing 1,507 edits of canonical images: 1,359 knowledge-only forgeries, each contradicting one verifiable fact, and 148 anchor-free controls that preserve the editing process without introducing a factual contradiction, together with 474 untouched originals. We score not only whether a model detects a forgery, but whether its explanation identifies the inserted entity or the fact being violated. Across 36 multimodal models, from 0.8B parameters to frontier scale, we find a consistent failure mode. When a public figure is removed from a familiar photograph, models still name that person in up to 72.7% of responses. Some models can distinguish the replacement face from the original when shown in isolation, yet still judge the full edited photograph as authentic. Providing the true event and date does not improve knowledge-grounded detection, whereas providing the same information after cropping away the recognizable composition does. Even under explicit verification prompts, only one of the 36 models meets the KGR criterion on at least half of the forged images. These results suggest that the failures cannot be explained by missing knowledge or inadequate perception alone. Instead, they are consistent with recognition biasing verification toward the remembered canonical image rather than the observed edit.

</details>


### [152] [Precision As You Need: Stochastic Computing Is a Dense Adaptive Quantizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32922)
*Haoran Jin,Kangqi Zhang,Jirong Yang,Barry Lyu,Qiuyi Ding,Ruijie Gao,Nathan Bleier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出以随机计算（SC）作为视觉Transformer的稠密自适应量化底座，通过GPU库在图像分类、检测、分割、生成与视觉世界模型规划等任务上验证：在同等比特预算下SC可与固定格式INT量化竞争，且逐行动态混合精度可在更低平均流长度下保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉Transformer的推理开销主要由矩阵乘法主导，但现有后训练量化与混合精度推理受限于加速器支持的传统固定位宽格式（INT4、INT8、BF16、FP16），难以实现细粒度、连续可调的精度分配。

Method: 将随机计算（SC）视为稠密自适应量化器：其精度由比特流长度L控制而非固定数据通路，每次乘法可简化为单个AND/XNOR门。作者构建GPU库，大规模模拟SC矩阵乘法，将流长度作为一等内核参数；并设计逐行/逐token或分组的动态混合精度策略，在匹配平均预算下分配流长度，无需重训练，且不同调度复用同一SC硬件。

Result: 在图像分类、目标检测、实例分割、类条件图像生成和视觉世界模型规划等端到端任务上，SC在匹配比特预算下与固定格式INT量化保持竞争力；逐行混合精度策略在更低平均流长度下仍有助于维持精度。

Conclusion: 结果从软件层面提供了可行性证据：SC可作为现代视觉Transformer上进行细粒度混合精度推理的稠密精度基底，突破传统固定位宽格式的限制。

Abstract: Matrix multiplications dominate the inference cost of modern transformer-based vision models, yet existing efficiency techniques such as post-training quantization and mixed-precision inference are largely limited to the small set of fixed-width formats (INT4, INT8, BF16, and FP16) supported by conventional accelerators. We revisit stochastic computing (SC) as a way to lift this constraint: viewed as a dense adaptive quantizer, SC controls precision by bit-stream length L rather than a fixed datapath, while each multiplication reduces to a single AND/XNOR gate. We build a GPU library that emulates SC matrix multiplication at scale, exposes stream lengths as first-class kernel arguments, and evaluates SC end-to-end on image classification, object detection and instance segmentation, class-conditional image generation, and visual world-model planning. On top of this substrate, we develop a dynamic per-row mixed-precision policy that assigns stream length per token or group at matched average budget, requires no retraining, and uses the same SC hardware across schedules. Across tasks, SC remains competitive with fixed-format INT quantization at matched bit budgets, while per-row mixed precision helps maintain accuracy at lower average stream lengths. These results provide software-level feasibility evidence that SC can serve as a dense-precision substrate for fine-grained mixed-precision inference on modern vision transformers.

</details>


### [153] [ActiveMem: Dynamic Latent Memory Trees for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33244)
*Song-Li Wu,Jingyi Wang,Zhaocheng Du,Weinan Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ActiveMem——一种分层记忆框架，将智能体经验递归组织为依赖感知的隐式执行树，通过显式保留执行转移实现连贯的推理路径检索，并利用强化学习学习记忆的扩展、检索与剪枝策略，在多个智能体基准上提升了任务完成度、推理稳定性与记忆效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体依赖外部记忆支持长时程推理与决策，但其记忆系统通常将历史轨迹或摘要作为独立上下文片段进行检索，忽视了多步执行背后的过程性依赖。随着记忆规模扩大，这种扁平化检索会引入上下文碎片化与跨任务干扰，导致推理轨迹在结构上不一致。

Method: 提出 ActiveMem 分层记忆框架：递归地将智能体经验组织为依赖感知的隐式执行树；将轨迹抽象为可复用的子任务节点，同时显式保留执行转移，从而以当前执行状态为条件进行连贯的推理路径检索；并进一步通过强化学习学习动态的记忆扩展、检索与剪枝策略以支持持续适应。

Result: 在多种智能体基准上的实验表明，ActiveMem 在任务完成度、推理稳定性与记忆效率方面均持续优于现有基于记忆的智能体；此外，它使紧凑的开源权重模型能够取得与规模大得多的专有系统相竞争的性能。

Conclusion: 通过依赖感知的分层记忆组织与基于强化学习的记忆策略，ActiveMem 有效缓解了扁平化检索带来的上下文碎片化与跨任务干扰，实现了更连贯的推理路径检索，为 LLM 智能体的长时程记忆提供了一种高效且可持续适应的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external memory to support long-horizon reasoning and decision making. Existing memory systems typically retrieve historical trajectories or summaries as independent context fragments, overlooking the procedural dependencies underlying multi-step execution. As memory scales, such flat retrieval introduces context fragmentation and cross-task interference, leading to structurally inconsistent reasoning trajectories. We propose ActiveMem, a hierarchical memory framework that recursively organizes agent experiences into dependency-aware latent execution trees. ActiveMem abstracts trajectories into reusable subtask nodes while explicitly preserving execution transitions, enabling coherent reasoning-path retrieval conditioned on the current execution state. To support continual adaptation, ActiveMem further learns dynamic memory expansion, retrieval, and pruning policies through reinforcement learning. Experiments across various agent benchmarks demonstrate that ActiveMem consistently improves task completion, reasoning stability, and memory efficiency over existing memory-based agents. Moreover, ActiveMem enables compact open-weight models to achieve competitive performance with substantially larger proprietary systems.

</details>


### [154] [CompoWorld: Compositional Environment Scaling for General Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33665)
*Xiao-Wen Yang,Weiyi Xu,Wen Da,Hang Xu,Canwei Li,Hong-Jie You,Pusen Dong,Yucheng Zeng,Zhaokai Luo,Yu-Feng Li,Yao Hu,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CompoWorld：通过组合可复用服务与依赖图随机游走，自动生成跨服务任务，并用 SFT 与完成度导向奖励 RL 训练通用智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动生成环境主要在单一环境内生成任务，而真实工作流需要智能体跨多个服务连接信息与动作，因此任务空间和交互复杂度受限。

Method: 1）构建有限的可复用服务库，由编码智能体将工具规范转化为带类型状态和共享接口的已验证服务；2）对无法可靠实现的工具使用世界模型处理；3）通过随机游走在依赖图上连接服务，生成并验证需要跨服务信息流动的任务；4）用已验证轨迹进行监督微调 SFT；5）提出 Completion-Focused Rubric Reward，在 RL 中强调同一 rollout 组内通过率较低的评价标准，引导完整任务完成。共构建 448 个服务、暴露 10,130 个工具，并用 3K SFT 轨迹和 1K RL 任务训练 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B。

Result: 在八个基准上平均比骨干模型提升 9.17 分；在 AutomationBench 上超过 Claude Opus 4.6 等前沿模型，并领先所有对比的 35B-A3B 智能体专用模型。

Conclusion: 组合式环境扩展能够以可扩展方式生成跨服务任务，并通过 SFT 与完成度导向 RL 显著提升通用智能体的跨服务任务完成能力。

Abstract: Automatically generated environments provide a scalable source of interaction data for training general agents. However, existing approaches mainly generate tasks within a single environment, while real-world workflows require agents to connect information and actions across multiple services. We introduce Compositional Environment Scaling (\textbf{CompoWorld}), which expands the task space by composing a finite library of reusable services. Coding agents turn tool specifications into verified services with typed states and shared interfaces, while a world model handles tools that cannot be reliably implemented. A random-walk procedure connects services through dependency graphs, enabling the generation and verification of tasks that require information to flow across services. Verified trajectories support supervised fine-tuning (SFT), while our Completion-Focused Rubric Reward guides reinforcement learning (RL) toward full task completion by emphasizing criteria with lower pass rates within each rollout group. We construct 448 services exposing 10,130 tools and use 3K SFT trajectories and 1K RL tasks to train Qwen3.6-35B-A3B. Experimental results show that CompoWorld improves on its backbone by 9.17 points on average across eight benchmarks. On AutomationBench, it surpasses frontier models such as Claude Opus 4.6 and leads all compared agent-specialized 35B-A3B models.

</details>


### [155] [When Does Selection Replace Extraction? A Pre-Registered Test of Agent Memory with a Typed Decision Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34227)
*Rishabh Sharma,Rishika Lall*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在预注册的 LoCoMo 与 LongMemEval 留出集上，紧预算下由 Jev 单次调用选出的原始对话轮次，在准确率上不劣于基于 LLM 抽取事实的记忆，且写入成本低约 3061 倍；重排序收益随预算增大而衰减，这解释了既有研究结论的分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究对“对话记忆是否需要 LLM 抽取事实，还是选好原始轮次即可”结论不一致；抽取式系统称蒸馏事实有增益，近期研究认为排序良好的原始历史同样有效，但对排序是否重要仍有分歧。本文旨在通过预注册、留出集实验厘清这一争议及其适用条件。

Method: 在 LoCoMo 与 LongMemEval 的留出对话上进行预注册研究。比较两类记忆：LLM 抽取事实式记忆，与由单次调用类型化决策模型 Jev 选出的原始轮次。在紧预算与宽松预算下评估；采用盲人工评分、第二个回答模型做稳健性检验；分析重排序在不同预算下的增益；并在匹配上下文条件下，将 Jev 与 LLM 重排序器及多调用图遍历方法比较，同时考察正确弃答率。

Result: LoCoMo 紧预算下，Jev 选择的原始轮次对 LLM 抽取式记忆达到非劣效（单侧 95% 下界 -3.0 分，非劣效边界 -5 分）；盲人工评分缩小差距但不改变结论。原始轮次写入成本低 3061 倍，且更换回答模型后结论仍成立。重排序增益随预算增加而缩小：保留 30 个候选中的 3 个时，LoCoMo 提升 17.4 分、LongMemEval 提升 9.1 分；预算宽松时仅提升 1.5 与 1.1 分，且抽取式系统更准确。匹配上下文下，Jev 与 LLM 重排序器准确率非劣（下界 -2.0），延迟约为其三分之一，并优于多调用图遍历；重排序会降低正确弃答率。

Conclusion: 在紧预算下，选好原始轮次即可媲美甚至非劣于 LLM 抽取式记忆，且成本显著更低；但预算宽松时抽取式方法更优。重排序收益随预算增大而衰减，可解释文献中看似矛盾的结论。Jev 在匹配上下文下以更低延迟达到 LLM 重排序器水平，并优于多调用图遍历。作者发布了计划、代码与评分答案。

Abstract: Does conversational memory need LLM-extracted facts, or is selecting the right raw turns enough? Published results disagree. Extraction-based systems report gains from distilled facts. Recent studies find raw history with good ranking does as well, but disagree about whether ranking matters. We ran a pre-registered study on held-out LoCoMo conversations and LongMemEval. At a tight budget on LoCoMo, raw turns selected by a single call to Jev, a typed decision model, are non-inferior to an LLM-extraction memory (one-sided 95% bound -3.0 points against a -5-point margin). Blind human grading narrows the margin but does not change the result. Raw turns cost 3,061 times less to write, and the result holds with a second answer model. Within this study, reranking's gain shrinks as the budget grows. It adds 17.4 points on LoCoMo and 9.1 on LongMemEval when three of 30 candidates are kept. At generous budgets it adds 1.5 and 1.1, and extraction systems are more accurate. This suggests why published results disagree. At matched context, Jev selects as accurately as an LLM reranker (non-inferiority bound -2.0) at a third of the latency, and more accurately than a multi-call graph traversal. Reranking lowers correct abstention. Plans, code and graded answers are released.

</details>


### [156] [Does Adversarial Training Improve Generalization in Multi-View VLAs? Revealing and Mitigating View Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33707)
*Futa Waseda,Shuhei Kurita,Isao Echizen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究多视角VLA在对预训练VLM进行对抗训练(AT)后的分布偏移泛化能力，发现AT存在"视角崩溃"失效模式并区分了鲁棒感知与鲁棒融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将预训练VLM的感知与语义能力迁移至动作预测，在分布内表现优异，但在部署偏移下往往性能下降。对抗训练(AT)是一种无需预先针对具体偏移的模型自适应鲁棒性方法，但其对多视角VLA自然分布偏移泛化性的影响尚不明确，本文旨在研究该问题。

Method: 使用一个直接从预训练VLM适配而来的多视角VLA，在LIBERO-Plus的七个偏移轴上评估泛化性。进行直接AT实验；并通过受控视角干预（View Swap）来检验跨视角依赖关系，随后在View Swap下重新评估AT效果。

Result: 直接AT显著改善仅影响第三人称视角的两个偏移(相机视角与传感器噪声)，但对其他偏移效果混杂甚至为负。受控视角干预揭示了"视角崩溃"(view collapse)：直接AT会过度改变跨视角依赖，使策略被手腕视角主导，暴露出"鲁棒性捷径"——对某偏移视角的表面鲁棒可能源于减少对该视角的使用，而非感知更鲁棒。引入简单的View Swap干预后，AT进一步改善相机视角、传感器噪声与机器人初始状态，但对其他偏移仍效果混杂。

Conclusion: 多视角鲁棒性需要区分"鲁棒感知"(在视角内偏移下提取可靠信息)与"鲁棒融合"(根据各视角可靠性调整依赖)。AT提供的是选择性而非通用的分布偏移收益；View Swap可减少固定视角依赖。研究结果强调需将感知改进与跨视角依赖变化分离考量。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models adapt pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) for closed-loop robot control, transferring their perceptual and semantic capabilities to action prediction. Despite strong in-distribution performance, however, VLAs often degrade under deployment shifts. Adversarial training (AT) offers a model-adaptive approach to robustness without explicitly anticipating individual shifts, but its effect on natural distribution-shift generalization in multi-view VLAs remains unclear. We study this question using a multi-view VLA directly adapted from a pretrained VLM and evaluate generalization across seven LIBERO-Plus shift axes. Direct AT substantially improves Camera Viewpoint and Sensor Noise, the two shifts affecting only the third-person view, yet produces mixed or negative effects on other shifts. Controlled view interventions reveal a surprising failure mode that we term view collapse: Direct AT can shift cross-view reliance so strongly that the policy becomes dominated by the wrist view. This exposes a \textit{robustness shortcut}: apparent robustness to a shifted view can arise from reduced use of that view rather than more robust perception of it. This motivates a distinction between robust perception, extracting reliable information under within-view shifts, and robust fusion, adapting reliance across views according to their reliability. To reduce fixed view reliance, we use a simple View Swap intervention and then re-evaluate AT. With View Swap, AT further improves Camera Viewpoint, Sensor Noise, and Robot Initial State, while its effects remain mixed on other shifts. Our results show that multi-view robustness requires separating improved perception from changes in cross-view reliance, and that AT provides selective rather than generic distribution-shift benefits.

</details>


### [157] [Self-Designed Evaluators and Warm Memory for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33717)
*Saeid Asgari,Emre Kiciman,Leonardo de Oliveira Nunes,Ranveer Chandra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SelfSuite 让工具型语言模型智能体仅凭世界公开材料，用自身基础模型设计一套带权评判器与任务简报的评测套件并冻结，用于门控"保留最优"重试并标注带类型、追踪结果的内存；在 tau2-bench 与 AppWorld 上，无标签即超越朴素智能体、在 tau2-bench 上追平使用十个人工标签的方法，在 AppWorld 上不及 ACE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长任务流中部署的工具型语言模型智能体没有奖励信号，因而无法判断是否成功、无法安全重试，也无法为其需要改进的经验打标签。核心痛点是缺乏监督信号（无奖励、无标签），导致难以自我评估与持续改进。

Method: SelfSuite 框架：让智能体自身的基础模型仅依据世界的公开材料，自行设计一个小型评测套件（由带权评判器和有依据的逐任务简报组成），随后将其冻结，并用于两处：一是门控"保留最优（keep-best）"的重试机制，二是标注一个带类型、追踪结果（outcome-tracked）的内存。还设置了消融实验（ablation campaign）、引入模拟领域专家为每个世界标注十个入职任务用于校准评测器或为 ACE 预热内存、并在第二模型族上做单次运行研究。

Result: 在 tau2-bench 与 AppWorld 的五次重复匹配基准上：SelfSuite 无任何标签即高于朴素智能体，在 tau2-bench 上追平使用十个人工标签的方法，在 AppWorld 上落后于 ACE（后者因代码执行可直接获得成功信号）。消融实验显示其每次重复都高于无标签 ACE，且被门控的第二次尝试是唯一每次重复移除都会带来损害的组件。用十个/世界的专家标签校准评测器可带来小幅且一致的提升；用这些标签为 ACE 预热内存则使 ACE 提升至与校准后的 SelfSuite 打平。第二模型族单次运行研究显示相同排序。

Conclusion: 智能体可利用自身基础模型配合公共材料自建冻结评测套件，在无奖励、无标签条件下实现可靠的自我评估、门控重试与内存标注，性能接近或达到依赖专家标签的方法；其中门控的第二次尝试是关键组件，且其有效性在多个模型族与重复实验中保持一致。

Abstract: A tool-using language-model agent deployed over a long stream of tasks receives no reward, so it cannot tell whether it succeeded, cannot safely retry, and cannot label the experience it needs to improve. We present SelfSuite, in which the agent's own base model, given only the world's public materials, designs a small evaluation suite of weighted judges and grounded per-task briefs, freezes it, and uses it to gate a keep-best retry and to label a typed, outcome-tracked memory. On matched five-repeat benchmarks over tau2-bench and AppWorld, SelfSuite scores above the plain agent without any labels, matches methods given ten expert labels on tau2-bench, and trails Agentic Context Engineering (ACE) on AppWorld, where code execution gives a direct success signal. In an ablation campaign run on the same tasks, it is above label-free ACE in every repeat, and the gated second attempt is the only component whose removal hurts in every repeat. We also simulate a subject-matter expert who grades ten onboarding tasks per world. Using those labels to calibrate SelfSuite's evaluator gives a small, consistent gain, and using them to warm up ACE's memory lifts ACE to tie calibrated SelfSuite. A single-run study on a second model family shows the same ordering.

</details>


### [158] [Jev in Medicine: A Benchmark Evaluation. Preliminary Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34024)
*Alfredo Madrid-García,Beatriz Merino-Barbancho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Jev 是一种非生成式“系统一”模型，只能在预定义答案选项中分配概率。研究在四个医学基准上评估其准确性与校准性，并与 GPT-6 Sol 对比：Jev 速度快、成本低，在 PubMedQA 上与前沿 LLM 相当，但在考试题和复杂诊断病例上明显落后，临床使用前需任务特异性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jev 作为非生成式“系统一”模型，只能在预定义选项间分配概率，无法回答选项之外的问题；其在医学问答与基于病例的诊断推理任务上的准确性和校准性尚不明确。

Method: 评估 Jev 1.13 在四个医学基准上的表现：MetaMedQA、PubMedQA、DiagnosisArena-MCQ 和 NEJM Case Challenges。参考模型为 GPT-6 Sol，设置中等推理与无推理两种模式。主要结局为 top-1 准确率；关键次要结局为校准、选择性预测和对不可回答问题的识别。共 8,469 次请求。

Result: 全部 8,469 次请求均返回有效答案。Jev 在 PubMedQA 上准确率与 GPT-6 Sol（中等推理）相近（78.4% vs 78.2%）；在 MetaMedQA 上较低（74.8% vs 82.7%）；在 DiagnosisArena-MCQ（59.8% vs 82.4%）和 NEJM 病例（61.8% vs 82.4%）上明显更低。MetaMedQA 上 Jev 概率校准最佳（期望校准误差 0.063 vs 0.146），概率≥0.9 的答案（占 52.9% 题目）准确率为 93.4%，但 GPT-6 Sol 在接受相似比例题目时同样准确。DiagnosisArena-MCQ 上 Jev 的概率区分度较差（AUROC 0.645 vs 0.768）。在 162 个正确答案为“我不知道或无法回答”的题目中，Jev 选择该选项的比例为 10.5%（GPT-6 Sol 为 8.6%）。中位延迟为 0.27–0.31 秒；全部 2,823 个项目成本为 0.08 美元。

Conclusion: Jev 快速且成本低，在研究摘要类任务上的准确率与前沿 LLM 相近，但在考试题上较低，在复杂诊断病例上明显更低。临床使用前需要进行任务特异性验证。

Abstract: Jev is a non-generative "System One" model that assigns probabilities to predefined answer options and cannot answer outside them. Its accuracy and calibration on medical question-answering and case-based diagnostic-reasoning tasks are unknown. We evaluated Jev 1.13 on four medical benchmarks: MetaMedQA, PubMedQA, DiagnosisArena-MCQ and the NEJM Case Challenges. GPT-6 Sol, with (medium) and without reasoning, was the reference. The primary outcome was top-1 accuracy; key secondary outcomes were calibration, selective prediction and recognition of unanswerable questions. All 8,469 requests returned a valid answer. Jev's accuracy was similar to that of GPT-6 Sol with medium reasoning on PubMedQA (78.4% vs 78.2%;), lower on MetaMedQA (74.8% vs 82.7%) and much lower on DiagnosisArena-MCQ (59.8% vs 82.4%;) and the NEJM cases (61.8% vs 82.4%). On MetaMedQA, Jev's probabilities were the best calibrated (expected calibration error 0.063 vs 0.146), and its answers with a probability of at least 0.9 (52.9% of questions) were 93.4% accurate, but GPT-6 Sol was as accurate when it accepted a similar proportion of questions. On DiagnosisArena-MCQ, Jev's probabilities discriminated poorly (AUROC 0.645 vs 0.768). Of the 162 questions whose correct answer was "I don't know or cannot answer", Jev chose that option for 10.5% (GPT-6 Sol, 8.6%). Median latency was 0.27-0.31 s; all 2,823 items cost USD 0.08. Jev was fast and inexpensive, and its accuracy was similar to that of a frontier LLM on research abstracts but lower on examination questions and much lower on complex diagnostic cases. Task-specific validation is required before clinical use.

</details>


### [159] [GenoMorph: Pathway-Grounded Genomic Disease Reasoning via Adaptive Latent Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34079)
*Tanmoy Kanti Halder,Akash Ghosh,Arijit Roy,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenoMorph 是一个多模态基因组推理框架，将疾病预测从“记忆型基因-疾病关联”转向“通路驱动的推理”，通过冻结 DNA 基础模型、问题条件跨注意力融合、自适应潜在推理、残差推理门、拒绝采样微调与分层最优传输正则，实现更高 F1 与更低延迟，并在匿名 KEGG 基准上验证去除记忆捷径后仍具强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在生物推理上表现较强，但基因组疾病推断仍主要依赖对基因-疾病关联的记忆，而非对生物通路的理解。这种捷径学习削弱了模型的鲁棒性与泛化性，并在分子标识不可用时失效，因此需要将疾病预测转向基于通路的推理范式。

Method: 提出 GenoMorph：耦合冻结的 DNA 基础模型与问题条件跨注意力融合；引入自适应潜在推理 LatentSp，按推理置信度动态分配计算；设计残差推理门，迭代重注入基因组证据；采用拒绝采样微调，并以分层最优传输 OT 进行正则化。模型不直接学习基因-疾病映射，而是将基因组序列表示与潜在通路动态对齐，使推理轨迹沿分子相互作用展开。另构建 KEGG 匿名基准，将基因与分子标识替换为匿名符号，同时保留序列与通路拓扑，以消除记忆捷径。

Result: 加权 F1 从 BioReason 的 0.7863 提升至 0.9412；结合拒绝采样微调与自适应潜在推理后达到 0.9725，同时延迟降低近 60%。在匿名基准上达到 0.9465 F1，显著优于已有系统。

Conclusion: 准确的疾病预测可来自通路推理，而非记忆化的基因-疾病关联。GenoMorph 通过通路 grounded 的多模态推理提升了基因组疾病推断的鲁棒性、泛化性与推理效率，并在去除记忆捷径的匿名基准上验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in biological reasoning; however, genomic disease inference remains largely dependent on memorized gene-disease associations rather than understanding biological pathways. This shortcut learning undermines robustness and generalization, and breaks down when molecular identifiers are unavailable. We present GenoMorph, a multimodal genomic reasoning framework that shifts disease prediction from associative gene-disease mapping toward pathway-grounded reasoning. GenoMorph couples a frozen DNA foundation model with question-conditioned cross-attention fusion, self-adaptive latent reasoning (LatentSp), a residual reasoning gate for iterative genomic evidence reinjection, and rejection sampling fine-tuning regularized by hierarchical optimal transport (OT). Rather than learning direct gene-disease mappings, GenoMorph aligns genomic sequence representations with latent pathway dynamics, enabling reasoning trajectories that follow molecular interactions before producing disease predictions. LatentSp dynamically allocates computation according to reasoning confidence, reducing unnecessary reasoning steps and improving inference efficiency. We further construct an anonymized benchmark from the Kyoto Encyclopedia of Genes and Genomes (KEGG), replacing every gene and molecular identifier with anonymous symbols while preserving sequences and pathway topology, thereby removing memorization shortcuts. GenoMorph raises the weighted F1 from 0.7863 (BioReason) to 0.9412, and rejection sampling fine-tuning with self-adaptive latent reasoning pushes it to 0.9725 while cutting latency nearly 60%. On the anonymized benchmark it reaches 0.9465 F1, substantially outperforming prior systems and confirming that accurate disease prediction can arise from pathway reasoning rather than memorized gene-disease associations.

</details>


### [160] [Waggle: Learning One Anonymous Local Law for Self-Organizing LLM Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34136)
*Mingxi Zou,Wei Zhu,Zhuo Wang,Langzhang Liang,Zhiwen Tang,Yinghui Xu,Zenglin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出将LLM多智能体协调的学习目标从显式角色、层级、路由或通信拓扑，转向一个可跨可替换智能体共享的“局部法则”（Waggle），并通过 Swarm-Consistent Distillation（SCD）学习该法则，使多智能体组织能够在重复执行中自发形成、持续并在线重组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统通常学习或适配显式角色、层级、路由策略或通信拓扑，当智能体群体或交互条件变化时，往往需要重新设计全局组织。作者希望将学习目标从“全局组织结构”转移到可复用的局部法则，使协调能力能跨可替换智能体共享，并在群体规模和交互预算变化时保持适应性与可迁移性，而无需重新定义全局组织。

Method: 论文提出 Waggle：一种在有限局部视图上运行的共享匿名策略，同时选择任务动作、语义通信和局部承诺更新。重复执行同一局部法则即可让协调在线形成、持续和重组，不依赖显式角色或全局拓扑。为在可替换智能体和演化协调中学习该法则，作者提出 Swarm-Consistent Distillation（SCD），将匿名轨道一致性（anonymous-orbit consistency）与基于rollout的下一个局部协调场预测相结合，且不增加推理时组件。

Result: 在多种协调设置中，同一学习到的局部法则在群体规模和交互预算变化时仍然有效，保留超过96%的特定基底 oracle 质量，并可无需再训练地迁移；SCD 还能在出现反证据后改善重组能力。

Conclusion: LLM智能体组织可以通过反复执行一个学习到的局部法则而自发涌现并持续适应，这为多智能体协调提供了一种不依赖显式全局组织设计的可复用范式。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly collaborate on complex tasks, how to organize their interactions becomes a central design question. Existing multi-agent systems typically learn or adapt explicit roles, hierarchies, routing policies, or communication topologies. We shift the learning target to a reusable local law that can be shared across interchangeable agents and adapt coordination as populations or interaction conditions change, without redefining a global organization. We introduce Waggle, a shared anonymous policy over bounded local views that jointly selects task actions, semantic communication, and local commitment updates. Repeated execution of the same law allows coordination to form, persist, and reorganize online without explicit roles or global topology. To learn this law across interchangeable agents and evolving coordination, we develop Swarm-Consistent Distillation (SCD), combining anonymous-orbit consistency with rollout-grounded prediction of the next local coordination field, with no added inference-time components. Across diverse coordination settings, the same learned law remains effective as populations and interaction budgets change, retains over 96% of substrate-specific oracle quality, and transfers without retraining; SCD further improves reorganization after counterevidence. Together, these results show that LLM-agent organization can emerge and adapt through repeated execution of a learned local law.

</details>


### [161] [Decision Readouts for Text-Mediated Video Anomaly Detection: An Exploratory Evaluation of Jev and Qwen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34180)
*Xukui Qin,Youting Wang,Xinjie He,Ziyang Luo,Runxiong Wu,Yan-Syuan Chen,Zhongyao Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定视频文本证据的前提下，评估不同决策读出方式对视频异常/犯罪检测的影响：Jev 类型化决策与三类 Qwen 读出在 UCF-Crime 和 XD-Violence 的稀疏样本上表现不一致，结果仅具探索性，不能支持类型化接口的因果优势或通用优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是回答：当视频衍生的文本证据（摘要与有序字幕）保持不变时，仅改变决策读出方式，是否会显著影响排序与概率质量。作者希望检验 Jev 类型化决策接口是否优于 Qwen 的概率/序数似然读出，并审计历史 LAVAD 分数，同时区分“响应结构”与“数值一致性”。

Method: 使用 UCF-Crime 与 XD-Violence 的稀疏开发样本：40 个视频、400 个目标锚点，每个数据集 20 个来源组、200 个锚点，正例分别仅 10 和 37。文本证据固定为摘要与有序字幕。对比 Jev 类型化决策与三种 Qwen 读出。原五后端试点计划请求 4,000 条预测，但 Jev Choice 在严格数值策略下仅返回 800 条中的 776 条有效响应，导致无法进行全覆盖率质量比较。另在精确匹配锚点上审计历史 LAVAD 分数。

Result: 在 XD 字幕上，Jev Noul 的平均精度为 75.99%，高于 Qwen 生成概率的 48.47% 和更强的局部序数似然期望的 57.81%；与后者的配对差异为 18.18 个百分点（95% 来源组 bootstrap 区间 5.53–31.50）。UCF 未出现对应优势：字幕 ROC-AUC 为 Noul 52.26%、序数似然 65.95%。两种概率读出的 UCF Brier 分数均高于评估流行率参考值 0.0475，因而更差。研究还审计了精确匹配锚点上的历史 LAVAD 分数，并区分响应结构与数值一致性。缺少二值似然对照。

Conclusion: 这些离线探索性结果刻画了排序、概率质量与接口失败模式，但既不能证明类型化接口具有因果收益，也不能证明其通用优越性、校准性或端到端加速。由于样本稀疏、正例少、覆盖率受限且缺少二值似然对照，结论应谨慎解释。

Abstract: How much does the decision readout matter when video-derived textual evidence is held fixed? We evaluate Jev typed decisions and three Qwen readouts on a sparse development sample of 40 videos and 400 target anchors from UCF-Crime and XD-Violence, each presented as a summary and ordered captions. Each dataset contributes 20 source groups and 200 anchors, including only 10 and 37 positives, respectively. The original five-backend pilot requested 4,000 predictions; Jev Choice returned 776 valid responses out of 800 under the study's strict numerical policy, blocking its full-coverage quality comparison. On XD captions, Jev Noul achieved 75.99% average precision versus 48.47% for Qwen generated probability and 57.81% for the stronger local ordinal-likelihood expectation. The latter paired difference was 18.18 percentage points (95% source-group bootstrap interval 5.53-31.50). UCF did not show a corresponding advantage: caption ROC-AUC was 52.26% for Noul and 65.95% for ordinal likelihood. Both probability readouts had higher, hence worse, UCF Brier scores than the evaluation-prevalence reference of 0.0475. We additionally audit historical LAVAD scores at exactly matched anchors and distinguish response structure from numerical consistency. A binary-likelihood control is missing. These exploratory offline results characterize ranking, probability quality and interface failures; they establish neither a causal typed-interface benefit nor general superiority, calibration or end-to-end acceleration.

</details>


### [162] [Fuzzy Distribution Modeling for Synthetic Tabular Data Generation with Causality Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34349)
*Michael Vasilakakis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模糊集理论的模糊分布建模方法，用于合成表格数据生成：以模糊集表示特征分布、以模糊认知图建模特征依赖，通过采样模糊概念生成样本，从而原生支持混合类型、缺失值和领域约束，并支持语言查询与IF-THEN推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当真实世界数据有限或不可获取时，合成表格数据是训练机器学习模型的有效替代方案。然而，表格数据具有异构、非平滑和不完整等特点，传统概率模型与深度生成模型难以有效处理，且其可解释性仍然有限。

Method: 基于模糊集理论提出模糊分布建模方法。使用模糊集表示特征分布，使用模糊认知图（Fuzzy Cognitive Maps）建模特征依赖关系，形成低参数、可解释的数据表示。合成样本通过采样模糊概念而非原始值生成，因此能够原生支持混合数据类型、缺失值和领域约束。该方法还支持语言查询和IF-THEN推理，便于透明地模拟决策过程。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在效用、保真度和隐私方面与当前最先进方法具有竞争力，同时可解释性显著提升。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了模糊分布建模作为一种原则性且有效的合成表格数据生成方法，适用于模糊系统与决策支持应用。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data generation provides an effective alternative for the training of machine learning models when real-world data is limited or inaccessible. However, the heterogeneous, non-smooth, and incomplete nature of tabular data poses fundamental challenges to conventional probabilistic and deep generative models, where their interpretability remains limited. This paper proposes a novel fuzzy distribution modeling methodology for synthetic tabular data generation based on fuzzy sets theory. Feature distributions are represented using fuzzy sets and feature dependencies are modeled through Fuzzy Cognitive Maps, resulting in a low-parameter, and an interpretable data representation. Synthetic samples are generated by sampling fuzzy concepts rather than raw values, enabling native support for mixed data types, missing values, and domain constraints. The methodology further supports linguistic queries and IF-THEN reasoning, facilitating transparent simulation of decision-making processes. Experimental results on benchmark datasets demonstrate competitive performance with respect to utility, fidelity and privacy compared to state-of-the-art methods, while offering substantially improved interpretability. These results establish fuzzy distribution modeling as a principled and effective approach for synthetic tabular data generation in fuzzy systems and decision support applications.

</details>


### [163] [PDE-JEPA: Predictive Representation Learning of Latent Dynamics Modeling for Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34715)
*Zhentao Tan,Jianrong Zhang,Ruijie Quan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PDE-JEPA，用预测式表征预训练替代重构式目标，用于参数化 PDE 动力学建模；通过掩码隐变量预测、几何投影器与物理结构隐空间预测器，在九个 PDE 基准上显著优于 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理轨迹不仅包含系统状态快照，还揭示状态在控制条件下的演化规律。然而现有参数化 PDE 表征学习多依赖重构目标，只强调恢复观测物理场，可能无法充分捕捉动力学演化信息。作者发现预测式表征虽保留丰富物理信息，但仅凭这一优势并不能保证准确的场演化，因此需要显式对齐动力学结构。

Method: 提出 PDE-JEPA 框架：1）先用掩码隐变量预测训练编码器，捕捉 PDE 动力学的潜在规律；2）引入几何投影器，将隐轨迹几何与物理场演化几何对齐，使预训练表征适应更符合动力学的状态空间；3）在几何对齐隐空间上构建物理结构隐预测器，将动力学分解为参数无关演化分量与参数相关响应分量。

Result: 在九个广泛使用的 PDE 基准上，PDE-JEPA 在同分布设置下平均超过现有 SOTA 33.4%，在向未见控制参数外推时平均提升 51.4%。

Conclusion: 预测式表征预训练可作为重构式学习的有效替代，但需通过几何对齐和物理结构化解耦来保证场演化精度；PDE-JEPA 在参数化 PDE 动力学建模中兼具更强泛化与外推能力。

Abstract: Physical trajectories contain more than snapshots of a system: they also reveal how its states evolve under governing conditions. However, representation learning for parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) has largely relied on reconstruction-based objectives that emphasize recovering observed physical fields. In this paper, we investigate predictive representation pretraining as an alternative to reconstruction-based learning. We find that predictive representations preserve rich physical information, yet this advantage alone does not ensure accurate field evolution. Based on these observations, we introduce PDE-JEPA for parametric PDE dynamics. Specifically, we first train an encoder using a masked-latent prediction to capture the underlying regularities of PDE dynamics. To explicitly adapt the pretrained representation toward a more dynamics-aligned state space, we then introduce a geometry projector that aligns latent trajectory geometry with the evolution geometry of physical fields. Finally, building on this geometry-aligned latent space, we further develop a physics-structured latent predictor that decomposes the dynamics into parameter-independent evolution and parameter-dependent response components. Extensive experiments on nine widely used PDE benchmarks demonstrate that our framework outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods by an average of 33.4\% in-distribution, while achieving an average improvement of 51.4\% when extrapolating to unseen governing parameters. The project page is available \href{ this https URL }{here}.

</details>


### [164] [APEX-Voice: Can Voice Agents Complete Professional Workflows Through Full-Duplex Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34973)
*Puneet Mathur,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 APEX-Voice，一个面向全双工语音智能体的专业工作流基准，包含 120 个交互式任务、10 类工作原型，并在有状态 Voice Workbench 中考察最终工作产物准确率与端到端工作流成功率。测试五个前沿实时语音智能体，Pass@1 均低于 25%，最佳 Reliable@3 仅 10.8%，表明现有系统难以将对话流畅性转化为可靠的专业工作执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工语音智能体已能听、说、调用工具并在对话中行动，但流畅对话并不等于能正确完成被委派的专业工作流；缺乏系统评估语音智能体专业任务可靠性的基准。

Method: 构建 APEX-Voice 基准：120 个交互式专业工作流，覆盖表单填写、企业谈判、协调、咨询、面试等 10 类工作原型；每个工作流运行于有状态 Voice Workbench，包含任务知识、类型化工具、人工标注的最终工作产物、授权约束，以及基于经验证预编译语音实现器的用户模拟策略；评估最终产物字段准确率和端到端工作流成功率，后者要求正确终态、有效流程、动作完成和有效最终产物。

Result: 在 GPT-Live-1、Gemini-3.8-Live、Grok-Voice-Think-2.0、Step-Audio3、GPT-realtime-2.1 五个前沿实时语音智能体上，无系统 Pass@1 超过 25%，最佳 Reliable@3 仅 10.8%；有状态协调是主要失败点，知识检索需求更高和语音中途修正的任务成功率进一步下降。

Conclusion: APEX-Voice 是首个评估语音智能体能否将对话能力转化为可靠专业工作的基准，揭示了当前实时语音智能体在复杂专业工作流执行上的显著不足。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents can now listen, speak, use tools, and act during spoken interactions, but fluent dialogue does not guarantee correct completion of delegated professional workflows. We introduce APEX-Voice, a benchmark of 120 interactive professional workflows spanning ten work archetypes such as form completion, corporate negotiation, coordination, consulting, and interviewing.
