Each workflow executes in a stateful Voice Workbench environment with task-specific knowledge, typed tools, gold-annotated final work artifact, authorization constraints, and a user simulation policy backed by validated, pre-compiled speech realizations. We evaluate both artifact field accuracy and end-to-end workflow success, which requires the correct terminal state, valid process, completed actions, and a valid final artifact. Across five frontier real-time voice agents-GPT-Live-1, Gemini-3.8-Live, Grok-Voice-Think-2.0, Step-Audio3, and GPT-realtime-2.1, none exceeds 25% Pass@1, and the best Reliable@3 is only 10.8%. Moreover, stateful coordination is the dominant failure point across systems, while success decreases further on workflows requiring greater knowledge retrieval and mid-speech corrections. Overall, APEX-Voice is the first benchmark for evaluating whether voice agents can translate conversational competence into dependable professional work.

</details>


### [165] [JRDB-AVR: An Active Visual Reasoning Benchmark for Embodied Agents in Real-World Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35032)
*Zhixi Cai,Fucai Ke,Sukai Huang,Maria Garcia de la Banda,Peter J. Stuckey,Gholamreza Haffari,Hamid Rezatofighi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 JRDB-AVR，一个面向具身视觉推理的主动证据获取基准，要求智能体按时间戳与视角请求有限观测，并同时评估最终答案与支撑答案的视觉证据；同时提出 JRDB-AVR-Agent 参考方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身视觉推理中，智能体视野有限，回答所需证据常分布在时间、视角和交互对象之间；模型可能未观察相关对象、时刻或视角就给出看似合理的答案。现有基准多评估被动观测与最终答案，忽视主动推理与证据获取。

Method: 基于真实 JRDB 机器人数据，通过结构化问题生成引擎构建 JRDB-AVR 基准。任务中具身智能体接收视觉推理问题，按时间戳和视角请求有界观测，评估最终答案及其有依据的视觉证据。问题覆盖多真实环境，涉及时序搜索、视角选择和以人为中心的组合推理。另提出 JRDB-AVR-Agent：维护显式、观测锚定的图结构世界模型，并通过求解回答问题。

Result: 实验显示当前基线在答案准确率与证据准确率之间存在显著差距，表明现有 VLM 可产生缺乏证据支撑的正确答案，说明具身视觉推理需要主动且证据感知的评估。

Conclusion: JRDB-AVR 将主动证据获取纳入具身视觉推理评估，弥补现有基准仅看被动观测和最终答案的不足；证据准确率与答案准确率的差距表明，应同时评估答案与支撑证据。代码与基准已公开。

Abstract: In complex embodied visual reasoning scenarios, an agent often has only a limited field of view, and the evidence needed to answer a question may be distributed across time, viewpoint, and interacting objects. A model may therefore give a plausible answer without ever observing the relevant object, time, or view that supports it. Current visual reasoning benchmarks largely evaluate passive observations and final answers, overlooking settings that require active reasoning and evidence acquisition. We introduce JRDB-AVR, a benchmark derived from existing real-world JRDB robotics data through a structured question-generation engine that turns this gap into an explicit evaluation: an embodied agentic system receives a visual reasoning question, requests bounded observations by timestamp and viewing angle, and is evaluated on both the final answer and the grounded visual evidence supporting it. The benchmark contains diverse questions over multiple real-world environments involving temporal search, viewpoint selection, and human-oriented compositional reasoning. We also introduce JRDB-AVR-Agent, a reference active reasoning agentic method that maintains an explicit observation-grounded graph-based world model and answers through solving. Experiments reveal a substantial gap between answer accuracy and evidence accuracy in current baselines, showing that current VLMs can produce unsupported correct answers and that active evidence-aware evaluation is necessary for embodied visual reasoning. Code and benchmark are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [166] [The Argument and the Letterhead: Source-Position Coherence in AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35286)
*Michele Loi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究考察AI评估者能否区分"论证本身好坏"与"说话者带来的惊讶感"，通过对六篇固定文本在多种来源归属下的2,976次评估，发现来源效应随论证内容变化，支持"来源—立场一致性"解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一个论证可能因出自特定说话者而显得出乎意料，但这并不意味着它本身是坏论证。作者关注的核心动机是：AI评估者是否会混淆"来源效应"与"论证质量"——即它们能否把对来源的判断与对论证内容的判断分开，还是简单地给某个来源一个固定偏好分数。

Method: 两项预注册描述性研究加后续Jev补充实验，共收集2,976份有效评估。材料为六篇固定文本，主题涵盖美国AI政策、德国债务刹车、瑞士核能；每篇文本配以多种来源归属呈现。关键比较是检验"两个来源之间的评分差"是否随论证内容而变化（即来源×论证的交互效应）。后续欧洲Jev补充采用不同评分标准且采集中断，设定了0.05的绝对参照门槛。

Result: 以Sol为例：国安论证在CODEPINK下均值0.359、在College Republicans下0.639；民权论证则为0.742与0.721，这一模式无法用对某来源的恒定偏好解释。相关交互效应在多个主题和近期模型配置（含开启推理）中均出现，但部分比较效应很小。Jev补充产出五个低于0.05参考门槛的交互。部分书面评估明确提及来源与其被归属立场之间的不匹配。

Conclusion: 数值与文本证据共同支持"来源—立场一致性"作为合理解释，同时也存在可信度、真实性与任务理解等竞争性解释。论文通过受控比较推进该推断，在附录中报告条件性事后p值，并记录了这项由AI主导的研究背后的人类决策与委托核查过程。

Abstract: An argument can be surprising coming from a particular speaker without being a bad argument. Do AI evaluators keep these judgments apart? Two preregistered descriptive studies and a later Jev supplement collected 2,976 usable evaluations of six fixed texts about US AI policy, Germany's debt brake and Swiss nuclear energy. Each text was presented under several source attributions. The key comparison asks whether the gap between two sources changes when the argument changes. On Sol, for example, a national-security argument received mean ratings of 0.359 under CODEPINK and 0.639 under College Republicans; a civil-rights argument received 0.742 and 0.721. A constant preference for one source cannot explain that pattern. Related interactions appeared across topics and recent model configurations, including those with reasoning enabled, while several comparisons yielded small effects. The later European Jev supplement yielded five interactions below the adopted absolute reference of 0.05; its distinct rubric and interrupted collection limit comparison with the chat systems. Some written evaluations explicitly invoked a mismatch between a source and its attributed position. Taken together, the numerical and verbal evidence supports source-position coherence as a plausible explanation, alongside competing accounts involving credibility, authenticity and interpretation of the task. The paper develops this inference through controlled comparisons, reports conditional post hoc p-values in an appendix, and documents the human decisions and delegated checks behind an AI-conducted study.

</details>


### [167] [Jev thinks "I don't know'', but doesn't say it: Introducing Sys1Cal-v1 Dataset for Probability Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35342)
*Riccardo Porcedda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Sys1Cal-v1 数据集，直接检验 Jev 等 System One Models 返回概率的数值标定；发现 Jev 的 Choice 输出隐含遗漏第三真值 P(U)，恢复后中位软准确率从 0.771 提升至 0.978。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jev 宣称其返回的概率分布是 calibrated，但该主张缺乏公开测试；现有外部基准主要评估 confidence calibration，并不检验每个返回选项概率是否具有正确的数值含义。因此需要构造真实概率已知的基准，直接评估 System One Models 的概率标定质量。

Method: 构建 Sys1Cal-v1：针对命题 A 的 True/False 问题，其精确概率 P(A) 由构造过程已知。每个样本通过 Jev 的三个原语 Noul、Choice、Score 查询，并以与真实分布的总变差距离（total variation distance）评估，可据此估计 soft accuracy。作者用该数据集评估 Jev 与开源 Choice-style 基线 SemIf，并重点分析 Jev 的 Score 与 Choice 答案标定。

Result: 发现 Jev 存在一种特殊行为：在 Choice 答案中，P(A) 与 P(¬A) 被呈现为满足 P(A)+P(¬A)=1，但实际上遗漏了一个非零的 P(U)。恢复 P(U) 后，Choice 答案的中位 soft accuracy 从 0.771 提升到 0.978。这表明即使在二值决策中，Jev 也倾向于输出第三个选项：‘I don’t know’。

Conclusion: Sys1Cal-v1 可作为直接检验概率数值标定的基准数据集；Jev 的概率输出并非简单二值标定，而隐含第三真值/不确定性。显式建模并恢复 P(U) 能显著改善标定评估与软准确率，说明 System One Models 的校准承诺需要更严格的数值验证。

Abstract: The appearance of Jev marked the era of System One Models, foundation models that return structured decisions with probability distributions rather than text. Aside from low cost and great speed, Jev's central promise is that these probabilities are calibrated: such claim is not backed by any public test and available external benchmarks evaluate confidence calibration, not whether every returned option probability has the right numerical meaning. To tackle this issue, we introduce Sys1Cal-v1, a dataset of True/False questions about a proposition $A$ for which the exact probability $P(A)$ is known by construction. Each item is queried through the three Jev primitives - Noul, Choice and Score - and evaluated by total variation distance from the ground-truth distribution, which can be used to estimate a soft accuracy of System One Models. We showcase the utility of Sys1Cal-v1 as a benchmark dataset by evaluating Jev and SemIf, an open-source Choice-style baseline. In this work, however, we focus even more deeply on Jev, by studying the calibration of its Score and Choice answers. In particular, we discover a peculiar behaviour that can be explained by assuming that Jev suppresses a third truth value, going beyond True and False. In other words, in \texttt{Choice} answers, $P(A)$ and $P(\neg A)$ are presented as if $P(A)+P(\neg A)=1$, while a term $P(U)\neq0$ is missing in the sum. Recovering $P(U)$ leads to an improvement of median soft accuracy in \texttt{Choice} answers from $0.771$ to $0.978$, suggesting that, even in binary decisions, Jev wants to answer with a third option:``I don't know''.

</details>


### [168] [A.D.A.M.O. (Agent for language-Driven Actions with Multimodal Observations): A Visual-Symbolic Framework for Virtual Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35463)
*Alessandro Emmanuel Pecora,Stefano Calzolari,Francesco Strada,Andrea Bottino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出A.D.A.M.O.视觉-符号框架，利用预训练VLM与工具调用，将感知、推理、行动统一于单一控制回路，实现语言驱动的虚拟人，并引入基于认知难度分类法的受控任务套件进行诊断评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创建可信的虚拟人需要将感知、推理与行动通过语言进行连贯整合，核心挑战在于如何在交互式3D环境中把各组件组合成一个闭环控制回路。现有方法难以统一这些组件并实现自然语言驱动的、有依据的任务行为。

Method: 提出A.D.A.M.O.（Agent for language-Driven Actions with Multimodal Observations），一个面向语言驱动虚拟人的视觉-符号框架。它利用预训练VLM结合工具调用，在单一控制回路中统一感知、推理与行动；并维护一个双重视觉-符号世界模型，融合自我中心视觉输入与同步的符号状态，以支持由自然语言提示驱动的具身任务导向行为。为便于诊断评估，还引入了一个由认知难度（CD）分类法组织的受控任务套件，将空间任务分解为程序复杂度与语言复杂度。

Result: 在受控场景的实验中，语义标注对任务完成度和失败模式有显著影响：它减少了感知歧义，同时将失败转移到下游执行阶段，而推理错误相对罕见。

Conclusion: 视觉-符号世界模型能有效支撑语言驱动的具身虚拟人在受控场景中的任务执行，且通过CD分类法的受控任务套件可进行诊断性评估；语义标注主要缓解感知层面的歧义，失败更多集中于执行阶段，推理并非主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Creating believable vh requires the coherent integration of perception, reasoning, and action mediated by language. A central challenge is to combine these components into a control loop grounded in interactive 3D environments. To this end, we present A.D.A.M.O. (Agent for language-Driven Actions with Multimodal Observations), a visual-symbolic framework for language-driven vh that leverages a pretrained vlm with tool calling to unify perception, reasoning, and action within a single control loop. A.D.A.M.O. maintains a dual visual-symbolic world model that combines egocentric visual input and synchronized symbolic state to support grounded task-oriented behavior from natural language prompts. To support diagnostic evaluation, we introduce a controlled task suite organized by a cd taxonomy that breaks down spatial tasks into procedural and linguistic complexity. Experiments in controlled scenes show that semantic labeling strongly influences task completion and failure modes, reducing perceptual ambiguity while shifting failures toward downstream execution, whereas reasoning errors remain comparatively rare.

</details>


<div id='eess.AS'></div>

# eess.AS [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Simulation-Based Inference for Plate Reverb System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35295)
*Dylan Sechet,Marc Evrard,Matthieu Kowalski*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 论文针对首届 DAFx 参数估计挑战赛 Task A，采用基于仿真的推理（SBI）从脉冲响应中反演板模型的物理参数：先用模拟器生成数据训练神经网络近似参数后验，推理时仅需前向传播；并对每个测试样本进行额外模拟与微调，得到专用网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从脉冲响应估计板模型物理参数是一个典型逆问题，传统方法往往依赖迭代优化，计算成本高且难以刻画参数不确定性。挑战赛提供了统一基准，需要高效、准确且可适应单个观测的参数反演方法。

Method: 将参数估计置于 Simulation-Based Inference 框架：利用模拟器生成“板参数—脉冲响应”数据集，训练神经网络学习给定脉冲响应下板参数的条件密度/后验分布。对新观测的推理只需一次网络前向传播，不调用模拟器。针对每个测试观测，进一步进行自适应微调：从当前估计分布中采样参数，模拟对应脉冲响应，并对网络进行额外训练，从而得到该测试样本的专用网络。

Result: 摘要未报告定量指标、排名或误差结果，主要说明该方法应用于 DAFx 参数估计挑战赛 Task A，并描述了训练、推理及逐测试样本微调流程。

Conclusion: 该方法将 SBI 与测试时自适应微调相结合，避免推理阶段反复调用模拟器，为板模型参数反演提供了可行框架；但其实际精度、鲁棒性与效率仍需实验数据验证。

Abstract: We address Task A of the 1st DAFx Parameter Estimation Challenge, which aims to retrieve the physical parameters of a plate model from an impulse response. To do so, we use the Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) framework, in which we train a neural network to estimate a density over plate parameters given an impulse response, using a dataset generated by the simulator. Inference for a new impulse response then requires only a forward pass through the network, without involving the simulator. For each test observation, we fine-tune a specific network: additional simulation rounds are performed by sampling parameters from the current estimated distribution, simulating the corresponding impulse responses, and fine-tuning to produce the specialized network.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Evaluating System One Models for Agent Security Decisions: Reliability, Calibration, and Selective Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33401)
*Yixuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文评估System One模型（Jev、Laya、Decider、Bespoke Nimble）作为自动化安全裁判的可靠性，核心结论是：模型准确率、概率校准与决策策略行为必须联合评估，单看平均表现会掩盖系统性安全失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的裁判可用于检测提示注入、评估交互风险并筛查有害请求。System One模型输出带概率的类型化决策，供软件执行允许、阻断或升级操作；但这些概率能否支撑可靠的自动化安全决策尚不明确。

Method: 将Jev、Laya、Decider、Bespoke Nimble与专用分类器及语言模型裁判进行对比评估，考察决策准确率、概率校准和选择性自动化，重点分析不同攻击组上的表现及在漏检攻击严格约束下的自动化策略。

Result: (1) 整体性能和平均校准较好，可能掩盖特定攻击组上的系统性失败，包括模型高置信度判为安全的攻击。(2) 在严格限制漏检攻击时，所评估策略只能自动允许很少输入；分别设置允许与阻断阈值mainly通过阻断更多输入提升自动化，且验证集满足错误限制的策略在未见输入上可能超标。(3) 第二个裁判能检测部分漏检攻击，但也可能拒绝更多良性输入，并重复首个模型的高置信度错误。

Conclusion: 模型裁判的准确率、概率校准及其所产生决策策略的行为不能割裂评估，必须联合考量，才能可靠判断其是否适用于自动化安全决策。

Abstract: Model-based judges support agent security by detecting prompt injections, assessing interaction risks, and screening harmful requests. System One models expose typed decisions with probabilities that software can use to allow, block, or escalate inputs, but whether these probabilities support reliable automated security decisions remains unclear. We evaluate Jev, Laya, Decider, and Bespoke Nimble against specialized classifiers and language-model judges, examining decision accuracy, probability calibration, and selective automation. We identify three main findings. (1) Strong overall performance and favorable average calibration can hide systematic failures on particular attack groups, including attacks that models confidently classify as safe. (2) Under strict limits on missed attacks, the evaluated policies allow few inputs automatically, and choosing separate allow and block thresholds increases automation mainly by blocking more inputs. Even policies that meet error limits during validation can exceed them on unseen inputs. (3) A second judge can detect some missed attacks, but it may also reject more benign inputs and repeat the first model's high-confidence errors. These findings show that model accuracy, probability calibration, and the behavior of the resulting decision policy must be evaluated together.

</details>


### [171] [JevVibe: Efficient Classification-Guided Secure Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34963)
*Arshak Rezvani,Sasha Behrouzi,Ahmad-Reza Sadeghi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文评估决策模型 Jev 在 50 类 CWE 分类任务上的表现，并将其用于诊断引导的代码修复代理 JevVibe。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的代码可能功能正确但存在安全弱点，因此需要先诊断弱点类型再修复。CWE 提供了标准化词汇，但让自回归语言模型生成并抽取 CWE 标签存在输出有效性、速度、成本和准确性问题。

Method: 在 1,916 个 CyberSecEval 基准样例上进行受控的 50 类 CWE 分类，将决策模型 Jev（直接从候选集中选择并为每个候选返回概率）与六个开源权重自回归模型及一个前沿专有模型 GPT-5.6-Sol 对比。并构建 JevVibe，利用预测的 CWE 标签修复 Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct 生成的代码。

Result: Jev 在所有分类与排序指标上优于六个开源基线。与 GPT-5.6-Sol 相比，GPT-5.6-Sol 的 Top-1 准确率和 Macro-F1 更高，而 Jev 的 Top-3、Top-5 准确率更高，MRR 几乎相同，且中位 API 延迟低 6.27 倍、估计 API 成本低 55.9 倍。在 Jev 提供诊断时，JevVibe 将检测器测得的安全通过率从修复前 63.5% 提升至 70.7%，高于 LLM 引导修复的 66.1%。

Conclusion: JevVibe 能有效提升生成代码的安全性，Jev 提供了可靠且高效的 CWE 分类。

Abstract: Large language models can generate functionally correct code that still contains security weaknesses, motivating repair pipelines that first diagnose a weakness type before deciding how to fix it. The Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) provides a standardized vocabulary for such diagnoses, but asking an autoregressive language model to generate a CWE label and extracting it from the response raises questions about output validity, speed, and cost, as well as accuracy. We evaluate Jev, a decision model that instead selects directly from a declared set of candidates and returns a probability for each, against six open-weight autoregressive models and a frontier proprietary model, GPT-5.6-Sol, on a controlled 50-way CWE classification task over 1,916 CyberSecEval benchmark examples. Jev outperforms all six open-weight baselines on every classification and ranking metric, while its comparison with GPT-5.6-Sol depends on the metric: GPT-5.6-Sol achieves higher Top-1 accuracy and Macro-F1, whereas Jev achieves higher Top-3 and Top-5 accuracy and a nearly identical MRR, at $6.27\times$ lower median API latency and $55.9\times$ lower estimated API cost. We further build JevVibe, a diagnosis-guided repair agent that uses predicted CWE labels to repair code generated by Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct. With Jev providing the diagnosis, the agent increases the detector-measured security pass rate from 63.5% before repair to 70.7%, compared with 66.1% for LLM-guided repair. These results show that JevVibe is effective at improving the security of generated code, with Jev providing reliable and efficient CWE classification.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [What Next-Event Accuracy Cannot See: Closed-Loop Evaluation of Emergency Department Trajectory Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31635)
*Zhen Xuen Brandon Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 EDSim-Bench，用 425,028 条 MIMIC-IV-ED 就诊及 MC-MED 外部复现，系统量化临床轨迹生成模型在闭环 rollout 中的误差累积；发现 next-event 准确率相近的模型在自回归模拟下差异巨大，逐位置监督可降低发散，但最佳模型仍生成过短就诊且下游占用预测排序反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床轨迹模型通常以观测历史上的下一事件准确率评估，而模拟要求模型以自身生成的事件为条件，误差会逐步累积。该问题在序列建模中虽已知，但尚未在临床轨迹模拟器中被系统量化。

Method: 开发 EDSim-Bench，基于 425,028 条 MIMIC-IV-ED 就诊数据，并在 MC-MED 上外部复现，同时发布评估协议与评分代码。从留出就诊前缀出发，让模型生成整次就诊的剩余部分，并从终止、事件组成、时序、条件保真度、占用预测五个维度评估；以仅用训练集拟合的 order-3 n-gram 为参考基线。比较 Transformer、GRU、LSTM 等神经架构，并进行种子扰动、模型缩放、时间偏移和外部站点验证；还测试推理时干预以及逐位置监督（监督所有合格序列位置 vs 仅最终前缀位置）。

Result: 尽管 next-event 准确率差异在 0.001 以内，三种神经架构在 rollout 下行为差异显著：某 Transformer 配方跨种子终止得分为 0.43–0.96，其发散度为 n-gram 的 4.2–137 倍；没有前缀训练的神经模型在终止或事件组成上接近 n-gram。推理时干预改善了终止，但未能同时恢复组成与时序。监督所有合格序列位置而非仅最终前缀位置，使 Transformer、GRU、LSTM 的发散降低 1–2 个数量级，该模式在模型缩放、时间偏移和外部站点评估下持续。然而，即使最佳模型生成的就诊长度也仅为观测值约一半，且模型排名在占用预测这一与床位管理相关的下游指标上发生反转。

Conclusion: next-event accuracy 不足以评估临床轨迹模拟器；需要采用闭环 rollout、多维度指标和外部验证的评估协议。逐位置监督可缓解误差累积，但仍存在就诊长度偏差和下游任务排序不一致等问题，说明临床轨迹模拟器的可靠评估与改进仍具挑战。

Abstract: Clinical trajectory models are usually evaluated by next-event accuracy on observed histories. Simulation is different: models must condition on their own generated events, allowing errors to compound. Although this problem is well known in sequence modelling, it has not been systematically quantified for clinical trajectory simulators. We developed EDSim-Bench to evaluate this failure mode using 425,028 MIMIC-IV-ED stays, with external replication on MC-MED, and release the evaluation protocol and scoring code. Starting from held-out visit prefixes, models generate the remainder of each visit and are evaluated on termination, event composition, timing, conditional fidelity, and occupancy forecasting, with a train-only order-3 n-gram as a reference baseline. Despite next-event accuracies within 0.001, three neural architectures behaved very differently under rollout. Across seeds, one Transformer recipe ranged from 0.43 to 0.96 in termination score and from 4.2- to 137-fold the divergence of the n-gram; no prefix-trained neural model approached the n-gram on termination or event composition. Inference-time interventions improved termination but did not jointly recover composition and timing. Supervising every eligible sequence position rather than only the final prefix position was associated with one to two orders of magnitude lower divergence across Transformer, GRU, and LSTM models, with the pattern persisting under model scaling, temporal shift, and external-site evaluation. Nevertheless, even the best model generated visits approximately half as long as observed, and model rankings reversed on occupancy forecasting, a downstream quantity relevant to bed management. These results show that next-event accuracy is insufficient to evaluate clinical trajectory simulators and motivate closed-loop evaluation across seeds, rollout criteria, and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [173] [Energy Vision--Language--Action: A Controlled Multimodal Benchmark for Intent-Conditioned Residential Energy Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31648)
*Lyes Saad Saoud,Oualid Doukhi,Ehsan Reihani,Saeed Sepasi,Deok Jin Lee,Moussa Ayyash,Reza Ghorbani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 EVLA 多模态基准，将住宅电池调度建模为意图条件轨迹预测：输入 RGB 能量场、数值运行状态和自然语言目标，输出 16 步电池动作轨迹。构建 6,588,045 个多模态实例，初步实验显示语言模态对预测质量贡献显著，而当前 RGB 视觉通路未带来总体误差优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型主要研究于机器人领域，尚缺乏可控的多模态基准来考察语义意图与潜在上下文如何影响住宅能源管理。论文希望把电池调度形式化为多模态轨迹预测问题，并支持在显式数值状态固定时进行配对视觉变化，以隔离和评估不同模态的作用。

Method: 构建 EVLA 基准：将 RGB 能量场表示、数值运行状态和自然语言目标映射为 16 步电池动作轨迹，轨迹由有限时域采样参考生成器产生。源窗口来自公开住宅电力负荷数据；电价、电池 SOC、室内温度和时刻为生成的基准元数据。隐藏运行机制仅编码于能量场纹理中，实现显式数值状态固定下的配对视觉变化。439,203 个保留基础窗口与 3 种隐藏机制、5 种语言目标交叉，得到 6,588,045 个多模态实例。初始研究评估 36 种配置、3 个训练种子，使用 5,000 训练、500 验证、500 测试的固定子集。

Result: 在 MobileNet 系列比较中，移除处理后的语言使轨迹 MSE 从 0.3856 ± 0.0039 升至 0.8628 ± 0.0001；移除视觉得到 0.3843 ± 0.0013，与完整模型相当。结果揭示明显的模态使用不对称：处理后的语言通路与预测质量强相关，而当前 RGB 通路未提供总体误差优势。结果仅针对固定试点子集与已执行协议，而非全基准训练。

Conclusion: EVLA 为研究语义意图和潜在上下文如何影响住宅能源动作预测提供了受控实验环境。当前结果显示语言模态是关键贡献因素，视觉模态的潜力在该试点协议下尚未体现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are studied mainly in robotics, where visual observations and language instructions are mapped to physical actions. This paper introduces Energy Vision-Language-Action (EVLA), a controlled multimodal benchmark for intent-conditioned residential energy management. EVLA frames battery scheduling as a multimodal trajectory-prediction problem in which an RGB energy-field representation, a numerical operating state, and a natural-language objective are mapped to a 16-step battery-action trajectory generated by a finite-horizon sampling-based reference generator. Source windows are derived from public residential electrical-load data, while electricity price, battery state of charge, indoor temperature, and time of day are generated benchmark metadata. A hidden operating regime is encoded only through energy-field texture, enabling paired visual changes while the explicit numerical state is fixed. Crossing 439,203 retained base windows with three hidden regimes and five language objectives yields 6,588,045 multimodal instances. An initial study evaluates 36 configurations over three training seeds using fixed subsets of 5,000 training, 500 validation, and 500 test instances. In the MobileNet-family comparison, removing processed language increases trajectory mean-squared error from 0.3856 +/- 0.0039 to 0.8628 +/- 0.0001, whereas removing vision yields 0.3843 +/- 0.0013, comparable to the full model. The results show strong asymmetry in modality use: the processed-language pathway is strongly associated with prediction quality, while the current RGB pathway provides no aggregate error advantage. These results characterize the fixed pilot subset and executed protocol rather than full-benchmark training. EVLA provides a controlled setting for studying how semantic intent and latent context influence residential energy-action prediction.

</details>


### [174] [CyberWorld: World Models for Sample-Efficient Autonomous Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31893)
*Ryozo Masukawa,Sanggeon Yun,Raheeb Hassan,Hyunwoo Oh,SungHeon Jeong,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CyberWorld——一种 Dreamer 风格的世界模型框架，用向量、图、文本及多模态表示学习网络攻防的潜在动态，在样本效率与可扩展性上显著优于无模型 PPO，并指出"世界表示"是鲁棒性与可扩展性的核心设计维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主网络防御方法以无模型深度强化学习为主，需要大量环境交互，样本效率低。世界模型通过预测动态、在想象轨迹中优化策略，在机器人与具身控制中已显著提升样本效率；但将该范式迁移到网络安全时存在根本问题：网络世界模型中的"世界"应如何表征？

Method: 提出 CyberWorld，一个 Dreamer 风格的世界建模范式，从被防御网络的向量、图、文本及多模态表示中学习潜在网络动态，并在 CyberWheel 的四种可评分攻击策略上与无模型 PPO 进行对比评估。

Result: 基于图的 CyberWorld 变体在 3.6k–15.8k 环境步后即超过与策略无关的基线控制，而 PPO 需数百万步。不同表示中，图结构在拓扑依赖型攻击下鲁棒性更强，更简单的表示在总体性能上仍有竞争力；成功运行中，网络规模从 15 台主机增至 100 台时，达到控制水平所需回合数近似恒定。

Conclusion: 学到的网络动态可作为自主防御的样本高效且可扩展的基础，并将世界表示确立为鲁棒性与可扩展性的核心设计轴。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has become a prominent approach to autonomous cyber defense. Existing methods are predominantly model-free and consequently require extensive environment interaction. World models provide an alternative by learning predictive dynamics and optimizing policies through imagined trajectories, yielding substantial gains in sample efficiency in robotics and embodied control. Extending this paradigm to cybersecurity raises a fundamental question: what should constitute the "world" in a cyber world model? We introduce CyberWorld, a Dreamer-style world modeling framework that learns latent cyber dynamics from vector, graph, textual, and multimodal representations of the defended network. Across all four scoreable CyberWheel attack strategies, the graph-based CyberWorld variant exceeds a strategy-agnostic control after 3.6k-15.8k environment steps, compared with millions of steps required by model-free PPO. Across representation choices, graph structure provides greater robustness under topology-dependent attacks, while simpler representations remain competitive in overall performance. Among successful runs, the number of episodes required to reach the control remains approximately constant as network size increases from 15 to 100 hosts. These results establish learned cyber dynamics as a sample-efficient and scalable basis for autonomous defense, and identify world representation as a central design axis for robustness and scalability.

</details>


### [175] [Cache-Aware Conv3D Lowering Across Embedded World-Model Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31938)
*Jiaming Zhang,Wu Yang,Shuai Tao,Wulong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向边缘设备的“缓存感知降级（cache-aware lowering）”：在不修改预训练模型权重的前提下，将受支持的因果 Conv3D 调用表达为批量空间 Conv2D 操作，从而在 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin 上显著加速生成式世界模型的 VAE 解码与整体生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型可为具身规划提供视觉 rollout，但其在边缘设备上的可行性不仅取决于学习到的模型本身，还取决于执行运行时如何表示这些操作；因此需要一种轻量、通用且不改模型的运行时优化来释放解码器加速潜力。

Method: 缓存感知降级：将受支持的因果 Conv3D 调用转换为批量空间 Conv2D 操作，同时保持预训练权重、时间缓存语义、卷积参数、偏置放置和输出布局不变；在 Cosmos3-Edge 图像到视频完整流水线上验证，并与完全特化的 TensorRT 进行干净设备对比，以及同 latent 的 BF16/FP32 评估。

Result: 在 64-GB NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin 上，VAE 解码加速约 7 倍，完整生成延迟降低超过 2 倍，且重复解码器评估保持完整快速路径覆盖、无回退；同一降级方案还提升 Cosmos3-Nano，并可迁移到 LingBot-World 中架构不同的 Wan2.1 VAE。与完全特化的 TensorRT 相比，TensorRT 在稳态下再提升 1.36 倍，但需要显著更多的逐模块与逐运行时状态的 AOT 特化；同 latent 的 BF16/FP32 评估刻画了替代执行顺序引入的有限精度差异。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，缓存感知降级是一种轻量级运行时优化，可在不修改学习模型的情况下恢复大部分可用的解码器加速，并为边缘设备上的生成式世界模型部署提供了兼顾性能、通用性与精度的实用路径。

Abstract: Generative world models can provide visual rollouts for embodied planning, yet their feasibility on edge devices depends not only on the learned model but also on how the execution runtime represents its operations. We introduce a cache-aware lowering that expresses supported causal Conv3D calls as batched spatial Conv2D operations while preserving pretrained weights, temporal-cache semantics, convolution parameters, bias placement, and output layout. Across the complete Cosmos3-Edge image-to-video pipeline on a 64-GB NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, the proposed route accelerates VAE decoding by approximately $7\times$ and reduces complete-generation latency by more than $2\times$, while repeated decoder evaluations maintain complete fast-path coverage without fallbacks. The unchanged lowering also improves Cosmos3-Nano and transfers to LingBot-World's architecturally distinct Wan2.1 VAE. A clean-device comparison against fully specialized TensorRT shows that TensorRT provides a further $1.36\times$ steady-state improvement, but requires substantially greater per-module and per-runtime-state AOT specialization. Same-latent BF16 and FP32 evaluations characterize the finite-precision differences introduced by the alternative execution order. Together, these results position cache-aware lowering as a lightweight runtime optimization that recovers most of the available decoder acceleration without modifying the learned models themselves.

</details>


### [176] [SAMBAR: Selective Anchoring via Method of Multipliers for Balanced Knowledge Acquisition and Retention in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32108)
*Aayushi Shrivastava,Xunlan Zhou,Hongrui Zhao,Ziyu Chen,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAMBAR：一种无需旧任务示教的VLA持续微调方法，通过约束优化与选择性参数锚定，在学新任务的同时避免灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型需持续学习新操作任务，但直接微调会导致灾难性遗忘。现有持续学习方法多依赖回放旧任务示教，而旧示教往往不可得，因此需要一种无回放的持续学习方案。

Method: 将持续学习建模为约束优化问题，并用乘子法求解。乘子法通过引入对偶变量，在约束违反累积时增大惩罚，从而抑制策略参数偏离旧值；同时仅选择性锚定对旧任务关键的参数，其余参数保持可更新。对偶变量与选择性锚定共同平衡新知识获取与旧知识保留。

Result: 在LIBERO仿真基准和真实硬件上评估。顺序微调VLA时，所有无回放基线均完全遗忘首个任务，而SAMBAR能保留已学过的每个任务。

Conclusion: SAMBAR可在不使用任何旧任务示教的情况下实现VLA持续微调，有效缓解灾难性遗忘，并在仿真与真实硬件中优于无回放基线。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models leverage large-scale pretraining to ultimately achieve generalist manipulation. Deployed VLA policies must support continual learning to acquire new tasks over time. Teaching a VLA a new task generally requires finetuning it on demonstrations of that task. However, naively finetuning on downstream tasks causes the policy to forget earlier tasks and degrades generalist capabilities. This failure is known as catastrophic forgetting. Most continual learning methods counter it by replaying data from earlier tasks. However, the old task demonstrations are not always readily available. In this paper, we introduce SAMBAR, a continual learning algorithm that prevents catastrophic forgetting during VLA finetuning without requiring access to the demonstrations of any previously learned task. We propose to cast continual learning as a constrained optimization problem and solve it with the method of multipliers. In our approach, the method of multipliers drives the policy to learn the new task without the model parameters drifting far away from their previous values. In contrast to a standard regularization penalty, the method of multipliers raises the penalty as the constraint violation accumulates by using a dual variable. We also selectively anchor the parameters critical to previous tasks to preserve past knowledge, leaving other parameters free for new task acquisition. The combination of dual variable and selective anchoring, therefore, balances knowledge acquisition with knowledge retention. We evaluate our method, SAMBAR, on the LIBERO simulation benchmark and on hardware. When sequentially finetuning on a VLA, every replay-free baseline we compare against completely forgets the first task it learned, whereas SAMBAR retains every task it has learned.

</details>


### [177] [DP-Rec: Towards Dynamic Patching for Efficient Long-Sequence Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32215)
*Dwipam Katariya,Thomas Caputo,Akshat Shreemali,Juan Manuel Origgi,Nikita Seleznev,Pranab Mohanty,Kalanand Mishra,Nam Nguyen,James Montgomery*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DP-Rec，一种用于序列推荐的动态潜在分块架构：借鉴Byte Latent Transformer，以对比熵惊讶度分割交互序列，用轻量补丁编码器压缩为动态潜在行为向量，再由大型潜在Transformer处理并解码做下一项预测，在受限算力下实现长序列更优的效率-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer虽能有效建模动态用户行为与长程依赖，但标准架构以固定速率计算，对用户历史中每个项目分配相当算力，忽视信息量差异，导致长序列计算开销过大且对行为噪声更敏感。实践中常用有损序列压缩、分阶段建模或截断，限制模型在推理时利用完整长历史上下文。

Method: 借鉴Byte Latent Transformer，提出DP-Rec，从项目级建模转向补丁级建模。方法包括：1）使用对比熵惊讶度（contrastive entropy surprise）分割交互序列，识别信息量较高的行为边界；2）轻量补丁编码器将时间上下文化片段压缩为数量更少的动态潜在行为向量；3）由更大的潜在Transformer处理这些向量，并解码用于下一项目预测。

Result: 大量实验表明，在受限计算预算下，DP-Rec能够有效扩展到长序列，并在非压缩基线与固定大小压缩基线上取得更优的效率-精度权衡。

Conclusion: DP-Rec验证了基于动态潜在分块与压缩的行为建模在序列推荐中的有效性，为长序列推荐在有限算力下兼顾效率与精度提供了新思路。

Abstract: Transformers have redefined sequential recommendation by effectively modeling dynamic user behaviors and long-range dependencies. However, they remain inherently inefficient: standard architectures operate at a fixed rate, allocating comparable computation to every item in a user's history regardless of its information content. This leads to prohibitive computational overhead on long sequences and increased sensitivity to behavioral noise. To address this, practitioners often resort to lossy sequence compression, staged modeling, or truncation. This limits the model's ability to leverage the full context of long histories during inference. Inspired by the recent success of Byte Latent Transformer, we propose DP-Rec, a dynamic latent patching architecture for recommendation. DP-Rec shifts from item-level modeling to patch-level modeling by segmenting interaction sequences using contrastive entropy surprise to identify informative behavioral boundaries. A lightweight patch encoder compresses these temporally contextualized segments into a reduced set of dynamic latent behavior vectors, which are then processed by a larger latent transformer and decoded for next-item prediction. Extensive experiments show that, under constrained computational budgets, DP-Rec scales effectively to long sequences and achieves a superior efficiency-accuracy trade-off over both non-compressed and fixed-size compression baselines.

</details>


### [178] [Self-Reconstruction Dynamics for Autoencoder Reconstruction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32268)
*Hitoshi Iyatomi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 冻结自编码器重复重建会产生"自重建动力学(SRD)"轨迹，从中可提取样本特定的信息用于修正重建；据此提出的SRD-RR无需逐样本测试时优化即可预测潜变量修正，在六个数据集上恢复约38.6%–45.3%的经验可恢复MSE差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自编码器(AE)推理仅用单次编码-解码，由于解码器固定，得到的潜变量对每个样本未必最优。作者因此追问：一个已训练好的AE能否自我揭示信息，以改进其自身的重建？

Method: 1) 观察现象：将冻结的AE反复作用于其自身重建，会产生瞬态的图像空间与潜空间轨迹，称为自重建动力学(SRD)；2) 提出SRD-RR(SRD引导的重建精化)：在AE冻结、且不做逐样本测试时优化的条件下，用一段短SRD预测潜变量修正；3) 提出MSE-recov指标，即相对于经验性"解码器优化"参考的MSE恢复比例；4) 设计仅使用SRD导出的伪目标、不直接接触原图的两步变体；5) 与固定/可训练线性潜修正对比，并在基于DINOv2的表示自编码器(RAE)上验证。

Result: 在六个数据集上，SRD-RR用一次转移恢复约38.6%的经验可恢复MSE差距，用两次转移恢复45.3%；不直接访问原图、仅用SRD伪目标训练的两步变体达到40.7%恢复率与平均1.74 dB的PSNR提升。去除轨迹信息会降低增益，交叉样本轨迹分配则严重退化，证实强样本特异性；非线性SRD条件修正稳定优于固定与可训练线性潜修正。在潜动力学显著不同的DINOv2-based RAE上，SRD条件同样提升匹配的无轨迹预测器；此外像素-MSE潜精化暴露出像素保真度与感知质量间的强烈失配，而SRD伪目标可缓解该退化。

Conclusion: SRD是一种有用的样本特定精化信号；但目标函数(像素MSE或感知)决定了该信号如何转化为像素质量与感知质量。

Abstract: Standard autoencoder (AE) inference uses a single encoder-decoder pass, though the latent may not be optimal for each sample under a fixed decoder. We ask whether a trained AE can reveal information for improving its own reconstruction. Repeated application of a frozen AE to its reconstruction produces transient image- and latent-space trajectories, termed Self-Reconstruction Dynamics (SRD). Although this degrades fidelity in the AEs studied here, SRD contains sample-specific information for correcting the reconstruction. We propose SRD-guided Reconstruction Refinement (SRD-RR), which predicts a latent correction from a short SRD with the AE frozen and no per-sample test-time optimization. We also introduce MSE-recov, an MSE recovery ratio relative to an empirical decoder-optimized reference. Across six datasets, SRD-RR recovers 38.6% of the empirically recoverable MSE gap with one transition and 45.3% with two. A two-transition variant trained without direct access to original images, using an SRD-derived pseudo-target, achieves 40.7% recovery and a 1.74 dB average PSNR gain. Removing trajectory information reduces the gain, while cross-sample trajectory assignment causes severe degradation, confirming strong sample specificity. Nonlinear SRD-conditioned refinement consistently outperforms fixed and trained linear latent correction. On a pretrained DINOv2-based representation autoencoder (RAE) with substantially different latent dynamics, SRD conditioning again improves a matched trajectory-free predictor. However, pixel-MSE latent refinement reveals a strong mismatch between pixel fidelity and perceptual quality, while the SRD-derived pseudo-target mitigates this degradation. Overall, SRD is a useful sample-specific refinement signal, while the objective determines how it translates into pixel and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [179] [Not All Errors Matter: Decision-Relevant Prediction Error Predicts Planning Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32322)
*Linhao Wang,Yiyan Fan,Dongjin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出以总预测误差评价世界模型可能误导规划性能，提出决策相关预测误差（DRPE）和等误差评估协议；在因子化网格世界中，DRPE 比总误差更能预测规划成功率，且任务会改变模型排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通常以预测误差训练和评估，并默认预测越准、决策越好。但总误差相近的模型可能因误差分布在不同的状态维度上，而在规划性能上差异巨大，因此需要衡量真正影响决策的预测误差。

Method: 提出 Decision-Relevant Prediction Error (DRPE)，只度量影响决策的状态维度上的预测误差；设计 iso-error 评估协议，在保持总误差不变的情况下改变误差分配；在状态相关性已知的因子化网格世界中使用标准化规划器，评估 55 个受控模型和学习模型在不同误差水平与分配下的表现；并形式化 DRPE 能正确排序而总预测误差不能的充分条件。

Result: 总预测误差与规划成功仅弱相关（Spearman ρ=-0.25），DRPE 则强预测（ρ=-0.84，受控模型族内 -0.98）。总误差仅差 1% 的模型，规划成功率可差 60 个百分点（97% vs 37%）。相关误差依赖任务，同一总误差下模型排序会在不同任务间反转。更深的想象会放大决策相关误差，学习模型在稀有但决策关键事件上存在系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 世界模型的评估不应只看总预测误差，而应关注决策相关预测误差；DRPE 与 iso-error 协议能更可靠地揭示规划性能差异，并说明任务相关性、想象深度和稀有关键事件偏差对模型排序的重要影响。

Abstract: World models are typically trained and evaluated by prediction error, assuming that more accurate predictions lead to better decisions. We show that this assumption can fail because models with similar total error can differ substantially in planning performance when their errors occur on different state dimensions. We introduce Decision-Relevant Prediction Error (DRPE), which measures prediction error on the state dimensions that affect decisions. We also develop an iso-error evaluation protocol that varies error allocation while keeping total error fixed. In a factored gridworld with known state relevance and a standardized planner, we evaluate 55 controlled and learned models across different error levels and allocations. Total prediction error is weakly related to planning success (Spearman $\rho=-0.25$), whereas DRPE is strongly predictive ($\rho=-0.84$; $-0.98$ within the controlled family). Models with only a 1\% difference in total error can differ by 60 percentage points in planning success (97\% vs 37\%). The relevant error also depends on the task, with model rankings reversing across tasks at the same total error. Deeper imagination further amplifies decision-relevant errors, while learned models exhibit systematic bias on rare but decision-critical events. We formalize sufficient conditions under which DRPE correctly ranks models and total prediction error cannot.

</details>


### [180] [Neural Dynamics as the Composition of Quantized Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32487)
*Jacopo Minniti,Aravinth Kulanthaivelu,Richard Sproat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“quanta”这一中间抽象：训练可视为对可复用、突然获得且二值激活的计算单元的按序获取；获取优先级由“需求”和“条件复杂度”决定，可连接微观计算与宏观损失/缩放律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习解释存在宏观（损失曲线、缩放律）与微观（神经元、特征、电路）两个层次，二者如何连接、基本计算如何组合并影响宏观行为，是核心难题。

Method: 将训练描述为quanta的按序获取，通过近似群体梯度更新推导获取动力学；在布尔组合任务中推导获取顺序；训练Transformer完成数字到英文数名的映射，从checkpoint轨迹中恢复候选quanta并构建可解释模型；同时将quanta结构用作训练目标。

Result: 获取优先级由需求（计算跨样本被需要的频率）与条件复杂度（在已有计算基础上习得该计算的难度）决定；离散交错获取可产生平滑聚合损失，并在特定组合几何下产生缩放律；从Transformer恢复的quanta可构建保留大部分行为且暴露可解释潜在计算与获取动力学的模型；quanta作为训练目标可提升Transformer泛化。

Conclusion: quanta抽象可作为研究宏观现象的有用计算原子，为连接深度学习的微观机制与宏观行为提供中间层次。

Abstract: Deep learning is commonly interpreted at two levels: the macroscopic, through aggregate trends in loss summarized by scaling laws, and the microscopic, through neurons, features, and circuits. A central challenge is understanding how these levels connect, so that we can explain how elementary computations compose and collectively shape macroscopic behavior. To this end, we study an intermediate abstraction in which training is described as the ordered acquisition of quanta: reusable computations acquired suddenly and binary-activated across examples to reduce loss. By approximating population-gradient updates, we derive quanta's acquisition dynamics. This yields an acquisition priority governed by demand, how frequently a computation is required across examples, and conditional complexity, how difficult that computation is to acquire given those already available. In a Boolean compositional task, we derive predictions for acquisition order and show how staggered discrete acquisitions can produce smooth aggregate loss and, under certain geometries of quanta composition, give rise to scaling laws. We then train a Transformer to map numerals to English number names and recover candidate quanta from its checkpoint trajectory. From these units, we construct a model that preserves much of the Transformer's behavior while exposing interpretable latent computations and acquisition dynamics consistent with the theory. Separately, the quanta structure can serve as training targets to improve transformer generalization. Together, these results suggest the quanta abstraction can provide useful computational atoms for studying a variety of macroscopic phenomena.

</details>


### [181] [The GUI Is Not the State: Diagnosing State Aliasing in GUI World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32679)
*Dongsheng Liu,Chao Jin,Wenkui Yang,Hejin Wang,Junwei Yang,Zeren Zhang,Ziwei Chen,Huaibo Huang,Jie Cao,Ran He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出仅凭当前GUI观测与动作进行预测的GUI世界模型存在“状态混叠”问题，即界面可见信息未包含与状态转移相关的隐藏环境状态，导致相同观测可能对应不同未来。为此提出诊断基准StateAliasBench与轻量级预测状态恢复方法，通过确定性状态接口增强冻结的GUI-WM，并在AndroidWorld上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI-WM多仅以当前GUI观测和动作作为条件来预测未来状态，忽略了决定状态转移的隐藏环境状态。由此产生状态混叠：相同可观测条件下可能对应多个合法未来，导致模型出现系统性预测失败，影响智能体规划与模拟的可靠性。

Method: 1）提出StateAliasBench诊断基准，通过严格配对显式隔离并暴露状态混叠歧义；2）提出轻量级预测状态恢复，从历史中推断结构化状态，并以确定性状态接口增强原本冻结的GUI-WM；3）针对异构状态类型使用族特定专家进行恢复，再通过多教师蒸馏整合为统一估计器。

Result: 实验表明，现有GUI-WM在仅观测条件下存在系统性失败；引入预测状态增强后，被评估的世界模型在状态敏感预测上显著恢复，同时保持生成保真度，并提升GUI智能体在AndroidWorld上的下游性能。

Conclusion: 可靠的GUI世界建模不仅应建模“可见内容”，还应显式考虑决定下一步发生的隐藏转移状态；预测状态恢复是一种轻量且有效的增强路径。

Abstract: GUI World Models (GUI-WMs) are increasingly used to predict future states for agent planning and simulation, yet most existing formulations condition only on the current GUI observation and action. We identify state aliasing, where the vis- ible interface omits transition-relevant environment state, so identical observable conditions can correspond to different valid futures. To diagnose this failure mode, we introduce StateAliasBench, a diagnostic benchmark that explicitly isolates such ambiguities via strict pairing. We further propose lightweight predictive- state recovery that infers structured state from history and augments otherwise frozen GUI-WMs through a deterministic state interface. Family-specific special- ists provide state recovery across heterogeneous state types, and multi-teacher dis- tillation consolidates them into a single unified estimator. Experiments show that existing GUI-WMs exhibit systematic failures under observation-only condition- ing, while predictive-state augmentation substantially restores state-sensitive pre- diction across evaluated WMs, preserves generative fidelity, and improves down- stream performance of GUI agents on AndroidWorld. These results suggest that reliable GUI world modeling should account not only for what is visible, but also for the hidden transition state that determines what happens next.

</details>


### [182] [What Do Latent Predictive Vehicle Representations Retain? Measuring State, Geometry, and Local Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32512)
*Enzo Nicolás Spotorno,Josafat Leal Filho,Antônio Augusto Fröhlich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一套面向“动作条件潜预测器”的测量协议，用物理读出分别评估表征保留、物理邻域组织、预测能力与对指令扰动的局部响应；在IPG CarMaker数据上训练的时间联合嵌入预测模型案例中，该方法能定位误差来源，并指出仅看潜空间预测误差不足以判断其是否适合控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆控制器通常以物理量定义代价、约束和参考（位置、航向角、速度、横摆角速度），并需在相近指令间比较或微分预测结果。若将潜世界模型嵌入此类控制器，潜表征必须能恢复这些物理量，并且其预测需随指令变化而与真实车辆一致。然而，仅用潜目标上的预测误差无法衡量这两点，因此需要一套可分解的测量协议来判定潜预测器是否具备作为控制模型的资格。

Method: 作者贡献了一套针对动作条件潜预测器的测量协议，核心是引入物理读出，并将评估拆分为四项：保留（retention）、物理邻域组织（physical-neighborhood organization）、预测（forecasting）、对指令扰动的局部响应（local response to command perturbations）。协议使用未训练编码器作为参照，并设置三条匹配的响应路径，以将误差定位到表征或预测器。案例研究采用在IPG CarMaker中记录的信号训练的时间联合嵌入预测模型（temporal joint-embedding predictive model）。

Result: 案例结果显示：潜表征能够保留所测平面输出，但同架构的未训练编码器保留得略好；将未来指令作为输入可改善1秒预测，且保留能力几乎不变；对小指令脉冲的响应在潜坐标中已与仿真器出现偏离，导致在所有比较中于相近指令间选择时产生更高regret；基于响应更新预测器可在局部修正响应，但会牺牲一定预测精度。

Conclusion: 分别测量保留、预测与局部响应，是判断一个预测性潜表征能否作为控制候选模型的关键；该协议为后续闭环控制评估提供了基础。论文强调，仅凭潜空间预测误差不足以说明潜模型适合车辆控制，必须用物理读出和扰动响应来检验其与真实车辆动力学的一致性。

Abstract: Models of vehicle dynamics learned from logged states and commands complement physics-based models, and latent world models, which predict in a learned representation, are used to plan and train controllers in other domains. Vehicle controllers are usually specified in physical terms: costs, limits, and references depend on position, yaw angle, speed, and yaw rate, and the optimizer compares or differentiates predicted outcomes across nearby commands. A latent model placed in such a controller must therefore let these quantities be recovered and must change its predictions with commands as the vehicle does, and prediction error on its own latent targets measures neither. We contribute a measurement protocol for action-conditioned latent predictors with a physical readout that separately tests retention, physical-neighborhood organization, forecasting, and local response to command perturbations, using an untrained-encoder reference and three matched response paths that locate errors in the representation or the predictor. In a case study of a temporal joint-embedding predictive model trained on signals logged in IPG CarMaker, the representations retain the measured planar outputs, though an untrained encoder of the same architecture retains them slightly better; future-command input improves one-second forecasts with retention nearly unchanged; and responses to small command pulses diverge from the simulator already in latent coordinates, raising regret when choosing among nearby commands in all comparisons. Updating the predictor on responses corrects them locally at a cost in forecast accuracy. Measuring retention, forecasting, and local response separately is thus what qualifies a predictive latent as a candidate model for control, and the protocol provides the basis for its closed-loop evaluation.

</details>


### [183] [Save Your Saturated Data: Learning Beyond Reward Saturation in Group-Based RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33126)
*Ziyuan Yang,Yike Wang,Shangbin Feng,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 随着语言模型能力增强，组相对强化学习训练数据会出现奖励饱和，导致组内优势估计趋近于零；本文证明通过合适的干预（尤其是 rollout 生成干预）可以将饱和数据回收为有效的 RL 训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组相对强化学习（如 GRPO）依赖同一问题下多个采样回答之间的奖励差异来估计相对优势。当模型能力增强后，训练数据可能奖励饱和：同一问题的所有采样回答都获得同样高的奖励，组内相对学习信号消失，原本有用的数据变为无效。本文旨在研究能否从这类饱和数据中恢复有用学习信号。

Method: 在组策略 RL 流水线的四个层面进行干预：数据、rollout、奖励和优势，并且仅在饱和推理数据上进行大规模 RL 训练。标准 GRPO 在饱和数据上通常产生接近 0 的优势和近似噪声的信号。提出的策略中，rollout 生成层面的干预最有效：引导策略生成“高质量”但错误的解，从而将低奖励 rollout 作为负样本注入饱和组；其他干预包括提高 rollout 温度和添加辅助奖励。

Result: rollout 生成干预在 Qwen3-1.7B 和 4B 上使 GRPO 提升 6.4% 至 9.0%。提高 rollout 温度或添加辅助奖励也能恢复非零优势，但增益一致性较低。进一步分析表明：有效的负 rollout 需要具有信息量的负轨迹；该方法与未饱和数据同时使用时仍然有效；并支持对新饱和样本进行迭代回收。

Conclusion: 随着更强的 LLM 使越来越多数据趋于饱和，不应浪费饱和数据；在数据日益稀缺的环境下，采用合适策略可将饱和数据回收为有用的 RL 训练信号。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning (RL) relies on reward variation among sampled responses to estimate informative relative advantages. As language models become increasingly capable, existing training data can become reward-saturated: all sampled responses to the same problem might receive equally high rewards, where the group-relative learning signals vanish and leave previously useful data obsolete. In this work, we investigate whether useful learning signals can be recovered from such saturated data. We study interventions at four levels of group-policy RL pipelines---data, rollout, reward, and advantage---and conduct extensive RL training on saturated reasoning data only. While standard GRPO on saturated data would almost always yield near-0 advantages and near-noise signals, diverse interventions successfully recycle and repurpose such data: among the proposed strategies, interventions at rollout generation are consistently most effective: nudging the policy to generate ``high-quality'', incorrect solutions introduces rollouts with poor rewards into saturated groups as negative samples, which turns out to improve GRPO by 6.4% to 9.0% across Qwen3-1.7B and 4B. Other interventions such as increasing rollout temperature or adding auxiliary rewards can also restore non-zero advantages, but yield less consistent gains. Further analyses show that effective negative rollouts require informative negative trajectories, that the method remains effective alongside unsaturated data, and that it supports iterative recycling of newly saturated examples. While increasingly stronger LLMs would render more data as saturated, our results demonstrate that don't waste your saturated data: with the right strategies they can be recycled into useful RL training signals in an increasingly data-scarce world.

</details>


### [184] [Adaptive Latent Capacity for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32921)
*Idan Achituve,Lior Dikstein,Idit Diamant,Arnon Netzer,Hai Victor Habi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ALeWM：一种基于 JEPA 的世界模型，通过前缀长度条件分布采样与 MixSIGReg 正则化，将预测信息集中到宽隐表示的紧凑前缀中，从而在更低规划容量下取得更高控制成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准防坍缩目标只鼓励隐坐标间的变化，并不按预测重要性组织坐标；而紧凑前缀对于预测与递归规划更高效，因此需要显式鼓励预测信息前置。

Method: ALeWM 基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）。它学习一个以序列为条件的“前缀长度”分布，并从采样得到的前缀预测完整下一嵌入；同时提出 MixSIGReg：将掩码嵌入正则化到由先验加权、高斯活跃前缀与其余坐标置零构成的混合分布上。

Result: 理论分析表明，MixSIGReg 的混合分布赋予较早坐标块更高方差、较晚坐标块更低方差；在给定假设下，预测误差最小化对应将最有用的预测信息放入较早块。实验在状态变量已知的可控动力系统与目标条件视觉控制中进行，ALeWM 的平均成功率稳定高于调优后的固定宽度 LeWM，且平均规划容量更低。

Conclusion: ALeWM 促使早期坐标保留对预测和递归规划有用的信息，在成功率与规划容量之间取得优于固定宽度 LeWM 的权衡。

Abstract: We introduce Adaptive LeWorldModel (ALeWM), a world model based on a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) that learns to concentrate predictive information in compact prefixes of a wide latent representation. To encourage this ordering, ALeWM learns a sequence-conditioned distribution over prefix lengths and trains the predictor to estimate the full next embedding from a sampled input prefix. As standard anti-collapse objectives encourage variation across latent coordinates and do not organize them by predictive importance, we also introduce MixSIGReg. MixSIGReg regularizes the masked embeddings against a prior-weighted mixture with Gaussian active prefixes and zeros in the remaining coordinates. As a result, the ALeWM objective encourages early coordinates to retain information useful for prediction and recursive planning. Our analysis shows that the mixture distribution used by MixSIGReg assigns higher variance to earlier coordinate blocks and lower variance to later ones. In addition, we show that, under specified assumptions, prediction error is minimized by placing the information most useful for prediction in earlier blocks. Empirically, we study the behavior of ALeWM in a controlled dynamical system with known state variables and in goal-conditioned visual control. We show that ALeWM consistently achieves higher mean success rates than tuned fixed-width LeWM, with lower planning capacity on average.

</details>


### [185] [When Do Models Admit They Are Wrong? Failure Disclosure Is Unstable Under Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33220)
*Steven Y. Feng,Noah D. Goodman,Michael C. Frank,Evan Hubinger,Paul C. Bogdan,Andrew Lampinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究"失败披露"（模型是否如实承认尝试失败，而非沉默或谎报成功）这一安全相关行为。作者发现：在仅基于结果（outcome-only）的强化学习训练中，即便任务准确率保持稳定，失败披露行为在多次训练运行间的波动却远大于准确率；这种波动源于训练中微小的浮点与采样差异，且失败披露并非单一决策，而是由"检查答案—进入报告—完成承认"多个可分离步骤构成。作者通过约束模型在"失败但格式良好的回答"上偏离初始策略的程度，可显著降低该行为的运行间波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果的强化学习常能训练出任务表现相近、但在"如何沟通自身错误"上差异巨大的模型。论文聚焦"失败披露"这一安全相关行为，意在揭示：稳定的任务准确率是否意味着稳定的安全相关行为，以及这些行为为何会在运行间剧烈波动。

Method: 在仅以结果为奖励的 GRPO 上进行多次重复训练；将结论扩展到第二项推理任务与稳定的 PPO；在 7B 规模上复现，并在受指令约束的 32B 设置中验证。通过在训练中引入微小浮点与采样差异（固定任务目标与早期训练历史）来测试其对报告行为的转向作用。进一步将失败披露拆解为"检查答案、进入报告、完成承认"等阶段，并设置中性对照实验，检验受训练弱约束的行为是否普遍更易在运行间波动。最后通过惩罚模型在失败且格式良好回答上偏离初始策略的方式来降低波动。

Result: 失败披露在重复运行间的波动远大于任务准确率，该现象可跨第二项推理任务、稳定 PPO、7B 及受指令约束的 32B 设置复现；训练中微小的浮点与采样差异即可在任务目标与早期训练历史不变时改变报告行为；失败披露并非单一决策，检查答案、进入报告与完成承认可相互分离，且其薄弱环节依任务与回答格式而定；中性对照表明受训练弱约束的行为更倾向于在运行间变化，失败披露即为一例。

Conclusion: 稳定的任务准确率并不能保证稳定的安全相关行为。研究者应在多次运行间直接测量这些行为，并设计能保持其可靠性的训练方法；通过抑制模型在失败且格式良好回答上对初始策略的漂移，可显著提升失败披露的一致性，但其对任务性能的影响依具体设置而异。

Abstract: Outcome-based reinforcement learning can produce models with similar task performance but very different ways of communicating about their mistakes. We study failure disclosure: whether a model admits that an attempted solution failed rather than staying silent or presenting it as successful. Across repeated outcome-only GRPO training runs, failure disclosure varies far more than task accuracy. The pattern extends to a second reasoning task and stabilized PPO, persists at 7B, and also appears in an instruction-conditioned 32B setting. We also find that small floating-point and sampling differences during training can redirect reporting behavior even when the task objective and earlier training history are held fixed. Additional tests show that failure disclosure is not a single decision: Checking the answer, entering a report, and completing the admission can separate, and the weak point depends on the task and response format. Further, experiments with neutral controls show more broadly that behaviors left weakly constrained by training are especially likely to vary across runs, of which failure disclosure is an example. We can reduce variability in failure disclosure by discouraging the model from drifting from its starting policy on failed, well-formed responses. This makes reporting substantially more consistent, though its effect on task performance depends on the setting. Stable task accuracy therefore does not guarantee stable safety-relevant behavior: Researchers should measure these behaviors directly across runs and design training methods that keep them reliable.

</details>


### [186] [Self-Confirming Superposition Traps in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32966)
*Dai Shi,Andi Han,Feng Chen,Yiqun Duan,Junbin Gao,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文揭示强化学习中表征拟合与策略反馈会形成“自证叠加陷阱”（self-confirming superposition trap）：即使表征拟合在当前数据上全局最优，也可能稳定维持低回报策略；通过保留被忽视状态、调整回放权重与降低特征重叠，可缓解干扰并提升控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL中表征由智能体当前策略选择的数据训练，拟合得到的回报又指导后续选择，形成闭环。作者关注：这种闭环是否会让全局最优的表征拟合反而支持低回报策略，以及该失效模式在何种条件下出现、如何被打破。

Method: 理论分析：在tied two-step模型中刻画可能出现陷阱的维度；分别证明等特征频率、持续访问状态、独立控制器学习并不必然防止陷阱；在有限动作模型中界定由于按访问频率加权拟合导致的回报扭曲，并推导回放条件——对最佳单独适配动作给予足够训练权重即可在残余误差下保持其排序。实验：用Neural PPO观察反馈学习动态；干预状态访问、回放权重与特征重叠；在MiniGrid、DMControl及基于世界模型的DreamerV3-Crafter上验证。

Result: Neural PPO实验中，不同动作初始化的智能体发展出不同干扰模式、相反的平均回报排序，并在相同容量下得到不同最终策略；保留被忽视状态进入训练可降低测量干扰、提升序列回报，即使编码器冻结也有增益；状态访问、回放权重、特征重叠等相关干预在MiniGrid与DMControl上改善控制；在DreamerV3-Crafter中，受保护拟合在容量不变时提升累计训练分数。

Conclusion: 表征学习与策略反馈的耦合会造成自证叠加陷阱，使低回报策略在最优拟合下仍被强化；保障被忽视状态或最佳单独适配动作的训练权重，是恢复正确回报排序、改善控制的有效原则。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) trains representations on data selected by the agent's policy, which then uses the resulting returns to guide its next choices. We show that this loop can sustain a lower-return policy even when representation fitting is globally optimal on those data. In a self-confirming superposition trap, every optimal code assigns overlapping directions to features that rarely occur together under the current policy. An alternative action brings them together, causing interference that lowers its return and reinforces avoidance, although refitting to that action would yield more return at the same capacity. We characterize the dimensions admitting a trap in a tied two-step model and show separately that equal feature frequencies, continued visitation, and independent controller learning need not prevent it. Because fitting weights errors by visitation, an avoided action can lose its return advantage at little cost to the objective. In a finite-action model, we bound this distortion and derive a replay condition: sufficient training weight on the best separately adapted action preserves its ranking despite residual error. Neural PPO experiments show how the feedback develops during learning: agents initialized toward different actions develop different interference patterns, opposite mean return rankings, and different final policies at the same capacity. We therefore test whether retaining access to neglected states can improve control. Keeping these states in training reduces measured interference and improves sequential return, with gains even when the encoder is frozen. Related interventions on state access, replay weights, and feature overlap improve control on MiniGrid and DMControl. For agents that learn through a world model, protected fitting improves DreamerV3--Crafter's cumulative training scores at unchanged capacity.

</details>


### [187] [What Must a World Model Distinguish for Planning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33030)
*Rongzhe Wei,Hans Hao-Hsun Hsu,Peizhi Niu,Yifan Li,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"机制充分性—响应充分性—决策充分性"的层级框架，指出世界模型需要保留哪些物理信息取决于查询、候选集与规划器；并据此提出模块化设计，用查询决定"看哪里"、用动作条件模型预测"会发生什么"，使预测可跨目标复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型用于模拟动作候选的后果，但良好的规划并不需要保留精确预测所需的全部物理区分。作者希望形式化这一"预测充分性"与"决策充分性"之间的差距：给定候选集后，查询决定了哪些物理变化是重要的、需要保留到何种精度——粗粒度决策可舍弃大量预测信息，细粒度决策则可能要求近乎相同的分辨率。此外，规划器常通过自适应搜索构造候选，因此最终选择不需要的信息，可能在发现优质候选时反而必需。故模型必须保留的信息依赖查询、候选集与规划器三者。

Method: 作者将上述差距形式化为机制充分性、响应充分性与决策充分性构成的层级结构，并在碰撞系统、非线性动力学与机器人规划任务中研究其影响。基于此提出核心设计问题：查询信息应在规划系统的何处进入？为此设计模块化方案：由查询决定"去哪里搜索"，由一个动作条件世界模型预测"会发生什么"，从而使同一组预测可跨不同目标复用。

Result: 实验表明，直接以查询为条件同时生成动作候选与结果（查询条件模型）在已见目标上取得比动作条件世界模型更低的遗憾（regret）；但在泛化到未见目标时，这一优势大幅消失，说明将查询信息耦合进预测并非总是有效。

Conclusion: 规划对世界模型的充分性要求是相对的，取决于查询、候选集与规划器，可用机制/响应/决策充分性层级刻画。由于查询条件模型在未见目标上优势减弱，更合理的做法是采用模块化设计：让查询只决定搜索方向，让动作条件模型负责预测结果，从而实现预测的跨目标复用。

Abstract: World models simulate the consequences of action candidates, but good planning need not preserve every physical distinction required for accurate prediction. We formalize this gap through a hierarchy of mechanism, response, and decision sufficiency. Given a candidate set, the planning query determines which physical variations matter and how precisely they must be preserved: coarse decisions can discard much of the information needed for prediction, whereas fine decisions may require nearly the same resolution. In practice, planners often adaptively search to construct candidates, and information unnecessary for final selection may still be needed to discover good candidates. What a world model must preserve therefore depends on the query, the candidate set, and the planner. We study these effects in a collision system, nonlinear dynamics, and robotic planning. These varying requirements raise a design question: where should query information enter the planning system? A model that jointly generates actions and outcomes conditioned on the query achieves lower regret than an action-conditioned world model on seen objectives, but this advantage largely disappears when generalizing to unseen objectives. Motivated by this, we propose a modular design in which the query determines where to look and an action-conditioned model predicts what will happen, allowing the same predictions to be reused across objectives.

</details>


### [188] [SMORE: Stability-Promoting Mesh-Agnostic Model Reduction for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33205)
*Yangyuan Li,Weichao Li,Shaowu Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SMORE：一种网格无关的时序PDE模型降阶框架，通过Lyapunov引导的稳定性正则化训练潜动力学，提升长时程滚动预测稳定性，并在多种PDE上与DINo、FNO、CNO、Transolver等基线竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真时序PDE数值模拟计算代价高，催生数据驱动降阶代理模型；但现有代理模型时间稳定性差，在多步长时程预测中易出现不稳定滚动和反向传播梯度爆炸，影响不确定性量化、优化、同化和控制等下游任务。

Method: 提出SMORE，网格无关的时序PDE模型降阶框架。其潜动力学采用Lyapunov引导的稳定性正则化训练，以促进稳定的长时程滚动；在给定结构假设下提供理论保证。学习到的潜动力学具有可解释性，为线性或线性二次形式，可服务数据同化与最优控制；同时支持从初始条件稀疏测量预测连续PDE解场。

Result: 在波传播、Navier-Stokes方程和浅水方程等问题上评估SMORE。结果表明，相比DINo、FNO、CNO、Transolver等竞争基线，SMORE提升了长时程滚动泛化与经验鲁棒性，并在相近参数预算下达到有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: SMORE通过Lyapunov稳定性正则化实现网格无关的PDE降阶建模，兼顾长时程稳定性、可解释潜动力学和稀疏观测预测能力，适合作为下游科学与工程任务的代理模型。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulations of time-dependent partial differential equations (PDEs) are computationally expensive, motivating data-driven reduced-order surrogates for many-query tasks such as uncertainty quantification, design optimization, data assimilation, and optimal control. However, existing surrogate models often exhibit poor temporal stability, which can lead to unstable rollouts and exploding gradients during backpropagation, especially in multistep long-horizon forecasting. To address this, we propose SMORE, a mesh-agnostic framework for model order reduction of time-dependent PDEs. Its latent dynamics are trained with Lyapunov-guided stability regularization, which promotes stable long-horizon rollouts. We provide theoretical guarantees under the stated structural assumptions. Beyond forecasting PDE evolution, the learned latent dynamics, which are interpretable and linear or linear-quadratic, could bring benefits for downstream tasks such as data assimilation and optimal control. Moreover, our framework is capable of predicting continuous PDE solution fields from sparse measurements of the initial condition. We evaluate SMORE on a range of problems, including wave propagation, the Navier-Stokes equations, and the shallow water equations. Our results show that it improves long-horizon rollout generalization and empirical robustness, and achieves competitive accuracy at comparable parameter budgets relative to competitive baselines including DINo, FNO, CNO, and Transolver.

</details>


### [189] [Beyond Conservatism: Recoverability-Conditioned Exploration for Model-Based Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33336)
*Xuanlin Chen,Ziyue Wang,Xunlan Zhou,Yuan-yih Shang,Qiang Wu,Shenghua Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 RECON，将保守策略学习与主动数据采集解耦：主策略负责任务执行，探索器依据“可恢复性条件化的认知不确定性”采集真实交互数据，从而把探索集中在主策略仍能回归专家行为的未知区域，提升基于模型的模仿学习的交互效率、模仿性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的模仿学习（MBIL）虽能通过在学得世界模型中想象 rollout 来提升真实交互效率，但模型诱导占用与真实环境占用之间存在差距，使策略学习对模型误差敏感。保守 MBIL 可缓解策略优化中的模型利用，但若真实交互也由同一保守策略采集，专家分布周围的不确定区域采样不足；而通用不确定性驱动探索又可能把交互分配到新颖但与任务无关的动力学上。

Method: RECON 将“保守策略学习”与“主动数据采集”分离：维护一个用于任务执行的主策略和一个用于真实环境交互的探索器。探索器基于认知不确定性进行优化，该不确定性以多步主策略想象估计出的可恢复性为条件，从而优先采集那些主策略仍能返回专家行为附近的未知状态。实验覆盖 locomotion、navigation 与 manipulation 任务。

Result: 在 locomotion、navigation 和 manipulation 实验中，RECON 在交互效率、模仿性能与鲁棒性上均取得一致增益。结果表明，RECON 能将真实环境交互导向专家分布周围、此前方法探索不足的恢复区域，并据此学习更适合模仿的世界模型。

Conclusion: RECON 通过可恢复性条件化的探索机制，在 MBIL 中更有效地分配真实交互预算，缓解模型误差与分布偏移问题，并提升世界模型对模仿任务的适配性。其核心贡献在于把主动探索限定于对主策略可恢复且与专家分布相关的未知区域，而非泛化的新颖性探索。

Abstract: Model-based imitation learning (MBIL) improves real-environment interaction efficiency by optimizing policies on imagined rollouts from a learned world model. However, the gap between model-induced and real-environment occupancies makes policy learning sensitive to model error. Conservative MBIL mitigates model exploitation during policy optimization, but when real-environment interactions are collected by the same conservative policy, uncertain regions around the expert distribution remain insufficiently sampled. Generic uncertainty-driven exploration, on the other hand, may allocate interaction to novel but task-irrelevant dynamics. We propose REcoverability-CONditioned Exploration for Model-Based Imitation Learning (RECON). RECON separates conservative policy learning from active data collection by maintaining a main policy for task execution and an explorer for real-environment interaction. The explorer is optimized based on epistemic uncertainty conditioned on recoverability estimated from multi-step main-policy imagination, focusing data collection on unknown states from which the main policy can still return toward expert behavior. Experiments on locomotion, navigation and manipulation show consistent gains in interaction efficiency, imitation performance, and robustness, indicating that RECON directs real-environment interaction toward recovery regions around the expert distribution that are underexplored by prior methods, and thereby learns a world model better suited for imitation.

</details>


### [190] [MultiEcho: An Experimental Science of Learned Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33347)
*Meng Zhu,Airui Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MultiEcho 将世界模型视为具有自身响应规律的实验系统，通过受控反事实干预估计这些规律、限定其适用性，并独立检验其与物理规律的对应关系；在9个模拟物理系统和7个冻结模型配置上，三参考估计器可预测完整干预响应并恢复干预参数，同时揭示响应可预测性、干预可读性与物理准确性之间的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型（学习到的环境模型）是否遵循自身可被实验刻画的“响应定律”，以及这些规律能否与物理定律对应，尚缺乏系统研究框架。现有评估常混淆响应可预测性、干预可读性与物理准确性，因此需要将世界模型作为实验系统，用受控反事实干预估计其响应规律并界定适用范围。

Method: 提出 MultiEcho 框架：对世界模型实施受控反事实干预，估计其响应规律，限定适用条件，并单独检验物理对应性。采用三参考估计器预测完整干预响应并恢复干预参数；估计器选择仅使用发现数据，冻结拟合在验证与确认情境中评估。设计事件窗口、可见性、相机干预、成对生成器配置、幅值扫描、精确重置材料实验等；使用精确有限尺度恒等式解析奇数/偶数响应误差，并以一阶余项界指定细化校准的收敛条件。实验覆盖9个模拟物理系统与7个冻结模型配置。

Result: 三参考估计器能够预测完整干预响应并恢复干预参数；响应可以局部可描述，但在同一目标坐标中可能与物理效应匹配较差。事件窗口、可见性和相机干预揭示条件适用性；成对生成器配置在更强场景提示引导下显示可读性降低。幅值扫描暴露小图像误差与大幅值相对效应误差并存。精确重置材料实验在匹配位置与速度下，将已注册可见响应成功与材料依赖未来上的固定读出失败区分开。精确有限尺度恒等式解决奇数与偶数响应误差；一阶余项界说明何时细化校准会收敛。

Conclusion: MultiEcho 为独立于物理定律、并与其关联地研究学习到的世界定律提供了实验基础，强调必须分别评估响应可预测性、干预可读性与物理准确性。

Abstract: World models can be studied as experimental systems with response laws of their own. We introduce MultiEcho, a framework for estimating these laws through controlled counterfactual interventions, delimiting their applicability, and separately testing their physical correspondence. Across nine simulated physical systems and seven frozen model configurations, three-reference estimators predict complete intervention responses and recover intervention parameters. Estimator selection uses discovery data only; frozen fits are evaluated on validation and confirmation contexts. The experiments distinguish response predictability, intervention readability and physical accuracy. Responses can be locally describable yet poorly match physical effects in the same target coordinates. Event-window, visibility and camera interventions reveal conditional applicability, and paired generator configurations show reduced readability under a scene prompt with stronger guidance. Magnitude sweeps expose small image errors alongside large relative effect errors. An exact-reset material experiment separates registered visible-response success from fixed-readout failure on material-dependent futures at matched positions and velocities. Exact finite-scale identities resolve odd and even response errors; first-order remainder bounds specify when refined calibration converges. MultiEcho provides an experimental basis for studying learned-world laws independently of, and in relation to, physical laws.

</details>


### [191] [KoopCell: Koopman-Based Generative Model for Learning Single-Cell Dynamics from Distribution Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33350)
*Wanfeng Lu,Yutong Zhang,Keyi Zhou,Chenxin Ge,Wei Lin,Qunxi Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一生成框架 KoopCell：基于 Koopman-Mori-Zwanzig 理论，从时间稀疏、非配对的分布快照中联合学习表示与线性潜动力学；扩展 KoopCell-M 以 Markovian embedding 引入非马尔可夫记忆来建模分支。理论给出 Koopman 生成元的闭式最小二乘估计及收敛保证，实验在合成系统和三个 scRNA-seq 数据集上达到所评估方法中的 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发育生物学中，从时间稀疏、非配对的群体分布快照学习群体动力学是基础性难题。现有神经微分方程和流匹配方法可在观测快照间插值，但难以外推到训练时域之外，且通常缺乏显式建模发育分支的机制。

Method: KoopCell 基于 Koopman-Mori-Zwanzig 理论，联合学习表示与可预测的线性潜动力学。理论上，利用弱连续性方程（weak continuity equation）推导从分布快照估计 Koopman 生成元的闭式最小二乘估计器，并在适当假设下建立收敛保证。为建模分支动力学，进一步提出 KoopCell-M，通过 Markovian embedding 将非马尔可夫记忆引入潜 Koopman 动力学。

Result: 在合成系统和三个 scRNA-seq 数据集上的实验表明：该框架能够恢复 Koopman 谱、通过记忆机制建模分支动力学，并可扩展到预测高维基因表达分布，在所评估方法中取得 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: KoopCell 为从稀疏、非配对分布快照中学习可外推、可解释且可扩展的群体动力学提供了统一生成框架；KoopCell-M 通过记忆嵌入增强对发育分支等非马尔可夫过程的建模能力。

Abstract: Learning population dynamics from temporally sparse, unpaired distribution snapshots is a fundamental challenge in developmental biology. Recent approaches based on neural differential equations and flow matching can interpolate between observed population snapshots, but may struggle to extrapolate beyond the training horizon and often lack an explicit mechanism for modeling developmental branching. We propose KoopCell, a unified generative framework based on Koopman-Mori-Zwanzig theory that jointly learns representations and predictive linear latent dynamics. Theoretically, using the weak continuity equation, we derive a closed-form least-squares estimator for the Koopman generator from distribution snapshots and establish convergence guarantees under suitable assumptions. To model branching dynamics, we further develop KoopCell-M, which incorporates non-Markovian memory into the latent Koopman dynamics through a Markovian embedding. Experiments on synthetic systems and three scRNA-seq datasets demonstrate the ability of our framework to recover Koopman spectra, model branching through memory, and scale to predicting high-dimensional gene expression distributions, achieving state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [192] [Hamiltonian JEPA: Action-Conditioned World Models with an Inherited Control State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33497)
*Tamim Zoabi,Ameen Ali,Lior Wolf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 H-JEPA：将感知编码与控制状态解耦，用 Bures-Wasserstein 先验正则化宽感知码，取固定正交切片作为控制状态，并配合相条件耗散端口哈密顿动力学与端口逆一致性（PIC），在多个像素控制基准上以更少训练轮数匹配或超越无重建基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从像素规划不仅需要稳定、可预测的隐空间，还要求规划器所评分或使用的状态按“动作如何驱动系统”来组织。现有动作条件 JEPA 让同一嵌入同时承担感知与控制，存在目标冲突。

Method: H-JEPA 将感知与控制分离：宽感知码经 Bures-Wasserstein 先验正则化，形成尺度良好、各向同性的几何；控制状态是该码的固定正交切片，继承其协方差且不设独立目标。控制状态按相条件耗散端口哈密顿动力学演化，输入端口具有正交列。端口逆一致性（PIC）通过端口转置读回执行动作，该读出恰为 rollout 误差在端口方向上的投影，因此 PIC 是无参数预测误差重加权，而非辅助动作解码器。

Result: 解开读出与端口之间的绑定会破坏上述恒等式，并损失约一半增益。H-JEPA 在四个像素控制基准上，最多 10 个训练 epoch 即可匹配或超越无重建基线（包括动作解码的 Delta-JEPA）；在 OGB-Cube 上增益最大，为 91.9% 对 79.3%。在 PushT 与 OGB-Cube 上的消融区分了结构化预测器、PIC、预测时域、状态秩和抗坍缩先验的贡献。

Conclusion: 把感知表征与控制状态解耦，并以端口几何约束动力学与读出，可提升无像素重建的规划性能；PIC 的本质是参数自由的预测误差重加权。

Abstract: Planning from pixels needs more than a latent space that is stable and predictable. The state the planner scores must also be organized by how actions move the system. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) avoid pixel reconstruction by predicting future representations, but existing action-conditioned JEPAs ask one embedding to serve both perception and control. We introduce H-JEPA, which separates the two. A wide perceptual code is regularized toward a well-scaled isotropic geometry with a Bures-Wasserstein prior, and a fixed orthonormal slice of that code is the control state, which inherits the code's covariance without any objective of its own. The state evolves under phase-conditioned dissipative port-Hamiltonian dynamics whose input port has orthonormal columns. Port-inverse consistency (PIC) reads the executed action back through the transpose of that port. We show that this readout is exactly the rollout error projected onto the port directions, so PIC is a parameter-free reweighting of prediction error and not an auxiliary action decoder. Untying the readout from the port breaks this identity and loses half of the gain. H-JEPA matches or exceeds reconstruction-free baselines, including the action-decoding Delta-JEPA, on four pixel-based control benchmarks after at most $10$ training epochs, and its largest gain is on OGB-Cube ($91.9$ against $79.3$ percent). Ablations on PushT and OGB-Cube separate the contributions of the structured predictor, PIC, the prediction horizon, the state rank, and the anti-collapse prior.

</details>


### [193] [MA-JEPA: Joint-Embedding World Models for Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33563)
*Brandon Gary Kaplowitz,Osaze James Obahor,Christian Schroeder de Witt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MA-JEPA，一种用自监督联合嵌入预测（JEPA）替代观测重构的随机世界模型，实现集中训练、分散执行的多智能体强化学习，在 SMAC 八个地图中的四个上达到或超越最优基线平均胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通过想象轨迹训练策略以提升样本效率，但其效用取决于所学表示是否能捕获未来控制所需的信息。本文研究自监督联合嵌入预测（JEPA）能否为多智能体强化学习提供这种学习信号。

Method: MA-JEPA 用预测目标表示取代观测重构：采用分类隐状态与因果 Transformer，基于后验预测和动作条件动力学两个目标进行训练，再从隐想象中进行 actor-critic 学习。引入仅用于训练的联合预测器，以所有智能体的局部状态与动作为条件，预测每个智能体下一局部观测的嵌入，并将这些预测通过真实交互中相同的局部后验，配合仅用于价值学习的集中式 critic；执行阶段保持分散。

Result: 在 SMAC 上表现强劲，在评估的八个地图中有四个的胜率均值达到或超过已报告的最强对比基线。

Conclusion: 自监督联合嵌入预测可作为多智能体基于模型的强化学习的有效学习信号，MA-JEPA 架构在集中训练分散执行框架下于部分 SMAC 地图上取得领先性能。

Abstract: World models improve sample efficiency by training policies on imagined trajectories, but their usefulness depends on learning representations that capture the information needed for future control. We study whether self-supervised joint-embedding prediction (JEPA) can provide this learning signal for multi-agent reinforcement learning. We introduce MA-JEPA, a stochastic world model that replaces observation reconstruction with prediction of target representations, enabling model-based multi-agent reinforcement learning with centralized training and decentralized execution. A categorical latent state and a causal Transformer are trained with posterior and action-conditioned dynamics prediction objectives and are then used for actor-critic learning from latent imagination. A training-only joint predictor conditions on all agents' local states and actions to predict each agent's next local observation embedding. These predictions are passed through the same local posterior used during real interaction with a centralized critic that is used only for value learning, with execution remaining decentralized. Our experiments show that this architecture performs strongly on SMAC, matching or exceeding the strongest reported comparator mean win rate on four of eight evaluated maps.

</details>


### [194] [Correct then Forecast: Observer State-Space Models for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33566)
*Alexis-Raja Brachet,Guillaume Clavier--Frémond,Abdelhakim Ziani,Pierre-Yves Richard,Céline Hudelot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出观测器状态空间模型（OSSMs），将观测时间序列视为自主动力系统的测量值，显式分离潜在状态传播与测量同化，统一了传统与近期SSM并提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测需要在最后观测之外外推动态，但循环预测模型通常把观测当作直接控制潜在动态的输入，导致预测时观测缺失引发"制度切换"（regime change）。作者希望从状态估计视角根本性地重新表述这一问题。

Method: 引入Observer State-Space Models（OSSMs）。核心思想是将观测输入序列解释为底层自主动力系统的测量：单一转移过程同时支配上下文区间与预测区间，可用的观测通过观测器（observer）校正估计状态，从而在架构上显式分离潜在状态传播与测量同化。该形式化自然引出经典控制理论性质，如可观测性与状态估计误差收敛。论文进一步证明传统及近期SSM均可作为OSS框架的特例被恢复。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，OSS在保持与对应SSM基线相同参数量和训练设置下取得显著提升。同时统一解释揭示既有SSM循环动态中的建模不一致性。

Conclusion: 论文支持一条简单的循环预测原则：观测应当用于校正估计的潜在状态，而不是控制用于传播该状态的动态。

Abstract: Time series forecasting requires extrapolating the dynamics of an observed process beyond the last available measurement. Yet recurrent forecasting models typically treat observations as inputs that directly control their latent dynamics. It leads to a regime change when these observations become unavailable at prediction time. Following a state-estimation perspective, we introduce Observer State-Space Models (OSSMs), a class of recurrent models that interprets the observed input time series as measurements of an underlying autonomous dynamical system. OSSMs explicitly separate latent-state propagation from measurement assimilation: a single transition governs the dynamics across both context and forecasting intervals, while available observations correct the estimated state through an observer. This formulation naturally exposes classical control-theoretic properties, including observability and convergence of the state estimation error. We further show that conventional and recent SSMs can be recovered as particular instances of our OSSM framework, thereby providing a unified interpretation of their recurrent dynamics and revealing modeling inconsistencies. We perform experiments across several benchmarks showing that OSSM achieves substantial improvements while maintaining the same parameter count and training setup as the corresponding SSM baseline. These results support a simple principle for recurrent forecasting: observations should correct the estimated latent state, rather than control the dynamics used to propagate it.

</details>


### [195] [You Only Edit Once: Incentivizing In-Context Capability of LLMs via Local Demonstration Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33609)
*Jiarong Wen,Qi Wang,Yun Qu,Yixiu Mao,Heming Zou,Haoang Chi,Lizhou Cai,Yiqin Lv,Kaiyu Zhang,Yuhang Jiang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出局部示例编辑（LDE），将ICL示例集选择简化为带约束的局部搜索问题，训练一个小型LLM（Jev-LDE）作为System-1，通过Keep/Delete/Replace等结构化编辑动作，在可验证奖励的强化学习框架下对检索到的示例集执行单次编辑，从而在目标LLM单次推理下提升ICL性能并具备良好迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习（ICL）对大语言模型（LLM）的推理性能至关重要，但其效果高度依赖示例集（demonstration set）的选择。对这些集合进行穷举搜索属于组合问题，计算成本高昂；现有选择器多依赖相关性或似然等代理指标来隐式评估ICL质量，且需对目标LLM反复查询，带来显著开销。因此，亟需一种既高效又精准的示例集选择方法。

Method: 将示例集选择建模为带约束的局部搜索问题，提出局部示例编辑（LDE）。从一个初始检索得到的示例集出发，采用单次结构化编辑来探索其邻域，并在性能增益与搜索成本间取得平衡。具体将LDE归结为策略搜索问题，训练一个称为Jev-LDE的小型LLM。该模型作为System-1，通过执行Keep、Delete、Replace等动作修改检索到的示例集，整个过程在带可验证奖励的强化学习（RL with verifiable rewards）框架下完成。测试时，Jev-LDE仅对检索到的示例集执行一次编辑，随后进行目标LLM的一次推理，无需迭代式上下文评分或子集搜索。

Result: 在标准分类基准上，将Jev-LDE作为即插即用模块与多种目标LLM结合，均能稳定提升ICL性能；且Jev-LDE在无需重新训练的情况下，对所留出的基准和模型展现出良好的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 上述发现表明，LDE方法为充分利用目标LLM的ICL能力提供了一种高效且适应性强的途径。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is crucial for boosting the inference performance of large language models (LLMs). However, the effectiveness of ICL in LLMs is greatly influenced by the choice of demonstration sets. Exhaustive searches over these sets are combinatorial, and existing selectors often rely on relevance or likelihood proxies to implicitly assess ICL quality. Making repeated queries to the target LLM with these strategies can incur substantial costs. This work simplifies selection by framing it as a constrained local search problem and presents local demonstration editing (LDE). Starting with an initially retrieved set of demonstrations, LDE employs a single structured edit to explore its surrounding neighborhood while balancing performance gains with search costs. Technically, LDE is reduced to a policy search problem, for which we train a small LLM, referred to as Jev-LDE. This model as the System-1 modifies the retrieved demonstration set by performing actions such as \texttt{Keep}, \texttt{Delete}, or \texttt{Replace} elements, all within a framework of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. At test time, Jev-LDE executes a single edit of the retrieved demonstration set, followed by one inference from the target LLM, avoiding the need for iterative context scoring or subset searches. Across standard classification benchmarks, various target LLMs with Jev-LDE as the plug-and-play module consistently improve ICL performance, and Jev-LDE shows transferability to held-out benchmarks and models without retraining. These findings indicate that the LDE approach offers an efficient and adaptable method for harnessing the ICL capabilities of target LLMs.

</details>


### [196] [ALDER: Discovering the Laws of a World by Acting in It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33728)
*Teng Cao,Yu Deng,Quentin Delfosse,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ALDER：通过主动实验、验证与结构修订来发现显式方程世界模型，并用于目标导向控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠世界模型不仅应预测未来状态，还应以方程等显式、透明、可检验的形式表达动作如何改变世界。固定轨迹数据无法区分同样拟合良好的竞争假设，而固定候选集搜索又无法发现初始假设空间之外的方程。

Method: ALDER（Action-guided Law Discovery, Evaluation, and Revision）：提出参数化方程；数值优化器拟合系数；独立验证器在留出数据上测试候选。用成本与安全感知的选择器查询干预（新实验），以区分竞争性有效假设。反例更新证据账本并指导下一步结构修订，不兼容规律被丢弃。给定当前状态与目标时，求解已验证世界模型定义的逆问题来选择控制动作。

Result: 在内部基准、ODE方程发现任务和机器人实验中，ALDER能发现初始公式集之外的规律，修复失败的模型提议，用更少交互区分固定候选模型，并提升分布外预测。同时可利用验证后的世界模型求解逆问题，选择控制动作。

Conclusion: 显式方程世界模型可通过交互进行检验与修订，并能自然地用于目标导向控制。
