Abstract: Reliable world models should not only predict future states but express how actions change the world in an explicit, transparent and testable form, such as equations. Yet methods that rely on a fixed set of trajectories cannot distinguish equally good competing hypotheses, while searches over a fixed set of predefined candidates cannot discover equations outside the initial hypothesis space. We introduce ALDER (Action-guided Law Discovery, Evaluation, and Revision), a method that actively proposes novel experiments to test and revise models. Specifically, ALDER proposes parametric equations; a numerical optimizer fits their coefficients; an independent verifier tests these candidates on held-out data. To distinguish between competing valid hypotheses, a cost- and safety-aware selector queries interventions, in the form of novel experiments. The resulting counterexamples update the evidence ledger and guide the next structural revision, while incompatible laws are discarded. Across an in-house benchmark, ODE equation discovery tasks, and robotic experiments, ALDER discovers laws beyond its initial formula set, repairs failed model proposals, distinguishes fixed candidate models with fewer interactions, and improves out-of-distribution prediction. Furthermore, given a current state and a target, ALDER selects control actions by solving the inverse problem defined by its validated world model. Together, these results show that explicit equation-based world models can be tested and revised through interaction, then naturally used to guide goal-directed control.

</details>


### [197] [JET: Justification Evaluation in Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33874)
*Shenghao Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JET 在不额外训练的前提下，利用预训练语言模型与视觉-语言模型，通过直接评估候选答案似然并共享计算，在有限答案集合中完成选择；在 CPU 与消费级 GPU 上验证了准确率与执行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注无需额外训练的本地决策推理需求，希望在桌面 CPU 和消费级 GPU 等常见硬件上，直接用现有预训练模型完成有限答案集合的选择任务，并兼顾准确率与执行效率。

Method: JET 复用预训练语言与视觉-语言模型，无需微调即可在有限候选集内选择答案：直接评估候选似然，并通过跨候选共享计算来降低开销。实验在桌面 CPU 与消费级 GPU 上测量决策准确率和执行成本，并以模型的准确率-吞吐量对比为框架，涵盖模型、硬件与推理方式选择，同时引入 Jev 作为外部参照。此外通过受控执行实验考察前缀复用、缓存管理与输入准备优化。

Result: Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 在完整 MMLU 测试集上达到 87.48% 准确率，在单独计时的 MMLU 子集上达到每秒 3.69 个请求。受控执行实验显示，前缀复用与缓存管理带来 2.18–2.23 倍的加速，输入准备优化使处理时间减少 30.8%，且输出保持不变。可选推理则表现出与任务相关的准确率-吞吐量权衡。

Conclusion: 结果表明，借助现有模型即可实现高效的本地决策推理；通过共享计算、前缀复用、缓存管理和输入准备优化，可在不改变输出的情况下显著提升执行效率，而可选推理的取舍需视任务而定。

Abstract: JET uses pretrained language and vision-language models to select among a finite set of answers without additional training. It evaluates candidate likelihoods directly and shares computation across candidates. Experiments on desktop CPUs and consumer GPUs assess decision accuracy and execution cost. Qwen3.6-35B-A3B achieves 87.48% accuracy on the full MMLU test set and 3.69 requests per second on a separately timed MMLU subset. The accuracy-throughput comparison covers model, hardware, and reasoning choices, with Jev as an external reference. Controlled execution experiments show 2.18-2.23-fold speedups from prefix reuse and cache management, and a 30.8% reduction in process time from input preparation optimizations, with unchanged outputs. Optional reasoning has a task-dependent accuracy-throughput trade-off. These results support local decision inference from existing models.

</details>


### [198] [Behavioral Monitoring of JEPA World Models with Jacobian Centroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33940)
*Thomas Walker,Randall Balestriero,Richard Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用"质心"（centroids，即子组件Jacobian行和）来刻画世界模型（WM）的 behavioral properties，补充传统基于激活的知识信号。质心可通过Jacobian向量积高效计算，能生成任务相关显著图。在JEPA WM的连续控制任务上，揭示了编码器与预测器的结构性分离，可提前预测规划失败并通过目标重采样恢复成功，且优于分布漂移检测基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测基于世界模型（WM）规划的失败，需要监测模型是否在当前任务上与行为对齐，而这依赖对其内部表征的研究。传统基于激活的"知识信号"不足以刻画行为层面的对齐，因此需要一种能反映行为属性的内部表征视角。

Method: 提出"质心"（centroids）——子组件Jacobian的行和——作为刻画WM行为属性的指标。质心通过Jacobian向量积（JVP）高效计算，刻画模型如何组织输入空间的几何结构，可生成任务相关显著图，并与传统基于激活的知识信号互补。在JEPA WM的连续控制任务上评估。

Result: 在JEPA WM连续控制任务上，该行为视角揭示出一种结构性分离：编码器正确表征目标，而预测器在行为上无响应。该失败模式可在采取任何动作前预测规划失败，通过目标重采样恢复分布外成功。此外，基于质心的方法作为分布漂移检测器优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 质心提供了一种高效、可操作且具影响力的行为监测栈，能够预测规划失败并支持分布漂移下的目标重采样，为WM的行为对齐监测提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Detecting failures in World Model (WM)-based planning requires monitoring whether the model is behaviorally aligned with the current task, which in turn requires studying its internal representations. Here, we show that centroids---sub-component Jacobian row-sums---effectively identify the behavioral properties of WMs, complementing traditional activation-based knowledge signals. The centroids of a model are easily computed through Jacobian vector products and characterize how the model organizes the geometry of its input space, yielding an efficient perspective on internal representations, including the generation of task-relevant saliency maps. Evaluated on continuous control tasks using JEPA WMs, this behavioral view reveals a structural dissociation, where the encoder correctly represents the goal while the predictor remains behaviorally unresponsive. This failure mode directly predicts planning failure before any action is taken, allowing for goal resampling to recapture out-of-distribution success. Moreover, centroid-based methods outperform baseline methods as distribution-shift detectors. Together, these tools yield a behavioral monitoring stack that is operational and consequential under distribution shifts.

</details>


### [199] [Do World Models Learn Global Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34058)
*Alexander Detkov,Matt Thomson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将"理解"形式化为从局部转移学习全局约束并传播其后果，在幺半群世界构建任务，发现常规下一状态训练无法传播非平凡约束，而组合式训练可显著提升约束传播与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统常表现出碎片化与脆弱性：模型能解释概念却无法应用，或在某情境遵循安全指令而在另一情境失效。作者认为这反映了从局部信息提升为全局理解的普遍失败，因此希望从根本机制上研究"理解"。

Method: 将理解定义为学习约束并传播其后果；在"幺半群世界"（由动作转移连接的状态集合）上构造学习任务，其中可见训练转移与一个未见约束共同决定留出转移。考察逆、交换、复合、周期性四类约束，覆盖空间与语义结构；在注意力、循环、状态空间三类架构上比较下一状态训练与组合式训练（使用相同路径但隐藏中间状态输入），并引入证明深度d（推断留出转移所需最少推理轮数）与组合路径长度T进行分析。

Result: 下一状态训练虽能拟合数据，但无法传播非平凡约束；组合式训练在逆、交换、复合约束上跨架构达到96%准确率，并提升具身环境世界模型的几何泛化与Wikidata微调LLM的关系泛化。模型泛化随证明深度d增加而急剧下降；增加组合路径长度T可改善泛化。

Conclusion: 研究提供了形式化考察语言与世界模型中全局理解的方法，表明组合式训练能促进信息传播与整合，是提升模型约束推理与泛化能力的关键机制。

Abstract: AI systems often feel brittle and fragmented. A large language model (LLM) may correctly explain a concept but fail to apply it, or follow safety instructions in one context but not another. This behavior suggests a general failure to lift local information to a global understanding. To gain fundamental insight, we frame "understanding" as learning constraints and propagating their consequences. We construct learning tasks on monoid worlds, sets of states connected by action transitions, where observed training transitions and an unseen constraint jointly determine held-out transitions. Measuring generalization tests whether models can learn global constraints from local transitions and propagate their consequences. We consider inverse, commutativity, composition, and periodicity constraints relevant to spatial and semantic structure. Across attention, recurrent, and state-space architectures, next-state training fits the data but fails to propagate non-trivial constraints. Compositional training, which uses identical paths but hides intermediate states from the input, achieves 96% accuracy on inverse, commutativity, and composition constraints across architectures, yields corresponding improvements in geometric generalization of world models trained on embodied environments and relational generalization in Wikidata-finetuned LLMs. How far do models propagate constraints when inferring an unseen fact may depend on first inferring others? We define proof depth d of a held-out transition, measuring the minimum number of inference rounds to infer the transition, and find that model generalization decreases sharply with proof depth. Increasing compositional path length T improves generalization. These results provide a formal way to investigate global understanding in language and world models and demonstrate that compositional training promotes information propagation and integration.

</details>


### [200] [WorldGraph: Graph-Native World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34159)
*Zezhong Ding,Yipeng Li,Xike Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“图世界建模（GWM）”，把图本身的演化视为世界动态，并构建GWM-Zero基准与WorldGraph模型，在节点、边、图三级转移预测上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境具有关系性，常以演化图形式被观测，实体、关系及其属性随时间变化。已有图相关世界模型多将图结构用于组织内部状态或支持任务推理，而非把“演化图”本身作为被建模的世界，因此作者转向研究图世界建模。

Method: 论文将GWM形式化为：观测图演化、潜在图状态与异构图转移预测。基于该形式化构建GWM-Zero基准，覆盖8个时序图数据集及节点级、边级、图级转移。提出WorldGraph：使用状态感知图Transformer进行多粒度结构与转移条件演化建模，并采用转移感知GRPO，结合动态分组与结构感知可验证奖励。

Result: 在GWM-Zero上的大量实验表明，WorldGraph在节点级、边级、图级三种转移粒度上均持续优于代表性图表示学习、时序图学习、图预训练及图世界模型基线。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了以图演化作为世界动态的建模范式，提供了系统基准GWM-Zero和有效模型WorldGraph，证明其在多粒度图转移预测上的优越性，推动了图世界建模研究。

Abstract: World models infer latent states of an environment to capture its underlying dynamics and predict future evolution. Many real-world environments, however, are inherently relational and observed as evolving graphs, where entities, relations, and their properties change over time. Prior graph-related world models use graph structures to organize internal states or support task-specific reasoning, rather than treating an evolving graph itself as the modeled world. We instead study graph world modeling (GWM), where graph evolution itself constitutes the world dynamics. We formulate graph world modeling over observed graph evolution, latent graph states, and heterogeneous graph-transition predictions. Based on this formulation, we construct GWM-Zero, a benchmark covering node-, edge-, and graph-level transitions over eight temporal graph datasets. We propose WorldGraph, which combines a state-aware graph transformer for multi-granularity structural and transition-conditioned evolution modeling with transition-aware GRPO using dynamic grouping and structure-aware verifiable rewards. Extensive experiments on GWM-Zero show that WorldGraph consistently outperforms representative graph representation, temporal graph learning, graph pretraining, and graph world-model baselines across all three transition granularities.

</details>


### [201] [LRC-JEPA: Disentangling Dynamics and Residual Context for Efficient World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34375)
*Luzhe Huang,Lei Chu,Jingyi Liang,Yuhuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LRC-JEPA 通过将紧凑预测潜变量 z 与残差上下文嵌入 u 解耦，仅让 z 参与动力学传播与规划，在仿真和真实控制任务中以更少参数取得更优规划性能与更强表征解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑 JEPA 世界模型可高效进行潜空间规划，但无奖励自监督下的低维表征需同时编码动作条件动力学与可预测视觉上下文；二者竞争会把可控状态与高阶干扰外观纠缠，导致复杂场景规划性能下降。

Method: 提出 LRC-JEPA：将信息路由为紧凑预测潜变量 z 与可学习查询的残差上下文嵌入 u。仅 z 由动力学模型传播并用于规划；u 捕获时间持久信息，用于交叉注意力重建；可微残差连接鼓励 z 保留互补动态内容。在显式假设下证明该表征满足充分性、最小性、干扰不变性与解耦性。

Result: 在四个仿真控制环境中，LRC-JEPA 平均规划成功率比参数匹配的 JEPA 基线提升 9 个百分点，并匹配或超过显著更大的预训练模型。在真实 Bridge-v2 数据上，其 5.5M 参数主动编码器优于 DINO-WM（22.1M）与 V-JEPA2（303.9M）编码器，同时规划更快。物理状态探针、重建干预与消融实验验证了解耦表征的有效性。

Conclusion: LRC-JEPA 表明，将可控动态潜变量与 nuisance 上下文显式分离，可在小参数量下实现高效、可解耦且适用于真实世界规划的 JEPA 世界模型。

Abstract: Compact JEPA world models enable efficient latent-space planning, but low-dimensional representation trained under reward-free self-supervision must encode both action-conditioned dynamics and predictable visual context. This competition can entangle controllable state with high-rank nuisance appearance and degrade planning as scenes become more complex. We introduce LRC-JEPA, a lightweight end-to-end world model that routes information into a compact predictive latent $\mathbf{z}$ and learned-query residual-context embeddings $\mathbf{u}$. Only $\mathbf{z}$ is propagated by the dynamics model and used for planning, while $\mathbf{u}$ captures temporally persistent information for cross-attention reconstruction; a differentiable residual connection encourages the latent to retain complementary dynamic content. Under explicit assumptions, we show that the resulting representation is sufficient, minimal, nuisance-invariant, and disentangled. Across four simulated control environments, LRC-JEPA improves average planning success over a parameter-matched JEPA baseline by 9 percentage points and matches or exceeds substantially larger pretrained models. On the real-world Bridge-v2 set, its 5.5M-parameter active encoder outperforms DINO-WM (22.1M) and V-JEPA2 (303.9M) encoders while also enabling faster planning. Physical-state probes, reconstruction interventions, and ablations confirm the effectiveness of LRC-JEPA's representation disentanglement.

</details>


### [202] [Admissible Diffusion for Multimodal Interventional Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34433)
*Xing Han,Shravan Chaudhari,Jiarui Shao,Paul Pu Liang,Suchi Saria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADMIT是一个结合不规则多模态表示、治疗条件潜在扩散与显式状态/动作约束的框架，用于生成不同治疗下的干预轨迹。初步实验显示多模态输入可改善隐藏状态恢复并降低治疗对比误差，但约束执行仍不完全，且平均预测可被确定性循环基线匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅生成看似合理的临床轨迹，并不能说明在不同治疗干预下会发生什么。因此需要一种以因果干预为目标、并能区分因果假设与约束满足的轨迹生成方法。

Method: 提出ADMIT：融合不规则多模态表示、治疗条件潜在扩散，以及对生成状态或动作的显式约束。以序贯g-computation定义干预目标，并区分因果假设与约束满足。其可采纳性机制将生理先验知识转化为对生成状态和候选动作的显式约束；治疗暴露动态调节潜在转移，状态投影或动作门控在轨迹展开中施加约束，从而影响后续轨迹生成。

Result: 初步实验中，多模态输入提升了监督隐藏状态恢复，并降低了治疗对比误差。在无泄漏历史编码的模拟给药方案实验中，ADMIT预测了固定总剂量再分配所导致的大部分肿瘤体积变化。暴露输入在临时减量附近改善了预测，无论假定清除率是否正确；但它缩小了预测的剂量效应，且确定性循环基线在平均预测上与ADMIT相当。暴露投影减少了约束违反，但执行仍不完整。基于eICU的半合成实验展示了固定策略与自适应策略下的治疗反应生成，观察性示例进一步刻画了模型的治疗敏感性。

Conclusion: ADMIT为检验互补观测与生理限制能否改善干预轨迹提供了框架，并将表示恢复、效应准确性和规则执行分开评估。

Abstract: Generating a plausible clinical trajectory does not establish what would happen under a different treatment. We present ADMIT, a framework combining irregular multimodal representations, treatment-conditioned latent diffusion and explicit constraints on generated states or actions. We formulate its interventional target through sequential g-computation and distinguish causal assumptions from constraint satisfaction. Its admissibility mechanism translates physiological prior knowledge into explicit constraints on generated states and proposed actions. Treatment-exposure dynamics condition latent transitions, while state projection or action gating applies the constraints during rollout so that they influence subsequent trajectory generation. In our preliminary experiments, multimodal inputs improved supervised hidden-state recovery and reduced treatment-contrast error. In a simulated dosing-schedule experiment with leak-free history encoding, ADMIT predicted most of the tumor-volume change caused by redistributing a fixed total dose. An exposure input improved these predictions around a temporary dose reduction whether or not the assumed clearance rate was correct, but reduced the predicted size of a dose effect, and a deterministic recurrent baseline matched ADMIT's average predictions. Exposure projection reduced constraint violations, although enforcement remained incomplete. Semi-synthetic experiments using eICU context illustrated treatment-response generation under fixed and adaptive policies. Observational examples further characterize model treatment sensitivity. ADMIT provides a framework for testing whether complementary observations and physiological restrictions improve intervention trajectories, with representation recovery, effect accuracy and rule enforcement assessed separately.

</details>


### [203] [Alignment-Guided Flow Transformer for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34467)
*Shengchao Hu,Peng Wang,Qiyang Zhou,Guodong Zheng,Yuqi Huang,Li Shen,Ya Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出对齐引导流变换器（AGFT），通过显式三模态对齐损失弥合视觉-语言-动作表征差距，结合流匹配目标加速推理，在基准上取得更高成功率与更低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型存在视觉、语言、动作之间的三模态失配（tri-modal misalignment），导致动作接地能力弱、泛化差、微调效率低；而此前研究多聚焦于双模态视觉-语言对齐，对三模态对齐缺乏系统研究。

Method: AGFT框架通过专门的对齐损失显式强制三模态对齐，系统化形式化并研究VLA中的三模态对齐；采用流匹配（flow-matching）目标替代扩散策略以减少推理步数；理论层面建立三模态对齐差距与流匹配优化紧致性之间的定量联系。

Result: 在广泛基准上的实验表明，AGFT相比SOTA基线取得了更高的成功率与更低的推理延迟；消融和分析验证了对齐在提升适应性与鲁棒性中的作用。

Conclusion: 三模态对齐是扩展鲁棒VLA操作的关键要素，AGFT在对齐、适应性与推理效率上均具优势。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models point toward general-purpose robotic intelligence by unifying perception, instruction, and control. Despite impressive progress, existing VLA models often adapt poorly due to \emph{tri-modal misalignment} among vision, language, and action, which weakens action grounding and hurts generalization and fine-tuning efficiency. In this work, we present Alignment-Guided Flow Transformer (AGFT), a novel framework that explicitly enforces tri-modal alignment through a dedicated alignment loss, bridging the representational gap across modalities and enhancing task adaptation. While prior research has predominantly emphasized bi-modal vision--language alignment, we systematically formalize and study tri-modal alignment in VLA models, and provide both ablations and analysis to isolate its role in improving adaptation and robustness. To further accelerate deployment, we adopt a flow-matching objective, enabling substantially fewer inference steps than diffusion-based policies while maintaining accuracy. Theoretically, we establish a quantitative connection between the tri-modal alignment gap and the optimization tightness of flow matching; empirically, experiments on the extensive benchmark show that AGFT achieves superior success rates and lower inference latency compared to SOTA baselines, underscoring tri-modal alignment as a key ingredient for scaling robust VLA manipulation.

</details>


### [204] [Brain-Conditioned Action Policies for Neural Motor Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34561)
*Luyao Jin,Running Zhao,Huan Zhao,Vincent C. K. Cheung,Wei-Hsin Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainVLA：通过语言中介对齐，将神经活动映射到预训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，实现数据高效的神经运动解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动脑机接口旨在解码运动意图，使瘫痪患者控制外部设备。现有神经运动解码通常学习从神经活动到运动学的任务特定映射，受限于稀缺的配对神经-动作数据。

Method: 提出BrainVLA，利用预训练VLA模型并通过语言中介对齐进行神经运动解码。首先构建VLA兼容数据集，包含配对神经活动、动作信号、语言指令和渲染视觉观测；随后通过LoRA微调将OpenVLA-OFT策略适配到目标动作空间；再以语言表示为语义目标训练神经编码器，进行神经-语言对齐，从神经活动中提取潜在运动意图。所得神经表示作为内源性意图信号引导VLA策略生成可执行动作，视觉观测提供任务状态补充信息。

Result: 在两个不同动作维度的神经运动数据集上使用因果展开解码评估。BrainVLA在跨会话解码R²和任务成功率上优于所评估基线，并表现出高训练数据效率。

Conclusion: 该工作为神经运动解码利用大规模机器人先验提供了路径，即通过脑条件化的VLA策略实现解码。

Abstract: Motor brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) aim to decode motor intention, enabling people with paralysis to control external devices. Neural motor decoding typically learns task-specific mappings from neural activity to kinematics, yet remains constrained by scarce paired neural-action data. We propose BrainVLA, a framework that enables neural motor decoding by drawing on a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model through language-mediated alignment. BrainVLA mitigates reliance on scarce paired neural-action data by leveraging VLA policies. We first construct VLA-compatible datasets including paired neural activity, action signals, language instructions, and rendered visual observations. Then, we adapt the OpenVLA-OFT policy to the target action spaces through LoRA fine-tuning. To establish an effective interface through which neural activity can convey motor intention to adapted VLA policies and guide action generation, we train a neural encoder via neural-language alignment, using language representations as semantic targets to capture latent motor intent from neural activity. The resulting neural representations serve as an endogenous intention signal to guide VLA policies to generate executable actions, while visual observations provide complementary information about the evolving task state. BrainVLA is evaluated on two neural motor datasets with different action dimensionalities using causal rollout decoding. It outperforms the evaluated baselines in cross-session decoding $R^2$ and task success rate, while demonstrating high training data efficiency. These results establish a route for neural motor decoding to draw on large-scale robotic priors through brain-conditioned VLA policies.

</details>


### [205] [The Low-Rank Structure of VLA Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34599)
*Minjae Oh,Yoonah Park,Jongwon Lim,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统探究了强化学习（RL）如何重塑视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略：发现RL在主流流式VLA模型（如π0.5、GR00T N1.5/N1.6）上诱发的参数更新具有显著低秩性，且高度集中于动作专家中一个此前被忽视的小组件——Timestep Modules；这些模块承载了RL性能增益的绝大部分，其编码的shift向量更新方向可高精度预测任务成功并刻画任务间关系，沿该方向进行引导还能在不额外RL训练的情况下提升策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL正被越来越多地用于对VLA模型进行后训练，但RL究竟如何重塑这些策略仍缺乏理解。作者希望从参数空间的编码视角，揭示RL对VLA策略的内在学习信号，从而为更高效、更可解释的VLA后训练提供依据。

Method: 在LIBERO、ManiSkill、MetaWorld、CALVIN等基准上，对多种主流流式VLA模型（π0.5、GR00T N1.5/N1.6）开展RL后训练分析。通过参数更新秩分析定位关键模块；用系统性的模块替换实验验证Timestep Modules的贡献；进一步对其输出进行探针（probing）分析，刻画其对离散去噪时间步的特化、shift向量的变化方向与任务成功及任务间关系的关联，并据此设计沿shift更新方向的引导方法。

Result: RL诱发的参数更新呈显著低秩，且高度集中于动作专家的Timestep Modules；模块替换实验表明这些模块贡献了不成比例的性能增益。RL将这些模块特化到rollout中使用的离散去噪时间步，该离散时间步训练正是低秩更新的根源。在其输出中，shift向量变化最显著，shift更新方向能极强地预测任务成功（ROC-AUC最高达99.6%）；shift更新的几何结构反映任务关系，其成对相似度与跨任务迁移模式相关。此外，沿shift更新方向引导可在不进行额外RL训练的情况下进一步提升RL训练后的策略。

Conclusion: RL对VLA策略的重塑高度集中于动作专家的Timestep Modules及其shift向量更新，并受离散去噪时间步训练驱动。通过研究学习信号在参数空间中的编码方式，本文提供了对RL如何重塑VLA策略的系统性理解，并为更高效、更可解释的VLA后训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) models, yet how RL reshapes these policies remains poorly understood. We find that RL across widely used flow-based VLA models, including $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T~N1.5/N1.6, on LIBERO, ManiSkill, MetaWorld, and CALVIN induces substantially lower-rank parameter updates that are highly concentrated in the action expert's Timestep Modules, a small and previously overlooked component. Through systematic module-replacement experiments, we further show that these modules capture a disproportionate share of the performance gains from RL. We then characterize what is encoded in these Timestep Modules. First, we show that RL specializes them to the discrete denoising timesteps used during rollouts, and that this discrete-timestep training underlies the low-rank updates. Second, we find that among their outputs, the shift vector changes most distinctly under RL, and through probing, we show that shift update directions strongly predict task success (ROC-AUC up to $99.6\%$). Third, we find that the geometry of shift updates reflects task relationships, as their pairwise similarity correlates with cross-task transfer patterns. Building on these findings, we show that steering along shift update directions further improves RL-trained policies without additional RL training. Overall, we provide a systematic understanding of how RL reshapes VLA policies by studying how learned signals are encoded in parameter space, offering insights into more efficient and interpretable VLA post-training.

</details>


### [206] [Shaping Persistent Representations from Independent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34604)
*Ji Dai,Quan Fang,Junyu Gao,Rongfeng Guo,Haoyan Rong,YipingHuang,Yongxi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SPRII 训练原则：利用交互之间的关系作为弱监督，学习可复用的持久上下文，以提升世界模型的持久属性建模与下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型需从交互经验中学习环境动力学，而动力学不仅依赖当前状态与动作，还依赖跨交互持续存在的属性。标准预测训练仅凭局部证据即可降低误差，难以将持久信息组织为可复用上下文，因此需要一种无需数值属性标签的持久上下文学习机制。

Method: SPRII 以交互间关系作为弱监督，同时保留学习器原生目标。其两个可组合组件为：Align，鼓励相关交互的上下文一致；Cross，用一个交互的上下文预测另一个交互的未来。文中将问题拆分为 Formation（学习到的上下文中可访问哪些持久信息）、Use（该上下文如何影响固定预测器）与 Value（是否降低任务误差）三个环节，并进行受控实验与多类学习器验证。

Result: 受控实验表明：更可靠的关系可改善表示组织，但加入共享属性约束可能反而降低对仍共享属性的可访问性；在固定模型权重下，上下文替换会改变预测，而历史带来的收益取决于预测时域与读出方式。在十三个设置上评估，包括受控物理系统、公开动力学任务、机器人与触觉数据、伙伴交互，以及多种学习器族，SPRII 相对对应基线平均提升超过 10% 的下游任务性能与超过 15% 的持久属性读出。

Conclusion: SPRII 表明，利用交互间关系作为弱监督可有效组织持久上下文，并在多类动力学任务中优于基线；但持久上下文的形成、使用与价值是三阶段问题，需分别评估，且约束设计可能影响属性的可访问性。

Abstract: World models learn environment dynamics from interaction experience. These dynamics depend on the current state and actions, as well as on properties that persist across interactions. Yet standard predictive training can reduce error using local evidence alone, without organizing persistent information into reusable context. We introduce SPRII, a training principle that uses relations between interactions as weak supervision for persistent context while retaining the learner's native objective. For example, different trajectories of the same system share persistent properties even when their states and actions differ. SPRII uses such relations to guide context learning without numerical property labels. Two composable components encourage contexts from related interactions to agree (Align) and use one interaction's context to predict another's future (Cross). Our analysis distinguishes three linked questions: what persistent information is accessible in the learned context (Formation), how that context influences a fixed predictor (Use), and whether it reduces task error (Value). Success at one stage does not guarantee success at the next. Controlled experiments show that more reliable relations improve representation organization, but adding a shared-property constraint can reduce access to a property that remains shared. Context substitutions change predictions at fixed model weights, while the benefit from history depends on prediction horizon and readout. Evaluations span thirteen settings, including controlled physical systems, public dynamics tasks, robotic and tactile data, and partner interaction, across multiple learner families. Relative to the corresponding baselines, SPRII yields average gains of over 10% in downstream task performance and over 15% in persistent-property readout. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [207] [Learning What to Recall: Adaptive Multi-Cue Episodic Memory for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34677)
*Beomsu Kim,Chieh-Hsin Lai,Bac Nguyen,Amir Bar,Jong Chul Ye,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Future-Aware Recall (FAR) 框架，通过未来感知的预测监督和自适应多线索评分来学习情节记忆召回，使世界模型能动态选择可信检索线索并召回对当前预测有用的历史，在三种设定下优于手工设计召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的未来预测可能依赖久远过去的观测；情节记忆不断累积后，核心难题是判断哪些记忆对当前预测有用，以及应信任哪些可用检索线索来找回它们。基于近因、位姿重叠或视觉相似度等固定准则在不同环境和查询下并不可靠。

Method: FAR 在训练时以已实现未来在召回上下文条件下的对数似然衡量预测效用，并用负扩散预测损失近似该效用，据此训练检索器；该检索器在推理阶段对未来盲。检索器学习线索特定相关性，并针对每个查询自动确定应信任哪些可用线索，如时间、位姿、视觉和音频，从而选择记忆。

Result: 在三种互补设定中，FAR 即使使用相同检索线索也优于手工设计的召回策略；能够自动适应应信任的可用线索；并在世界发生变化时召回正确的历史。

Conclusion: FAR 为世界模型中的情节记忆访问提供了一种灵活且有原则的方法，表明未来感知监督与自适应多线索评分可有效提升情节召回。

Abstract: World models predict future observations from current experience and actions, yet prediction can depend on observations seen far in the past. Episodic memory preserves past observations for later recall; however, as memory accumulates, it raises a fundamental question: which memories are useful for the current prediction, and which available retrieval cues should be trusted to find them? This is challenging because fixed criteria based on recency, pose overlap, or visual similarity can be unreliable across environments and queries. We propose Future-Aware Recall (FAR), a framework that learns episodic recall from future-aware predictive supervision and adaptive multi-cue scoring. During training, FAR measures predictive utility by the conditional log-likelihood of the realized future given recalled context, approximated by negative diffusion prediction loss, and uses it to train a retriever that remains future-blind at inference. The retriever learns cue-specific relevance and automatically determines which available retrieval cues, such as time, pose, vision, and audio, to trust for each query when selecting memories. Across three complementary settings, FAR outperforms hand-designed recall even with the same retrieval cues, automatically adapts which available cues to trust, and recalls the right history as the world changes. Together, these results establish FAR as a flexible, principled approach to episodic memory access in world models.

</details>


### [208] [Adjoint Guidance Flow: Amortized Critic Guidance for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34944)
*Jeongsol Kim,Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Youngmin Kim,Seonghyun Jin,Sunwoo Park,Jangho Park,Kwanyoung Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Adjoint Guidance Flow (AGF)，将轨迹感知的 critic 引导摊销进轻量引导网络，通过确定性最优控制中的 costate 回归实现，在保持预训练 VLA 与 critic 冻结的前提下提升性能并显著降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的 VLA 策略通常用行为克隆训练，未显式优化长期任务回报；现有 critic 引导方法用采样器的一步代理来对 critic 求导，并在每个 flow 步骤反向传播 critic ensemble，导致计算与显存开销大、扩展性差。

Method: 将 critic 引导的流生成建模为确定性最优控制问题，其最优引导对应于携带终端 critic 梯度沿剩余 flow 反向传播的 costate；在冻结 VLA 与 critic 的情况下，让轻量引导网络回归该 costate，从而将轨迹感知的 critic 引导摊销到网络中。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboCasa 与 LIBERO-Pro 上，AGF 持续提升预训练 VLA 性能，与 critic 引导及策略微调基线相当，且在跨任务统一引导强度下最鲁棒；相比 QGF，每步引导快 3.6×、参数少 7.0×，性能相当甚至更优。

Conclusion: critic 引导可以是轨迹感知且轻量的：AGF 在训练时具备更优的显存与吞吐扩展性，推理时每步仅需一次引导网络前向，无需 critic ensemble、反向传播或伴随计算。

Abstract: Flow-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are typically trained by behavior cloning and thus do not explicitly optimize long-term task return. Critic guidance steers generation toward higher-value actions, but existing methods differentiate the critic through a one-step surrogate of the sampler and back-propagate a critic ensemble at every flow step. In contrast, here we propose Adjoint Guidance Flow (AGF), which amortizes trajectory-aware critic guidance into a lightweight guidance network while preserving the pretrained VLA policy. Specifically, we formulate critic-guided flow generation as a deterministic optimal control problem, whose optimal guidance is a costate that carries the terminal critic gradient back through the remaining flow, and regress the guidance network onto this costate while keeping both the VLA and critic frozen. This design provides favorable memory and throughput scaling during training, and inference needs one guidance-network forward pass per step, without the critic ensemble, back-propagation, or adjoint computation. Across LIBERO, RoboCasa, and LIBERO-Pro, AGF consistently improves pretrained VLAs, remains competitive with critic-guidance and policy-fine-tuning baselines, and is the most robust method when a single guidance strength is deployed across tasks. Compared with QGF, AGF runs $3.6\times$ faster per guidance step with $7.0\times$ fewer parameters, with comparable and even better performance, showing that critic guidance can be trajectory-aware and lightweight.

</details>


### [209] [FlexiWorld: Learning and Planning via Flexible Action Chunks Across Multiple Time Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35138)
*Shidu Ren,Qilin Gu,Zhenghao Ni,Junhan Sun,Jiaqi Wang,Damien Scieur,Yunze Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlexiWorld 是一种基于 JEPA 的潜世界模型，通过混合目标跨度监督与可变长度动作块，提升长时程目标导向规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜世界模型多用固定长度动作块，且通常缺少目标条件动作生成，或仅用较短目标跨度进行监督，限制长时程控制与规划灵活性。

Method: 训练时采样不同目标跨度，并将动作随机划分为可变长度块；联合训练因果动作编码器（嵌入变长块）与自回归 actor（顺序生成原始动作）；采用 Student Forcing 在生成动作前缀上训练以缓解曝光偏差；规划时提出 Actor-Residual Cross-Entropy Method（ARCEM），结合动作残差搜索、块内自回归反馈与块边界潜状态预测。

Result: 在四个基准和不同目标距离上，FlexiWorld+ARCEM 平均成功率为 89.29%，最强基线为 83.98%。PushT 消融表明混合跨度监督、可变长度块和 Student Forcing 均提升直接控制。无需重训练即可支持不同规划块长：更长块使 ARCEM 平均加速约 1.3×，平均成功率相当。

Conclusion: FlexiWorld 通过混合跨度目标监督、变长动作块与 ARCEM 规划，在保持规划灵活性的同时显著提升长时程控制成功率与规划效率。

Abstract: Latent world models predict future states for goal-directed planning using action chunks spanning multiple primitive steps. Existing methods typically use fixed-length chunks and either omit goal-conditioned action generation or limit their supervision to short goal spans. We introduce FlexiWorld, a JEPA-based world model that combines mixed-span goal supervision with variable-length action chunks to improve long-horizon control. During training, we sample varying goal spans and randomly partition the actions into variable-length chunks. We jointly train the world model with a causal action encoder that embeds variable-length chunks and an autoregressive actor that generates primitive actions sequentially. Student Forcing reduces exposure bias by training on generated action prefixes. For planning, Actor-Residual Cross-Entropy Method (ARCEM) combines action-residual search with within-chunk autoregressive feedback and chunk-boundary latent prediction. Across four benchmarks and goal distances, FlexiWorld with ARCEM achieves 89.29% mean success, compared with 83.98% for the strongest baseline. PushT ablations show improved direct control from mixed-span supervision, variable-length chunks, and Student Forcing. Without retraining, FlexiWorld supports different planning chunk lengths: longer chunks accelerate ARCEM by approximately $1.3\times$ on average while maintaining comparable average success.

</details>


### [210] [$λ$-JEPA Spectral Anti-Collapse Regularization for Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35288)
*Berker Demirel,Clémentine Dominé,Valentino Maiorca,Marco Fumero,Marco Mondelli,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出谱反坍缩正则化器 SACReg，通过诱导 λ-balance 抑制骨干网络的维度坍缩，并构建 λ-JEPA，在图像分类、迁移学习及视频自监督基准上超越 LeJEPA、VISReg、LeVJEPA 与 V-JEPA 2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合嵌入自监督学习通常将增强视图间的不变性目标与防表示坍缩机制结合，但这些目标多施加于投影头之后，而下游任务使用的是投影器之前的骨干表示。作者发现这一目标与使用位置的不匹配并不必然阻止骨干发生维度坍缩，骨干可能保持较低的有效秩并限制下游迁移能力。

Method: 提出 SACReg，一种受 λ-balance（刻画跨层权重矩阵相对尺度）分析启发的谱反坍缩正则化器。在两层线性网络中证明：(i) λ-balance 可防止坍缩；(ii) 施加于骨干的正则化可诱导 λ-balance。非线性情形下该正则化同样带来反坍缩效果。将 SACReg 应用于 JEPA，构建 λ-JEPA。

Result: 在 ImageNet100 的真实架构上经验性地提升了表示的秩；在 ImageNet-1k 分类及八个下游图像数据集平均线性探测迁移性能上优于 LeJEPA 与 VISReg；在视频自监督学习上，于 Something-Something-v2 与 Kinetics-400 基准上优于 LeVJEPA 与 V-JEPA 2。

Conclusion: SACReg 通过对骨干表示施加谱正则化诱导 λ-balance，有效缓解维度坍缩并提升下游迁移与视频自监督性能；代码已开源。

Abstract: Joint-embedding self-supervised learning typically combines an invariance objective across augmented views with additional mechanisms to prevent representational collapse. These objectives are often applied after a projection head, while downstream tasks use the backbone representation before the projector. We find that this mismatch does not necessarily prevent dimensional collapse in the backbone, which can retain low effective rank and potentially limit downstream transfer. To address this, we introduce SACReg, a spectral anti-collapse regularizer motivated by an analysis of $\lambda$-balance, which captures the relative scale of weight matrices across layers. In a two-layer linear network, we show that (i) $\lambda$-balance prevents collapse, and (ii) our regularizer applied to the backbone induces $\lambda$-balance. In the nonlinear case, this regularizer leads to anti-collapse as well and, in realistic architectures on ImageNet100, it empirically increases the representations' ranks. We apply SACReg to JEPA and propose $\lambda$-JEPA, which improves over LeJEPA and VISReg on ImageNet-1k classification and in average linear-probe transfer performance across eight downstream image datasets. On video self-supervised learning, $\lambda$-JEPA improves over LeVJEPA and V-JEPA 2 on the Something-Something-v2 and Kinetics-400 benchmarks. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [211] [NeuronDiscover: Agent-in-Twin for Mechanistic Discovery in Neuronal Microenvironments with World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35338)
*Haowei Xu,Wanyi Fu,Hongbin Han,Zhaoheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuronDiscover：一种"孪生体中的智能体"框架，用共享的机制约束世界行动模型（WAM）联合进行预测、干预提议与观测设计，并通过独立裁决的结果修订机制–干预–观测–结果（MIOY）图，从而在神经元微环境中分离"真实机制改变"与"计算孪生体误差"两类混淆解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经元微环境中的机制发现需要能区分溶质输运与神经元响应竞争性解释的干预与测量。然而预测精度无法解决该问题：真实的机制改变与计算孪生体中的误差在稀疏观测下留下相同特征（即"孪生体混淆"）。作者形式化该混淆，并针对机制–偏差的联合信念设计实验以分离二者。

Method: 提出 NeuronDiscover 框架：其共享、机制约束的 World Action Model（WAM）耦合预测、干预提议与观测设计；独立裁决的结果用于修订带作用域的 MIOY（机制–干预–观测–结果）图，图中被支持的关系编译为可执行程序，并携带经偏差调整的可接受边界。

Result: 在由独立冻结细网格参考求解器裁决的模拟脑流体示踪输运世界，以及供体不相交的公开皮层神经元电流钳记录上评估：匹配 16 次实验/32 个源单元的预算下，仅计入达到认证终态的关系、并将弃权按未解决计分时，NeuronDiscover 每分配世界解决 4.0 个关系，最强基线 3.4，无图修订 3.2，假支持率 5%、作用域准确率 82%。机制–偏差联合获取解决 3.8 个关系（插件式期望信息增益为 2.9）；偏差调整验证将已接受程序失败率从 15% 降至 9%（接受覆盖率 60%）；迁移至记录数据得每分配世界 1.94 个关系（对比 1.53）。

Conclusion: NeuronDiscover 通过显式建模孪生体混淆并联合获取机制与偏差信念，在匹配预算下优于基线，且经偏差调整的验证提升了接受程序可靠性；该框架可迁移至真实电生理记录。所有正确性均在声明的模型世界与存档记录范围内裁决。

Abstract: Mechanistic discovery in neuronal microenvironments requires interventions and measurements that separate competing explanations of solute transport and neuronal response. Predictive accuracy cannot settle the question: a real mechanistic change and an error in the computational twin leave the same signature in sparse observations. We formalize this twin confounding and reason over a joint mechanism--discrepancy belief, designing experiments that separate the two. NeuronDiscover is an Agent-in-Twin framework whose shared, mechanism-grounded World Action Model (WAM) couples prediction, intervention proposals, and observation design; independently adjudicated outcomes revise a scoped Mechanism--Intervention--Observation--Outcome (MIOY) graph, whose supported relations compile into executable programs carrying discrepancy-adjusted acceptance bounds. We evaluate on simulated brain-fluid tracer-transport worlds adjudicated by an independently frozen finer-mesh reference solver, and on donor-disjoint public current-clamp recordings of cortical neurons. Counting only relations that reach a certified terminal status, and scoring abstentions as unresolved for every method, at a matched budget of 16 experiments over 32 source units NeuronDiscover resolves 4.0 relations per assigned world against 3.4 for the strongest baseline and 3.2 without graph revision, at 5% false support and 82% scope accuracy. Joint mechanism--discrepancy acquisition resolves 3.8 relations versus 2.9 for plug-in expected information gain; discrepancy-adjusted verification lowers accepted-program failure from 15% to 9% at 60% acceptance coverage; and transfer to the recordings yields 1.94 versus 1.53 relations per assigned world. Correctness is adjudicated within declared model worlds and archival recordings.

</details>


### [212] [Graph World Models for Constrained Epidemic Policy Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35545)
*Yiqi Su,Rashed Shelim,Lingyi Wang,Walid Saad,Naren Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EpiMind 是一个面向跨区域疫情政策规划的图世界模型框架：用图因子化循环状态空间模型生成策略条件下的联合推演，并用图-时序 ADMM 优化区域干预、通过投影保证共享资源每期可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疫情政策规划需在多个地理区域间协调，既要考虑人员流动带来的溢出效应，又要合理利用有限资源。现有方法存在两类缺陷：要么缺乏可动作条件化（action-conditioned）的耦合动力学建模，要么无法保证按期（per-period）可行性。

Method: 提出 EpiMind 图世界模型框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）图因子化循环状态空间模型（graph-factored recurrent state-space model），从各区域隐状态信念出发生成策略条件化的联合时序推演；（2）图-时序 ADMM（graph-temporal ADMM），在学到的模型下优化区域干预策略，并通过投影强制满足共享资源可行性约束，同时评估时序规范（temporal specifications）。

Result: 相对无图动力学建模，入院人数预测 RMSE 降低 29%；在保证共享预算可行性的前提下，规划结果与最优可行恒定策略的差距仅 1–5%；在真实情境评估中，于三种资源预算下均优于所有可部署基线方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明，图结构化的“策略想象”（policy imagination）配合显式的约束协调机制，能够基于学习到的动力学有效支撑疫情干预决策。

Abstract: Epidemic policy planning often requires coordination between geographical regions, taking into account mobility-driven spillovers and how to make use of limited resources. Existing methods either lack action-conditioned models of coupled dynamics or cannot guarantee per-period feasibility. We present EpiMind, a graph world model framework for constrained epidemic policy planning across regions. A graph-factored recurrent state-space model generates joint policy-conditioned rollouts from regional latent beliefs, while graph-temporal ADMM optimizes regional interventions, enforces shared-resource feasibility through projection, and evaluates temporal specifications under the learned model. EpiMind reduces admission RMSE by 29% relative to graph-free dynamics modeling, plans within 1-5% of the best feasible constant policy with guaranteed shared-budget feasibility, and outperforms all deployable baselines across three resource budgets in real-context evaluation. These results demonstrate that graph-structured policy imagination with explicit constrained coordination supports effective epidemic interventions from learned dynamics.

</details>


### [213] [Control-Geometry Straightening for Sampling-Based Latent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35603)
*Ziang Fu,Ning Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出控制几何拉直（CGS）辅助损失，通过让动作间的余弦相似度与对应潜变量差分间的余弦相似度一致，学习对规划器友好的潜世界模型表示，从而在有限采样预算下提升规划效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型规划中，仅保证转移预测准确并不等于规划目标容易优化；采样规划器在有限预算下仍可能难以找到好动作序列，因此需要直接塑造利于规划的潜空间控制几何。

Method: CGS 是一种单一辅助损失：仅利用像素-动作对的局部转移，匹配动作两两余弦相似度与对应潜变量差分两两余弦相似度。该方法可加在端到端学习或预训练表示的世界模型架构上。在线性动力学假设下，作者将目标与时间拉直及全规划时域内更均衡的终端代价曲率联系起来，并给出 MPPI 有限预算保证、CEM 局部收缩结果与梯度下降收敛界。

Result: 在四个控制环境和多种规划器上，CGS 用更少采样候选和细化步数提升规划表现；相比 LeWorldModel（LeWM）和其时间拉直变体（LeWM+TS），成功率最高分别提升 20 和 12.6 个百分点，采样规划器每次更新使用 128 个候选。探针实验、与 DINO-WM 架构的对比及规划器侧消融进一步说明潜运动组织、状态依赖性和动力学上下文如何影响规划行为。

Conclusion: 拉直控制几何能使好的动作序列在有限规划预算下更容易被找到，表明表示学习应直接面向规划优化难度，而不仅是转移预测精度。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures enable planning with latent world models, but accurate transition prediction alone does not ensure that the planning objective is easy to optimize. We introduce Control-Geometry Straightening (CGS), a single auxiliary loss that learns planner-friendly representations by directly straightening control geometry for sampling-efficient planning. CGS matches pairwise cosine similarities among actions to those among corresponding latent differences only using local transitions from pixel-action pairs. The loss can be applied across world-model architectures using end-to-end learned or pretrained representations. Under linear-dynamics, our theoretical analysis connects this objective to temporal straightening and more balanced terminal-cost curvature across the full planning horizon, yielding finite-budget guarantees for MPPI, local contraction results for CEM, and convergence bounds for gradient descent. Across four control environments and multiple planners, CGS improves planning with fewer sampled candidates and refinement steps, achieving success-rate gains up to 20 and 12.6 percentage points over LeWorldModel (LeWM) and its temporal-straightening variant (LeWM+TS), respectively, with sampling-based planners using 128 candidates per update. Probes, comparisons with DINO-WM architecture, and planner-side ablations clarify how latent motion organization, state dependence, and dynamical context shape planning behavior. Straightening control geometry thus makes good action sequences easier to find under limited planning budgets.

</details>
